--[[
================================================================================
  Orian  v1.0.0
  A complete, animated, themeable UI library for Roblox (Studio + live games)
================================================================================

  STRUCTURE (3 ModuleScripts)
     ORIANUI          (ModuleScript)  <- this file
    ├── Core          (Folder)
    │   └── Core      (ModuleScript)  <- the other half of the library, required
    └── CodeEngine    (ModuleScript)  <- language table, highlighter, autocomplete

  QUICK START
    local Aurora = require(path.to.AuroraUI)
    local Window = Aurora:CreateWindow({
        Title = "My Hub", Subtitle = "v1.0", Theme = "Midnight",
        Keybind = Enum.KeyCode.RightControl,
        Loading = { Duration = 3, Steps = {"Loading assets", "Building UI", "Ready"} },
        Settings = true,   -- adds the built-in Settings page
    })
    local Page = Window:AddPage({ Name = "Main" })
    Page:AddButton({ Title = "Hello", Callback = function() print("hi") end })

  WINDOW OPTIONS
    Title, Subtitle, Icon (image id), Size (UDim2/Vector2), Theme (name/table),
    Render ("3D" default: SurfaceGui on a camera part with real perspective tilt | "2D": ScreenGui),
    Effects.TiltAngle (degrees, default 6), Effects.TiltDirection (1 = lifts towards the cursor, -1 = presses away),
    SurfaceDistance (studs in front of the camera, default 8),
    Layout = { SidebarWidth (capped at 45% of the window), SidebarHeight (% of the window, 35-100),
               SidebarAlign ("Top"|"Middle"|"Bottom"), SidebarSide ("Left"|"Right"), HeaderSide ("Top"|"Bottom"), HeaderHeight,
               Brand ("Sidebar"|"Header"|"Hidden"), LogoSize, TitleSize, Subtitle, Credit, BrandOffsetX, BrandOffsetY }
             -- Settings > Layout edits it live; Aurora.LayoutPresets / Window:ApplyLayoutPreset(name) for named arrangements,
    Accent (Color3 override), Keybind (KeyCode), CornerRadius, FontFamily,
    Draggable (bool), SearchBar (bool), Settings (bool), Sounds (table),
    Effects (table of booleans, see list below), Loading (table or false):
      Loading = { Enabled, Title, Subtitle, Duration, Steps = {...}, Tips = {...},
                  Manual (drive it yourself with Window.Loader:SetProgress / :Finish),
                  Skippable, Style = "Rings" }

  PAGE / SECTION METHODS   (Section = Page:AddSection({Title=...}))
    AddTitle, AddText, AddButton, AddToggle, AddSlider, AddSelection,
    AddDropdown, AddKeybind, AddInput, AddSearchBox, AddColorPicker,
    AddSlideBox, AddCodeBox, AddDiscord, AddDivider, AddSection

  BACKGROUNDS   Window:SetBackground(name, { Intensity, Speed, Color, Image }) or Background = "Stars" on the window;
                Default, None, Stars, Snow, Rain, Bubbles, Fireflies, Shapes, Grid, Matrix, Aurora, Image; Aurora:RegisterBackground
  CUSTOMIZE     AddPage({ Icon, IconColor, Image, Badge, Tooltip }) + page:SetIcon / SetBadge / SetImage / SetName / SetVisible,
                Icon on rows, sections and notifications, ButtonIcon, Window:SetLogo / SetSubtitle / SetFooter / SetSize,
                Icon = "Custom" + CustomIcon = { Image, RectSize, RectOffset, Color } works anywhere an icon is accepted,
                Window:AddSidebarButton, Page:AddCustom, Aurora:RegisterElement, Aurora.Util
  AUTOMATIONS   Page:AddAutomation({ Name, When = {...}, If = {...}, Do = {...} }) or Page:AddAutomations({ Rules })
                or Window:AddAutomationsPage(); "When / If / Do" rule cards that run on the logic runtime
  LOGIC EDITOR  Window:AddLogicPage({ Name, Flag, Execute }) or Page:AddLogicEditor({ Title, Height, Flag, Execute })
                visual scripting: wire Events -> Logic / Flow -> Actions; Aurora:RegisterNode(id, def) adds custom nodes
  EXTRA ELEMENTS
    AddTabs, AddProgress, AddStat, AddImage, AddVideo, AddChips, AddTable, AddConsole,
    AddStepper, AddRangeSlider, AddGradient (gradient editor with add/drag/remove stops)
  PROMPTS       Window:Prompt / Confirm / PromptInput / PromptChoice
  PANELS        Window:CreatePanel({Title, Size, Offset}) -> floating panel (own camera part); Section:Detach()
  PROFILES      Window:SaveConfig(name) / LoadConfigFile(name) / ListConfigs() / DeleteConfig(name), AutoSave = true
  KEY SYSTEM    KeySystem = { Keys = {...} or KeyUrl = "...", Validate = fn, KeyLink = "...", SaveKey = true }
  EDITIONS      Aurora.Edition ("Free" | "Elite"), Aurora:Has(feature), Aurora.FeatureList, Aurora.EliteLink.
                This file is the Elite build; python3 build_free.py writes the Free one beside it.
                A preset carries what the other edition could not use, so a look survives the round trip.
  MORE          Window:AddThemeEditorPage(), Window:SetSoundPack(name), Aurora:AddLocale(code, strings) + Window:SetLanguage(code),
                Window:SetFontFamily("rbxasset://fonts/families/Inter.json"), Window:SetDebug(true),
                element:OnChanged(fn), element:Bind(table or instance, key), right-click any row to reset it,
                keyboard: Up/Down move, Enter activates, Left/Right adjust, Escape closes, "/" focuses search
  CODE BOX TABS AddCodeBox({ Tabs = true }) or Tabs = { {Name, Text, Language}, ... }; element:AddTab / SelectTab / GetTabs
  CODE BOX IDE  AddCodeBox({ Advanced = true, Files = { ["main.lua"] = "...", ["lib/util.lua"] = "..." },
                  OnRun = function(text, path) end, OnSave = function(path, text) end, OnFilesChanged = function(files) end })
                Explorer with folders + right-click menu (new/rename/delete), tabs per file, find & replace (Ctrl+F / Ctrl+H),
                go to line (Ctrl+G), save (Ctrl+S), duplicate line (Ctrl+D), delete line (Ctrl+Shift+K), move line (Alt+Up/Down),
                run (Ctrl+Enter), minimap, status bar, output panel (element:Print), element:GetFiles / SetFiles / OpenFile

  DEFAULT LOOK
    "Void" theme (near-black + lime), floating inset sidebar with accent-tinted active tab,
    separated element cards on pages, rows grouped inside section cards.

  BUTTONS
    Monochrome system: Primary = inverse (light on dark themes), Secondary = outlined, Ghost = text, Danger = outlined red,
    Accent = opt-in coloured. AddButton({Title}) renders a compact button; add Description or ButtonText for a row layout.

  DISCORD
    Window option  Discord = { Invite = "abc123", Title = "Join the Discord" }  -> sidebar button
    Element        Page:AddDiscord({ Invite = "abc123", Title = ..., Description = ... })
    Copies the link to the clipboard where allowed (setclipboard), otherwise shows it in a notification.

  WINDOW METHODS
    AddPage, SelectPage, SearchPages(query), AddSettingsPage, Notify,
    Toggle / Open / Close / Minimize, SetTheme, SetAccent, SetScale,
    SetCornerRadius, SetTransparency, SetFontFamily, SetKeybind,
    MakeDraggable, GetConfig, LoadConfig, Destroy

  VISUAL EFFECTS (all in Window.Effects, all live-toggleable from Settings)
    Tilt             Real 3D tilt: the camera-attached panel rotates towards the cursor
                     (TiltStrength / TiltAngle in Effects); plus depth layers + specular
    Sway             Spring-damped follow: the panel lags a touch behind camera motion
    Float            Slow idle drift + micro-roll while the cursor is away
    DepthLighting    Lit top edge and vertical shade that swing with the tilt
    ElementDepth     Rows lift slightly on hover; buttons press in and spring back
    Parallax         UI layers float at different depths above a deep background (stars, blobs); DepthAmount scales it
    Ripples          Material click ripples on every interactive element
    Shadows          Soft single-layer drop shadow under the window
    Glow             Animated gradient border stroke around the window
    Hover            Hover highlight + lift on every element
    Spotlight        Cursor-following light spot inside hovered elements
    Blur             Screen blur (Lighting.BlurEffect) while the window is open
    Particles        Drifting particles in the loading screen
    AnimatedGradient Slowly rotating accent gradients on titles & strokes
    Tooltips         Hover tooltips
    Sounds           Click / hover / toggle / notify sounds
    OpenAnimation    Slow spring fly-in / fly-out when toggling the window; OpenDuration scales how long it takes
    PageTransitions  Slide + fade when switching pages
    CameraInertia    The panel swings with a spring when the camera turns, then settles
    Magnet           The panel leans a few pixels towards the cursor in 3D space
    DragTilt         Dragging the window leans it in the direction of travel
    PressDepth       Clicking pushes the whole panel slightly away from the camera
    HoverDepth       Hovering pulls the panel slightly towards the camera
    PageSwing        Switching pages swings the panel around its vertical axis
    OpenSpin         The fly-in on open also turns the panel into place
    DepthOfField     Blurs the world behind the panel while it is open (Lighting.DepthOfFieldEffect)
    WorldDim         Desaturates and dims the world while open (ColorCorrectionEffect)
    CameraZoom       Narrows the camera field of view slightly while open, restored on close
    ElementEntrance  Cards pop in one after another when a page or section opens
    Acrylic          Frosted glass: the world behind the window, panels and notifications is blurred.
                     Uses Dependencies/UIBlur when it is inside this module (rounded corners), otherwise a built-in
                     glass part. Needs graphics quality 8+. While on it replaces Blur and DepthOfField, because
                     Roblox only renders one depth-of-field effect. AcrylicStrength (0-1), AcrylicTint (see-through)
================================================================================
]]

local TweenService     = game:GetService("TweenService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local RunService       = game:GetService("RunService")
local TextService      = game:GetService("TextService")
local Players          = game:GetService("Players")
local Lighting         = game:GetService("Lighting")
local CoreGui          = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local GuiService       = game:GetService("GuiService")

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local Aurora = {}
Aurora.__index = Aurora
Aurora.Version = "1.0.0"
-- Bumped when the shape of a saved file changes. Every file Aurora writes carries it, and every
-- loader accepts files without it (they were written before this existed).
Aurora.FileVersion = 1
Aurora.AnimationSpeed = 1
Aurora.EffectList = {
	"Tilt", "Sway", "Float", "DepthLighting", "ElementDepth", "EdgeGlow", "Parallax", "Ripples", "Shadows", "Glow",
	"Hover", "Spotlight", "Blur", "Particles", "AnimatedGradient", "Tooltips", "Sounds", "OpenAnimation", "PageTransitions",
	"KeyboardNavigation", "RightClickReset",
	"CameraInertia", "Magnet", "DragTilt", "PressDepth", "HoverDepth", "PageSwing", "OpenSpin",
	"DepthOfField", "WorldDim", "CameraZoom", "ElementEntrance", "Acrylic",
}
-- No bundled sound ids: pass your own audio asset ids through the Sounds option or Aurora.SoundPacks.
Aurora.DefaultSounds = { Click = "", Hover = "", Toggle = "", Notify = "" }

-- ============================================================================
-- EDITIONS  (Free and Elite)
-- ============================================================================
-- One source builds both. build_free.py strips every --@elite-begin / --@elite-end region and
-- uncomments every "--@free:" line, so the Free module does not merely hide the Elite features: it
-- does not contain them, and no flag in it can bring them back. What stays in both builds is
-- Aurora:Has(feature), so a script written against Elite still builds on Free: a missing element
-- becomes a row that says what it needs, keeps its flag value, and answers the usual calls quietly.
Aurora.Edition = "Elite"
Aurora.Edition = "Free"

Aurora.FeatureList = {
	"CodeBox",            -- code boxes, and the CodeEngine runtime behind them
	"Logic",              -- logic editor, automations, the logic hub page
	"ThemeEditor",        -- the live colour editor page
	"ThemeFiles",         -- saving, loading and sharing themes as JSON
	"SlideBox",           -- the two-axis (XY) slider
	"Acrylic",            -- frosted glass behind the window
	"DepthOfField",       -- camera depth of field while the window is open
	"BackgroundPack",     -- every background beyond Stars, Fireflies and Shapes
	"BackgroundStudio",   -- build, name, save and share your own backgrounds
	"KeySecurity",        -- remote key lists, server validation, binding, expiry, sealed key files
	"AutoOptimize",       -- hold a frame rate by moving the optimization level itself
	"PageOptimization",   -- per-page optimization overrides, pins and trim reports
	"AdvancedEffects",    -- the per-effect sliders and the full effect toggle list
}

Aurora.Features = {}
for _, feature in ipairs(Aurora.FeatureList) do
	Aurora.Features[feature] = true
	Aurora.Features[feature] = false
end

-- Aurora:Has("Logic") -> can this build do that?
function Aurora:Has(feature)
	return Aurora.Features[feature] == true
end

function Aurora:IsElite()
	return Aurora.Edition ~= "Free"
end

-- How locked features introduce themselves. Point EliteLink at your purchase page and every lock
-- row offers it; leave it empty and the rows simply say what is missing.
Aurora.EliteName = "Elite"
Aurora.EliteLink = ""
Aurora.EliteTagline = "Part of AuroraUI Elite"
Aurora.FeatureNames = {
	CodeBox = "Code boxes", Logic = "Logic editor and automations", ThemeEditor = "Theme editor",
	ThemeFiles = "Saving and sharing themes", SlideBox = "Two-axis sliders", Acrylic = "Acrylic blur",
	DepthOfField = "Depth of field", BackgroundPack = "The full background pack",
	BackgroundStudio = "Background studio", KeySecurity = "Hardened key system",
	AutoOptimize = "Automatic optimization", PageOptimization = "Per-page optimization",
	AdvancedEffects = "Per-effect controls",
}

-- Effect keys only an Elite build can honour. A Free build never writes these into a preset, so a
-- look shared Free -> Elite cannot quietly switch them off: it carries them through untouched.
Aurora.EliteEffectKeys = {
	Acrylic = "Acrylic", AcrylicStrength = "Acrylic", AcrylicTint = "Acrylic",
	DepthOfField = "DepthOfField", DepthOfFieldAmount = "DepthOfField",
}

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- CodeEngine (child module)
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
local CodeEngine = nil
do
	local child = script:FindFirstChild("CodeEngine")
	if child then
		local ok, result = pcall(require, child)
		if ok then
			CodeEngine = result
		else
			warn("[AuroraUI] CodeEngine failed to load: " .. tostring(result))
		end
	else
		warn("[AuroraUI] CodeEngine module not found under AuroraUI. CodeBox will run in plain mode.")
	end
end

-- ============================================================================
-- UTILITIES
-- ============================================================================
local function Create(className, props, children)
	local inst = Instance.new(className)
	local parent = nil
	if props then
		for key, value in pairs(props) do
			if key == "Parent" then
				parent = value
			else
				inst[key] = value
			end
		end
	end
	if children then
		for _, child in ipairs(children) do
			child.Parent = inst
		end
	end
	if parent then
		inst.Parent = parent
	end
	return inst
end

-- Where transparency tweens are heading, per object, so a fade that starts while a hover or theme tween is still
-- running can read the value the object was going to rest at instead of the one it was passing through.
-- FadeTree's own tweens are transient and stay out of this (they are the thing being corrected for).
local TweenTargets = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local FadeProps = {
	BackgroundTransparency = true, TextTransparency = true, TextStrokeTransparency = true,
	ImageTransparency = true, ScrollBarImageTransparency = true, Transparency = true,
}

local function Tween(inst, props, time, style, direction, transient)
	local speed = Aurora.AnimationSpeed
	if type(speed) ~= "number" or speed <= 0 then
		speed = 1
	end
	local info = TweenInfo.new((time or 0.25) / speed, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, direction or Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
	local tween = TweenService:Create(inst, info, props)
	if not transient then
		local tracked = nil
		for key, value in pairs(props) do
			if FadeProps[key] then
				tracked = tracked or TweenTargets[inst] or {}
				tracked[key] = { Value = value, Tween = tween }
			end
		end
		if tracked then
			TweenTargets[inst] = tracked
			tween.Completed:Once(function()
				local current = TweenTargets[inst]
				if not current then
					return
				end
				for key in pairs(props) do
					local entry = current[key]
					if entry and entry.Tween == tween then   -- a newer tween on the same property keeps its own entry
						current[key] = nil
					end
				end
				if next(current) == nil then
					TweenTargets[inst] = nil
				end
			end)
		end
	end
	tween:Play()
	return tween
end

-- The value an object is resting at, or heading to if a tween is in flight.
local function RestingValue(obj, key)
	local tracked = TweenTargets[obj]
	local entry = tracked and tracked[key]
	if entry then
		return entry.Value
	end
	return obj[key]
end

-- How long a Tween of `time` really takes, so task.delay stays in step with the animation speed slider.
local function Timed(time)
	local speed = Aurora.AnimationSpeed
	if type(speed) ~= "number" or speed <= 0 then
		speed = 1
	end
	return time / speed
end

local function Lerp(a, b, t)
	return a + (b - a) * t
end

local function RoundTo(value, increment)
	if not increment or increment <= 0 then
		return value
	end
	return math.floor(value / increment + 0.5) * increment
end

local function FormatNumber(value, decimals, digits, prefix, suffix)
	decimals = math.max(0, math.floor(decimals or 0))
	local s = string.format("%." .. decimals .. "f", value)
	if s == "-0" or s:match("^%-0%.0+$") then
		s = s:sub(2)
	end
	if digits and digits > 0 then
		local sign = ""
		if s:sub(1, 1) == "-" then
			sign = "-"
			s = s:sub(2)
		end
		local intPart, rest = s:match("^(%d+)(.*)$")
		if intPart then
			while #intPart < digits do
				intPart = "0" .. intPart
			end
			s = sign .. intPart .. rest
		end
	end
	return (prefix or "") .. s .. (suffix or "")
end

local function DeepCopy(tbl)
	local copy = {}
	for key, value in pairs(tbl) do
		if type(value) == "table" then
			copy[key] = DeepCopy(value)
		else
			copy[key] = value
		end
	end
	return copy
end

local function Merge(base, override)
	local out = DeepCopy(base)
	if override then
		for key, value in pairs(override) do
			if type(value) == "table" and type(out[key]) == "table" then
				out[key] = Merge(out[key], value)
			else
				out[key] = value
			end
		end
	end
	return out
end

local function IsClick(input)
	return input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
end

local function IsMove(input)
	return input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
end

local function SafeCall(fn, ...)
	if type(fn) ~= "function" then
		return
	end
	local args = { ... }
	task.spawn(function()
		local ok, err = pcall(fn, unpack(args))
		if not ok then
			warn("[AuroraUI] Callback error: " .. tostring(err))
		end
	end)
end

local function Alive(inst)
	return inst and inst.Parent ~= nil
end

-- Maid: collects connections / instances / functions for cleanup
local Maid = {}
Maid.__index = Maid

function Maid.new()
	return setmetatable({ _items = {} }, Maid)
end

function Maid:Add(item)
	self._items[#self._items + 1] = item
	return item
end

function Maid:Clean()
	for index = #self._items, 1, -1 do
		local item = self._items[index]
		local kind = typeof(item)
		if kind == "RBXScriptConnection" then
			item:Disconnect()
		elseif kind == "Instance" then
			item:Destroy()
		elseif kind == "function" then
			pcall(item)
		elseif kind == "table" then
			if type(item.Destroy) == "function" then
				pcall(item.Destroy, item)
			elseif type(item.Clean) == "function" then
				pcall(item.Clean, item)
			end
		end
		self._items[index] = nil
	end
end

-- The resting value of one property, cached in an attribute. fresh = true reads it again from the object (or from
-- the tween it is following); otherwise the cache is only filled the first time the object is seen.
local function RestingCached(obj, attr, key, fresh)
	local cached = obj:GetAttribute(attr)
	if fresh or cached == nil then
		cached = RestingValue(obj, key)
		obj:SetAttribute(attr, cached)
	end
	return cached
end

-- Fades every visual property of a tree towards fully transparent (alpha = 1) or back to its resting values
-- (alpha = 0). Resting values are cached the first time an object is seen; recapture = true reads them again,
-- except on objects still inside an earlier fade (their AuroraFadeUntil stamp is in the future), because what such
-- an object shows right now is a moment of a transition, not where it rests. markUntil writes that stamp, so a fade
-- that is about to start protects its objects from a recapture by a fade that starts a little later.
local function FadeTree(root, alpha, time, style, recapture, markUntil)
	local objects = root:GetDescendants()
	table.insert(objects, root)
	local now = os.clock()
	for _, obj in ipairs(objects) do
		local isGui = obj:IsA("GuiObject")
		if isGui or obj:IsA("UIStroke") then
			local fresh = recapture and ((obj:GetAttribute("AuroraFadeUntil") or 0) <= now)
			if markUntil then
				obj:SetAttribute("AuroraFadeUntil", markUntil)
			end
			local props = {}
			if isGui then
				props.BackgroundTransparency = Lerp(RestingCached(obj, "AuroraBG", "BackgroundTransparency", fresh), 1, alpha)
				if obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("TextBox") then
					props.TextTransparency = Lerp(RestingCached(obj, "AuroraTT", "TextTransparency", fresh), 1, alpha)
					-- a stroke is only tracked once it has been seen below 1; after that it is always restored
					if obj:GetAttribute("AuroraTST") ~= nil or RestingValue(obj, "TextStrokeTransparency") < 1 then
						props.TextStrokeTransparency = Lerp(RestingCached(obj, "AuroraTST", "TextStrokeTransparency", fresh), 1, alpha)
					end
				elseif obj:IsA("ImageLabel") or obj:IsA("ImageButton") then
					props.ImageTransparency = Lerp(RestingCached(obj, "AuroraIT", "ImageTransparency", fresh), 1, alpha)
				elseif obj:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
					props.ScrollBarImageTransparency = Lerp(RestingCached(obj, "AuroraST", "ScrollBarImageTransparency", fresh), 1, alpha)
				end
			else
				props.Transparency = Lerp(RestingCached(obj, "AuroraUT", "Transparency", fresh), 1, alpha)
			end
			if time and time > 0 then
				Tween(obj, props, time, style, nil, true)
			else
				for key, value in pairs(props) do
					obj[key] = value
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

-- "Phone" | "Tablet" | "Desktop". Touch devices are split by screen size, the way Roblox's own layouts do it.
local function DetectDevice()
	if not UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
		return "Desktop"
	end
	if UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled and UserInputService.MouseEnabled then
		return "Desktop"
	end
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	local viewport = camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
	local short, long = math.min(viewport.X, viewport.Y), math.max(viewport.X, viewport.Y)
	if short >= 620 or long >= 1100 then
		return "Tablet"
	end
	return "Phone"
end
Aurora.GetDevice = DetectDevice
Aurora._Tween, Aurora._RestingValue = Tween, RestingValue   -- for Tests.lua

	},
	Smooth = {

		Float = false, Sway = true, OpenSpin = false, PageSwing = false, PageTransitions = true, ElementEntrance = true, Magnet = true,
	},
	["Reduced motion"] = {

	},
}

-- ============================================================================
-- OPTIMIZATION LEVELS
-- One dial, ten steps. 1 is the look this library was designed around and changes nothing.
-- 2 and 3 trim work you cannot see. 4 to 7 give up effects in the order they cost the most for
-- what they add. 8 upwards also hides the heaviest pages the player made, worst first.
-- Each level lists only what it changes; Window:SetOptimization applies them on top of the window's
-- own settings, so going back to 1 restores exactly what the author asked for.
-- ============================================================================
Aurora.OptimizationLevels = {
	[1] = { Label = "Full", Note = "Everything on, the way it was designed" },
	[2] = { Label = "Tidy", Note = "Cheap wins only, nothing you can see",
		Effects = { BackgroundRate = 30, Spotlight = false }, StepSkip = 0, PageBudget = nil },
	[3] = { Label = "Light", Note = "Still looks the same, steadier frames",
		Effects = { BackgroundRate = 24, Spotlight = false, AnimatedGradient = false, EdgeGlow = false }, StepSkip = 0 },
	[4] = { Label = "Balanced", Note = "Screen blur and depth of field go",

-- ============================================================================
-- PRESETS
-- A preset is a plain table of strings, numbers and booleans, so it survives JSONEncode and can be
-- written by hand, saved to a file or pasted from someone else:
--   Aurora:RegisterPreset("My look", {
--       Theme = "Midnight", Accent = "#7AA2FF", Font = "Inter", Scale = 1, CornerRadius = 12,
--       Transparency = 0.2, Size = { 680, 480 }, Perspective = 8, Motion = "Cinematic",
--       AnimationSpeed = 1, Keybind = "RightControl",
--       Background = { Name = "Stars", Intensity = 1.2, Speed = 0.8, Color = "Accent" },
--       Effects = { Acrylic = true, DepthAmount = 1.4 },
--       Flags = { AutoFarm = true },        -- optional: values for elements that have a Flag
--   })
-- Window:ApplyPreset("My look") / :GetPreset() / :ExportPreset() / :ImportPreset(json)
-- Window:SavePreset(name) / :LoadPreset(name) / :ListPresets() / :DeletePreset(name)
-- The same preset written as JSON works just as well:
--   Aurora:RegisterPreset("Team DJ", '{ "Theme": "Void", "Accent": "#92d91f", "Size": [590, 510] }')
-- ============================================================================
Aurora.Presets = {}
Aurora.PresetOrder = {}

function Aurora:RegisterPreset(name, data)
	if type(data) == "string" then
		-- a preset handed over as JSON: decode it once here so every other path still sees a plain table
		local decoded = Aurora:ParsePreset(data)
		if not decoded then
			warn("[AuroraUI] RegisterPreset: '" .. tostring(name) .. "' is a string but not valid preset JSON")
			return nil
		end
		data = decoded
	end
	if type(data) ~= "table" then
		warn("[AuroraUI] RegisterPreset needs a table or a JSON string for '" .. tostring(name) .. "'")
		return nil
	end
	data.Name = name
	if not Aurora.Presets[name] then
		table.insert(Aurora.PresetOrder, name)
	end
	Aurora.Presets[name] = data
	return data
end

function Aurora:GetPresetNames()
	local names = {}
	for _, name in ipairs(Aurora.PresetOrder) do
		names[#names + 1] = name
	end
	return names
end

function Aurora:EncodePreset(preset)
	local ok, json = pcall(function()
		return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode(preset)
	end)
	if not ok then
		warn("[AuroraUI] Could not encode the preset: " .. tostring(json))
		return nil
	end
	return json
end

-- Carried holds what an earlier build had no code for. It is laid underneath the preset, so the build
-- that does have the code applies it as though it had been written there all along, and the build that
-- does not simply carries it again.
function Aurora.UnpackCarried(preset)
	if type(preset) ~= "table" or type(preset.Carried) ~= "table" then
		return preset
	end
	local merged = Merge(DeepCopy(preset.Carried), preset)
	merged.Carried = nil
	return merged
end

-- What a background the build does not have falls back to.
Aurora.FallbackBackground = "Stars"

function Aurora:ParsePreset(json)
	if type(json) ~= "string" or json == "" then
		return nil
	end
	local ok, decoded = pcall(function()
		return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(json)
	end)
	if ok and type(decoded) == "table" then
		return decoded
	end
	return nil
end

local function ColorToHex(color)
	if typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then
		return nil
	end
	local ok, hex = pcall(function()
		return color:ToHex()
	end)
	if ok and type(hex) == "string" then
		return "#" .. hex
	end
	return string.format("#%02X%02X%02X", math.floor(color.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(color.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(color.B * 255 + 0.5))
end

-- accepts "#rrggbb", "rrggbb", a Color3, or { 122, 162, 255 }
local function HexToColor(value)
	if typeof(value) == "Color3" then
		return value
	end
	if type(value) == "table" and tonumber(value[1]) then
		return Color3.fromRGB(tonumber(value[1]) or 0, tonumber(value[2]) or 0, tonumber(value[3]) or 0)
	end
	if type(value) ~= "string" then
		return nil
	end
	local hex = value:gsub("#", ""):gsub("%s", "")
	if #hex == 6 and hex:match("^%x+$") then
		local ok, color = pcall(function()
			return Color3.fromHex(hex)
		end)
		if ok then
			return color
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function GetGuiParent()
	if not RunService:IsStudio() then
		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			local probe = Instance.new("Folder")
			probe.Parent = CoreGui
			probe:Destroy()
			return CoreGui
		end)
		if ok and result then
			return result
		end
	end
	if LocalPlayer then
		return LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
	end
	return CoreGui
end

local function KeyName(keyCode)
	if not keyCode or keyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
		return "None"
	end
	local name = keyCode.Name
	local pretty = {
		LeftControl = "L-Ctrl", RightControl = "R-Ctrl", LeftShift = "L-Shift", RightShift = "R-Shift",
		LeftAlt = "L-Alt", RightAlt = "R-Alt", Return = "Enter", BackSpace = "Backspace",
	}
	return pretty[name] or name
end

-- ============================================================================
-- PROCEDURAL GLYPHS (no image assets needed)
-- ============================================================================
local function MakeChevron(parent, size, color, direction)
	size = size or 12
	local holder = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Chevron", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size),
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Parent = parent,
	})
	local len = math.floor(size * 0.62)
	local spread = math.floor(len * 0.33)
	local function bar(rotation, x, y)
		return Create("Frame", {
			BackgroundColor3 = color, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(len, 2), Rotation = rotation,
			Position = UDim2.new(0.5, x, 0.5, y), Parent = holder,
		}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
	end
	local a, b
	if direction == "Down" then
		a = bar(45, -spread, 0)
		b = bar(-45, spread, 0)
	elseif direction == "Up" then
		a = bar(-45, -spread, 0)
		b = bar(45, spread, 0)
	elseif direction == "Left" then
		a = bar(-45, 0, -spread)
		b = bar(45, 0, spread)
	else -- Right
		a = bar(45, 0, -spread)
		b = bar(-45, 0, spread)
	end
	return holder, { a, b }
end

local function MakeCheck(parent, size, color)
	size = size or 12
	local holder = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Check", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size),
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Parent = parent,
	})
	local short = Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = color, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(size * 0.42), 2), Rotation = 45,
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -size * 0.22, 0.5, size * 0.12), Parent = holder,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
	local long = Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = color, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(size * 0.75), 2), Rotation = -50,
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, size * 0.12, 0.5, -size * 0.02), Parent = holder,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
	return holder, { short, long }
end

local function MakeSearchIcon(parent, size, color)
	size = size or 14
	local holder = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "SearchIcon", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size),
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Parent = parent,
	})
	local ring = Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size * 0.62, size * 0.62),
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Parent = holder,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
	local stroke = Create("UIStroke", { Color = color, Thickness = 2, Parent = ring })
	local handle = Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = color, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size * 0.42, 2), Rotation = 45,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, size * 0.74, 0, size * 0.74), Parent = holder,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
	return holder, { stroke, handle }
end

local function CodeColors(bg, gutter, text, keyword, builtin, str, num, comment, op, call, prop, line)
	return {
		Background = bg, Gutter = gutter, Text = text, Keyword = keyword, Builtin = builtin,
		String = str, Number = num, Comment = comment, Operator = op, Call = call,
		Property = prop, CurrentLine = line,
	}
end
