			Color3.fromRGB(130, 170, 240), Color3.fromRGB(240, 140, 130), Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)),
	},
	-- pure black, white accent
	Amoled = {
		Background = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0), Secondary = Color3.fromRGB(5, 5, 6),
		Element = Color3.fromRGB(13, 13, 15), ElementHover = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 25),
		ElementActive = Color3.fromRGB(31, 31, 35), Stroke = Color3.fromRGB(36, 36, 41),
		Text = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 247), SubText = Color3.fromRGB(140, 140, 150),
		Placeholder = Color3.fromRGB(92, 92, 102), Accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
		AccentSecondary = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 210), AccentText = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
		Success = Color3.fromRGB(52, 199, 123), Warning = Color3.fromRGB(240, 165, 48),
		Error = Color3.fromRGB(235, 87, 87), Shadow = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 40),
		Code = CodeColors(Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0), Color3.fromRGB(5, 5, 6), Color3.fromRGB(232, 232, 236),
			Color3.fromRGB(210, 160, 255), Color3.fromRGB(110, 210, 230), Color3.fromRGB(160, 220, 140),
			Color3.fromRGB(240, 180, 110), Color3.fromRGB(96, 96, 106), Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 192),
			Color3.fromRGB(120, 180, 255), Color3.fromRGB(250, 130, 140), Color3.fromRGB(11, 11, 14)),
	},
	-- light neutral, blue accent
	Light = {
		Background = Color3.fromRGB(250, 250, 250), Secondary = Color3.fromRGB(244, 244, 245),
		Element = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), ElementHover = Color3.fromRGB(243, 244, 246),
		ElementActive = Color3.fromRGB(233, 234, 238), Stroke = Color3.fromRGB(226, 226, 230),
		Text = Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 27), SubText = Color3.fromRGB(113, 113, 122),
		Placeholder = Color3.fromRGB(161, 161, 170), Accent = Color3.fromRGB(37, 99, 235),
		AccentSecondary = Color3.fromRGB(59, 130, 246), AccentText = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
		Success = Color3.fromRGB(22, 163, 74), Warning = Color3.fromRGB(217, 119, 6),
		Error = Color3.fromRGB(220, 38, 38), Shadow = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 60),
		Code = CodeColors(Color3.fromRGB(252, 252, 253), Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 247), Color3.fromRGB(36, 36, 42),
			Color3.fromRGB(166, 38, 164), Color3.fromRGB(1, 132, 188), Color3.fromRGB(80, 161, 79),
			Color3.fromRGB(152, 104, 1), Color3.fromRGB(160, 161, 167), Color3.fromRGB(56, 58, 66),
			Color3.fromRGB(64, 120, 242), Color3.fromRGB(228, 86, 73), Color3.fromRGB(243, 244, 246)),
	},
}
Aurora.Themes.Rose = Aurora.Themes.Ember   -- old name kept for compatibility

-- the themes that ship with the library; saved themes are added on top and can be deleted again, these cannot
Aurora.BuiltInThemes = {}
for name in pairs(Aurora.Themes) do
	Aurora.BuiltInThemes[name] = true
end

function Aurora:AddTheme(name, theme)
	Aurora.Themes[name] = Merge(Aurora.Themes.Midnight, theme)
end

function Aurora:GetThemeNames()
	local names = {}
	for name in pairs(Aurora.Themes) do
		names[#names + 1] = name
	end
	table.sort(names)
	return names
end

-- ============================================================================
-- FONTS
-- ============================================================================
Aurora.FontFamilies = {
	Gotham      = { Regular = "Gotham", Medium = "GothamMedium", Bold = "GothamBold" },
	BuilderSans = { Regular = "BuilderSans", Medium = "BuilderSansMedium", Bold = "BuilderSansBold" },
	SourceSans  = { Regular = "SourceSans", Medium = "SourceSansSemibold", Bold = "SourceSansBold" },
	Arimo       = { Regular = "Arimo", Medium = "Arimo", Bold = "ArimoBold" },
	Ubuntu      = { Regular = "Ubuntu", Medium = "Ubuntu", Bold = "Ubuntu" },
	Roboto      = { Regular = "Roboto", Medium = "Roboto", Bold = "Roboto" },
}

local function ResolveFont(name, fallback)
	local ok, font = pcall(function()
		return Enum.Font[name]
	end)
	if ok and font then
		return font
	end
	return fallback or Enum.Font.Gotham
end

local function BuildFonts(familyName)
	local family = Aurora.FontFamilies[familyName] or Aurora.FontFamilies.Gotham
	return {
		Regular = ResolveFont(family.Regular, Enum.Font.Gotham),
		Medium = ResolveFont(family.Medium, Enum.Font.GothamMedium),
		Bold = ResolveFont(family.Bold, Enum.Font.GothamBold),
		Code = ResolveFont("Code", Enum.Font.Code),
	}
end

-- ============================================================================
-- DRAGGABLE
-- ============================================================================
-- Aurora:MakeDraggable(object, { Handle=, Smooth=, SmoothTime=, Bounds=, Axis="X"/"Y",
--                                OnDragStart=, OnDrag=, OnDragEnd= })
function Aurora:MakeDraggable(object, options)
	options = options or {}
	local handle = options.Handle or object
	local maid = Maid.new()
	local enabled = true
	local dragging = false
	local dragInput, dragStart, startPos = nil, nil, nil

	local function clampToParent(pos)
		local parent = object.Parent
		if not parent or not (parent:IsA("GuiObject") or parent:IsA("ScreenGui")) then
			return pos
		end
		local parentSize = parent.AbsoluteSize
		local size = object.AbsoluteSize
		local anchor = object.AnchorPoint
		local topLeftX = parentSize.X * pos.X.Scale + pos.X.Offset - anchor.X * size.X
		local topLeftY = parentSize.Y * pos.Y.Scale + pos.Y.Offset - anchor.Y * size.Y
		topLeftX = math.clamp(topLeftX, 0, math.max(0, parentSize.X - size.X))
		topLeftY = math.clamp(topLeftY, 0, math.max(0, parentSize.Y - size.Y))
		return UDim2.new(pos.X.Scale, topLeftX + anchor.X * size.X - parentSize.X * pos.X.Scale,
			pos.Y.Scale, topLeftY + anchor.Y * size.Y - parentSize.Y * pos.Y.Scale)
	end

	local function update(input)
		local delta = input.Position - dragStart
		local dx, dy = delta.X, delta.Y
		if options.Axis == "X" then
			dy = 0
		elseif options.Axis == "Y" then
			dx = 0
		end
		local newPos = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + dx, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + dy)
		if options.Bounds then
			newPos = clampToParent(newPos)
		end
		if options.Smooth then
			Tween(object, { Position = newPos }, options.SmoothTime or 0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		else
			object.Position = newPos
		end
		if options.OnDrag then
			options.OnDrag(newPos)
		end
	end

	maid:Add(handle.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if not enabled or not IsClick(input) then
			return
		end
		dragging = true
		dragStart = input.Position
		startPos = object.Position
		if options.OnDragStart then
			options.OnDragStart()
		end
		local conn
		conn = input.Changed:Connect(function()
			if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
				dragging = false
				conn:Disconnect()
				if options.OnDragEnd then
					options.OnDragEnd(object.Position)
				end
			end
		end)
	end))

	maid:Add(handle.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if IsMove(input) then
			dragInput = input
		end
	end))

	maid:Add(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if dragging and enabled and input == dragInput then
			update(input)
		end
	end))

	return {
		Enable = function() enabled = true end,
		Disable = function() enabled = false; dragging = false end,
		IsDragging = function() return dragging end,
		Destroy = function() maid:Clean() end,
	}
end

-- ============================================================================
-- LOADING SCREEN
-- ============================================================================
local Loader = {}
Loader.__index = Loader

local DefaultTips = {
	"Tip: Press the keybind to hide or show the window.",
	"Tip: Use the sidebar search to jump to any page or element.",
	"Tip: Open Settings to change the theme and effects live.",
	"Tip: Click anywhere on a slider to snap the value there.",
	"Tip: In the code box, press Tab to accept an autocomplete suggestion.",
}

local function Keypoint(t, color)
	return ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(t, color)
end

local function NumKey(t, value)
	return NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(t, value)
end

function Aurora:_CreateLoader(window, cfg)
	cfg = cfg or {}
	local theme = window.Theme
	local fonts = window.Fonts
	local self = setmetatable({
		Window = window, Config = cfg, Maid = Maid.new(), Alive = true, Done = false,
		Progress = 0, _display = 0, _stepIndex = 0, _t0 = os.clock(), _status = "",
	}, Loader)

	local duration = math.max(0.5, cfg.Duration or 3.2)
	local steps = cfg.Steps or { "Initializing", "Loading assets", "Building interface", "Applying theme", "Ready" }
	local tips = cfg.Tips or DefaultTips

	local gui = Create("ScreenGui", {
		Name = "AuroraLoader", IgnoreGuiInset = true, ResetOnSpawn = false,
		ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling, DisplayOrder = 10000, Parent = window.GuiParent,
	})
	self.Gui = gui
	self.Maid:Add(gui)

	-- backdrop with slowly rotating gradient
	local backdrop = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Backdrop", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = theme.Background,
		BorderSizePixel = 0, Parent = gui,
	})
	local bgGradient = Create("UIGradient", {
		Rotation = 30,
		Color = ColorSequence.new({ Keypoint(0, theme.Background), Keypoint(0.5, theme.Secondary), Keypoint(1, theme.Background) }),
		Parent = backdrop,
	})

	-- glowing blobs (parallax)
	local blobs = {}
	local blobSpecs = {
		{ color = theme.Accent, size = 520, x = 0.2, y = 0.25, depth = 26, alpha = 0.9 },
		{ color = theme.AccentSecondary, size = 460, x = 0.8, y = 0.75, depth = 40, alpha = 0.92 },
		{ color = theme.Accent, size = 300, x = 0.75, y = 0.2, depth = 60, alpha = 0.94 },
	}
	for index, spec in ipairs(blobSpecs) do
		local blob = Create("Frame", {
			Name = "Blob" .. index, BackgroundColor3 = spec.color, BackgroundTransparency = spec.alpha,
			BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(spec.x, spec.y),
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(spec.size, spec.size), Parent = backdrop,
		}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
		blobs[index] = { Frame = blob, Spec = spec }
	end

	-- particles
	local particleHolder = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Particles", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ClipsDescendants = true, Parent = backdrop,
	})
	if window.Effects.Particles then
		local rng = Random.new()
		local particleCount = (window.Device == "Phone") and 12 or ((window.Device == "Tablet") and 22 or 34)
		for _ = 1, particleCount do
			task.spawn(function()
				task.wait(rng:NextNumber(0, 3))
				while self.Alive do
					local size = rng:NextNumber(2, 5)
					local particle = Create("Frame", {
						BackgroundColor3 = (rng:NextNumber() > 0.5) and theme.Accent or theme.AccentSecondary,
						BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
						Position = UDim2.fromScale(rng:NextNumber(), 1.05), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size),
						Parent = particleHolder,
					}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
					local life = rng:NextNumber(5, 9)
					local drift = rng:NextNumber(-0.08, 0.08)
					Tween(particle, { BackgroundTransparency = rng:NextNumber(0.3, 0.7) }, 1.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine)
					Tween(particle, {
						Position = UDim2.fromScale(particle.Position.X.Scale + drift, -0.05),
					}, life, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear)
					task.delay(life - 1.2, function()
						if Alive(particle) then
							Tween(particle, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, 1.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine)
						end
					end)
					task.wait(life)
					particle:Destroy()
				end
			end)
		end
	end

	-- centre stage
	local center = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Center", BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.47), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(460, 400), Parent = gui,
	})
	self.Center = center

	-- rings
	local rings = {}
	local ringSpecs = {
		{ size = 150, thickness = 2.6, speed = 55, arc = 0.45 },
		{ size = 194, thickness = 1.9, speed = -38, arc = 0.35 },
		{ size = 240, thickness = 1.3, speed = 24, arc = 0.28 },
	}
	for index, spec in ipairs(ringSpecs) do
		local ring = Create("Frame", {
			Name = "Ring" .. index, BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
			Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 120), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Rotation = index * 60, Parent = center,
		}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
		local stroke = Create("UIStroke", { Thickness = spec.thickness, Color = theme.Accent, Transparency = 0, Parent = ring })
		Create("UIGradient", {
			Color = ColorSequence.new({ Keypoint(0, theme.Accent), Keypoint(1, theme.AccentSecondary) }),
			Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumKey(0, 0), NumKey(spec.arc, 0.1), NumKey(math.min(1, spec.arc + 0.25), 1), NumKey(1, 1) }),
			Parent = stroke,
		})
		rings[index] = { Frame = ring, Stroke = stroke, Spec = spec }
		task.delay(0.1 * index, function()
			if Alive(ring) then
				Tween(ring, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(spec.size, spec.size) }, 0.9, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
			end
		end)
	end
	self.Rings = rings

	-- orb + glow
	local glowOuter = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "GlowOuter", BackgroundColor3 = theme.Accent, BackgroundTransparency = 0.93, BorderSizePixel = 0,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 120), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Parent = center,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
	local glowInner = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "GlowInner", BackgroundColor3 = theme.Accent, BackgroundTransparency = 0.82, BorderSizePixel = 0,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 120), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Parent = center,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
	local orb = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Orb", BackgroundColor3 = theme.Accent, BorderSizePixel = 0,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 120), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Parent = center,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
	Create("UIGradient", {
		Color = ColorSequence.new({ Keypoint(0, theme.Accent), Keypoint(1, theme.AccentSecondary) }), Rotation = 45, Parent = orb,
	})
	self.Orb, self.GlowInner, self.GlowOuter = orb, glowInner, glowOuter
	task.delay(Timed(0.25), function()
		if Alive(orb) then
			Tween(orb, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(46, 46) }, 0.8, Enum.EasingStyle.Elastic)
			Tween(glowInner, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(80, 80) }, 0.8, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
			Tween(glowOuter, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(130, 130) }, 1.0, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		end
	end)

	-- title with sweeping shine
	local title = Create("TextLabel", {
		Name = "Title", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = cfg.Title or window.Title, Font = fonts.Bold, TextSize = 32,
		TextColor3 = theme.Text, TextTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 280),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40), Parent = center,
	})
	local shine = Create("UIGradient", {
		Color = ColorSequence.new({ Keypoint(0, theme.Text), Keypoint(0.45, theme.Text), Keypoint(0.5, theme.AccentSecondary), Keypoint(0.55, theme.Text), Keypoint(1, theme.Text) }),
		Offset = Vector2.new(-1, 0), Parent = title,
	})
	local subtitle = Create("TextLabel", {
		Name = "Subtitle", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = cfg.Subtitle or window.Subtitle or "Loading experience",
		Font = fonts.Medium, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = theme.SubText, TextTransparency = 1,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 330), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18), Parent = center,
	})
	task.delay(Timed(0.35), function()
		if Alive(title) then
			Tween(title, { TextTransparency = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 262) }, 0.7)
			Tween(subtitle, { TextTransparency = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 302) }, 0.7)
		end
	end)

	-- progress bar
	local track = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Track", BackgroundColor3 = theme.Element, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 350), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(320, 6), Parent = center,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
	local fill = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Fill", BackgroundColor3 = theme.Accent, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1),
		ClipsDescendants = true, Parent = track,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
	Create("UIGradient", {
		Color = ColorSequence.new({ Keypoint(0, theme.Accent), Keypoint(1, theme.AccentSecondary) }), Parent = fill,
	})
	local shimmer = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Shimmer", BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 70, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromScale(-0.3, 0), Parent = fill,
	})
	Create("UIGradient", {
		Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumKey(0, 1), NumKey(0.5, 0.55), NumKey(1, 1) }), Parent = shimmer,
	})
	local percent = Create("TextLabel", {
		Name = "Percent", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "0%", Font = fonts.Bold, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = theme.Text,
		TextTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 160, 0, 364), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(60, 16),
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, Parent = center,
	})
	local status = Create("TextLabel", {
		Name = "Status", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", Font = fonts.Medium, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = theme.SubText,
		TextTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -160, 0, 364), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(240, 16),
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Parent = center,
	})
	task.delay(Timed(0.6), function()
		if Alive(track) then
			Tween(track, { BackgroundTransparency = 0 }, 0.5)
			Tween(percent, { TextTransparency = 0 }, 0.5)
			Tween(status, { TextTransparency = 0 }, 0.5)
		end
	end)

	-- tips
	local tip = Create("TextLabel", {
		Name = "Tip", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = tips[1] or "", Font = fonts.Regular, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = theme.SubText,
		TextTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -40), Size = UDim2.new(0.8, 0, 0, 18),
		TextWrapped = true, Parent = gui,
	})
	if #tips > 0 then
		task.delay(Timed(0.8), function()
			local tipIndex = 1
			while self.Alive do
				Tween(tip, { TextTransparency = 0.25 }, 0.5)
				task.wait(3.2)
				if not self.Alive then
					break
				end
				Tween(tip, { TextTransparency = 1 }, 0.4)
				task.wait(0.45)
				tipIndex = tipIndex % #tips + 1
				tip.Text = tips[tipIndex]
			end
		end)
	end

	if cfg.Skippable then
		local skip = Create("TextLabel", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "Press any key to skip", Font = fonts.Regular, TextSize = 12,
			TextColor3 = theme.Placeholder, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -18),
			Size = UDim2.new(0.8, 0, 0, 16), Parent = gui,
		})
		self.Maid:Add(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard or IsClick(input) then
				self:Finish()
			end
		end))
	end

	self._statusLabel = status
	self._backdrop = backdrop
	self._tip = tip
	self:_SetStatus(steps[1] or "")

	-- animation loop
	local lastMouse = Vector2.new()
	self.Maid:Add(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
		if not self.Alive then
			return
		end
		local t = os.clock() - self._t0
		bgGradient.Rotation = 30 + t * 12
		for _, ring in ipairs(rings) do
			ring.Frame.Rotation = ring.Frame.Rotation + ring.Spec.speed * dt
		end
		if not self.Done and t > 1.15 then
			local pulse = 1 + 0.08 * math.sin(t * 3.2)
			orb.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(46 * pulse, 46 * pulse)
			glowInner.BackgroundTransparency = 0.8 + 0.08 * math.sin(t * 3.2 + 1)
			glowOuter.BackgroundTransparency = 0.92 + 0.05 * math.sin(t * 2.1)
		end
		shine.Offset = Vector2.new(((t * 0.55) % 2.4) - 1.2, 0)
		shimmer.Position = UDim2.fromScale(((t * 0.8) % 1.6) - 0.3, 0)

		-- parallax blobs
		local mouse = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
		lastMouse = lastMouse:Lerp(mouse, math.min(1, dt * 6))
		local viewport = gui.AbsoluteSize
		if viewport.X > 0 and viewport.Y > 0 then
			local nx = (lastMouse.X / viewport.X - 0.5) * 2
			local ny = (lastMouse.Y / viewport.Y - 0.5) * 2
			for index, blob in ipairs(blobs) do
				local spec = blob.Spec
				local driftX = math.sin(t * 0.35 + index) * 18
				local driftY = math.cos(t * 0.28 + index * 2) * 14
				blob.Frame.Position = UDim2.new(spec.x, nx * spec.depth + driftX, spec.y, ny * spec.depth + driftY)
			end
		end

		-- progress
		if not cfg.Manual and not self.Done then
			local raw = math.clamp(t / duration, 0, 1)
			local eased = raw * raw * (3 - 2 * raw)
			self.Progress = eased
			if t >= duration + 0.4 then
				self:Finish()
			end
		end
		self._display = Lerp(self._display, self.Progress, math.min(1, dt * 7))
		fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(self._display, 1)
		percent.Text = tostring(math.floor(self._display * 100 + 0.5)) .. "%"
		if not cfg.Manual and #steps > 0 then
			local stepIndex = math.clamp(math.floor(self._display * #steps) + 1, 1, #steps)
			if stepIndex ~= self._stepIndex then
				self._stepIndex = stepIndex
				self:_SetStatus(steps[stepIndex])
			end
		end
	end))

	return self
end

function Loader:_SetStatus(text)
	if text == self._status then
		return
	end
	self._status = text
	local label = self._statusLabel
	if not label then
		return
	end
	local goal = label.TextTransparency
	Tween(label, { TextTransparency = 1 }, 0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
	task.delay(Timed(0.16), function()
		if Alive(label) then
			label.Text = text
			Tween(label, { TextTransparency = 0 }, 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		end
	end)
end

-- Manual mode API
function Loader:SetProgress(progress, statusText)
	self.Progress = math.clamp(tonumber(progress) or 0, 0, 1)
	if statusText then
		self:_SetStatus(statusText)
	end
end

function Loader:SetStatus(text)
	self:_SetStatus(text)
end

function Loader:Finish()
	if self.Done then
		return
	end
	self.Done = true
	self.Progress = 1
	local center, orb, backdrop = self.Center, self.Orb, self._backdrop
	local window = self.Window
	window:_Sound("Notify")

	-- outro: rings burst, orb washes the screen, everything fades
	for index, ring in ipairs(self.Rings) do
		local size = ring.Spec.size * 2.8
		Tween(ring.Frame, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size) }, 0.8, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		Tween(ring.Stroke, { Transparency = 1 }, 0.55 + index * 0.05, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
	end
	Tween(self.GlowInner, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
	Tween(self.GlowOuter, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
	Tween(orb, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 30) }, 0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
	task.delay(Timed(0.22), function()
		if Alive(orb) then
			local viewport = self.Gui.AbsoluteSize
			local big = math.max(viewport.X, viewport.Y) * 2.4
			Tween(orb, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(big, big) }, 0.75, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
			task.delay(Timed(0.35), function()
				if Alive(orb) then
					Tween(orb, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, 0.45, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
	for _, child in ipairs(center:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("TextLabel") or child.Name == "Track" then
			FadeTree(child, 1, 0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		end
	end
	FadeTree(self._tip, 1, 0.3)
	task.delay(Timed(0.45), function()
		if Alive(backdrop) then
			FadeTree(backdrop, 1, 0.55, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		end
	end)
	task.delay(Timed(0.7), function()
		if self.OnFinish then
			SafeCall(self.OnFinish)
		end
	end)
	task.delay(Timed(1.25), function()
		self:Destroy()
	end)
end

function Loader:Destroy()
	self.Alive = false
	self.Maid:Clean()
end

-- ============================================================================
-- ACRYLIC GLASS
-- Blurs the world behind a panel. A Glass part at 0.98 transparency sits a hair in front of the camera,
-- covering the panel exactly on screen, and a DepthOfFieldEffect's near blur softens what is seen through it.
-- Dependencies/UIBlur (a Folder named Dependencies inside this module) is used when present: its rounded corner
-- meshes, its graphics-quality check and its depth-of-field. Aurora places the parts itself so the glass also
-- follows tilted SurfaceGui panels and IgnoreGuiInset ScreenGuis. Without the dependency the same trick runs inline.
-- ============================================================================
local Glass = { Entries = {}, QualityOK = true, QualityAt = 0 }
local GLASS_OFFSET = 0.05

local function LoadUIBlur()
	if Glass.Loaded then
		return Glass.Module
	end
	Glass.Loaded = true
	local found, source = pcall(function()
		local folder = script:FindFirstChild("Dependencies")
		return folder and folder:FindFirstChild("UIBlur")
	end)
	if not found or typeof(source) ~= "Instance" or not source:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		return nil
	end
	local ok, result = pcall(require, source)
	if ok and type(result) == "table" and type(result.new) == "function" then
		Glass.Module = result
		local corners = source:FindFirstChild("Corners")
		Glass.CornerCount = corners and #corners:GetChildren() or 0
	else
		warn("[AuroraUI] Dependencies.UIBlur failed to load, using the built-in glass: " .. tostring(result))
	end
	return Glass.Module
end

local function GlassQualityOK()
	local ok, level = pcall(function()
		return UserSettings().GameSettings.SavedQualityLevel
	end)
	if not ok or typeof(level) ~= "EnumItem" then
		return true
	end
	return level == Enum.SavedQualitySetting.Automatic or level.Value >= 8
end

local function GlassCheckQuality()
	local now = os.clock()
	if now >= Glass.QualityAt then
		Glass.QualityAt = now + 2
		Glass.QualityOK = GlassQualityOK()
	end
	return Glass.QualityOK
end

-- One depth-of-field serves every glass part: UIBlur's own when it exists, otherwise Aurora's (same settings).
local function GlassDoF()
	local shared = Lighting:FindFirstChild("UIBlur")
	if shared and shared:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") then
		return shared
	end
	if not Alive(Glass.DoF) then
		Glass.DoF = Create("DepthOfFieldEffect", {
			Name = "AuroraGlass", FarIntensity = 0, FocusDistance = 0, InFocusRadius = 0, NearIntensity = 1, Parent = Lighting,
		})
	end
	return Glass.DoF
end

-- Acrylic borrows the one depth-of-field Roblox renders. Nothing used to give it back, so turning Acrylic
-- off (or closing the window) left the whole world blurred until the player rejoined.
local function GlassReleaseDoF()
	local shared = Lighting:FindFirstChild("UIBlur")
	if shared and shared:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") and Glass.SharedPrev ~= nil then
		shared.NearIntensity = Glass.SharedPrev
		Glass.SharedPrev = nil
	end
	if Alive(Glass.DoF) then
		Glass.DoF:Destroy()
	end
	Glass.DoF = nil
end

-- The point UIBlur uses for a viewport pixel: a hair in front of the camera along that pixel's ray.
local function GlassPoint(camera, x, y)
	local ray = camera:ViewportPointToRay(x, y, GLASS_OFFSET)
	return ray.Origin + ray.Direction * GLASS_OFFSET
end

local function GlassNew(frame, spec)
	local entry = { Frame = frame, Spec = spec }
	local module = LoadUIBlur()
	local radius = spec.Radius or 0
	if module then
		local corner = nil
		if (Glass.CornerCount or 0) > 0 and radius > 0 then
			corner = math.clamp(math.floor(radius / 25 + 0.5), 1, Glass.CornerCount)
		end
		local ok, blur = pcall(module.new, frame, corner, true)
		if ok and type(blur) == "table" and typeof(blur._Part) == "Instance" then
			-- Aurora positions the part (tilted surfaces, inset-free guis), so UIBlur's own per-frame update is silenced
			blur.Update = function() end
			entry.Blur, entry.Part, entry.Cornered = blur, blur._Part, corner ~= nil
		end
	end
	if not entry.Part then
		local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		local folder = camera and camera:FindFirstChild("AuroraGlass")
		if camera and not folder then
			folder = Create("Folder", { Name = "AuroraGlass", Parent = camera })
		end
		entry.Part = Create("Part", {
			Name = frame.Name, Anchored = true, CanCollide = false, CanQuery = false, CanTouch = false, CastShadow = false,
			Material = Enum.Material.Glass, Size = Vector3.new(0.1, 0.1, 0.001), Parent = folder,
		})
	end
	entry.Part.Transparency = 1
	GlassDoF()
	return entry
end

local function GlassDestroy(entry)
	if not entry then
		return
	end
	Glass.Entries[entry] = nil
	if entry.Blur then
		pcall(entry.Blur.Destroy, entry.Blur)
	end
	if entry.Part then
		pcall(entry.Part.Destroy, entry.Part)
		entry.Part = nil
	end
end

local function GlassHide(entry)
	local part = entry.Part
	if part and part.Parent and part.Transparency < 1 then
		part.Transparency = 1
	end
end

local function GlassUpdate(entry, visible)
	local part, frame = entry.Part, entry.Frame
	if not part or not part.Parent then
		return
	end
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	if not visible or not camera or not Alive(frame) or not frame.Visible then
		GlassHide(entry)
		return
	end
	if entry.Blur then
		if entry.Blur._Rendering == false then
			GlassHide(entry)
			return
		end
	elseif not GlassCheckQuality() then
		GlassHide(entry)
		return
	end
	local absPos, absSize = frame.AbsolutePosition, frame.AbsoluteSize
	if absSize.X < 2 or absSize.Y < 2 then
		GlassHide(entry)
		return
	end
	local camCF = camera.CFrame
	local eye, look = camCF.Position, camCF.LookVector
	local center, rotation, width, height
	local surface = entry.Spec.Surface
	if surface then
		local surfacePart, canvas = surface.Part, surface.Canvas
		if not surfacePart or not surfacePart.Parent or surface.Enabled == false then
			GlassHide(entry)
			return
		end
		local cf, size = surfacePart.CFrame, surfacePart.Size
		local sx, sy = size.X / math.max(1, canvas.X), size.Y / math.max(1, canvas.Y)
		local cx, cy = absPos.X + absSize.X / 2, absPos.Y + absSize.Y / 2
		local world = cf * Vector3.new(cx * sx - size.X / 2, size.Y / 2 - cy * sy, size.Z / 2)
		local depth = (world - eye):Dot(look)
		if depth < 0.1 then
			GlassHide(entry)
			return
		end
		-- shrink the panel's rectangle towards the eye until it sits where UIBlur puts its parts:
		-- identical on screen, tilted with the panel, but in front of the whole world
		local screen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(world)
		local nearDepth = (GlassPoint(camera, screen.X, screen.Y) - eye):Dot(look)
		local k = math.max(0.001, nearDepth - (entry.Spec.Bias or 0)) / depth
		center = eye + (world - eye) * k
		width, height = absSize.X * sx * k, absSize.Y * sy * k
		rotation = cf - cf.Position
	else
		local gui = frame:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("ScreenGui")
		if gui and not gui.IgnoreGuiInset then
			absPos = absPos + GuiService:GetGuiInset()
		end
		local topLeft = GlassPoint(camera, absPos.X, absPos.Y)
		center = GlassPoint(camera, absPos.X + absSize.X / 2, absPos.Y + absSize.Y / 2)
		width = (GlassPoint(camera, absPos.X + absSize.X, absPos.Y) - topLeft).Magnitude
		height = (GlassPoint(camera, absPos.X, absPos.Y + absSize.Y) - topLeft).Magnitude
		rotation = (camCF - eye) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, -math.rad(frame.AbsoluteRotation))
	end
	if entry.Cornered then
		-- UIBlur's corner meshes face the other way round
		rotation = rotation * CFrame.Angles(0, math.pi, 0)
	end
	part.Size = Vector3.new(width, height, 0)
	part.CFrame = CFrame.new(center) * rotation
	if part.Transparency > 0.99 then
		part.Transparency = 0.98
	end
end

-- Cards in ScreenGuis (notifications) update themselves every frame.
local function GlassAttachLoose(entry, isVisible)
	entry.IsVisible = isVisible
	Glass.Entries[entry] = true
	if Glass.Connection then
		return
	end
	Glass.Connection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		local any = false
		for item in pairs(Glass.Entries) do
			any = true
			if not Alive(item.Frame) then
				GlassDestroy(item)
			else
				local ok, shown = pcall(item.IsVisible)
				GlassUpdate(item, ok and shown)
			end
		end
		if not any and Glass.Connection then
			Glass.Connection:Disconnect()
			Glass.Connection = nil
		end
	end)
end
Aurora.LoadUIBlur = LoadUIBlur

-- ============================================================================
-- WINDOW
-- ============================================================================
local Window = {}
Window.__index = Window

local Container = {}
Container.__index = Container

local Page = setmetatable({}, { __index = Container })
Page.__index = Page

local Section = setmetatable({}, { __index = Container })
Section.__index = Section

local Element = {}
Element.__index = Element

local DefaultEffects = {
	Tilt = true, TiltStrength = 1, Parallax = true, Ripples = true, Shadows = true, Glow = true,
	Hover = true, Spotlight = false, Blur = true, Particles = true, AnimatedGradient = true,
	Tooltips = true, Sounds = false, OpenAnimation = true, PageTransitions = true,
	Sway = true, Float = true, DepthLighting = true, ElementDepth = true, EdgeGlow = true,
	KeyboardNavigation = true, RightClickReset = true,
	-- camera / depth
	CameraInertia = true, InertiaStrength = 1, Magnet = true, MagnetStrength = 1, DragTilt = true,
	PressDepth = true, HoverDepth = true, PageSwing = true, OpenSpin = true,
	DepthOfField = true, DepthOfFieldAmount = 0.35, WorldDim = false, CameraZoom = false, CameraZoomAmount = 5,
	ElementEntrance = true,
	-- glass + depth
	Acrylic = true, AcrylicStrength = 1, AcrylicTint = 0.22, DepthAmount = 1, OpenDuration = 1, BackgroundRate = 40,
}

local function With(defaults, props)
	local out = {}
	for key, value in pairs(defaults) do
		out[key] = value
	end
	if props then
		for key, value in pairs(props) do
			out[key] = value
		end
	end
	return out
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Theme / font / corner binding helpers
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function Window:_LoadTheme(theme)
	local base
	if type(theme) == "table" then
		base = Merge(Aurora.Themes.Midnight, theme)
		self.ThemeName = theme.Name or "Custom"
	else
		base = Aurora.Themes[theme]
		if not base then
			warn("[AuroraUI] Unknown theme '" .. tostring(theme) .. "', falling back to Midnight")
			base = Aurora.Themes.Midnight
			theme = "Midnight"
		end
		self.ThemeName = theme
	end
	self.Theme = DeepCopy(base)
	if self._accentOverride then
		self.Theme.Accent = self._accentOverride
	end
	self._themeDirty = nil
end

function Window:_T(token)
	local value = self.Theme
	for part in string.gmatch(token, "[^%.]+") do
		value = value and value[part]
	end
	if typeof(value) ~= "Color3" then
		return Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 255)
	end
	return value
end

function Window:_Bind(inst, property, token)
	inst[property] = self:_T(token)
	self._themeBindings[#self._themeBindings + 1] = { Instance = inst, Property = property, Token = token }
	return inst
end

function Window:_OnTheme(fn)
	self._themeCallbacks[#self._themeCallbacks + 1] = fn
	pcall(fn, self.Theme)
end

function Window:_ApplyTheme(animate)
	local kept = {}
	for _, binding in ipairs(self._themeBindings) do
		local inst = binding.Instance
		if inst.Parent ~= nil then
			kept[#kept + 1] = binding
			local color = self:_T(binding.Token)
			if animate then
				Tween(inst, { [binding.Property] = color }, 0.35)
			else
				inst[binding.Property] = color
			end
		end
	end
	self._themeBindings = kept
	for _, fn in ipairs(self._themeCallbacks) do
		pcall(fn, self.Theme)
	end
end

function Window:_Font(obj, role)
	if typeof(role) == "EnumItem" then
		obj.Font = role
		return obj
	end
	obj.Font = self.Fonts[role or "Medium"] or self.Fonts.Medium
	if self.FontAsset and role ~= "Code" then
		pcall(function()
			local weights = { Regular = Enum.FontWeight.Regular, Medium = Enum.FontWeight.Medium, Bold = Enum.FontWeight.Bold }
			obj.FontFace = Font.new(self.FontAsset, weights[role or "Medium"] or Enum.FontWeight.Regular, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
		end)
	end
	self._fontObjects[#self._fontObjects + 1] = { Object = obj, Role = role or "Medium" }
	return obj
end

function Window:_Corner(inst, radius, fixed)
	radius = radius or self.CornerRadius
	local scaled = math.floor(radius * (self.CornerScale or 1) + 0.5)
	local corner = Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, scaled), Parent = inst })
	if not fixed then
		self._corners[#self._corners + 1] = { Corner = corner, Base = radius }
	end
	return corner
end

-- TextLabel with themed color + registered font
function Window:_Label(props, role, token)
	local label = Create("TextLabel", With({
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		TextSize = 14, RichText = true, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
	}, props))
	self:_Font(label, role or "Medium")
	if token ~= false then
		self:_Bind(label, "TextColor3", token or "Text")
	end
	if type(props.Text) == "string" and props.Text ~= "" then
		self._localized[#self._localized + 1] = { Label = label, Key = props.Text }
		local locale = self.Language and Aurora.Locales[self.Language]
		if locale and locale[props.Text] then
			label.Text = locale[props.Text]
		end
	end
	return label
end

-- Aurora:AddLocale("de", { ["Settings"] = "Einstellungen", ... }) then Window:SetLanguage("de")
Aurora.Locales = {}
function Aurora:AddLocale(code, strings)
	Aurora.Locales[code] = strings
end

function Window:SetLanguage(code)
	self.Language = code
	local locale = Aurora.Locales[code]
	local kept = {}
	for _, entry in ipairs(self._localized) do
		if entry.Label.Parent ~= nil then
			kept[#kept + 1] = entry
			entry.Label.Text = (locale and locale[entry.Key]) or entry.Key
		end
	end
	self._localized = kept
end

function Window:Translate(key)
	local locale = self.Language and Aurora.Locales[self.Language]
	return (locale and locale[key]) or key
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Effect helpers
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function Window:_Sound(name)
	if not self.Effects.Sounds then
		return
	end
	local id = self.Sounds[name]
	if type(id) ~= "string" or not (id:match("^rbxassetid://%d+$") or id:match("^rbxasset://")) then
		return
	end
	local sound = Create("Sound", { SoundId = id, Volume = self.Sounds.Volume or 0.5, Parent = self.Gui })
	sound:Play()
	task.delay(4, function()
		sound:Destroy()
	end)
end

function Window:_Ripple(host, input, colorToken)
	if not self.Effects.Ripples or not Alive(host) then
		return
	end
	-- Ripples live in a layer sized in Scale and clipped to the host. Scale-sized children are ignored by an
	-- AutomaticSize ancestor and the clip keeps the circles inside, so clicking can never change any
	-- measured width. (A zero-size layer was not enough: its circles still counted towards the row's holder.)
	local layer = host:FindFirstChild("RippleLayer")
	if not layer then
		layer = Create("Frame", {
			Name = "RippleLayer", BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
			ClipsDescendants = true, ZIndex = 5, Parent = host,
		})
		host.ClipsDescendants = true
	end
	-- cap concurrent ripples so spam-clicking cannot pile up hundreds of frames
	local active = layer:GetChildren()
	if #active >= 4 then
		active[1]:Destroy()
	end
	local pos = self:_Pointer()
	local abs, size = host.AbsolutePosition, host.AbsoluteSize
	local x = math.clamp(pos.X - abs.X, 0, size.X)
	local y = math.clamp(pos.Y - abs.Y, 0, size.Y)
	local ripple = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Ripple", BackgroundColor3 = self:_T(colorToken or "Text"), BackgroundTransparency = 0.86,
		BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), ZIndex = 5, Parent = layer,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
	local diameter = math.min(math.max(size.X, size.Y) * 1.3, 420)
	Tween(ripple, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(diameter, diameter), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, 0.45, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart)
	task.delay(Timed(0.5), function()
		if ripple.Parent then
			ripple:Destroy()
		end
	end)
end

function Window:_Hover(frame, stroke, opts)
	opts = opts or {}
	local baseToken = opts.BaseToken or "Element"
	local hoverToken = opts.HoverToken or "ElementHover"
	local baseTransparency = opts.BaseTransparency or frame.BackgroundTransparency
	local hoverTransparency = opts.HoverTransparency or baseTransparency
	local state = { Hovered = false, Enabled = true }
	frame.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		state.Hovered = true
		if not self.Effects.Hover or not state.Enabled then
			return
		end
		Tween(frame, { BackgroundColor3 = self:_T(hoverToken), BackgroundTransparency = hoverTransparency }, 0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		if opts.Scale and self.Effects.ElementDepth then
			Tween(opts.Scale, { Scale = 1.012 }, 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		end
		if stroke then
			if opts.HoverStroke == false then
				Tween(stroke, { Transparency = math.max(0, (opts.StrokeTransparency or 0.75) - 0.3) }, 0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
			else
				Tween(stroke, { Color = self:_T("Accent"), Transparency = 0.7 }, 0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
			end
		end
		if opts.Sound ~= false then
			self:_Sound("Hover")
		end
	end)
	frame.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		state.Hovered = false
		if not state.Enabled then
			return
		end
		Tween(frame, { BackgroundColor3 = self:_T(baseToken), BackgroundTransparency = baseTransparency }, 0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		if stroke then
			Tween(stroke, { Color = self:_T("Stroke"), Transparency = opts.StrokeTransparency or 0.75 }, 0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		end
		if opts.Scale then
			Tween(opts.Scale, { Scale = 1 }, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		end
	end)
	return state
end

function Window:_Spotlight(frame)
	local holder = Create("Frame", {
		-- sized in Scale and clipped for the same reason as the ripple layer: it must not feed AutomaticSize
		Name = "Spotlight", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ClipsDescendants = true, ZIndex = 0, Parent = frame,
	})
	local outer = Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(170, 170), Parent = holder,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
	local inner = Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(80, 80), Parent = holder,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
	frame.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		if not self.Effects.Spotlight then
			return
		end
		Tween(outer, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.975 }, 0.25)
		Tween(inner, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.965 }, 0.25)
	end)
	frame.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		Tween(outer, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, 0.3)
		Tween(inner, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, 0.3)
	end)
	frame.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if not self.Effects.Spotlight or not IsMove(input) then
			return
		end
		local abs = frame.AbsolutePosition
		local pointer = self:_Pointer()
		local pos = UDim2.fromOffset(pointer.X - abs.X, pointer.Y - abs.Y)
		outer.Position = pos
		inner.Position = pos
	end)
	return holder
end

function Window:_Tooltip(obj, text)
	if not text or text == "" then
		return
	end
	obj.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		if not self.Effects.Tooltips then
			return
		end
		self._tooltipTarget = obj
		task.delay(Timed(0.45), function()
			if self._tooltipTarget == obj and Alive(obj) then
				self:_ShowTooltip(text)
			end
		end)
	end)
	obj.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		if self._tooltipTarget == obj then
			self._tooltipTarget = nil
			self:_HideTooltip()
		end
	end)
	-- touch: hold the row for a moment instead of hovering it
	obj.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch or not self.Effects.Tooltips then
			return
		end
		self._tooltipTarget = obj
		task.delay(Timed(0.4), function()
			if self._tooltipTarget == obj and Alive(obj) then
				self:_ShowTooltip(text)
			end
		end)
	end)
	obj.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and self._tooltipTarget == obj then
			self._tooltipTarget = nil
			task.delay(Timed(1.2), function()
				if self._tooltipTarget ~= obj then
					self:_HideTooltip()
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
end

function Window:_ShowTooltip(text)
	local tip = self.TooltipFrame
	tip.Label.Text = text
	local bounds = TextService:GetTextSize(text, 13, tip.Label.Font, Vector2.new(260, 1000))
	tip.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(bounds.X + 20, bounds.Y + 12)
	self._tooltipTicket = (self._tooltipTicket or 0) + 1
	local scale = tip:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale") or Create("UIScale", { Scale = 1, Parent = tip })
	local stroke = tip:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
	tip.Visible = true
	self._tooltipVisible = true
	tip.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	tip.Label.TextTransparency = 1
	scale.Scale = 0.9
	if stroke then stroke.Transparency = 1 end
	Tween(tip, { BackgroundTransparency = 0 }, 0.16)
	Tween(tip.Label, { TextTransparency = 0 }, 0.16)
	Tween(scale, { Scale = 1 }, 0.24, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
	if stroke then Tween(stroke, { Transparency = 0.2 }, 0.16) end
end

function Window:_HideTooltip()
	self._tooltipVisible = false
	local tip = self.TooltipFrame
	if not tip or not tip.Visible then
		return
	end
	self._tooltipTicket = (self._tooltipTicket or 0) + 1
	local ticket = self._tooltipTicket
	local scale = tip:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale")
	local stroke = tip:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
	Tween(tip, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, 0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
	Tween(tip.Label, { TextTransparency = 1 }, 0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
	if scale then Tween(scale, { Scale = 0.94 }, 0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In) end
	if stroke then Tween(stroke, { Transparency = 1 }, 0.12) end
	task.delay(Timed(0.12), function()
		if ticket == self._tooltipTicket then
			tip.Visible = false
		end
	end)
end

-- Pops the direct children of a list in one after another (scale + fade).
function Window:_AnimateEntrance(list, delay)
	if not self.Effects.ElementEntrance or not list then
		return
	end
	local items = {}
	for _, child in ipairs(list:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("GuiObject") and child.Visible then
			items[#items + 1] = child
		end
	end
	table.sort(items, function(a, b) return a.LayoutOrder < b.LayoutOrder end)
	local now = os.clock()
	delay = delay or 0
	for index, item in ipairs(items) do
		if index > 14 then
			break
		end
		local scale = item:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale") or Create("UIScale", { Scale = 1, Parent = item })
		-- resting values are read again only while the window is fully shown; FadeTree itself skips any object
		-- still inside an earlier fade, and stamps these rows so a later fade leaves them alone in turn
		local settled = self._fadeState == "shown"
		local startAt = Timed(delay + (index - 1) * 0.035)
		scale.Scale = 0.95
		FadeTree(item, 1, 0, nil, settled, now + startAt + Timed(0.35))
		task.delay(startAt, function()
			if Alive(item) then
				Tween(scale, { Scale = 1 }, 0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
				FadeTree(item, 0, 0.26, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
			end
		end)
	end
end

-- Registers a depth layer. lift = how many pixels it floats above its parent; the step draws it where such a
-- surface would appear from the camera, so every tilt, swing and fly-in separates the layers slightly.
function Window:_ParallaxLayer(obj, lift)
	local layer = { Object = obj, Base = obj.Position, Depth = lift }
	self._parallaxLayers[#self._parallaxLayers + 1] = layer
	return layer
end

-- The step writes a layer's Position every frame, so moving one goes through here instead of a Tween.
-- from (optional) is where the animation starts; delay postpones it.
function Window:_SetLayerBase(obj, goal, time, from, delay)
	local speed = (type(Aurora.AnimationSpeed) == "number" and Aurora.AnimationSpeed > 0) and Aurora.AnimationSpeed or 1
	for _, layer in ipairs(self._parallaxLayers) do
		if layer.Object == obj then
			if time and time > 0 then
				layer.From = from or layer.Base
				layer.Start = os.clock() + (delay or 0) / speed
				layer.Duration = time / speed
			else
				layer.From = nil
			end
			layer.Base = goal
		end
	end
end

-- Slides a layer in from an offset to where it rests (used by the open animation).
function Window:_LayerIntro(obj, offset, time, delay)
	for _, layer in ipairs(self._parallaxLayers) do
		if layer.Object == obj then
			self:_SetLayerBase(obj, layer.Base, time, layer.Base + offset, delay)
			return
		end
	end
end

-- A faint top-lit shade on a card that swings with the panel, so each card reads as its own raised tile.
function Window:_DepthShade(frame)
	if frame:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient") then
		return nil
	end
	local gradient = Create("UIGradient", {
		Name = "DepthShade", Rotation = 90,
		Color = ColorSequence.new({ Keypoint(0, Color3.new(1, 1, 1)), Keypoint(1, Color3.new(0.91, 0.91, 0.94)) }),
		Parent = frame,
	})
	self._depthShades[gradient] = true
	self._shadeState = nil
	return gradient
end

function Window:_RegisterSearchable(element)
	self.Elements[#self.Elements + 1] = element
end

function Window:_NextOrder()
	self._layoutOrder = self._layoutOrder + 1
	return self._layoutOrder
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- ICONS
-- Page:Icon / Button Icon / Section Icon accept a built-in name below, an entry you add to Aurora.Icons,
-- or any "rbxassetid://" image. Built-ins are drawn from frames, so they need no assets and stay crisp.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
Aurora.Icons = {}

local IconShapes = {}
do
	local function L(x1, y1, x2, y2) return { "line", x1, y1, x2, y2 } end
	local function R(cx, cy, d) return { "ring", cx, cy, d, d, 1 } end
	local function E(cx, cy, w, h) return { "ring", cx, cy, w, h, 1 } end
	local function B(cx, cy, w, h, r) return { "box", cx, cy, w, h, r or 2 } end
	local function D(cx, cy, d) return { "dot", cx, cy, d } end
	local function poly(points, closed)
		local out = {}
		for i = 1, #points - 1 do
			out[#out + 1] = L(points[i][1], points[i][2], points[i + 1][1], points[i + 1][2])
		end
		if closed then
			out[#out + 1] = L(points[#points][1], points[#points][2], points[1][1], points[1][2])
		end
		return out
	end
	local function join(...)
		local out = {}
		for _, group in ipairs({ ... }) do
			if type(group[1]) == "table" then
				for _, item in ipairs(group) do out[#out + 1] = item end
			else
				out[#out + 1] = group
			end
		end
		return out
	end
