local function ToVector2(value)
	if typeof(value) == "Vector2" then
		return value
	end
	if type(value) == "table" then
		return Vector2.new(tonumber(value[1] or value.X or value.x) or 0, tonumber(value[2] or value.Y or value.y) or 0)
	end
	return nil
end

-- Icon = "Custom" swaps the preset for your own image. It needs the CustomIcon table:
--   CustomIcon = {
--     Image = "rbxassetid://123",          (a number id works too)
--     RectSize = Vector2.new(48, 48),       (ImageRectSize; omit for a whole image)
--     RectOffset = Vector2.new(96, 0),      (ImageRectOffset; RectPosition is accepted as well)
--     Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),(fixed colour; omit to follow the theme like presets do)
--     Transparency = 0, ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
--   }
function Window:_ResolveIcon(icon, custom)
	if type(icon) == "string" and icon:lower() == "custom" then
		if type(custom) ~= "table" or (custom.Image == nil and custom.Id == nil) then
			warn('[AuroraUI] Icon = "Custom" needs a CustomIcon table: { Image = "rbxassetid://...", RectSize = Vector2, RectOffset = Vector2, Color = Color3 }')
			return "dot"
		end
		return custom
	end
	return icon
end

-- Draws an icon into a holder frame and returns the holder. Colour follows the given theme token.
-- name: built-in preset name, "rbxassetid://" string, a CustomIcon table, or a name registered in Aurora.Icons.
function Window:_Icon(parent, name, size, token, props)
	size = size or 16
	token = token or "SubText"
	local holder = Create("Frame", With({
		Name = "Icon", BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size),
	}, props))
	holder:SetAttribute("IconToken", token)
	local color = self:_T(token)
	local spec = (type(name) == "table") and name or nil
	if not spec and type(name) == "string" and type(Aurora.Icons[name]) == "table" then
		spec = Aurora.Icons[name]
	end
	local asset = (not spec) and ((type(name) == "string" and name:find("rbxasset", 1, true)) and name or Aurora.Icons[name]) or nil
	if spec then
		local image = spec.Image or spec.Id or ""
		if type(image) == "number" or tostring(image):match("^%d+$") then
			image = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(image)
		end
		local fixed = spec.Color or spec.ImageColor or spec.ImageColor3
		local label = Create("ImageLabel", {
			Name = "Image", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = image, ImageColor3 = fixed or color,
			ImageTransparency = spec.Transparency or 0, ScaleType = spec.ScaleType or Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
			Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = holder.ZIndex, Parent = holder,
		})
		local rectSize = ToVector2(spec.RectSize or spec.ImageRectSize)
		local rectOffset = ToVector2(spec.RectOffset or spec.ImageRectOffset or spec.RectPosition)
		if rectSize then
			label.ImageRectSize = rectSize
		end
		if rectOffset then
			label.ImageRectOffset = rectOffset
		end
		if fixed then
			holder:SetAttribute("IconFixedColor", true)
		end
	elseif asset then
		Create("ImageLabel", { Name = "Image", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = asset, ImageColor3 = color, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = holder.ZIndex, Parent = holder })
	else
		local shape = IconShapes[name] or IconShapes.dot
		local unit = size / 16
		local thickness = math.max(1.2, 1.5 * unit)
		for _, part in ipairs(shape) do
			local kind = part[1]
			if kind == "line" then
				local x1, y1, x2, y2 = part[2] * unit, part[3] * unit, part[4] * unit, part[5] * unit
				local dx, dy = x2 - x1, y2 - y1
				local length = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) + thickness
				Create("Frame", {
					BackgroundColor3 = color, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
					Position = UDim2.fromOffset((x1 + x2) / 2, (y1 + y2) / 2), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(length, thickness),
					Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(dy, dx)), ZIndex = holder.ZIndex, Parent = holder,
				}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
			elseif kind == "ring" or kind == "box" then
				local w, h = part[4] * unit, part[5] * unit
				local ring = Create("Frame", {
					BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
					Position = UDim2.fromOffset(part[2] * unit, part[3] * unit), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w - thickness, h - thickness),
					ZIndex = holder.ZIndex, Parent = holder,
				})
				Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = (kind == "ring" or part[6] >= 99) and UDim.new(1, 0) or UDim.new(0, part[6] * unit), Parent = ring })
				Create("UIStroke", { Color = color, Thickness = thickness, Parent = ring })
			elseif kind == "dot" then
				local d = part[4] * unit
				Create("Frame", {
					BackgroundColor3 = color, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
					Position = UDim2.fromOffset(part[2] * unit, part[3] * unit), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(d, d),
					ZIndex = holder.ZIndex, Parent = holder,
				}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
			end
		end
	end
	holder.Parent = parent
	self:_OnTheme(function()
		if holder.Parent then
			self:_IconColor(holder, holder:GetAttribute("IconToken"), 0)
		end
	end)
	return holder
end

function Window:_IconColor(holder, token, time)
	if not holder or holder:GetAttribute("IconFixedColor") then
		return
	end
	if type(token) == "string" then
		holder:SetAttribute("IconToken", token)
	end
	local color = (typeof(token) == "Color3") and token or self:_T(token)
	for _, obj in ipairs(holder:GetDescendants()) do
		local props = nil
		if obj:IsA("ImageLabel") then
			props = { ImageColor3 = color }
		elseif obj:IsA("UIStroke") then
			props = { Color = color }
		elseif obj:IsA("Frame") and obj.BackgroundTransparency < 1 then
			props = { BackgroundColor3 = color }
		end
		if props then
			if time and time > 0 then
				Tween(obj, props, time)
			else
				for key, value in pairs(props) do obj[key] = value end
			end
		end
	end
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- BACKGROUNDS
-- Window:SetBackground(name, { Intensity, Speed, Color = "Text" | "Accent" | "SubText" | Color3, Image, ScaleType })
-- Aurora:RegisterBackground(name, { Color, Build = function(ctx) return state end, Step = function(state, ctx, dt, t) end, Destroy })
-- ctx.Layer is the frame to draw into. ctx.Intensity (already multiplied by the cross-fade), ctx.Speed, ctx.Color,
-- ctx.Color2, ctx.Tilt and ctx.Random are kept up to date. Positions in scale keep presets resolution independent.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
Aurora.Backgrounds = {}
Aurora.BackgroundOrder = {}

function Aurora:RegisterBackground(name, def)
	def.Name = name
	if not Aurora.Backgrounds[name] then
		table.insert(Aurora.BackgroundOrder, name)
	end
	Aurora.Backgrounds[name] = def
	return def
end

-- Scene budget: phones and tablets draw fewer particles for the same picture.
local function Budget(ctx, count)
	local window = ctx.Window
	local device = window and window.Device or "Desktop"
	local scale = (device == "Phone") and 0.45 or ((device == "Tablet") and 0.7 or 1)
	scale = scale * (tonumber(ctx.Options and ctx.Options.Quality) or 1)
	return math.max(math.min(count, 4), math.floor(count * scale + 0.5))
end

local function BgDot(parent, size, color)
	return Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = color, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size), Parent = parent,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
end

local function Parallax(ctx, depth, amount)
	local tilt = ctx.Tilt
	return -tilt.X * depth * amount, -tilt.Y * depth * amount
end

Aurora:RegisterBackground("Default", { Build = function() return {} end })
Aurora:RegisterBackground("None", { Build = function() return {} end })

Aurora:RegisterBackground("Stars", {
	Color = "Text",
	Build = function(ctx)
		local rng, stars = ctx.Random, {}
		for i = 1, Budget(ctx, 90) do
			local depth = rng:NextNumber(0.15, 1)
			stars[i] = {
				Frame = BgDot(ctx.Layer, 1 + depth * 1.8, ctx.Color), X = rng:NextNumber(), Y = rng:NextNumber(),
				Depth = depth, Phase = rng:NextNumber(0, 6.28), Rate = rng:NextNumber(0.6, 2.2),
			}
		end
		return { Stars = stars, NextShot = os.clock() + rng:NextNumber(2, 5) }
	end,
	Step = function(state, ctx, dt, t)
		for _, star in ipairs(state.Stars) do
			star.X = (star.X - dt * 0.004 * star.Depth * ctx.Speed) % 1
			local ox, oy = Parallax(ctx, star.Depth, 16)
			star.Frame.Position = UDim2.new(star.X, ox, star.Y, oy)
			local twinkle = 0.55 + 0.45 * math.sin(t * star.Rate + star.Phase)
			star.Frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - math.clamp((0.2 + 0.6 * star.Depth) * twinkle * ctx.Intensity, 0, 1)
		end
		if t > state.NextShot and ctx.Intensity > 0.05 then
			state.NextShot = t + ctx.Random:NextNumber(3, 8) / math.max(0.2, ctx.Speed)
			local streak = Create("Frame", {
				BackgroundColor3 = ctx.Color, BackgroundTransparency = 1 - 0.8 * ctx.Intensity, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
				Position = UDim2.fromScale(ctx.Random:NextNumber(0.15, 0.7), ctx.Random:NextNumber(0.05, 0.4)), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(90, 1.5),
				Rotation = 22, Parent = ctx.Layer,
			}, { Create("UIGradient", { Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumKey(0, 1), NumKey(1, 0) }) }) })
			local start = streak.Position
			Tween(streak, { Position = start + UDim2.fromScale(0.32, 0.14), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, 0.9, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
			task.delay(Timed(0.95), function() streak:Destroy() end)
		end
	end,
})


Aurora:RegisterBackground("Fireflies", {
	Color = "Accent",
	Build = function(ctx)
		local rng, flies = ctx.Random, {}
		for i = 1, Budget(ctx, 24) do
			local halo = BgDot(ctx.Layer, 16, ctx.Color)
			local core = BgDot(halo, 3, ctx.Color)
			core.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
			flies[i] = { Halo = halo, Core = core, X = rng:NextNumber(), Y = rng:NextNumber(), Seed = rng:NextNumber(0, 100), Phase = rng:NextNumber(0, 6.28), Rate = rng:NextNumber(0.8, 2), Depth = rng:NextNumber(0.3, 1) }
		end
		return { Flies = flies }
	end,
	Step = function(state, ctx, dt, t)
		for _, fly in ipairs(state.Flies) do
			fly.X = (fly.X + math.noise(fly.Seed, t * 0.15) * dt * 0.06 * ctx.Speed) % 1
			fly.Y = (fly.Y + math.noise(fly.Seed + 40, t * 0.15) * dt * 0.06 * ctx.Speed) % 1
			local ox, oy = Parallax(ctx, fly.Depth, 18)
			fly.Halo.Position = UDim2.new(fly.X, ox, fly.Y, oy)
			local pulse = 0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(t * fly.Rate + fly.Phase)
			fly.Core.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - math.clamp((0.3 + 0.6 * pulse) * ctx.Intensity, 0, 1)
			fly.Halo.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - math.clamp(0.16 * pulse * ctx.Intensity, 0, 1)
		end
	end,
})

Aurora:RegisterBackground("Shapes", {
	Color = "Text",
	Build = function(ctx)
		local rng, shapes = ctx.Random, {}
		local kinds = { "ring", "square", "triangle", "cross" }
		for i = 1, Budget(ctx, 16) do
			local size = rng:NextNumber(18, 56)
			local kind = kinds[(i - 1) % #kinds + 1]
			local holder = Create("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size), Parent = ctx.Layer })
			local parts = {}
			if kind == "ring" or kind == "square" then
				Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = (kind == "ring") and UDim.new(1, 0) or UDim.new(0, 4), Parent = holder })
				parts[1] = Create("UIStroke", { Color = ctx.Color, Thickness = 1.5, Transparency = 1, Parent = holder })
			elseif kind == "triangle" then
				local r = size / 2
				local points = {}
				for k = 0, 2 do
					local a = math.rad(-90 + 120 * k)
					points[k + 1] = Vector2.new(r + math.cos(a) * r, r + math.sin(a) * r)
				end
				for k = 1, 3 do
					local p1, p2 = points[k], points[k % 3 + 1]
					local d = p2 - p1
					parts[k] = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = ctx.Color, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
						Position = UDim2.fromOffset((p1.X + p2.X) / 2, (p1.Y + p2.Y) / 2), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(d.Magnitude, 1.5), Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(d.Y, d.X)), Parent = holder })
				end
			else
				for k, rotation in ipairs({ 0, 90 }) do
					parts[k] = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = ctx.Color, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
						Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1.5), Rotation = rotation, Parent = holder })
				end
			end
			shapes[i] = {
				Frame = holder, Parts = parts, X = rng:NextNumber(), Y = rng:NextNumber(), VX = rng:NextNumber(-0.012, 0.012), VY = rng:NextNumber(-0.012, 0.012),
				Spin = rng:NextNumber(-25, 25), Rotation = rng:NextNumber(0, 360), Depth = size / 56,
			}
		end
		return { Shapes = shapes }
	end,
	Step = function(state, ctx, dt)
		for _, shape in ipairs(state.Shapes) do
			shape.X = (shape.X + shape.VX * dt * ctx.Speed + 0.1) % 1.2 - 0.1
			shape.Y = (shape.Y + shape.VY * dt * ctx.Speed + 0.1) % 1.2 - 0.1
			shape.Rotation = shape.Rotation + shape.Spin * dt * ctx.Speed
			local ox, oy = Parallax(ctx, shape.Depth, 26)
			shape.Frame.Position = UDim2.new(shape.X, ox, shape.Y, oy)
			shape.Frame.Rotation = shape.Rotation
			local alpha = 1 - math.clamp((0.12 + 0.22 * shape.Depth) * ctx.Intensity, 0, 1)
			for _, part in ipairs(shape.Parts) do
				if part:IsA("UIStroke") then part.Transparency = alpha else part.BackgroundTransparency = alpha end
			end
		end
	end,
})



-- Switches the animated background; the old one fades out while the new one fades in.
function Window:SetBackground(name, options)
	if type(name) == "table" then
		options = name
		name = options.Name
	end
	name = name or self.BackgroundName or "Default"
	local def = Aurora.Backgrounds[name]
	if not def then
		warn("[AuroraUI] Unknown background '" .. tostring(name) .. "'")
		name, def = "Default", Aurora.Backgrounds.Default
	end
	self.BackgroundOptions = Merge(self.BackgroundOptions or { Intensity = 1, Speed = 1 }, options or {})
	self.BackgroundOptions.Name = nil
	local opts = self.BackgroundOptions
	if not self.BackgroundLayer then
		return
	end
	if self._background then
		self._background.FadingOut = true
		self._backgroundOld = self._backgroundOld or {}
		table.insert(self._backgroundOld, self._background)
	end
	local container = Create("Frame", { Name = name, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 0, Parent = self.BackgroundLayer })
	local colorOption = opts.Color or def.Color or "Text"
	local ctx = {
		Window = self, Layer = container, Options = opts, Random = Random.new(), Tilt = Vector2.new(),
		BaseIntensity = opts.Intensity or 1, Intensity = 0, Speed = opts.Speed or 1, Fade = 0,
		Color = (typeof(colorOption) == "Color3") and colorOption or self:_T(colorOption),
		Color2 = self:_T("AccentSecondary"),
	}
	local ok, state = pcall(def.Build, ctx)
	if not ok then
		warn("[AuroraUI] Background '" .. name .. "' failed to build: " .. tostring(state))
		state = {}
	end
	self.BackgroundName = name
	self._background = { Def = def, State = state or {}, Ctx = ctx, Container = container }
	for _, blob in ipairs(self.Blobs or {}) do
		Tween(blob.Frame, { BackgroundTransparency = (name == "Default") and blob.Spec.alpha or 1 }, 0.5)
	end
end

function Window:SetBackgroundOptions(options)
	self:SetBackground(self.BackgroundName or "Default", options)
end

function Window:_StepBackground(dt, t)
	-- Scenes do not need a step every frame. They run on a budget and are handed the time that passed,
	-- so nothing moves faster or slower, it is just recalculated less often.
	local rate = tonumber(self.Effects.BackgroundRate) or 40
	if rate > 0 then
		self._bgAccum = (self._bgAccum or 0) + dt
		if self._bgAccum < 1 / rate then
			return
		end
		dt = self._bgAccum
		self._bgAccum = 0
	end
	local function run(background, direction)
		local ctx = background.Ctx
		ctx.Fade = math.clamp(ctx.Fade + direction * dt / 0.5, 0, 1)
		ctx.Intensity = ctx.BaseIntensity * ctx.Fade
		-- stars and other scenes sit far below the panel: they shift against the floating layers
		ctx.Tilt = self.Effects.Parallax and (self._view or Vector2.new()) * (5 * (tonumber(self.Effects.DepthAmount) or 1)) or Vector2.new()
		if background.Def.Step and not background.Errored then
			local ok, err = pcall(background.Def.Step, background.State, ctx, dt, t)
			if not ok then
				background.Errored = true
				warn("[AuroraUI] Background '" .. tostring(background.Def.Name) .. "' step failed: " .. tostring(err))
			end
		end
	end
	if self._background then
		run(self._background, 1)
	end
	if self._backgroundOld then
		for index = #self._backgroundOld, 1, -1 do
			local old = self._backgroundOld[index]
			run(old, -1)
			if old.Ctx.Fade <= 0 then
				if old.Def.Destroy then pcall(old.Def.Destroy, old.State) end
				old.Container:Destroy()
				table.remove(self._backgroundOld, index)
			end
		end
	end
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Per-frame loop: tilt, parallax, glow, tooltip
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function Window:_Step(dt)
	local root = self.Root
	if not root.Visible then
		return
	end
	local t = os.clock()
	local effects = self.Effects
	self._frameStamp = (self._frameStamp or 0) + 1
	if self.AutoOptimize then
		self:_StepAutoOptimize(dt)   -- measured on real frames, before any of the skipping below
	end
	-- Optimization levels 4 and up run this loop on every second, third ... frame. Everything inside is
	-- driven by the time that passed, so motion keeps its speed; it is only recalculated less often.
	local skip = self._stepSkip or 0
	if skip > 0 then
		self._stepCarry = (self._stepCarry or 0) + dt
		if self._frameStamp % (skip + 1) ~= 0 then
			return
		end
		dt = self._stepCarry
		self._stepCarry = 0
	end

	-- tilt target
	local target = Vector2.new()
	local hovering = false
	local mouse = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
	local pointer = self:_Pointer()
	local abs, size = root.AbsolutePosition, root.AbsoluteSize
	if size.X > 0 and size.Y > 0 then
		-- hysteresis: enter when inside, leave only when well outside, so the moving
		-- edge of a tilted panel can never flip hover on and off under the cursor
		local margin = self._hovering and 40 or 0
		local nx = (pointer.X - abs.X) / size.X
		local ny = (pointer.Y - abs.Y) / size.Y
		local mx, my = margin / size.X, margin / size.Y
		if nx >= -mx and nx <= 1 + mx and ny >= -my and ny <= 1 + my then
			hovering = true
			target = Vector2.new(math.clamp((nx - 0.5) * 2, -1, 1), math.clamp((ny - 0.5) * 2, -1, 1))
		end
	end
	self._hovering = hovering
	local motionLocked = false
	for _, check in ipairs(self._motionLocks) do
		local ok, result = pcall(check)
		if ok and result then
			motionLocked = true
			break
		end
	end
	if not effects.Tilt or self.Minimized or (self.Drag and self.Drag.IsDragging()) or motionLocked then
		target = Vector2.new()
		hovering = false
	end
	local alpha = 1 - math.exp(-dt * 7)
	self._tilt = self._tilt:Lerp(target, alpha)
	self._hoverLift = Lerp(self._hoverLift or 0, hovering and 1 or 0, alpha)
	local tilt = self._tilt
	local strength = (effects.TiltStrength or 1)

	-- press depth eases in and out quickly
	self._press = Lerp(self._press or 0, (effects.PressDepth and self._pressing and not motionLocked) and 1 or 0, 1 - math.exp(-dt * 20))
	-- scale (user scale x open/close pop x hover lift x press)
	local visualScale = self.UIScale * self._popValue.Value * (1 + self._hoverLift * 0.004 * strength)
	if not self.Render3D then
		visualScale = visualScale * (1 - self._press * 0.008)
	end
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	if self.Render3D and camera and self.SurfacePart then
		-- real 3D tilt: the part in front of the camera is rotated towards the cursor
		local part = self.SurfacePart
		if part.Parent ~= camera then
			part.Parent = camera
		end
		local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
		local distance = self.SurfaceDistance
		local studsPerPixel = (2 * distance * math.tan(math.rad(camera.FieldOfView) / 2)) / math.max(1, viewport.Y)
		local canvas = self.CanvasSize
		part.Size = Vector3.new(canvas.X * studsPerPixel * visualScale, canvas.Y * studsPerPixel * visualScale, 0.05)
		local angle = math.rad(effects.TiltAngle or 6) * strength
		local offset = self._screenOffset
		angle = angle * (effects.TiltDirection or 1)
		-- idle float: a slow drift + micro-rotation while the cursor is away
		local floatX, floatY, floatRoll = 0, 0, 0
		if effects.Float then
			local idle = 1 - self._hoverLift
			floatX = math.sin(t * 0.7) * 3 * idle
			floatY = math.cos(t * 0.9) * 4 * idle
			floatRoll = math.rad(math.sin(t * 0.5) * 0.6) * idle
		end
		-- magnet: lean a few pixels towards the cursor
		local magnetTarget = Vector2.new()
		if effects.Magnet and not self.Minimized and hovering then
			local pull = (mouse - (viewport / 2 + offset)) * 0.03
			local limit = 16 * (effects.MagnetStrength or 1)
			magnetTarget = Vector2.new(math.clamp(pull.X, -limit, limit), math.clamp(pull.Y, -limit, limit))
		end
		self._magnet = (self._magnet or Vector2.new()):Lerp(magnetTarget, 1 - math.exp(-dt * 4))
		-- drag lean: velocity of the window across the screen
		local lastOffset = self._lastOffset or offset
		local velocity = (offset - lastOffset) / math.max(dt, 1 / 240)
		self._lastOffset = offset
		local leanTarget = Vector2.new()
		if effects.DragTilt then
			leanTarget = Vector2.new(math.clamp(velocity.X * 0.0025, -1, 1), math.clamp(velocity.Y * 0.0025, -1, 1))
		end
		self._dragLean = (self._dragLean or Vector2.new()):Lerp(leanTarget, 1 - math.exp(-dt * 10))
		-- camera inertia: turning the camera kicks a spring that swings the panel and lets it settle
		local inertia, inertiaVelocity = self._inertia or Vector2.new(), self._inertiaVelocity or Vector2.new()
		if effects.CameraInertia and self._lastCameraCF then
			local rx, ry = self._lastCameraCF:ToObjectSpace(camera.CFrame):ToEulerAnglesYXZ()
			-- the kick is the angle turned this frame, so it is frame-rate independent:
			-- a steady 3 rad/s turn settles around 0.06 rad (about 3.5 degrees) of swing at strength 1
			local gain = 1.8 * (effects.InertiaStrength or 1)
			inertiaVelocity = inertiaVelocity + Vector2.new(-math.clamp(ry, -0.15, 0.15), -math.clamp(rx, -0.15, 0.15)) * gain
		end
		self._lastCameraCF = camera.CFrame
		inertiaVelocity = inertiaVelocity + (-inertia * 90 - inertiaVelocity * 11) * dt
		inertia = inertia + inertiaVelocity * dt
		inertia = Vector2.new(math.clamp(inertia.X, -0.22, 0.22), math.clamp(inertia.Y, -0.22, 0.22))
		self._inertia, self._inertiaVelocity = inertia, inertiaVelocity
		-- fly-in: the panel starts further away and pitched back (and turned, with OpenSpin), then settles
		local fly = self._flyValue.Value
		local flyDistance = fly * 9
		local flyPitch = math.rad(-20) * fly
		local flyYaw = effects.OpenSpin and math.rad(34) * fly or 0
		local swing = effects.PageSwing and self._swingValue.Value or 0
		local depth = -distance - flyDistance - self._press * 0.35 + (effects.HoverDepth and self._hoverLift * 0.18 or 0)
		local lean = self._dragLean
		local total = offset + self._magnet
		local targetCF = camera.CFrame
			* CFrame.new((total.X + floatX) * studsPerPixel, -(total.Y + floatY) * studsPerPixel, depth)
			* CFrame.Angles(
				tilt.Y * angle + flyPitch + inertia.Y + lean.Y * math.rad(6),
				tilt.X * angle + flyYaw + swing * math.rad(14) + inertia.X,
				floatRoll - lean.X * math.rad(5)
			)
		if effects.Sway and self._swayCF then
			-- spring-damped follow: the panel lags a touch behind camera motion, which sells it as a physical object
			self._swayCF = self._swayCF:Lerp(targetCF, 1 - math.exp(-dt * 16))
			part.CFrame = self._swayCF
		else
			self._swayCF = targetCF
			part.CFrame = targetCF
		end
		-- how the camera sees the panel: tangents of its turn and pitch, used by the depth layers
		local look = part.CFrame:VectorToObjectSpace(camera.CFrame.LookVector)
		local facing = math.max(0.25, -look.Z)
		self._view = Vector2.new(look.X / facing, -look.Y / facing)
		self._scaleObj.Scale = 1
	else
		self._scaleObj.Scale = visualScale
		self._view = tilt * 0.2
	end

	-- shadow offset (light from above, opposite the tilt)
	if effects.Shadows then
		for _, layer in ipairs(self.ShadowLayers) do
			layer.Frame.Visible = true
			layer.Frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -tilt.X * layer.Spread * 0.3 * strength, 0.5, layer.Spread * 0.5 - tilt.Y * layer.Spread * 0.3 * strength)
			-- the shadow spreads and lightens as the panel lifts towards the viewer
			local lift = self._hoverLift
			layer.Frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, layer.Spread * 2 + lift * 10, 1, layer.Spread * 2 + lift * 10)
			if not self._shadowFading then
				layer.Frame.BackgroundTransparency = layer.Alpha + lift * 0.06
			end
		end
	else
		for _, layer in ipairs(self.ShadowLayers) do
			layer.Frame.Visible = false
		end
	end

	-- specular sheen + depth lighting (lit edge swings towards the light as the panel tilts)
	self.SpecularGradient.Offset = Vector2.new(tilt.X * 0.25, tilt.Y * 0.25)
	self.Specular.Visible = effects.Tilt
	if effects.DepthLighting then
		self.LightStroke.Enabled = true
		self.LightGradient.Rotation = 90 + tilt.X * 55 * strength
		self.LightStroke.Transparency = 0.82 - math.max(0, -tilt.Y) * 0.25 * strength
		self.ShadeGradient.Enabled = true
		self.ShadeGradient.Rotation = 90 + tilt.X * 20
	else
		self.LightStroke.Enabled = false
		self.ShadeGradient.Enabled = false
	end

	-- depth layers: sidebar, header and content float above the panel, the background blobs sit far below it
	local depthAmount = (effects.Parallax and 1 or 0) * (tonumber(effects.DepthAmount) or 1)
	local view = self._view or Vector2.new()
	for _, layer in ipairs(self._parallaxLayers) do
		local base = layer.Base
		if layer.From then
			local progress = math.clamp((t - layer.Start) / math.max(1e-3, layer.Duration), 0, 1)
			if progress >= 1 then
				layer.From = nil
			else
				base = layer.From:Lerp(layer.Base, 1 - (1 - progress) ^ 4)
			end
		end
		local dx = math.clamp(view.X * layer.Depth * depthAmount, -14, 14)
		local dy = math.clamp(view.Y * layer.Depth * depthAmount, -14, 14)
		layer.Object.Position = base + UDim2.fromOffset(dx, dy)
	end
	for index, blob in ipairs(self.Blobs) do
		local spec = blob.Spec
		local driftX = math.sin(t * 0.3 + index) * 6
		local driftY = math.cos(t * 0.25 + index * 2) * 5
		blob.Frame.Position = UDim2.new(spec.x, -view.X * spec.depth * 10 * depthAmount + driftX, spec.y, -view.Y * spec.depth * 10 * depthAmount + driftY)
	end
	-- card shades turn with the same light as the panel edge
	local shadeOn = effects.DepthLighting == true
	local shadeRotation = shadeOn and math.floor(90 - math.clamp(view.X * 260, -40, 40) + 0.5) or 90
	local shadeState = (shadeOn and 1000 or 0) + shadeRotation
	if shadeState ~= self._shadeState then
		self._shadeState = shadeState
		for gradient in pairs(self._depthShades) do
			if gradient.Parent then
				gradient.Rotation = shadeRotation
				gradient.Enabled = shadeOn
			else
				self._depthShades[gradient] = nil
			end
		end
	end

	-- glow border
	if effects.Glow then
		self.GlowStroke.Enabled = true
		if effects.AnimatedGradient then
			self.GlowGradient.Rotation = (t * 40) % 360
		end
	else
		self.GlowStroke.Enabled = false
	end
	if effects.AnimatedGradient and self.TitleGradient then
		self.TitleGradient.Offset = Vector2.new(math.sin(t * 0.8) * 0.5, 0)
	end
	-- credit chip: a shine drifting across the text and a slow pulse on the dot
	if self.CreditGradient then
		self.CreditGradient.Offset = Vector2.new(effects.AnimatedGradient and (((t * 0.22) % 2) - 1) or 0, 0)
		self.CreditDot.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1 + 0.4 * (0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(t * 2.1))
	end
	-- cursor edge glow
	if effects.EdgeGlow and not hovering and size.X > 0 then
		local cx = math.clamp(pointer.X, abs.X, abs.X + size.X)
		local cy = math.clamp(pointer.Y, abs.Y, abs.Y + size.Y)
		local dist = (Vector2.new(cx, cy) - pointer).Magnitude
		if dist < 160 and self.Root.Visible then
			self.EdgeGlow.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx - abs.X, cy - abs.Y)
			self.EdgeGlow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.86 + (dist / 160) * 0.14
		else
			self.EdgeGlow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		end
	else
		self.EdgeGlow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	end

	self:_StepBackground(dt, t)
	for _, hook in ipairs(self._stepHooks) do
		hook(t, dt)
	end
	self:_StepGlass()

	-- What this frame cost, charged to the page that is in front. Pages are judged on their own average,
	-- which is what lets the high optimization levels put the expensive ones away.
	local spent = os.clock() - t
	local page = self.CurrentPage
	if page and not page.IsSettings then
		page._costSamples = (page._costSamples or 0) + 1
		page._cost = ((page._cost or spent) * 0.98) + (spent * 0.02)
	end
	self._uiCost = ((self._uiCost or spent) * 0.95) + (spent * 0.05)

	if self.Debug and self.DebugLabel then
		self._debugFrames = (self._debugFrames or 0) + 1
		self._debugTime = (self._debugTime or 0) + spent
		if self._debugFrames >= 15 then
			local logic = self:LogicLoad()
			self.DebugLabel.Text = string.format("ui step  %.3f ms\nelements %d   pages %d\npointer  %d, %d\ntilt     %.2f, %.2f   lift %.2f\nlogic    %d/s over %d graph%s   opt %d",
				(self._debugTime / self._debugFrames) * 1000, #self.Elements, #self.Pages, pointer.X, pointer.Y, tilt.X, tilt.Y, self._hoverLift,
				logic.PerSecond, logic.Running, (logic.Running == 1) and "" or "s", self.Optimization or 1)
			self._debugFrames, self._debugTime = 0, 0
		end
	end

	-- tooltip follows the cursor
	if self._tooltipVisible and self.TooltipFrame then
		local viewport = self.Gui.AbsoluteSize
		local tipSize = self.TooltipFrame.AbsoluteSize
		local x = math.min(mouse.X + 14, viewport.X - tipSize.X - 4)
		local y = math.min(mouse.Y + 18, viewport.Y - tipSize.Y - 4)
		self.TooltipFrame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y)
	end
end

-- Registers a check; while any check returns true the panel holds still (no tilt, magnet or press depth).
-- Editors use this so dragging nodes and wires is not fighting a moving surface.
function Window:_AddMotionLock(check)
	table.insert(self._motionLocks, check)
	return function()
		for index = #self._motionLocks, 1, -1 do
			if self._motionLocks[index] == check then
				table.remove(self._motionLocks, index)
			end
		end
	end
end

-- One set of input connections for the whole window instead of two or three per element.
-- Hooks are skipped while the window is closed; _OnInputAlways keeps running (element keybinds).
function Window:_OnInput(kind, fn, always)
	local list = self._inputHooks[kind]
	if not list then
		return { Destroy = function() end }
	end
	local entry = { Fn = fn, Always = always == true }
	list[#list + 1] = entry
	return {
		Destroy = function()
			entry.Dead = true
			self._inputDirty = true
		end,
	}
end

function Window:_OnInputAlways(kind, fn)
	return self:_OnInput(kind, fn, true)
end

-- Is a key held? Headless input can pretend one is.
function Window:_KeyDown(keyCode)
	if self._simKeys then
		return self._simKeys[keyCode] == true
	end
	return UserInputService:IsKeyDown(keyCode)
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Headless input, for Tests.lua. The pointer is placed by hand and the same handlers the real input reaches are
-- called, so slider drags, node drags and box selects can be checked without a mouse:
--   window:_SimPointer(Vector2)                  -- where the pointer is (nil = the real one again)
--   window:_SimInput("Changed" | "Ended" | "Began", userInputType, keyCode)
--   window:_SimDrag(press, { Vector2, ... }, { [Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift] = true })
-- press is the element's own press handler (slider: element._press, logic node: view.Press, canvas: editor._pressCanvas).
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function Window:_SimPointer(position)
	self._simPointer = position
end

function Window:_SimInput(kind, userInputType, keyCode)
	local point = self._simPointer or Vector2.zero
	local input = {
		UserInputType = userInputType or Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1,
		UserInputState = (kind == "Ended") and Enum.UserInputState.End or ((kind == "Began") and Enum.UserInputState.Begin or Enum.UserInputState.Change),
		KeyCode = keyCode or Enum.KeyCode.Unknown,
		Position = Vector3.new(point.X, point.Y, 0),
		Delta = Vector3.zero,
	}
	self._simulating = true
	self:_Dispatch(kind, input, false)
	self._simulating = false
	return input
end

function Window:_SimDrag(press, points, keys)
	self._simKeys = keys
	self:_SimPointer(points[1])
	if type(press) == "function" then
		press({ UserInputType = Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1, UserInputState = Enum.UserInputState.Begin, KeyCode = Enum.KeyCode.Unknown })
	end
	for index = 2, #points do
		self:_SimPointer(points[index])
		self:_SimInput("Changed", Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement)
	end
	self:_SimInput("Ended", Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)
	self._simKeys = nil
	self:_SimPointer(nil)
end

function Window:_Dispatch(kind, input, processed)
	local list = self._inputHooks[kind]
	if not list then
		return
	end
	if self._inputDirty then
		self._inputDirty = false
		for _, hooks in pairs(self._inputHooks) do
			for index = #hooks, 1, -1 do
				if hooks[index].Dead then
					table.remove(hooks, index)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	local open = self.IsOpen or self._simulating
	for index = 1, #list do
		local entry = list[index]
		if entry and not entry.Dead and (open or entry.Always) then
			local ok, err = pcall(entry.Fn, input, processed)
			if not ok then
				warn("[AuroraUI] Input handler error: " .. tostring(err))
			end
		end
	end
end

function Window:_AddStepHook(fn)
	self._stepHooks[#self._stepHooks + 1] = fn
	return function()
		for index = #self._stepHooks, 1, -1 do
			if self._stepHooks[index] == fn then
				table.remove(self._stepHooks, index)
			end
		end
	end
end

function Window:_StartLoop()
	if self._loop then
		return
	end
	self._loop = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
		self:_Step(dt)
	end)
end

function Window:_StopLoop()
	if self._loop then
		self._loop:Disconnect()
		self._loop = nil
	end
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Build the GUI tree
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function Window:_BuildGui(options)
	local theme = self.Theme
	local radius = self.CornerRadius

	self.Gui = Create("ScreenGui", {
		Name = "AuroraUI", IgnoreGuiInset = true, ResetOnSpawn = false,
		ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling, DisplayOrder = 100, Parent = self.GuiParent,
	})
	self.Maid:Add(self.Gui)

	-- 3D mode: the window lives on a SurfaceGui adorned to a part held in front of the camera.
	-- The part's CFrame is tilted every frame, so the perspective is real and nothing is
	-- rotated in 2D (which would break ClipsDescendants / scrolling frames).
	local rootParent = self.Gui
	if self.Render3D then
		local w, h = self.Size.X.Offset, self.Size.Y.Offset
		local margin = self.CanvasMargin
		self.CanvasSize = Vector2.new(w + margin * 2, h + margin * 2)
		self.SurfacePart = Create("Part", {
			Name = "AuroraSurface", Anchored = true, CanCollide = false, CanQuery = false, CanTouch = false,
			CastShadow = false, Transparency = 1, Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 0.05), Parent = workspace.CurrentCamera,
		})
		self.Surface = Create("SurfaceGui", {
			Name = "AuroraUI3D", Adornee = self.SurfacePart, Face = Enum.NormalId.Back, AlwaysOnTop = true,
			SizingMode = Enum.SurfaceGuiSizingMode.FixedSize, CanvasSize = self.CanvasSize, LightInfluence = 0,
			Brightness = 1, MaxDistance = 1e6, ResetOnSpawn = false, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
			Parent = self.GuiParent,
		})
		self.Maid:Add(self.SurfacePart)
		self.Maid:Add(self.Surface)
		self.SurfaceEntry = { Part = self.SurfacePart, Canvas = self.CanvasSize, Gui = self.Surface }
		self._surfaces[#self._surfaces + 1] = self.SurfaceEntry
		rootParent = self.Surface
	end

	self.Root = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Root", BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = self.Size, Visible = false, Parent = rootParent,
	})
	self._scaleObj = Create("UIScale", { Scale = 1, Parent = self.Root })
	self._popValue = Create("NumberValue", { Name = "Pop", Value = 1, Parent = self.Root })
	self._flyValue = Create("NumberValue", { Name = "Fly", Value = 0, Parent = self.Root })
	self._swingValue = Create("NumberValue", { Name = "Swing", Value = 0, Parent = self.Root })

	-- soft layered shadow
	self.ShadowLayers = {}
	for index, layer in ipairs({ { spread = 10, alpha = 0.72 } }) do
		local shadow = Create("Frame", {
			Name = "Shadow" .. index, BackgroundColor3 = theme.Shadow, BackgroundTransparency = layer.alpha,
			BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
			Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, layer.spread * 0.5), Size = UDim2.new(1, layer.spread * 2, 1, layer.spread * 2),
			ZIndex = 0, Parent = self.Root,
		})
		self:_Corner(shadow, radius + layer.spread)
		self:_Bind(shadow, "BackgroundColor3", "Shadow")
		-- fade the shadow towards its edges so it reads as one soft glow, not a stacked frame
		Create("UIGradient", {
			Rotation = 90,
			Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumKey(0, 0.55), NumKey(0.5, 0), NumKey(1, 0.25) }),
			Parent = shadow,
		})
		self.ShadowLayers[index] = { Frame = shadow, Spread = layer.spread, Alpha = layer.alpha }
	end

	-- tilt layer + main panel
	self.Tilt = Create("Frame", { Name = "Tilt", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 1, Parent = self.Root })
	self.Main = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Main", BackgroundColor3 = theme.Background, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		ClipsDescendants = true, Parent = self.Tilt,
	})
	self:_Corner(self.Main, radius)
	self:_Bind(self.Main, "BackgroundColor3", "Background")
	-- frosted glass behind the panel (Acrylic effect)
	self:_AddGlass(self.Main, {
		Surface = self.SurfaceEntry, Radius = radius,
		IsVisible = function()
			return self.IsOpen and self.Root.Visible
		end,
	})
	self.GlowStroke = Create("UIStroke", { Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.35, Color = theme.Stroke, Parent = self.Main })
	self.GlowGradient = Create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 0, Parent = self.GlowStroke })
	self:_OnTheme(function(current)
		self.GlowGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({ Keypoint(0, current.Stroke), Keypoint(0.5, current.Stroke:Lerp(current.Accent, 0.5)), Keypoint(1, current.Stroke) })
	end)

	-- parallax blobs
	self.Backdrop = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Backdrop", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ClipsDescendants = true, ZIndex = 0, Parent = self.Main,
	})
	self.BackgroundLayer = Create("Frame", { Name = "Background", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 0, Parent = self.Backdrop })
	self.Blobs = {}
	local blobSpecs = {
		{ token = "Accent", size = 340, x = 0.9, y = 0.05, depth = 8, alpha = 0.965 },
		{ token = "AccentSecondary", size = 280, x = 0.1, y = 1, depth = 12, alpha = 0.97 },
	}
	for index, spec in ipairs(blobSpecs) do
		local blob = Create("Frame", {
			Name = "Blob" .. index, BackgroundTransparency = spec.alpha, BorderSizePixel = 0,
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(spec.x, spec.y),
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(spec.size, spec.size), Parent = self.Backdrop,
		}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
		self:_Bind(blob, "BackgroundColor3", spec.token)
		self.Blobs[index] = { Frame = blob, Spec = spec }
	end

	-- specular sheen
	self.Specular = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Specular", BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 0, Parent = self.Main,
	})
	self.SpecularGradient = Create("UIGradient", {
		Rotation = 35, Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumKey(0, 1), NumKey(0.45, 0.985), NumKey(0.5, 0.97), NumKey(0.55, 0.985), NumKey(1, 1) }),
		Parent = self.Specular,
	})

	-- ===== Sidebar (brand, search, pages, discord, footer) =====
	local sidebarWidth = self.Layout.SidebarWidth
	self.SidebarWidth = sidebarWidth
	-- floating panel inset from the window edge
	local sidebar = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Sidebar", BackgroundColor3 = theme.Secondary, BackgroundTransparency = 0, BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8), Size = UDim2.new(0, sidebarWidth - 8, 1, -16), ZIndex = 2, Parent = self.Main,
	})
	self:_Bind(sidebar, "BackgroundColor3", "Secondary")
	self:_Corner(sidebar, 10)
	local sidebarStroke = Create("UIStroke", { Color = theme.Stroke, Transparency = 0.5, Parent = sidebar })
	self:_Bind(sidebarStroke, "Color", "Stroke")
	self.Sidebar = sidebar
	-- the slot a shorter sidebar sits in (Settings > Layout > Sidebar height); hidden at full height
	local sidebarTrack = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "SidebarTrack", BackgroundColor3 = theme.Secondary, BackgroundTransparency = 0.82, BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8), Size = UDim2.new(0, sidebarWidth - 8, 1, -16), ZIndex = 1, Visible = false, Parent = self.Main,
	})
	self:_Bind(sidebarTrack, "BackgroundColor3", "Secondary")
	self:_Corner(sidebarTrack, 10)
	local trackStroke = Create("UIStroke", { Color = theme.Stroke, Transparency = 0.8, Parent = sidebarTrack })
	self:_Bind(trackStroke, "Color", "Stroke")
	self.SidebarTrack = sidebarTrack
	local sideDivider = Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = theme.Stroke, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false,
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -1, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 1, 0), Parent = sidebar,
	})
	self:_Bind(sideDivider, "BackgroundColor3", "Stroke")

	-- brand block
	local brand = Create("Frame", { Name = "Brand", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 78), Parent = sidebar })
	self.Brand = brand
	local mark = Create("Frame", {
		-- lines up with the title and subtitle, not with the whole brand block (the credit chip sits below them)
		Name = "Mark", BackgroundColor3 = theme.Accent, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 11), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 30), Parent = brand,
	})
	self:_Corner(mark, 9)
	local markGradient = Create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 45, Parent = mark })
	self:_OnTheme(function(current)
		markGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({ Keypoint(0, current.Accent), Keypoint(1, current.Accent:Lerp(current.AccentSecondary, 0.6)) })
	end)
	self.BrandMark = mark
	-- the brand mark leans and grows a little under the cursor
	local markScale = Create("UIScale", { Scale = 1, Parent = mark })
	mark.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		Tween(markScale, { Scale = 1.08 }, 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		Tween(mark, { Rotation = -8 }, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
	end)
	mark.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		Tween(markScale, { Scale = 1 }, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		Tween(mark, { Rotation = 0 }, 0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
	end)
	self:SetLogo(options.Icon or options.Logo, options.LogoColor, options.CustomIcon or options.LogoCustomIcon)
	local brandText = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "BrandText", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 54, 0, 9), Size = UDim2.new(1, -62, 0, 34), Parent = brand,
	})
	self.BrandText = brandText
	Create("UIListLayout", {
		SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center, Padding = UDim.new(0, 1), Parent = brandText,
	})
	self.TitleLabel = self:_Label({
		Name = "Title", Text = self.Title, TextSize = 14, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18), LayoutOrder = 1, Parent = brandText,
	}, "Bold", "Text")
	self.TitleGradient = Create("UIGradient", { Parent = self.TitleLabel })
	self:_OnTheme(function(current)
		self.TitleGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({ Keypoint(0, current.Text), Keypoint(1, current.Text) })
	end)
	self.SubtitleLabel = self:_Label({
		Name = "Subtitle", Text = self.Subtitle or "", TextSize = 11, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14), LayoutOrder = 2,
		Visible = self.Subtitle ~= nil and tostring(self.Subtitle) ~= "", Parent = brandText,
	}, "Regular", "SubText")

	-- credit chip under the title: a pill with a pulsing accent dot and a slow shine across the text
	local credit = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Credit", BackgroundColor3 = theme.Element, BackgroundTransparency = 0.3, BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 50), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 19), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Parent = brand,
	})
	self:_Corner(credit, 9)
	self:_Bind(credit, "BackgroundColor3", "Element")
	local creditStroke = Create("UIStroke", { Color = theme.Stroke, Transparency = 0.5, Parent = credit })
	self:_Bind(creditStroke, "Color", "Stroke")
	Create("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 7), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 9), Parent = credit })
	Create("UIListLayout", {
		FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center,
		Padding = UDim.new(0, 5), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = credit,
	})
	local creditDot = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Dot", BackgroundColor3 = theme.Accent, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 5), LayoutOrder = 1, Parent = credit,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
	self:_Bind(creditDot, "BackgroundColor3", "Accent")
	local creditLabel = self:_Label({
		Name = "Label", Text = "Made By Team DJ UI Lib", TextSize = 10, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 19),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None, LayoutOrder = 2, Parent = credit,
	}, "Bold", "SubText")
	local creditGradient = Create("UIGradient", { Parent = creditLabel })
	self:_OnTheme(function(current)
		creditGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
			Keypoint(0, current.SubText), Keypoint(0.45, current.Text), Keypoint(0.55, current.Accent), Keypoint(1, current.SubText),
		})
	end)
	self.CreditChip, self.CreditLabel, self.CreditGradient, self.CreditDot = credit, creditLabel, creditGradient, creditDot
	self:_Tooltip(credit, "Aurora UI  ·  Made By Team DJ UI Lib")

	local listTop = 82
	if options.SearchBar ~= false then
		local searchHolder = Create("Frame", {
			Name = "Search", BackgroundColor3 = theme.Element, BackgroundTransparency = 0.3, BorderSizePixel = 0,
			Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 82), Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 30), Parent = sidebar,
		})
		self.SearchHolder = searchHolder
		self:_Corner(searchHolder, 8)
		self:_Bind(searchHolder, "BackgroundColor3", "Element")
		local searchStroke = Create("UIStroke", { Color = theme.Stroke, Transparency = 0.6, Parent = searchHolder })
		self:_Bind(searchStroke, "Color", "Stroke")
		local iconHolder = Create("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16), Position = UDim2.new(0, 9, 0.5, -8), Parent = searchHolder })
		local _, iconParts = MakeSearchIcon(iconHolder, 13, theme.SubText)
		self:_Bind(iconParts[1], "Color", "SubText")
		self:_Bind(iconParts[2], "BackgroundColor3", "SubText")
		local searchBox = Create("TextBox", {
			Name = "Input", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", PlaceholderText = "Search",
			PlaceholderColor3 = theme.Placeholder, TextColor3 = theme.Text, TextSize = 12, ClearTextOnFocus = false,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Position = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -38, 1, 0),
			TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Parent = searchHolder,
		})
		self:_Font(searchBox, "Medium")
		self:_Bind(searchBox, "TextColor3", "Text")
		self:_Bind(searchBox, "PlaceholderColor3", "Placeholder")
		local searchScale = Create("UIScale", { Scale = 1, Parent = searchHolder })
		searchBox.Focused:Connect(function()
			Tween(searchStroke, { Color = self:_T("Accent"), Transparency = 0.2 }, 0.2)
			Tween(searchScale, { Scale = 1.03 }, 0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
			task.delay(Timed(0.12), function()
				Tween(searchScale, { Scale = 1 }, 0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
			end)
			Tween(iconHolder, { Rotation = -14 }, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		end)
		searchBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
			Tween(searchStroke, { Color = self:_T("Stroke"), Transparency = 0.6 }, 0.2)
			Tween(iconHolder, { Rotation = 0 }, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		end)
		local clearButton = Create("TextButton", {
			Name = "Clear", Text = "×", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundTransparency = 1, TextColor3 = theme.SubText,
			TextSize = 16, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20),
			Visible = false, Parent = searchHolder,
		})
		self:_Font(clearButton, "Medium")
		self:_Bind(clearButton, "TextColor3", "SubText")
		clearButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			searchBox.Text = ""
			searchBox:CaptureFocus()
		end)
		searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
			clearButton.Visible = searchBox.Text ~= ""
			-- typing fast should not rebuild the result list on every letter
			self._searchTicket = (self._searchTicket or 0) + 1
			local ticket = self._searchTicket
			task.delay(0.08, function()
				if ticket == self._searchTicket then
					self:SearchPages(searchBox.Text)
				end
			end)
		end)
		self.Maid:Add(self:_OnInput("Began", function(input)
			if not self.SearchResults or not self.SearchResults.Visible or not searchBox:IsFocused() then
				return
			end
			if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Down then
				self:_MoveSearchFocus(1)
			elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Up then
				self:_MoveSearchFocus(-1)
			elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Return or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.KeypadEnter then
				self:_ActivateSearchFocus()
			elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
				searchBox.Text = ""
			end
		end))
		self.SearchBox = searchBox
		listTop = 122
	end

	local bottomReserved = 34
	if options.Discord and options.Discord.Invite then
		bottomReserved = 78
	end
	self._sidebarBottomReserved = bottomReserved
	local pageList = Create("ScrollingFrame", {
		Name = "Pages", BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, listTop),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -listTop - bottomReserved), CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		ScrollBarThickness = 2, ScrollBarImageColor3 = theme.Accent, ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y, Parent = sidebar,
	})
	self:_Bind(pageList, "ScrollBarImageColor3", "Accent")
	Create("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10), PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 2), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 8), Parent = pageList })
	self.PageLayout = Create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 2), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = pageList })
	self.PageList = pageList

	if options.Discord and options.Discord.Invite then
		local discord = options.Discord
		local button = Create("TextButton", {
			Name = "Discord", Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(88, 101, 242), BorderSizePixel = 0,
			Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 1, -72), Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 34), ClipsDescendants = true, Parent = sidebar,
		})
		self:_Corner(button, 9)
		local logo = self:_MakeDiscordLogo(button, 16)
		logo.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0.5, -8)
		local label = Create("TextLabel", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = discord.Title or "Join the Discord", TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Position = UDim2.new(0, 36, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -44, 1, 0),
			TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Parent = button,
		})
		self:_Font(label, "Bold")
		button.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tween(button, { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(110, 122, 255) }, 0.15) end)
		button.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tween(button, { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(88, 101, 242) }, 0.2) end)
		button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			self:_Ripple(button, nil, "AccentText")
			self:_Sound("Click")
			self:CopyDiscordInvite(discord.Invite, discord.Callback)
		end)
		self.DiscordButton = button
	end

	local footer = self:_Label({
		Name = "Footer", Text = options.Footer or ("Aurora  v" .. Aurora.Version), TextSize = 10,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 1, -26), Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 16), TextTransparency = 0.45, Parent = sidebar,
	}, "Medium", "SubText")
	self.FooterLabel = footer

	-- ===== Header (page title + window buttons) =====
	local headerHeight = self.Layout.HeaderHeight
	self.HeaderHeight = headerHeight
	local topBar = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "TopBar", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, sidebarWidth, 0, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -sidebarWidth, 0, headerHeight), ZIndex = 3, Parent = self.Main,
	})
	self.TopBar = topBar
	self.PageTitle = self:_Label({
		Name = "PageTitle", Text = "", TextSize = 18, Position = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -120, 1, 0), Parent = topBar,
	}, "Bold", "Text")
	self.PageCount = self:_Label({
		Name = "PageCount", Text = "", TextSize = 11, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -96, 0.5, 0),

	}, "Medium", "SubText")

	local function windowButton(name, offsetX, onClick)

	self:_Bind(minGlyph, "BackgroundColor3", "SubText")
	self.WindowButtons = { Close = closeButton, Minimize = minButton }

	closeButton.MouseEnter:Connect(function()

	end)
	self:_Tooltip(closeButton, "Close  (" .. KeyName(self.Keybind) .. ")")
	self:_Tooltip(minButton, "Minimize")

	local topDivider = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Divider", BackgroundColor3 = theme.Stroke, BackgroundTransparency = 0.55, BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 22, 1, -1), Size = UDim2.new(1, -44, 0, 1), Parent = topBar,
	})

	self.TopDivider = topDivider

	-- ===== Content =====
	self.Content = Create("Frame", {

	self.PageCover = Create("Frame", {

	self:_Bind(self.PageCover, "BackgroundColor3", "Background")

	-- depth lighting: a lit top edge that moves with the tilt, and a vertical shade on the panel
	self.LightFrame = Create("Frame", { Name = "Light", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 2, Parent = self.Tilt })

	self.EdgeGlow = Create("Frame", {

	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
	self:_Bind(self.EdgeGlow, "BackgroundColor3", "Accent")

	-- mobile: sidebar becomes a slide-out drawer, header spans the full width, floating open button
	if self.Mobile then
		sidebar.ZIndex = 6
		sidebar.BackgroundTransparency = 0
		sidebar.Position = UDim2.new(0, -sidebarWidth, 0, 8)
		topBar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
		topBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, headerHeight)
		self.Content.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, headerHeight)
		self.Content.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -headerHeight)
		self.PageTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 58, 0, 0)
		self._pageTitleX = 58

			Name = "Menu", Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = theme.ElementHover, BackgroundTransparency = 1,

				BackgroundColor3 = theme.Text, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
				Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, i * 5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 2), Parent = menu,
			}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
			self:_Bind(bar, "BackgroundColor3", "Text")

		menu.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			self:SetSidebarOpen(not self.SidebarOpen)
		end)
		local floating = Create("TextButton", {
			Name = "OpenButton", Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = theme.Accent, BorderSizePixel = 0,
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1), Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 1, -18), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(46, 46), ZIndex = 60, Parent = self.Gui,
		}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
		self:_Bind(floating, "BackgroundColor3", "Accent")
		local floatingLabel = self:_Label({ Text = string.upper(string.sub(self.Title, 1, 1)), TextSize = 16, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = floating }, "Bold", "AccentText")
		floating.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			if not self.Loader then
				self:Toggle()
			end
		end)
		Aurora:MakeDraggable(floating, { Bounds = true })
		self.FloatingButton = floating
	end

	-- depth layers (pixels above the parent): the panel is the ground, the stars are the sky below it
	self:_ParallaxLayer(sidebar, 20)
	self:_ParallaxLayer(brand, 8)
	self:_ParallaxLayer(pageList, 10)
	self:_ParallaxLayer(topBar, 26)
	self:_ParallaxLayer(self.Content, 34)

	self:_ApplyLayout(false)

	-- ===== Tooltip =====

	-- ===== Drag =====
	if options.Draggable ~= false then
		if self.Render3D then

		else
			self.Drag = Aurora:MakeDraggable(self.Root, { Handle = self.TopBar, Bounds = true })
		end

end

-- Screen-space drag for 3D mode: moves the whole part instead of the Root frame.
function Window:_Make3DDrag(handles)
	local enabled, dragging = true, false
	local startMouse, startOffset = nil, nil
	if typeof(handles) == "Instance" then
		handles = { handles }
	end
	for _, handle in ipairs(handles) do
		self.Maid:Add(handle.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if not enabled or not IsClick(input) then
				return
			end
			dragging = true
			startMouse = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
			startOffset = self._screenOffset
		end))
	end
	self.Maid:Add(self:_OnInput("Changed", function(input)
		if dragging and enabled and IsMove(input) then
			local mouse = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
			self:_SetScreenOffset(startOffset + (mouse - startMouse))
		end
	end))
	self.Maid:Add(self:_OnInput("Ended", function(input)
		if dragging and IsClick(input) then
			dragging = false
		end
	end))
	return {
		Enable = function() enabled = true end,
		Disable = function() enabled = false; dragging = false end,
		IsDragging = function() return dragging end,
		Destroy = function() enabled = false; dragging = false end,
	}
end

-- Sets where the 3D window sits on screen (pixels from the viewport centre), clamped to the viewport.
function Window:_SetScreenOffset(offset)
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	if camera then
		local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
		local halfW = self.Size.X.Offset * 0.5 * self.UIScale
		local halfH = (self.Minimized and 50 or self.Size.Y.Offset) * 0.5 * self.UIScale
		local limitX = math.max(0, viewport.X * 0.5 - halfW)
		local limitY = math.max(0, viewport.Y * 0.5 - halfH)
		offset = Vector2.new(math.clamp(offset.X, -limitX, limitX), math.clamp(offset.Y, -limitY, limitY))
	end
	self._screenOffset = offset
end

-- Pointer position in the window's own coordinate space (canvas pixels in 3D mode, screen pixels in 2D).
-- Every element uses this instead of InputObject.Position so hit-testing stays correct while tilted.
function Window:_Pointer()
	if self._simPointer then
		return self._simPointer   -- headless input (Tests.lua) places the pointer by hand
	end
	local mouse = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
	if not self.Render3D then
		return Vector2.new(mouse.X, mouse.Y)
	end
	-- the projection is the same for every caller in a frame, so it is done once and shared
	local stamp = self._frameStamp or 0
	if self._pointerStamp == stamp and self._lastPointer then
		return self._lastPointer
	end
	self._pointerStamp = stamp
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	local fallback = self._lastPointer or Vector2.new(-100000, -100000)
	if not camera or not self.SurfacePart then
		return fallback
	end
	local ray = camera:ViewportPointToRay(mouse.X, mouse.Y)
	local function project(part, canvas)
		local cf, size = part.CFrame, part.Size
		local normal = cf:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(0, 0, 1))
		local denom = ray.Direction:Dot(normal)
		if math.abs(denom) < 1e-6 then
			return nil
		end
		local planePoint = cf.Position + normal * (size.Z / 2)
		local distance = (planePoint - ray.Origin):Dot(normal) / denom
		local hit = ray.Origin + ray.Direction * distance
		local localPos = cf:PointToObjectSpace(hit)
		local px = (localPos.X + size.X / 2) * (canvas.X / math.max(1e-6, size.X))
		local py = (size.Y / 2 - localPos.Y) * (canvas.Y / math.max(1e-6, size.Y))
		return Vector2.new(px, py), distance
	end
	-- detached panels sit closer to the camera, so test the nearest surface the cursor is over first
	local best, bestDistance = nil, math.huge
	for _, surface in ipairs(self._surfaces) do
		if surface.Part.Parent and surface.Enabled ~= false then
			local point, distance = project(surface.Part, surface.Canvas)
			if point and point.X >= 0 and point.Y >= 0 and point.X <= surface.Canvas.X and point.Y <= surface.Canvas.Y and distance < bestDistance then
				best, bestDistance = point, distance
			end
		end
	end
	if not best then
		best = project(self.SurfacePart, self.CanvasSize) or fallback
	end
	self._lastPointer = best
	return best
end

-- Procedural Discord mark (rounded "controller" silhouette) so no image asset is needed.
function Window:_MakeDiscordLogo(parent, size)
	local holder = Create("Frame", { Name = "DiscordLogo", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size), Parent = parent })
	local body = Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0.96, 0, 0.72, 0), Parent = holder,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0.45, 0) }) })
	for _, x in ipairs({ 0.36, 0.64 }) do
		Create("Frame", {
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(88, 101, 242), BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
			Position = UDim2.new(x, 0, 0.52, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0.16, 0, 0.26, 0), ZIndex = 2, Parent = body,
		}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
	end
	return holder
end

-- Copies a Discord invite to the clipboard when the environment allows it, otherwise shows it in a notification.
-- Returns true when the clipboard write succeeded.
function Window:CopyDiscordInvite(invite, callback)
	invite = tostring(invite or "")
	local link = invite
	if not link:find("://", 1, true) then
		link = "https://discord.gg/" .. link:gsub("^discord%.gg/", "")
	end
	local copied = false
	local ok = pcall(function()
		local clip = setclipboard
		if typeof(clip) == "function" then
			clip(link)
			copied = true
		end
	end)
	if copied then
		self:Notify({ Title = "Invite copied", Content = link, Type = "Success", Duration = 3 })
	else
		self:Notify({ Title = "Discord invite", Content = link, Type = "Info", Duration = 8 })
	end
	SafeCall(callback, link, copied)
	return copied
end

function Window:SetSidebarOpen(state)
	self.SidebarOpen = state and true or false
	if not self.Mobile then
		return
	end
	local y = self:_SidebarY()
	local goal = self.SidebarOpen and UDim2.new(0, 8, y.Scale, y.Offset) or UDim2.new(0, -self.SidebarWidth, y.Scale, y.Offset)
	self:_SetLayerBase(self.Sidebar, goal, 0.3)
end

function Window:SetDebug(enabled)
	if enabled and not self.DebugLabel then
		self.DebugLabel = Create("TextLabel", {
			Name = "Debug", BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 0.4, TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
			TextSize = 11, Font = Enum.Font.Code, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
			Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 1, -92), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(300, 80), ZIndex = 200, Parent = self.Gui,
		}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6) }), Create("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8), PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6) }) })
	end
	if self.DebugLabel then
		self.DebugLabel.Visible = enabled and true or false
	end
	self.Debug = enabled and true or false
end

function Window:ResetPosition()
	if self.Render3D then
		self._screenOffset = Vector2.new()
	else
		Tween(self.Root, { Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5) }, 0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
	end
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Input bindings
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function Window:_BindInput()
	-- the only three input connections the window needs; everything else rides on _OnInput
	self.Maid:Add(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
		self:_Dispatch("Began", input, processed)
	end))
	self.Maid:Add(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input, processed)
		self:_Dispatch("Changed", input, processed)
	end))
	self.Maid:Add(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input, processed)
		self:_Dispatch("Ended", input, processed)
	end))
	self.Maid:Add(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if IsClick(input) and self.IsOpen and self._hovering then
			self._pressing = true
		end
	end))
	self.Maid:Add(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if IsClick(input) then
			self._pressing = false
		end
	end))
	self.Maid:Add(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
		if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
			return
		end
		if not processed and input.KeyCode == self.Keybind and not self.Loader then
			self:Toggle()
			return
		end
		if processed or not self.IsOpen or UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
			return
		end
		self:_HandleNavKey(input.KeyCode)
	end))
end

-- Keyboard navigation: Up/Down move focus, Enter activates, Left/Right adjust, Escape closes menus/prompts.
function Window:_NavList()
	local list = {}
	local page = self.CurrentPage
	if not page then
		return list
	end
	for _, element in ipairs(self.Elements) do
		if element.Page == page and Alive(element.Frame) and element.Frame.Visible and element.Type ~= "Title" and element.Type ~= "Divider" and element.Type ~= "Text" then
			list[#list + 1] = element
		end
	end
	return list
end

function Window:_HandleNavKey(key)
	if not self.Effects.KeyboardNavigation then
		return
	end
	local list = self:_NavList()
	local current = self._navFocus
	local index = current and table.find(list, current) or 0
	if key == Enum.KeyCode.Down or key == Enum.KeyCode.Up then
		if #list == 0 then
			return
		end
		index = index + ((key == Enum.KeyCode.Down) and 1 or -1)
		if index < 1 then index = #list elseif index > #list then index = 1 end
		self:_SetNavFocus(list[index])
	elseif key == Enum.KeyCode.Return or key == Enum.KeyCode.Space then
		if current then
			self:_ActivateElement(current)
		end
	elseif key == Enum.KeyCode.Left or key == Enum.KeyCode.Right then
		if current then
			self:_AdjustElement(current, (key == Enum.KeyCode.Right) and 1 or -1)
		end
	elseif key == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
		if self.ActivePrompt then
			self.ActivePrompt:Close()
		elseif current and current.Close then
			pcall(current.Close, current)
		end
		self:_SetNavFocus(nil)
	elseif key == Enum.KeyCode.Slash and self.SearchBox then
		self.SearchBox:CaptureFocus()
	end
end

function Window:_SetNavFocus(element)
	if self._navFocus and self._navFocus ~= element then
		self._navFocus:SetFocused(false)
	end
	self._navFocus = element
	if element then
		element:SetFocused(true)
		local page = element.Page
		if page and page.Frame and page.Frame:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
			local y = element.Frame.AbsolutePosition.Y - page.Frame.AbsolutePosition.Y + page.Frame.CanvasPosition.Y
			local viewH = page.Frame.AbsoluteSize.Y
			local h = element.Frame.AbsoluteSize.Y
			if y < page.Frame.CanvasPosition.Y then
				Tween(page.Frame, { CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(0, y - 10)) }, 0.2)
			elseif y + h > page.Frame.CanvasPosition.Y + viewH then
				Tween(page.Frame, { CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, y + h - viewH + 10) }, 0.2)
			end
		end
	end
end

function Window:_ActivateElement(element)
	local kind = element.Type
	if kind == "Button" and element.Fire then
		element:Fire()
	elseif kind == "Toggle" and element.Toggle then
		element:Toggle()
	elseif (kind == "Dropdown" or kind == "ColorPicker" or kind == "Gradient") and element.Toggle then
		element:Toggle()
	elseif kind == "ColorPicker" and element.Open then
		element:Open()
	elseif (kind == "Input" or kind == "SearchBox") and element.Box then
		element.Box:CaptureFocus()
	elseif kind == "CodeBox" and element.Focus then
		element:Focus()
	elseif element.Activate then
		element:Activate()
	end
end

function Window:_AdjustElement(element, direction)
	local kind = element.Type
	if kind == "Slider" or kind == "Stepper" then
		local value = element:Get()
		local step = element.Step or 1
		element:Set(value + step * direction)
	elseif kind == "Selection" then
		if direction > 0 then element:Next() else element:Prev() end
	elseif kind == "Toggle" then
		element:Set(direction > 0)
	elseif kind == "Tabs" and element.Select then
		element:Select(direction)
	end
end

function Window:SetKeybind(keyCode)
	self.Keybind = keyCode or Enum.KeyCode.RightControl
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Open / close / minimize
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Acrylic glass (see ACRYLIC GLASS above)
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function Window:_AcrylicOn()
	return Aurora:Has("Acrylic") and self.Effects.Acrylic == true and GlassCheckQuality()
end

-- spec: { Surface = surface entry (SurfaceGui panels) or nil, Radius, Bias (nearer when panels overlap),
--         Loose = true for ScreenGui cards that update themselves, IsVisible = function, Tint = function(on) }
function Window:_AddGlass(frame, spec)
	spec = spec or {}
	spec.Frame = frame
	for index = #self._glassSpecs, 1, -1 do
		local old = self._glassSpecs[index]
		if not Alive(old.Frame) then
			GlassDestroy(old.Entry)
			table.remove(self._glassSpecs, index)
		end
	end
	table.insert(self._glassSpecs, spec)
	self:_SyncGlass(spec)
	return spec
end

function Window:_SyncGlass(spec)
	local on = self:_AcrylicOn() and Alive(spec.Frame) and true or false
	if on and not spec.Entry then
		spec.Entry = GlassNew(spec.Frame, spec)
		if spec.Loose then
			GlassAttachLoose(spec.Entry, spec.IsVisible)
		end
	elseif not on and spec.Entry then
		GlassDestroy(spec.Entry)
		spec.Entry = nil
	end
	if spec.Tint then
		pcall(spec.Tint, on)
	end
end

function Window:_StepGlass(forceHide)
	for _, spec in ipairs(self._glassSpecs) do
		local entry = spec.Entry
		if entry and not spec.Loose then
			if forceHide then
				GlassHide(entry)
			else
				local ok, shown = pcall(spec.IsVisible)
				GlassUpdate(entry, ok and shown)
			end
		end
	end
end

-- Re-applies the Acrylic effect after it, its strength or its tint changed.
function Window:_RefreshAcrylic()
	Glass.QualityAt = 0
	if self:_AcrylicOn() then
		LoadUIBlur()
		local dof = GlassDoF()
		if dof.Name == "UIBlur" and Glass.SharedPrev == nil then
			Glass.SharedPrev = dof.NearIntensity      -- remember what UIBlur had, to put it back later
		end
		dof.NearIntensity = math.clamp(tonumber(self.Effects.AcrylicStrength) or 1, 0, 1)
	end
	for _, spec in ipairs(self._glassSpecs) do
		self:_SyncGlass(spec)
	end
	if not self:_AcrylicOn() then
		GlassReleaseDoF()
	end
	self:_ApplyTransparency()
	self:_SetBlur(self.IsOpen)
	self:_SetCameraEffects(self.IsOpen)
end

-- "Dependencies.UIBlur", "Built-in glass", "Needs graphics quality 8+" or "Off"
function Window:GetAcrylicStatus()
	if not self.Effects.Acrylic then
		return "Off"
	end
	if not GlassQualityOK() then
		return "Needs graphics quality 8+"
	end
	return LoadUIBlur() and "Dependencies.UIBlur" or "Built-in glass"
end

function Window:SetAcrylic(enabled, strength, tint)
	if enabled ~= nil then
		self.Effects.Acrylic = enabled and true or false
	end
	if strength ~= nil then
		self.Effects.AcrylicStrength = strength
	end
	if tint ~= nil then
		self.Effects.AcrylicTint = tint
	end
	self:_RefreshAcrylic()
end

function Window:_SetBlur(enabled)
	-- Acrylic already blurs what is behind the panels, so the full-screen blur steps aside while it is on
	if enabled and self.Effects.Blur and not self:_AcrylicOn() then
		if not self.BlurEffect then
			self.BlurEffect = Create("BlurEffect", { Name = "AuroraBlur", Size = 0, Parent = Lighting })
			self.Maid:Add(self.BlurEffect)
		end
		Tween(self.BlurEffect, { Size = self.Effects.BlurSize or 8 }, 0.4)
	elseif self.BlurEffect then
		Tween(self.BlurEffect, { Size = 0 }, 0.3)
	end
end

-- Post effects and field of view that follow the window open state.
-- Effects this build has no code for stay off however they are asked for: by the window options, by a
-- preset from another edition, by a profile, or by hand. The asked-for value is not lost, it is carried
-- (see ApplyPreset), so the same look opened in Elite still turns them back on.
function Window:_ClampEffects()
	for key, feature in pairs(Aurora.EliteEffectKeys) do
		if not Aurora:Has(feature) and type(self.Effects[key]) == "boolean" then
			self.Effects[key] = false
		end
	end
end

function Window:_SetCameraEffects(open)
	local effects = self.Effects
	-- Roblox renders one depth-of-field at a time, and Acrylic needs it, so this effect waits while Acrylic is on
	if open and effects.DepthOfField and self.Render3D and not self:_AcrylicOn() then
		if not self.DepthOfFieldEffect then
			self.DepthOfFieldEffect = Create("DepthOfFieldEffect", {
				Name = "AuroraDepthOfField", FarIntensity = 0, NearIntensity = 0, InFocusRadius = 3,
				FocusDistance = self.SurfaceDistance, Parent = Lighting,
			})
			self.Maid:Add(self.DepthOfFieldEffect)
		end
		self.DepthOfFieldEffect.Enabled = true
		self.DepthOfFieldEffect.FocusDistance = self.SurfaceDistance
		Tween(self.DepthOfFieldEffect, { FarIntensity = effects.DepthOfFieldAmount or 0.35 }, 0.5)
	elseif self.DepthOfFieldEffect then
		local dof = self.DepthOfFieldEffect
		Tween(dof, { FarIntensity = 0 }, 0.3).Completed:Connect(function(state)
			if state == Enum.PlaybackState.Completed and Alive(dof) and self.DepthOfFieldEffect == dof then
				dof.Enabled = false
			end
		end)
	end
	if open and effects.WorldDim then
		if not self.WorldDimEffect then
			self.WorldDimEffect = Create("ColorCorrectionEffect", { Name = "AuroraWorldDim", Parent = Lighting })
			self.Maid:Add(self.WorldDimEffect)
		end
		Tween(self.WorldDimEffect, { Brightness = -0.06, Saturation = -0.3, Contrast = 0.04 }, 0.5)
	elseif self.WorldDimEffect then
		Tween(self.WorldDimEffect, { Brightness = 0, Saturation = 0, Contrast = 0 }, 0.3)
	end
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	if camera then
		if open and effects.CameraZoom and not self._zoomActive then
			self._zoomActive = true
			self._fovBase = camera.FieldOfView
			Tween(camera, { FieldOfView = math.max(20, self._fovBase - (effects.CameraZoomAmount or 5)) }, 0.6)
		elseif (not open or not effects.CameraZoom) and self._zoomActive then
			self._zoomActive = false
			Tween(camera, { FieldOfView = self._fovBase or 70 }, 0.4)
		end
	end
end

function Window:Open(pop)
	if self._wantSettings and not self.SettingsPage then
		self._wantSettings = false
		self:AddSettingsPage()
	end
	if self._wantPresets and not self.PresetsPage then
		self._wantPresets = false
		self:AddPresetsPage()
	end
	if self._wantLogic and not self.LogicPage then
		self._wantLogic = false
		self:AddLogicHubPage()
	end
	if self.IsOpen then
		return   -- already showing; running the intro again over a settled window would only restart the fade
	end
	self.IsOpen = true
	self.Root.Visible = true
	self:_StartLoop()
	self:_SetBlur(true)
	self:_SetCameraEffects(true)
	-- The fade has four states: "in" (opening), "shown", "out" (closing) and "hidden" (closed and put back to its
	-- resting values). Resting values are read again only from "hidden" (what the tree shows is the truth, plus
	-- anything changed while closed) or, at close, from "shown". Never from "in" or "out": the tree is mid-fade
	-- then, and reading it would make a moment of the fade the new resting look. Each fade takes a ticket, so a
	-- delayed step from an older open or close cannot act on a newer one.
	self._fadeTicket = (self._fadeTicket or 0) + 1
	local ticket = self._fadeTicket
	if self.Effects.OpenAnimation then
		local now = os.clock()
		-- OpenDuration stretches the whole fly-in; Effects.OpenSpin adds the turn
		local length = math.clamp(tonumber(self.Effects.OpenDuration) or 1, 0.25, 4)
		local settled = self._fadeState == nil or self._fadeState == "hidden"
		self._fadeState = "in"
		self._popValue.Value = 0.86
		Tween(self._popValue, { Value = 1 }, (pop and 1.1 or 0.9) * length, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		self._flyValue.Value = 1
		Tween(self._flyValue, { Value = 0 }, (pop and 1.35 or 1.1) * length, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		self._swayCF = nil
		-- the chrome assembles: sidebar, header and content settle into their depth layers one after another
		if not self.Minimized then
			local layout = self.Layout or Aurora.DefaultLayout
			self:_LayerIntro(self.Sidebar, UDim2.fromOffset((layout.SidebarSide == "Right" and not self.Mobile) and 24 or -24, 0), 0.85 * length, 0.08 * length)
			self:_LayerIntro(self.TopBar, UDim2.fromOffset(0, (layout.HeaderSide == "Bottom") and 16 or -16), 0.8 * length, 0.2 * length)
			self:_LayerIntro(self.Content, UDim2.fromOffset(0, 22), 0.85 * length, 0.32 * length)
		end
		FadeTree(self.Main, 1, 0, nil, settled, now + Timed(0.65 * length))
		FadeTree(self.Main, 0, 0.6 * length, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		task.delay(Timed(0.65 * length) + 0.02, function()
			if self._fadeTicket == ticket and self.IsOpen then
				self._fadeState = "shown"
			end
		end)
		self._shadowFading = true
		for _, layer in ipairs(self.ShadowLayers) do
			layer.Frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			Tween(layer.Frame, { BackgroundTransparency = layer.Alpha }, 1 * length)
		end
		task.delay(Timed(1 * length), function()
			self._shadowFading = false
		end)
	else
		self._popValue.Value = 1
		self._flyValue.Value = 0
		FadeTree(self.Main, 0, 0)   -- whatever an earlier animated close left mid-fade goes back to its resting values
		self._fadeState = "shown"
	end
	if self.Surface then
		self.Surface.Enabled = true
	end
	if not self._ready then
		self._ready = true
		if self.AutoSave then
			task.defer(function()
				self:LoadConfigFile(self.AutoSaveName, true)
			end)
		end
	end
	if self.CurrentPage then
		task.defer(function()
			self:_UpdateIndicator(false)
		end)
		if self.Effects.OpenAnimation then
			local current = self.CurrentPage
			-- the cards cascade in once the panel is most of the way home
			task.delay(Timed(0.3 * math.clamp(tonumber(self.Effects.OpenDuration) or 1, 0.25, 4)), function()
				if self.IsOpen and self.CurrentPage == current then
					self:_AnimateEntrance(current.List, 0)
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end

function Window:Close()
	if not self.IsOpen then
		return
	end
	self.IsOpen = false
	self:_HideTooltip()
	self:_SetBlur(false)
	self:_SetCameraEffects(false)
	self._fadeTicket = (self._fadeTicket or 0) + 1
	local ticket = self._fadeTicket
	if self.Effects.OpenAnimation then
		local now = os.clock()
		local length = math.clamp(tonumber(self.Effects.OpenDuration) or 1, 0.25, 4)
		local out = 0.5 * length
		-- resting values are read again only from a fully shown window (see Open); a tween still in flight on an
		-- object, a hover fading out for one, contributes the value it is heading to
		local settled = self._fadeState == "shown"
		self._fadeState = "out"
		Tween(self._popValue, { Value = 0.88 }, out, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
		-- the panel flies back out the way it came in, turning as it goes
		Tween(self._flyValue, { Value = 1 }, out * 1.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
		FadeTree(self.Main, 1, out * 0.9, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, settled, now + Timed(out * 1.1))
		self._shadowFading = true
		for _, layer in ipairs(self.ShadowLayers) do
			Tween(layer.Frame, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, out * 0.7)
		end
		task.delay(Timed(out * 1.1) + 0.02, function()
			if self._fadeTicket ~= ticket or self.IsOpen then
				return   -- reopened, or closed again, since: the newer fade owns the tree now
			end
			self.Root.Visible = false
			self:_StopLoop()
			self:_StepGlass(true)
			FadeTree(self.Main, 0, 0)   -- back to resting values while hidden, so the next open starts from the truth
			self._fadeState = "hidden"
			if self.Surface then
				self.Surface.Enabled = false
			end
		end)
	else
		self.Root.Visible = false
		self:_StopLoop()
		self:_StepGlass(true)
		FadeTree(self.Main, 0, 0)
		self._fadeState = "hidden"
		if self.Surface then
			self.Surface.Enabled = false
		end
	end
end

function Window:Toggle(state)
	if state == nil then
		state = not self.IsOpen
	end
	if state then
