		self:Open()
	else
		self:Close()
	end
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- LAYOUT: where the sidebar, the main bar and the title sit, and how big they are.
-- Nothing here rebuilds anything: the same frames are moved, so it is safe to change while the window is open.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
Aurora.DefaultLayout = {
	SidebarWidth = 176,        -- asked for; what you get is capped at Aurora.SidebarWidthShare of the window width
	SidebarHeight = 100,       -- percent of the window height (a shorter sidebar floats where SidebarAlign says)
	SidebarAlign = "Top",      -- "Top" | "Middle" | "Bottom", only matters when SidebarHeight < 100
	SidebarSide = "Left",      -- "Left" | "Right"
	HeaderSide = "Top",        -- the main bar (page title, window buttons): "Top" | "Bottom"
	HeaderHeight = 54,
	Brand = "Sidebar",         -- title and logo: "Sidebar" | "Header" | "Hidden"
	LogoSize = 30,
	TitleSize = 14,
	Subtitle = true,
	Credit = true,             -- the Made By chip under the title (sidebar only)
	BrandOffsetX = 0,          -- nudge the title and logo from where the placement puts them, in pixels
	BrandOffsetY = 0,
}
Aurora.LayoutLimits = {
	SidebarWidth = { 120, 320 }, SidebarHeight = { 35, 100 }, HeaderHeight = { 40, 90 }, LogoSize = { 18, 48 }, TitleSize = { 10, 22 },
	BrandOffsetX = { -60, 60 }, BrandOffsetY = { -60, 60 },
}
-- Named arrangements; each is the default layout with these changes. Settings > Layout > Arrangement lists them.
Aurora.LayoutPresets = {
	["Default"] = {},
	["Compact"] = { SidebarWidth = 140, HeaderHeight = 44, LogoSize = 24, TitleSize = 12, Subtitle = false, Credit = false },
	["Wide sidebar"] = { SidebarWidth = 240, LogoSize = 34, TitleSize = 16 },
	["Bottom bar"] = { HeaderSide = "Bottom" },
	["Right hand"] = { SidebarSide = "Right" },
	["Floating sidebar"] = { SidebarHeight = 70, SidebarAlign = "Middle", Brand = "Header", Credit = false },
	["Title in the bar"] = { Brand = "Header", HeaderHeight = 60, LogoSize = 26 },
}
Aurora.LayoutPresetOrder = { "Default", "Compact", "Wide sidebar", "Bottom bar", "Right hand", "Floating sidebar", "Title in the bar" }
Aurora.SidebarWidthShare = 0.45   -- the sidebar never takes more than this share of the window, however wide it was asked to be

local function ValidLayout(layout)
	layout = layout or {}
	local out = {}
	for key, default in pairs(Aurora.DefaultLayout) do
		local value = layout[key]
		local limits = Aurora.LayoutLimits[key]
		if limits then
			value = tonumber(value)
			out[key] = value and math.clamp(math.floor(value + 0.5), limits[1], limits[2]) or default
		elseif type(default) == "boolean" then
			out[key] = (value == nil) and default or (value == true)
		else
			out[key] = (type(value) == "string") and value or default
		end
	end
	if out.SidebarSide ~= "Right" then out.SidebarSide = "Left" end
	if out.SidebarAlign ~= "Middle" and out.SidebarAlign ~= "Bottom" then out.SidebarAlign = "Top" end
	if out.HeaderSide ~= "Bottom" then out.HeaderSide = "Top" end
	if out.Brand ~= "Header" and out.Brand ~= "Hidden" then out.Brand = "Sidebar" end
	return out
end

function Window:_LogoScale()
	return ((self.Layout and self.Layout.LogoSize) or 30) / 30
end

-- The sidebar width actually in use: what the layout asks for, capped by the window so the content keeps its room.
function Window:_SidebarWidth()
	local layout = self.Layout or Aurora.DefaultLayout
	local wanted = layout.SidebarWidth or 176
	local windowWidth = (self.Size and self.Size.X.Offset) or 640
	local cap = math.max(Aurora.LayoutLimits.SidebarWidth[1], math.floor(windowWidth * Aurora.SidebarWidthShare))
	return math.min(wanted, cap)
end

-- Where the top of the sidebar sits (scale, offset) for the layout's height and alignment; full height gives 0, 8.
function Window:_SidebarY()
	local layout = self.Layout or Aurora.DefaultLayout
	local share = (layout.SidebarHeight or 100) / 100
	local free = 1 - share
	local align = layout.SidebarAlign
	local scale = (align == "Bottom") and free or ((align == "Middle") and free / 2 or 0)
	return UDim.new(scale, 8)
end

-- How wide the title and subtitle are at the given sizes, for the brand in the main bar.
function Window:_BrandTextWidth(titleSize, subSize, withSubtitle)
	local width = 0
	local ok = pcall(function()
		width = TextService:GetTextSize(self.TitleLabel.Text, titleSize, self.TitleLabel.Font, Vector2.new(2000, 100)).X
		if withSubtitle then
			-- the subtitle may run a little past the title; a long one truncates rather than widening the whole brand
			local sub = TextService:GetTextSize(self.SubtitleLabel.Text, subSize, self.SubtitleLabel.Font, Vector2.new(2000, 100)).X
			width = math.max(width, math.min(sub, width + 40))
		end
	end)
	if not ok or width <= 0 then
		width = 150   -- a custom FontFace cannot be measured this way; fall back to the old fixed slot
	end
	return math.ceil(width) + 6
end

-- The window buttons sit at the window's outer corner: right end of the main bar normally, left end when the
-- sidebar is on the right (the main bar's right end is then next to the sidebar, not at the edge).
function Window:_PlaceWindowButtons(mirrored)
	local buttons = self.WindowButtons
	if not buttons then
		return
	end
	self._buttonsMirrored = mirrored and true or false
	local close, minimize = buttons.Close, buttons.Minimize
	if mirrored then
		close.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
		close.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0.5, 0)
		minimize.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
		minimize.Position = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0.5, 0)
		if self.PageCount then
			self.PageCount.Position = UDim2.new(1, -22, 0.5, 0)
		end
	else
		close.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
		close.Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0.5, 0)
		minimize.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
		minimize.Position = UDim2.new(1, -50, 0.5, 0)
		if self.PageCount then
			self.PageCount.Position = UDim2.new(1, -96, 0.5, 0)
		end
	end
end

-- The brand block (logo, title, subtitle, credit chip) and everything in the sidebar that sits under it.
function Window:_ApplyBrandLayout()
	local brand, mark, text = self.Brand, self.BrandMark, self.BrandText
	if not brand or not mark or not text or not self.Sidebar or not self.TopBar or not self.PageList then
		return
	end
	local layout = self.Layout
	local mode = layout.Brand
	if self.Mobile and mode == "Header" then
		mode = "Sidebar"   -- on phones the main bar holds the menu button
	end
	local size, titleSize = layout.LogoSize, layout.TitleSize
	local subSize = math.max(9, titleSize - 3)
	local showSubtitle = layout.Subtitle ~= false and self.SubtitleLabel.Text ~= ""
	local textHeight = (titleSize + 4) + (showSubtitle and (subSize + 4) or 0)
	self.TitleLabel.TextSize = titleSize
	self.TitleLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, titleSize + 4)
	self.SubtitleLabel.TextSize = subSize
	self.SubtitleLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, subSize + 4)
	self.SubtitleLabel.Visible = showSubtitle
	mark.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size)
	local logo = mark:FindFirstChild("Logo")
	local pop = logo and logo:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale")
	if pop then
		pop.Scale = self:_LogoScale()
	end
	local block = math.max(size, textHeight)
	-- with the window buttons at the left end of the main bar, everything in it starts after them
	local leftReserve = self._buttonsMirrored and 84 or 0
	local rightReserve = self._buttonsMirrored and 30 or 98
	local titleX = self.Mobile and 58 or (22 + leftReserve)
	local ox, oy = layout.BrandOffsetX or 0, layout.BrandOffsetY or 0
	local brandHeight = 6   -- what the brand takes off the top of the sidebar
	if mode == "Sidebar" then
		brand.Visible = true
		brand.Parent = self.Sidebar
		mark.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14 + ox, 0, 11 + oy)
		text.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14 + ox + size + 10, 0, 11 + oy + (block - textHeight) / 2)
		text.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(14 + ox + size + 10) - 8, 0, textHeight)
		local showCredit = layout.Credit ~= false
		if self.CreditChip then
			self.CreditChip.Visible = showCredit
			self.CreditChip.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14 + ox, 0, 11 + oy + block + 9)
		end
		brandHeight = (showCredit and (11 + block + 9 + 19 + 8) or (11 + block + 11)) + math.max(0, oy)
		brand.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, brandHeight)
		brand.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
		self:_SetLayerBase(brand, brand.Position, 0)
	elseif mode == "Header" then
		brand.Visible = true
		brand.Parent = self.TopBar
		-- the text slot fits the title, but leaves the page title at least 130 px and the buttons their corner
		local textWidth = self:_BrandTextWidth(titleSize, subSize, showSubtitle)
		local barWidth = (self.Size and self.Size.X.Offset or 640) - (self.Mobile and 0 or self:_SidebarWidth())
		local room = barWidth - (8 + leftReserve + 6 + size + 10 + 8 + 6) - 130 - rightReserve
		textWidth = math.clamp(textWidth, 40, math.max(40, room))
		local width = 6 + size + 10 + textWidth + 8
		brand.Size = UDim2.new(0, width, 1, 0)
		mark.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0.5, -size / 2)
		text.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6 + size + 10, 0.5, -textHeight / 2)
		text.Size = UDim2.new(0, textWidth, 0, textHeight)
		if self.CreditChip then
			self.CreditChip.Visible = false
		end
		brand.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8 + leftReserve + ox, 0, oy)
		self:_SetLayerBase(brand, brand.Position, 0)
		titleX = 8 + leftReserve + math.max(0, ox) + width + 6
	else
		brand.Visible = false
	end
	self._pageTitleX = titleX
	if self.PageTitle then
		self.PageTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, titleX, 0, 0)
		self.PageTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(titleX + rightReserve), 1, 0)
	end
	-- under the brand: the search box, then the page list
	local searchY = brandHeight + 2
	local listTop = searchY
	if self.SearchHolder then
		self.SearchHolder.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, searchY)
		listTop = searchY + 30 + 10
	end
	local reserved = self._sidebarBottomReserved or 34
	self.PageList.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -listTop - reserved)
	self.PageList.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, listTop)
	self:_SetLayerBase(self.PageList, self.PageList.Position, 0)
end

-- Sidebar, main bar and content. The frames are parallax layers, so they move through _SetLayerBase (the step
-- writes their position every frame from that base); with no animation the position is also set right away.
function Window:_ApplyLayout(animate)
	if not self.Main or not self.Sidebar or not self.TopBar or not self.Content then
		return
	end
	local layout = self.Layout
	local width, header = self:_SidebarWidth(), layout.HeaderHeight
	self.SidebarWidth, self.HeaderHeight = width, header
	local right = layout.SidebarSide == "Right" and not self.Mobile
	local bottom = layout.HeaderSide == "Bottom"
	local time = animate and 0.35 or 0
	local function place(obj, goal)
		self:_SetLayerBase(obj, goal, time)
		if time <= 0 then
			obj.Position = goal
		end
	end
	local y = self:_SidebarY()
	self.Sidebar.Size = UDim2.new(0, width - 8, (layout.SidebarHeight or 100) / 100, -16)
	if self.Mobile then
		self:SetSidebarOpen(self.SidebarOpen)   -- the drawer's closed position depends on the width
	else
		place(self.Sidebar, right and UDim2.new(1, -width, y.Scale, y.Offset) or UDim2.new(0, 8, y.Scale, y.Offset))
	end
	-- a shorter sidebar sits in a faint full-height slot, so the space it leaves reads as intended
	if self.SidebarTrack then
		local short = (layout.SidebarHeight or 100) < 100 and not self.Mobile
		self.SidebarTrack.Visible = short
		self.SidebarTrack.Size = UDim2.new(0, width - 8, 1, -16)
		self.SidebarTrack.Position = right and UDim2.new(1, -width, 0, 8) or UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8)
	end
	self:_PlaceWindowButtons(right)
	-- the Settings slider says when the window is holding the sidebar back
	local note = self._sidebarWidthSlider
	if note and note.Frame and Alive(note.Frame) and type(note.SetDescription) == "function" then
		if width < layout.SidebarWidth then
			note:SetDescription("Showing " .. width .. " px: " .. layout.SidebarWidth .. " was asked for, but the sidebar keeps to 45% of this window")
		else
			note:SetDescription("Never more than 45% of the window, so the pages keep their room")
		end
	end
	local mainX = (self.Mobile or right) and 0 or width
	local mainW = self.Mobile and 0 or -width
	if not self.Minimized then
		self.TopBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, mainW, 0, header)
		place(self.TopBar, UDim2.new(0, mainX, bottom and 1 or 0, bottom and -header or 0))
		self.Content.Size = UDim2.new(1, mainW, 1, -header)
		place(self.Content, UDim2.new(0, mainX, 0, bottom and 0 or header))
	end
	if self.TopDivider then
		self.TopDivider.Position = bottom and UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 0) or UDim2.new(0, 22, 1, -1)
	end
	self:_ApplyBrandLayout()
end

-- Window:SetLayout({ SidebarSide = "Right", SidebarWidth = 200, ... }, animate) -> the layout now in force
function Window:SetLayout(changes, animate)
	if type(changes) == "table" then
		self.Layout = ValidLayout(Merge(self.Layout or Aurora.DefaultLayout, changes))
	end
	self:_ApplyLayout(animate ~= false)
	return self.Layout
end

function Window:GetLayout()
	return DeepCopy(self.Layout or Aurora.DefaultLayout)
end

function Window:ResetLayout(animate)
	self.Layout = ValidLayout(Aurora.DefaultLayout)
	self:_ApplyLayout(animate ~= false)
	return self.Layout
end

-- Window:ApplyLayoutPreset("Floating sidebar") -> the layout now in force, or nil for an unknown name
function Window:ApplyLayoutPreset(name, animate)
	local preset = Aurora.LayoutPresets[tostring(name)]
	if not preset then
		return nil
	end
	self.Layout = ValidLayout(Merge(Aurora.DefaultLayout, preset))
	self:_ApplyLayout(animate ~= false)
	return self.Layout
end

-- The preset the current layout equals, or nil when it is a custom arrangement.
function Window:MatchLayoutPreset()
	local current = self.Layout or Aurora.DefaultLayout
	for _, name in ipairs(Aurora.LayoutPresetOrder) do
		local candidate = ValidLayout(Merge(Aurora.DefaultLayout, Aurora.LayoutPresets[name]))
		local same = true
		for key, value in pairs(candidate) do
			if current[key] ~= value then
				same = false
				break
			end
		end
		if same then
			return name
		end
	end
	return nil
end

function Window:Minimize(state)
	if state == nil then
		state = not self.Minimized
	end
	self.Minimized = state
	local fullSize = self.Size
	local shift = (self.Root.AbsoluteSize.Y / math.max(0.01, self._scaleObj.Scale) - 50) / 2
	local header = self.HeaderHeight or 54
	if state then
		self.Sidebar.Visible = false
		self.Content.Visible = false
		self.TopBar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
		self.TopBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, header)
		for _, layer in ipairs(self._parallaxLayers) do
			if layer.Object == self.TopBar then
				layer.Base = self.TopBar.Position
			end
		end
		self.PageTitle.Text = self.Title
		self._minimizeShift = (fullSize.Y.Offset - header) / 2
		Tween(self.Root, {
			Size = UDim2.new(fullSize.X.Scale, fullSize.X.Offset, 0, header),
			Position = self.Root.Position - UDim2.fromOffset(0, self._minimizeShift),
		}, 0.35)
	else
		Tween(self.Root, {
			Size = fullSize,
			Position = self.Root.Position + UDim2.fromOffset(0, self._minimizeShift or shift),
		}, 0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		task.delay(Timed(0.12), function()
			self.Sidebar.Visible = true
			self.Content.Visible = true
			if self.CurrentPage then
				self:_AnimateEntrance(self.CurrentPage.List, 0.02)
			end
			self:_ApplyLayout(false)
			if self.CurrentPage then
				self.PageTitle.Text = self.CurrentPage.Name
			end
		end)
	end
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Pages
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Options: Name, Icon (built-in icon name or image id), IconColor (false = keep image colours, Color3, or theme token),
--          Image (faint picture behind the button), ImageTransparency, Badge, Tooltip, Visible, LayoutOrder
function Window:AddPage(options)
	if type(options) ~= "table" then
		options = { Name = tostring(options or "Page") }
	end
	local theme = self.Theme
	local win = self
	local page = setmetatable({
		Window = self, Name = options.Name or "Page", Icon = options.Icon, IconColor = options.IconColor, CustomIcon = options.CustomIcon, Elements = {}, Sections = {},
		Maid = Maid.new(), IsSettings = options._settings == true,
	}, Page)
	page.Page = page

	local frame = Create("ScrollingFrame", {
		Name = page.Name, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ScrollBarThickness = 3,
		ScrollBarImageColor3 = theme.Accent, ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y, Visible = false,
		ZIndex = 2, Parent = self.Content,
	})
	self:_Bind(frame, "ScrollBarImageColor3", "Accent")
	Create("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 14), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 16), PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 8), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 18), Parent = frame })
	Create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 6), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = frame })
	Create("UIScale", { Name = "PageScale", Scale = 1, Parent = frame })
	page.Frame = frame
	page.List = frame

	local button = Create("TextButton", {
		Name = page.Name, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = theme.Accent, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32), ClipsDescendants = true,
		LayoutOrder = options.LayoutOrder or (page.IsSettings and 100000 or #self.Pages + 1), Parent = self.PageList,
	})
	self:_Corner(button, 8)
	self:_Bind(button, "BackgroundColor3", "Accent")
	local bar = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Bar", BackgroundColor3 = theme.Accent, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 0), ZIndex = 3, Parent = button,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
	self:_Bind(bar, "BackgroundColor3", "Accent")
	page.Bar = bar
	page.Button = button
	page._textX = 12
	local label = self:_Label({
		Name = "Label", Text = page.Name, TextSize = 13, Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, 0), ZIndex = 2, Parent = button,
	}, "Medium", false)
	label.TextColor3 = self:_T("SubText")
	page.Label = label

	local function layoutLabel()
		local badgeWidth = page.BadgeFrame and (page.BadgeFrame.Size.X.Offset + 6) or 0
		label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -page._textX - 8 - badgeWidth, 1, 0)
	end

	function page:_TintIcon(active, time)
		if self.IconColor == false then
			return
		end
		local target
		if typeof(self.IconColor) == "Color3" or type(self.IconColor) == "string" then
			target = self.IconColor
		else
			target = active and "Accent" or "SubText"
		end
		if self.IconLabel then
			Tween(self.IconLabel, { ImageColor3 = (typeof(target) == "Color3") and target or win:_T(target) }, time or 0)
		elseif self.IconHolder then
			win:_IconColor(self.IconHolder, target, time)
		end
	end

	-- page:SetIcon("home") | page:SetIcon("rbxassetid://1") | page:SetIcon("Custom", nil, { Image, RectSize, RectOffset, Color })
	function page:SetIcon(icon, iconColor, customIcon)
		if iconColor ~= nil then
			self.IconColor = iconColor
		end
		if customIcon ~= nil then
			self.CustomIcon = customIcon
		end
		self.Icon = icon
		icon = win:_ResolveIcon(icon, self.CustomIcon)
		if type(icon) == "table" and self.IconColor == false and icon.Color == nil then
			icon = setmetatable({ Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1) }, { __index = icon })
		end
		if self.IconLabel then self.IconLabel:Destroy() self.IconLabel = nil end
		if self.IconHolder then self.IconHolder:Destroy() self.IconHolder = nil end
		if icon and icon ~= "" then
			if tostring(icon):find("rbxasset", 1, true) then
				self.IconLabel = Create("ImageLabel", { Name = "Icon", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = icon, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, -8), ZIndex = 2, Parent = button })
			else
				self.IconHolder = win:_Icon(button, icon, 16, "SubText", { Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, -8), ZIndex = 2 })
			end
			self._textX = 34
			local pop = Create("UIScale", { Scale = 0.6, Parent = self.IconLabel or self.IconHolder })
			Tween(pop, { Scale = 1 }, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		else
			self._textX = 12
		end
		label.Position = UDim2.new(0, self._textX, 0, 0)
		layoutLabel()
		self:_TintIcon(win.CurrentPage == self, 0)
	end

	function page:SetImage(image, transparency)
		if self.ImageLabel then
			self.ImageLabel:Destroy()
			self.ImageLabel = nil
		end
		if not image or image == "" then
			return
		end
		self.ImageLabel = Create("ImageLabel", {
			Name = "Picture", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = image, ImageTransparency = 1, ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop,
			Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 1, Parent = button,
		}, {
			Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8) }),
			Create("UIGradient", { Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumKey(0, 0.6), NumKey(1, 0) }) }),
		})
		Tween(self.ImageLabel, { ImageTransparency = transparency or 0.55 }, 0.3)
	end

	function page:SetBadge(value)
		self.Badge = value
		local text = (value ~= nil and value ~= false) and tostring(value) or ""
		if text == "" then
			local old = self.BadgeFrame
			self.BadgeFrame = nil
			if old then
				local oldScale = old:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale")
				if oldScale then Tween(oldScale, { Scale = 0 }, 0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In) end
				task.delay(Timed(0.16), function() old:Destroy() end)
			end
			layoutLabel()
			return
		end
		local width = math.max(18, TextService:GetTextSize(text, 10, win.Fonts.Bold, Vector2.new(200, 20)).X + 12)
		if not self.BadgeFrame then
			local badge = Create("Frame", {
				Name = "Badge", BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("Accent"), BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
				Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width, 18), ZIndex = 4, Parent = button,
			}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
			win:_Bind(badge, "BackgroundColor3", "Accent")
			win:_Label({ Name = "Text", Text = text, TextSize = 10, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 5, Parent = badge }, "Bold", "AccentText")
			local pop = Create("UIScale", { Scale = 0, Parent = badge })
			Tween(pop, { Scale = 1 }, 0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
			self.BadgeFrame = badge
		else
			self.BadgeFrame.Text.Text = text
			Tween(self.BadgeFrame, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width, 18) }, 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
			local pop = self.BadgeFrame:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale")
			if pop then
				pop.Scale = 1.25
				Tween(pop, { Scale = 1 }, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
			end
		end
		self.BadgeFrame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width, 18)
		layoutLabel()
	end

	function page:SetName(text)
		self.Name = tostring(text)
		label.Text = self.Name
		frame.Name = self.Name
		if win.CurrentPage == self and win.PageTitle then
			win.PageTitle.Text = self.Name
		end
	end

	function page:SetVisible(visible)
		self.Hidden = not visible
		if visible then
			button.Visible = true
			button.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
			Tween(button, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32) }, 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		else
			Tween(button, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0) }, 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
			task.delay(Timed(0.2), function()
				if self.Hidden then button.Visible = false end
			end)
			if win.CurrentPage == self then
				for _, other in ipairs(win.Pages) do
					if other ~= self and not other.Hidden then
						win:SelectPage(other)
						break
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if options.Icon then
		page:SetIcon(options.Icon)
	end
	if options.Image then
		page:SetImage(options.Image, options.ImageTransparency)
	end
	if options.Badge ~= nil then
		page:SetBadge(options.Badge)
	end
	if options.Tooltip then
		self:_Tooltip(button, options.Tooltip)
	end
	self:_OnTheme(function()
		label.TextColor3 = self:_T(self.CurrentPage == page and "Text" or "SubText")
		page:_TintIcon(self.CurrentPage == page, 0)
	end)

	button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		if self.CurrentPage ~= page and self.Effects.Hover then
			Tween(button, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.95 }, 0.15)
			Tween(label, { Position = UDim2.new(0, page._textX + 3, 0, 0), TextColor3 = self:_T("Text") }, 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		end
	end)
	button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		if self.CurrentPage ~= page then
			Tween(button, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, 0.2)
			Tween(label, { Position = UDim2.new(0, page._textX, 0, 0), TextColor3 = self:_T("SubText") }, 0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		end
	end)
	button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		self:_Ripple(button)
		self:_Sound("Click")
		self:SelectPage(page)
	end)

	self.Pages[#self.Pages + 1] = page
	if options.Visible == false then
		page.Hidden = true
		button.Visible = false
	end
	if not self.CurrentPage or (self.CurrentPage.IsSettings and not page.IsSettings) then
		if not page.Hidden then
			self:SelectPage(page, true)
		end
	end
	return page
end

function Window:_UpdateIndicator(animate)
	-- each sidebar button owns its own accent bar; nothing to reposition
end

function Window:SelectPage(page, instant)
	if type(page) == "string" then
		for _, candidate in ipairs(self.Pages) do
			if candidate.Name == page then
				page = candidate
				break
			end
		end
	end
	if type(page) ~= "table" or not page.Frame then
		return
	end
	local previous = self.CurrentPage
	self.CurrentPage = page
	if page == self.LogicPage and self._refreshLogicList then
		-- the list is only ever as old as the last time the page came to the front
		self._refreshLogicList()
	end
	if self.SearchResults and self.SearchResults.Visible then
		self.SearchResults.Visible = false
	end

	for _, candidate in ipairs(self.Pages) do
		local active = candidate == page
		Tween(candidate.Button, { BackgroundTransparency = active and 0.88 or 1 }, 0.25)
		Tween(candidate.Label, { TextColor3 = self:_T(active and "Text" or "SubText"), Position = UDim2.new(0, candidate._textX or 12, 0, 0) }, 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		if candidate.Bar then
			Tween(candidate.Bar, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, active and 16 or 0) }, instant and 0 or 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		end
		if candidate._TintIcon then
			candidate:_TintIcon(active, 0.25)
		end
		if active and not instant and previous ~= page then
			local iconObject = candidate.IconLabel or candidate.IconHolder
			local pop = iconObject and iconObject:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale")
			if pop then
				pop.Scale = 0.7
				Tween(pop, { Scale = 1 }, 0.45, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
			end
		end
	end
	if self.Mobile then
		self:SetSidebarOpen(false)
	end
	self:_SetNavFocus(nil)
	if self.PageTitle then
		self.PageTitle.Text = page.Name
		self.PageCount.Text = (#page.Elements > 1) and (#page.Elements .. " items") or ""
		if self.Effects.PageTransitions and not instant then
			self.PageTitle.TextTransparency = 1
			local titleX = self._pageTitleX or 22
			self.PageTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, titleX + 8, 0, 0)
			Tween(self.PageTitle, { TextTransparency = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, titleX, 0, 0) }, 0.35)
			local count = self.PageCount
			count.TextTransparency = 1
			task.delay(Timed(0.12), function()
				if Alive(count) then
					Tween(count, { TextTransparency = 0 }, 0.3)
				end
			end)
		end
	end

	local animate = self.Effects.PageTransitions and not instant and previous ~= nil and previous ~= page
	if not animate then
		if previous and previous ~= page then
			previous.Frame.Visible = false
			previous.Frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
		end
		page.Frame.Visible = true
		page.Frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
		return
	end

	local direction = ((table.find(self.Pages, page) or 0) >= (table.find(self.Pages, previous) or 0)) and 1 or -1
	self._pageTicket = (self._pageTicket or 0) + 1
	local ticket = self._pageTicket

	-- the panel swings a little around its vertical axis (3D mode)
	if self.Effects.PageSwing then
		Tween(self._swingValue, { Value = direction }, 0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		task.delay(Timed(0.12), function()
			if ticket == self._pageTicket then
				Tween(self._swingValue, { Value = 0 }, 0.6, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
			end
		end)
	end

	-- out: the old page slides away while the cover fades up over it
	local oldFrame = previous.Frame
	Tween(oldFrame, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, -12 * direction) }, 0.13, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
	self.PageCover.Visible = true
	self.PageCover.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	Tween(self.PageCover, { BackgroundTransparency = 0 }, 0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)

	task.delay(Timed(0.13), function()
		if ticket ~= self._pageTicket then
			if self.CurrentPage ~= previous then
				oldFrame.Visible = false
				oldFrame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
			end
			return
		end
		oldFrame.Visible = false
		oldFrame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
		-- in: the new page slides from the other side, cover fades away, cards cascade
		page.Frame.Visible = true
		page.Frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 14 * direction)
		Tween(page.Frame, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0) }, 0.38, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		local pageScale = page.Frame:FindFirstChild("PageScale")
		if pageScale then
			pageScale.Scale = 0.98
			Tween(pageScale, { Scale = 1 }, 0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		end
		Tween(self.PageCover, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, 0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		task.delay(Timed(0.3), function()
			if ticket == self._pageTicket then
				self.PageCover.Visible = false
			end
		end)
		self:_AnimateEntrance(page.List, 0.02)
	end)
end

function Window:GetPage(name)
	for _, page in ipairs(self.Pages) do
		if page.Name == name then
			return page
		end
	end
	return nil
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Search (pages + elements)
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function Window:_EnsureSearchResults()
	if self.SearchResults then
		return self.SearchResults
	end
	local theme = self.Theme
	local frame = Create("ScrollingFrame", {
		Name = "SearchResults", BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ScrollBarThickness = 3,
		ScrollBarImageColor3 = theme.Accent, Visible = false, ZIndex = 5, Parent = self.Content,
	})
	self:_Bind(frame, "ScrollBarImageColor3", "Accent")
	Create("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 16), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 16), PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 14), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 16), Parent = frame })
	Create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 6), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = frame })
	self.SearchResults = frame
	return frame
end

-- Scores a match: a direct hit beats letters that merely appear in order, and earlier hits beat later ones.
local function MatchScore(haystack, query)
	if query == "" or haystack == "" then
		return nil
	end
	local found = haystack:find(query, 1, true)
	if found then
		return 1000 - found - math.max(0, #haystack - #query) * 0.05
	end
	local index, gaps, last = 1, 0, nil
	for position = 1, #query do
		local letter = query:sub(position, position)
		if letter ~= " " then
			local at = haystack:find(letter, index, true)
			if not at then
				return nil
			end
			if last and at > last + 1 then
				gaps = gaps + 1
			end
			last, index = at, at + 1
		end
	end
	return 420 - gaps * 8
end

local function JoinOptions(list)
	local parts = {}
	for _, value in ipairs(list or {}) do
		parts[#parts + 1] = tostring(value)
	end
	return table.concat(parts, " ")
end
Aurora._JoinOptions = JoinOptions

-- Moves the highlight through the search results (Down / Up while the search box is focused).
function Window:_MoveSearchFocus(direction)
	local rows = self._searchRows
	if not rows or #rows == 0 then
		return
	end
	local index = (self._searchIndex or 0) + direction
	if index < 1 then index = #rows end
	if index > #rows then index = 1 end
	self._searchIndex = index
	for position, entry in ipairs(rows) do
		local active = position == index
		if Alive(entry.Row) then
			Tween(entry.Stroke, { Color = self:_T(active and "Accent" or "Stroke"), Transparency = active and 0 or 0.2 }, 0.15)
			if active and self.SearchResults then
				local y = entry.Row.AbsolutePosition.Y - self.SearchResults.AbsolutePosition.Y + self.SearchResults.CanvasPosition.Y
				Tween(self.SearchResults, { CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(0, y - 20)) }, 0.2)
			end
		end
	end
end

function Window:_ActivateSearchFocus()
	local rows = self._searchRows
	local entry = rows and rows[self._searchIndex or 0]
	if entry then
		entry.Activate()
	end
end

function Window:SearchPages(query)
	query = tostring(query or ""):lower():gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
	local matches = { Pages = {}, Elements = {} }

	for _, page in ipairs(self.Pages) do
		local score = (query == "") and 0 or MatchScore(page.Name:lower(), query)
		local visible = score ~= nil and page.Hidden ~= true
		page.Button.Visible = visible
		if visible and query ~= "" then
			matches.Pages[#matches.Pages + 1] = { Page = page, Score = score }
		end
	end
	task.defer(function()
		self:_UpdateIndicator(false)
	end)

	local results = self:_EnsureSearchResults()
	for _, child in ipairs(results:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("GuiObject") then
			child:Destroy()
		end
	end

	if query == "" then
		results.Visible = false
		if self.CurrentPage then
			self.CurrentPage.Frame.Visible = true
		end
		return matches
	end

	for _, element in ipairs(self.Elements) do
		if Alive(element.Frame) then
			-- the title counts most, then the description, then the options inside dropdowns and selections
			local title = tostring(element.Title or ""):lower()
			local score = MatchScore(title, query)
			if not score then
				local rest = (tostring(element.Description or "") .. " " .. tostring(element.SearchExtra or "") .. " " .. tostring(element.Type or "")):lower()
				local restScore = MatchScore(rest, query)
				score = restScore and (restScore - 120) or nil
			end
			if score then
				matches.Elements[#matches.Elements + 1] = { Element = element, Score = score }
			end
		end
	end
	-- section headings, so "combat" finds the section as well as the rows inside it
	matches.Sections = {}
	for _, page in ipairs(self.Pages) do
		for _, section in ipairs(page.Sections or {}) do
			local score = MatchScore(tostring(section.Title or ""):lower(), query)
			if score and Alive(section.Frame) then
				matches.Sections[#matches.Sections + 1] = { Section = section, Page = page, Score = score }
			end
		end
	end
	local function byScore(a, b) return a.Score > b.Score end
	table.sort(matches.Pages, byScore)
	table.sort(matches.Sections, byScore)
	table.sort(matches.Elements, byScore)

	if self.CurrentPage then
		self.CurrentPage.Frame.Visible = false
	end
	results.Visible = true

	local order = 0
	self._searchRows = {}
	self._searchIndex = 0
	local function addRow(kind, title, subtitle, onClick)
		order = order + 1
		local row = Create("TextButton", {
			Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = self:_T("Element"), BorderSizePixel = 0,
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44), LayoutOrder = order, ClipsDescendants = true, Parent = results,
		})
		self:_Corner(row, 8)
		local stroke = Create("UIStroke", { Color = self:_T("Stroke"), Transparency = 0.2, Parent = row })
		self:_Hover(row, stroke)
		local badge = Create("Frame", {
			BackgroundColor3 = self:_T("Accent"), BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, -10),
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(52, 20), Parent = row,
		})
		self:_Corner(badge, 6)
		Create("TextLabel", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = kind, Font = self.Fonts.Bold, TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = self:_T("AccentText"),
			Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = badge,
		})
		Create("TextLabel", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = title, Font = self.Fonts.Medium, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = self:_T("Text"),
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Position = UDim2.new(0, 72, 0, 6), Size = UDim2.new(1, -84, 0, 16),
			TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Parent = row,
		})
		Create("TextLabel", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = subtitle, Font = self.Fonts.Regular, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = self:_T("SubText"),
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Position = UDim2.new(0, 72, 0, 23), Size = UDim2.new(1, -84, 0, 14),
			TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Parent = row,
		})
		row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			self:_Ripple(row)
			self:_Sound("Click")
			onClick()
		end)
		-- keyboard: Down / Up walk these, Enter opens the highlighted one
		self._searchRows[#self._searchRows + 1] = { Row = row, Stroke = stroke, Activate = onClick }
		if self.Effects.PageTransitions then
			local rowScale = Create("UIScale", { Scale = 0.96, Parent = row })
			FadeTree(row, 1, 0)
			task.delay(Timed(math.min(order, 12) * 0.03), function()
				if Alive(row) then
					Tween(rowScale, { Scale = 1 }, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
					FadeTree(row, 0, 0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
				end
			end)
		end
	end

	for _, entry in ipairs(matches.Pages) do
		local page = entry.Page
		addRow("PAGE", page.Name, "Open page", function()
			if self.SearchBox then
				self.SearchBox.Text = ""
			end
			self:SelectPage(page)
		end)
	end
	for _, entry in ipairs(matches.Sections) do
		local section, page = entry.Section, entry.Page
		addRow("SECTION", tostring(section.Title), "in " .. page.Name, function()
			if self.SearchBox then
				self.SearchBox.Text = ""
			end
			self:SelectPage(page)
			task.delay(Timed(0.12), function()
				if Alive(section.Frame) and Alive(page.Frame) then
					local y = section.Frame.AbsolutePosition.Y - page.Frame.AbsolutePosition.Y + page.Frame.CanvasPosition.Y - 14
					Tween(page.Frame, { CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(0, y)) }, 0.3)
				end
			end)
		end)
	end
	for _, entry in ipairs(matches.Elements) do
		local element = entry.Element
		local pageName = element.Page and element.Page.Name or "?"
		addRow(string.upper(string.sub(element.Type, 1, 7)), tostring(element.Title), "in " .. pageName, function()
			if self.SearchBox then
				self.SearchBox.Text = ""
			end
			if element.Page then
				self:SelectPage(element.Page)
			end
			task.delay(Timed(0.1), function()
				element:Flash()
			end)
		end)
	end
	if #matches.Pages == 0 and #matches.Elements == 0 and #matches.Sections == 0 then
		order = order + 1
		self:_Label({
			Text = "No results for \"" .. query .. "\"", TextSize = 13, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30),
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, LayoutOrder = order, Parent = results,
		}, "Medium", "SubText")
	end
	return matches
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Notifications
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function Window:Notify(options)
	options = options or {}
	local theme = self.Theme
	local title = tostring(options.Title or "Notification")
	local content = tostring(options.Content or "")
	local duration = options.Duration or 4
	local kind = options.Type or "Info"
	local typeToken = ({ Info = "Accent", Success = "Success", Warning = "Warning", Error = "Error" })[kind] or "Accent"

	local contentHeight = 0
	if content ~= "" then
		contentHeight = TextService:GetTextSize(content, 13, self.Fonts.Regular, Vector2.new(236, 1000)).Y
	end
	local height = 16 + 18 + (contentHeight > 0 and contentHeight + 4 or 0) + 16

	-- card: no side bar, so nothing can poke past the rounded corner
	local frame = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Notification", BackgroundColor3 = theme.Element, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height),
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(380, 0), ClipsDescendants = true, LayoutOrder = math.floor(os.clock() * 1000), Parent = self.NotifyHolder,
	})
	self:_Corner(frame, 12)
	self:_Bind(frame, "BackgroundColor3", "Element")
	local stroke = Create("UIStroke", { Color = theme.Stroke, Transparency = 0.5, Parent = frame })
	self:_Bind(stroke, "Color", "Stroke")
	local scale = Create("UIScale", { Scale = 0.9, Parent = frame })
	-- faint tint of the type colour across the card
	local tint = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = self:_T(typeToken), BackgroundTransparency = 0.94, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = frame })
	Create("UIGradient", { Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumKey(0, 0), NumKey(1, 1) }), Parent = tint })

	-- icon badge
	local badge = Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = self:_T(typeToken), BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 14), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 28), Parent = frame,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 9) }) })
	local badgeScale = Create("UIScale", { Scale = 0, Parent = badge })
	local badgeGlow = Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = self:_T(typeToken), BackgroundTransparency = 0.7, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 28), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 28), ZIndex = 0, Parent = frame,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
	if options.Image then
		badge.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		Create("ImageLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = options.Image, ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = badge },
		{ Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 9) }) })
	elseif options.Icon then
		self:_Icon(badge, self:_ResolveIcon(options.Icon, options.CustomIcon), 15, "AccentText", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5) })
	elseif kind == "Success" then
		MakeCheck(badge, 13, self:_T("AccentText"))
	elseif kind == "Error" then
		for _, rotation in ipairs({ 45, -45 }) do
			Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = self:_T("AccentText"), BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 2), Rotation = rotation, Parent = badge }, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
		end
	else
		Create("TextLabel", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = (kind == "Warning") and "!" or "i", Font = self.Fonts.Bold, TextSize = 15, TextColor3 = self:_T("AccentText"),
			Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = badge,
		})
	end

	local titleLabel = self:_Label({ Text = title, TextSize = 14, Position = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, 14), Size = UDim2.new(1, -92, 0, 18), Parent = frame }, "Bold", "Text")
	local bodyLabel = nil
	if content ~= "" then
		bodyLabel = self:_Label({
			Text = content, TextSize = 13, Position = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, 34), Size = UDim2.new(1, -72, 0, contentHeight + 2),
			TextWrapped = true, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None, Parent = frame,
		}, "Regular", "SubText")
	end
	local closeButton = Create("TextButton", {
		Text = "×", TextSize = 18, Font = self.Fonts.Medium, TextColor3 = theme.SubText, TextTransparency = 0.4, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 8), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 22), ZIndex = 3, Parent = frame,
	})
	self:_Bind(closeButton, "TextColor3", "SubText")
	-- progress bar inset inside the rounded card
	local progressTrack = Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = self:_T(typeToken), BackgroundTransparency = 0.85, BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 1, -8), Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 3), Parent = frame,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
	local progress = Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = self:_T(typeToken), BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = progressTrack,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })

	local closed = false
	local remaining = duration
	local paused = false
	-- frosted glass behind the card while Acrylic is on
	self:_AddGlass(frame, {
		Radius = 12, Loose = true,
		IsVisible = function()
			return not closed
		end,
		Tint = function(on)
			frame.BackgroundTransparency = on and 0.14 or 0
		end,
	})
	local function dismiss()
		if closed then
			return
		end
		closed = true
		Tween(frame, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(380, 0) }, 0.32, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
		Tween(scale, { Scale = 0.94 }, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
		FadeTree(frame, 1, 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		-- fold the card away so the notifications below glide up instead of jumping
		task.delay(Timed(0.3), function()
			if Alive(frame) then
				Tween(frame, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0) }, 0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
			end
		end)
		task.delay(Timed(0.5), function()
			frame:Destroy()
		end)
	end
	closeButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(dismiss)
	closeButton.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tween(closeButton, { TextTransparency = 0 }, 0.12) end)
	closeButton.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tween(closeButton, { TextTransparency = 0.4 }, 0.15) end)
	frame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if IsClick(input) and options.OnClick then
			SafeCall(options.OnClick)
			if options.DismissOnClick ~= false then
				dismiss()
			end
		end
	end)
	-- hovering pauses the timer and lifts the card slightly
	frame.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		paused = true
		Tween(scale, { Scale = 1.02 }, 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		Tween(stroke, { Color = self:_T(typeToken), Transparency = 0.3 }, 0.2)
	end)
	frame.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		paused = false
		Tween(scale, { Scale = 1 }, 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		Tween(stroke, { Color = self:_T("Stroke"), Transparency = 0.5 }, 0.25)
	end)

	-- entrance: spring slide from the right, card scales up, badge pops with a glow ring
	Tween(frame, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0) }, 0.55, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
	Tween(scale, { Scale = 1 }, 0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
	task.delay(Timed(0.12), function()
		if Alive(badge) then
			Tween(badgeScale, { Scale = 1 }, 0.55, Enum.EasingStyle.Elastic)
			Tween(badgeGlow, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(64, 64), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, 0.7, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart)
		end
	end)
	titleLabel.TextTransparency = 1
	titleLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 64, 0, 14)
	Tween(titleLabel, { TextTransparency = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, 14) }, 0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
	if bodyLabel then
		bodyLabel.TextTransparency = 1
		bodyLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 64, 0, 34)
		task.delay(Timed(0.06), function()
			if Alive(bodyLabel) then
				Tween(bodyLabel, { TextTransparency = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, 34) }, 0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
			end
		end)
	end
	self:_Sound("Notify")

	if duration > 0 then
		task.spawn(function()
			local last = os.clock()
			while Alive(frame) and not closed and remaining > 0 do
				local now = os.clock()
				if not paused then
					remaining = remaining - (now - last)
					progress.Size = UDim2.fromScale(math.max(0, remaining / duration), 1)
				end
				last = now
				task.wait()
			end
			if not closed then
				dismiss()
			end
		end)
	else
		progressTrack.Visible = false
	end
	return { Dismiss = dismiss, Frame = frame, SetTitle = function(text) titleLabel.Text = tostring(text) end }
end

-- ============================================================================
-- ELEMENT BASE
-- ============================================================================
function Element:SetVisible(visible)
	self.Visible = visible and true or false
	self.Frame.Visible = self.Visible
end

function Element:SetTitle(text)
	self.Title = text
	if self.TitleLabel then
		self.TitleLabel.Text = tostring(text)
	end
end

function Element:SetDescription(text)
	self.Description = text
	if self.DescLabel then
		self.DescLabel.Text = tostring(text)
	end
end

function Element:SetTooltip(text)
	self.Window:_Tooltip(self.Frame, text)
end

function Element:Flash()
	local frame, stroke = self.Frame, self.Stroke
	if self.Scale then
		self.Scale.Scale = 1.025
		Tween(self.Scale, { Scale = 1 }, 0.45, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
	end
	local page = self.Page
	if page and page.Frame and Alive(frame) then
		local y = frame.AbsolutePosition.Y - page.Frame.AbsolutePosition.Y + page.Frame.CanvasPosition.Y - 20
		Tween(page.Frame, { CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(0, y)) }, 0.35)
	end
	if stroke then
		stroke.Enabled = true
		stroke.Color = self.Window:_T("Accent")
		stroke.Transparency = 0
		stroke.Thickness = 2
		task.delay(Timed(0.9), function()
			if Alive(stroke) then
				local tween = Tween(stroke, { Color = self.Window:_T("Stroke"), Transparency = 0.75, Thickness = 1 }, 0.5)
				task.delay(Timed(0.5), function()
					if Alive(stroke) and not self.Filled then
						stroke.Enabled = false
					end
				end)
			end
		end)
	end
end

function Element:Destroy()
	self.Maid:Clean()
	if self.Frame then
		self.Frame:Destroy()
	end
	local windowList = self.Window.Elements
	for index = #windowList, 1, -1 do
		if windowList[index] == self then
			table.remove(windowList, index)
		end
	end
	if self.Container and self.Container.Elements then
		local list = self.Container.Elements
		for index = #list, 1, -1 do
			if list[index] == self then
				table.remove(list, index)
			end
		end
	end
	if self.Flag then
		self.Window._flagElements[self.Flag] = nil
	end
end

function Element:_SetFlag(value)
	self.Value = value
	if self.Flag then
		self.Window.Flags[self.Flag] = value
	end
	if self._listeners then
		for _, fn in ipairs(self._listeners) do
			SafeCall(fn, value, self)
		end
	end
	if self._bindings then
		for _, binding in ipairs(self._bindings) do
			pcall(function()
				if typeof(binding.Target) == "Instance" then
					binding.Target:SetAttribute(binding.Key, value)
				else
					binding.Target[binding.Key] = value
				end
			end)
		end
	end
	if self.Flag and self.Window.AutoSave and self.Window._ready then
		self.Window:_ScheduleAutoSave()
	end
end

-- element:OnChanged(function(value, element) end)
function Element:OnChanged(fn)
	self._listeners = self._listeners or {}
	self._listeners[#self._listeners + 1] = fn
	return self
end

-- element:Bind(table, "key")  or  element:Bind(instance, "AttributeName")
function Element:Bind(target, key)
	self._bindings = self._bindings or {}
	self._bindings[#self._bindings + 1] = { Target = target, Key = key }
	if self.Value ~= nil then
		self:_SetFlag(self.Value)
	end
	return self
end

function Element:Reset()
	if self.Default ~= nil and type(self.Set) == "function" then
		pcall(self.Set, self, self.Default)
	end
end

function Element:SetFocused(state)
	local stroke = self.Stroke
	if not stroke then
		return
	end
	if state then
		stroke.Enabled = true
		Tween(stroke, { Color = self.Window:_T("Accent"), Transparency = 0.2, Thickness = 1.5 }, 0.15)
	else
		Tween(stroke, { Color = self.Window:_T("Stroke"), Transparency = 0.75, Thickness = 1 }, 0.2)
		if not self.Filled then
			task.delay(Timed(0.2), function()
				if Alive(stroke) then stroke.Enabled = false end
			end)
		end
	end
end

-- ============================================================================
-- CONTAINER (shared by Page and Section)
-- ============================================================================
local function RowHeight(options, extra)
	local hasDesc = options.Description ~= nil and options.Description ~= ""
	return (hasDesc and 56 or 40) + (extra or 0)
end

function Container:_CreateBase(kind, options, height, plain)
	local win = self.Window
	local baseToken = options.BaseToken or "Element"
	local inGroup = getmetatable(self) == Section
	local filled = baseToken ~= "Element" or options.Filled == true or (options.Filled ~= false and not inGroup)
	local frame = Create("Frame", {
		Name = kind, BackgroundColor3 = win:_T(filled and baseToken or "ElementHover"), BackgroundTransparency = filled and 0 or 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height), LayoutOrder = win:_NextOrder(), ClipsDescendants = true,
	})
	win:_Corner(frame, 8)
	win:_Bind(frame, "BackgroundColor3", filled and baseToken or "ElementHover")
	local stroke = Create("UIStroke", { Color = win:_T("Stroke"), Transparency = 0.75, Enabled = filled, Parent = frame })
	win:_Bind(stroke, "Color", "Stroke")
	local scale = Create("UIScale", { Scale = 1, Parent = frame })
	if filled then
		win:_DepthShade(frame)
	end
	local separator = nil
	if not filled then
		separator = Create("Frame", {
			Name = "Separator", BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("Stroke"), BackgroundTransparency = 0.65, BorderSizePixel = 0,
			Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 1, -1), Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 1), Parent = frame,
		})
		win:_Bind(separator, "BackgroundColor3", "Stroke")
	end
	frame.Parent = self.List

	local element = setmetatable({
		Type = kind, Frame = frame, Stroke = stroke, Scale = scale, Separator = separator, Window = win, Page = self.Page, Container = self,
		Title = options.Title or options.Name or options.Text or kind, Description = options.Description,
		Maid = Maid.new(), Visible = true, Flag = options.Flag, BaseToken = baseToken, Filled = filled,
	}, Element)

	if not plain then
		element.HoverState = win:_Hover(frame, filled and stroke or nil, {
			BaseToken = filled and baseToken or "ElementHover",
			HoverToken = (baseToken == "Element") and "ElementHover" or baseToken,
			BaseTransparency = filled and 0 or 1,
			HoverTransparency = filled and 0 or 0.45,
			Scale = scale,
		})
		win:_Spotlight(frame)
	end
	if options.Tooltip then
		win:_Tooltip(frame, options.Tooltip)
	end
	element.Default = options.Default
	frame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 and win.Effects.RightClickReset ~= false then
			element:Reset()
		end
	end)
	self.Elements[#self.Elements + 1] = element
	win:_RegisterSearchable(element)
	if options.Flag then
		-- Two elements asking for one flag used to overwrite each other in saved profiles, and the
		-- second one silently took the first one's value. The later element gets a numbered flag instead.
		local flag = tostring(options.Flag)
		if win._flagElements[flag] and win._flagElements[flag] ~= element then
			local index = 2
			while win._flagElements[flag .. " " .. index] do
				index = index + 1
			end
			flag = flag .. " " .. index
			if flag:sub(1, 2) ~= "__" then
				warn("[AuroraUI] Flag '" .. tostring(options.Flag) .. "' was already in use; this one saves as '" .. flag .. "'")
			end
		end
		element.Flag = flag
		options.Flag = flag
		win._flagElements[flag] = element
		-- a preset applied before this element existed left its value waiting
		local pending = win._pendingFlags and win._pendingFlags[flag]
		if pending ~= nil then
			win._pendingFlags[flag] = nil
			task.defer(function()
				if type(element.Set) == "function" then
					pcall(element.Set, element, pending)
				end
			end)
		end
	end
	return element
end

-- Title / description on the left of a row. options.Icon (built-in name or image id) adds an icon before the text;
-- options.IconColor = false keeps an image's own colours, or pass a theme token.
function Container:_LabelPair(element, options, rightWidth)
	local win = self.Window
	local hasDesc = options.Description ~= nil and options.Description ~= ""
	local width = -(rightWidth + 28)
	local textToken = (element.BaseToken == "Accent") and "AccentText" or "Text"
	local subToken = (element.BaseToken == "Accent") and "AccentText" or "SubText"
	local textX = 14
	if options.Icon then
		local size = options.IconSize or 18
		local y = (hasDesc and 26 or 20) - size / 2
		local iconToken = (type(options.IconColor) == "string") and options.IconColor or subToken
		local iconSpec = win:_ResolveIcon(options.Icon, options.CustomIcon)
		if tostring(iconSpec):find("rbxasset", 1, true) then
			element.IconHolder = Create("ImageLabel", { Name = "RowIcon", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = iconSpec, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, y), Parent = element.Frame })
			if options.IconColor ~= false then
				win:_Bind(element.IconHolder, "ImageColor3", iconToken)
			end
		else
			element.IconHolder = win:_Icon(element.Frame, iconSpec, size, iconToken, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, y) })
		end
		textX = 14 + size + 10
	end
	local shift = textX - 14
	if hasDesc then
		element.TitleLabel = win:_Label({
			Name = "Title", Text = tostring(options.Title or ""), TextSize = 14, Position = UDim2.new(0, textX, 0, 9),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, width - shift, 0, 18), Parent = element.Frame,
		}, "Medium", textToken)
		element.DescLabel = win:_Label({
			Name = "Description", Text = tostring(options.Description), TextSize = 12, Position = UDim2.new(0, textX, 0, 28),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, width - shift, 0, 16), Parent = element.Frame,
		}, "Regular", subToken)
	else
		element.TitleLabel = win:_Label({
			Name = "Title", Text = tostring(options.Title or ""), TextSize = 14, Position = UDim2.new(0, textX, 0, 0),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, width - shift, 0, 40), Parent = element.Frame,
		}, "Medium", textToken)
	end
end

-- small rounded control button used inside elements (arrows, keybind, etc.)
function Container:_MiniButton(parent, size, position, anchor)
	local win = self.Window
	local button = Create("TextButton", {
		Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("ElementActive"), BorderSizePixel = 0,
		AnchorPoint = anchor or Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = position, Size = size, ClipsDescendants = true, ZIndex = 3, Parent = parent,
	})
	win:_Corner(button, 7)
	win:_Bind(button, "BackgroundColor3", "ElementActive")
	local stroke = Create("UIStroke", { Color = win:_T("Stroke"), Transparency = 0.35, Parent = button })
	win:_Bind(stroke, "Color", "Stroke")
	win:_Hover(button, stroke, { BaseToken = "ElementActive", HoverToken = "ElementHover", StrokeTransparency = 0.35, HoverStroke = false, Sound = false })
	local pressScale = Create("UIScale", { Scale = 1, Parent = button })
	button.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() Tween(pressScale, { Scale = 0.94 }, 0.08, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad) end)
	button.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() Tween(pressScale, { Scale = 1 }, 0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad) end)
	button.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tween(pressScale, { Scale = 1 }, 0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad) end)
	return button, stroke
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- TITLE
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Options: Text, Size, Font ("Regular"/"Medium"/"Bold" or Enum.Font), Color, Alignment,
--          Gradient (true or {Color3, Color3}), AnimatedGradient, Stroke {Color, Thickness, Transparency},
--          Shadow, Divider, Uppercase, Typewriter (true or seconds per character), Icon, Padding
-- A stand-in for an element this build does not have. The row says what it needs, and every method an
-- Elite script might call is present and quiet, so nothing downstream has to know. The flag value is
-- still kept and still travels in presets and profiles: text typed into an Elite code box survives a
-- trip through Free untouched, and comes back when the same config is opened in Elite again.
-- Everything an element in the full build can be asked to do, with the harmless answer for it here.
-- The list is not guesswork: it is every method the Elite-only elements define. A script written for
-- Elite calls these on Free without knowing, and nothing errors and nothing claims to have worked.
local LockedNoops = {
	"Refresh", "Focus", "Open", "Close", "Start", "Stop", "Pause", "Resume", "Clear", "LoadGraph", "AddNode",
	"Undo", "Redo", "AddRule", "RemoveRule", "Append", "SetText", "SetOptions", "Select", "AddTab", "RemoveTab",
	"RenameTab", "SelectTab", "Run", "Reset", "Insert", "Print", "ClearOutput", "ToggleOutput", "GoToLine",
	"SetLanguage", "SetReadOnly", "SetRules", "SetFiles", "CreateFile", "OpenFile", "SaveFile", "GetCurrentTab",
	"_View", "_ToAbsolute", "_LineOp", "_RepathTab", "_AcceptCompletion",
}
-- The ones that have to hand something back, so `for _, file in ipairs(box:GetFiles())` still runs.
local LockedAnswers = {
	GetText = "", IsRunning = false, GetGraph = "table", GetFiles = "table", GetTabs = "table",
	GetRules = "table", Selection = "table",
}
function Container:_Locked(feature, options)
	options = (type(options) == "table") and options or {}
	local win = self.Window
	local label = Aurora.FeatureNames[feature] or tostring(feature)
	local rowOptions = {
		Title = options.Title or options.Name or label,
		Description = options.Description or (label .. " " .. string.lower(Aurora.EliteTagline):gsub("^part", "is part")),
		Icon = options.Icon, Flag = options.Flag,
	}
	local element = self:_CreateBase("Locked", rowOptions, RowHeight(rowOptions), true)
	self:_LabelPair(element, rowOptions, 78)
	element.Frame.BackgroundTransparency = math.min(0.6, (element.Frame.BackgroundTransparency or 0) + 0.35)
	element.Frame:SetAttribute("AuroraBG", element.Frame.BackgroundTransparency)
	if element.TitleLabel then
		element.TitleLabel.TextTransparency = 0.25
		element.TitleLabel:SetAttribute("AuroraTT", 0.25)
	end
	-- the chip at the right edge, so a locked row is recognisable at a glance
	local chip = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Chip", BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("Accent"), BackgroundTransparency = 0.82, BorderSizePixel = 0,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(64, 22),
		ZIndex = 3, Parent = element.Frame,
	})
	win:_Corner(chip, 7)
	win:_Bind(chip, "BackgroundColor3", "Accent")
	local chipStroke = Create("UIStroke", { Color = win:_T("Accent"), Transparency = 0.55, Parent = chip })
	win:_Bind(chipStroke, "Color", "Accent")
	win:_Label({
		Name = "Label", Text = Aurora.EliteName, TextSize = 10, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 4, Parent = chip,
	}, "Bold", "Accent")
	if Aurora.EliteLink ~= "" then
		local button = Create("TextButton", { Text = "", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 5, Parent = chip })
		button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			if typeof(setclipboard) == "function" then
				pcall(setclipboard, Aurora.EliteLink)
			end
			win:Notify({ Title = label, Content = Aurora.EliteLink, Type = "Info", Duration = 5 })
		end)
		win:_Tooltip(button, Aurora.EliteLink)
	end

	local stored = options.Default
	element.Locked, element.Feature = true, feature
	function element:Get()
		return stored
	end
	function element:Set(value, silent)
		stored = value
		self:_SetFlag(value)
		if not silent then
			SafeCall(options.Callback, value)
		end
	end
	for _, name in ipairs(LockedNoops) do
		if rawget(element, name) == nil and Element[name] == nil then
			element[name] = function() return nil end
		end
	end
	for name, answer in pairs(LockedAnswers) do
		if rawget(element, name) == nil and Element[name] == nil then
			if answer == "table" then
				element[name] = function() return {} end
			else
				element[name] = function() return answer end
			end
		end
	end
	if element.Flag ~= nil and stored ~= nil then
		element:_SetFlag(stored)
	end
	return element
end

function Container:AddTitle(options)
	if type(options) ~= "table" then
		options = { Text = tostring(options) }
	end
	local win = self.Window
	local element = self:_CreateBase("Title", options, 30, true)
	local frame = element.Frame
	if element.Separator then
		element.Separator.Visible = false
	end
	frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	frame.ClipsDescendants = false
	frame.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
	frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
	element.Stroke.Enabled = false
	Create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 6), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = frame })
	Create("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, options.Padding or 6), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 2), Parent = frame })

	local text = tostring(options.Text or options.Title or "Title")
	if options.Uppercase then
		text = string.upper(text)
	end
	local textX = 0
	if options.Icon then
		local iconHolder = Create("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = 1, Parent = frame })
		local iconSpec = win:_ResolveIcon(options.Icon, options.CustomIcon)
		if tostring(iconSpec):find("rbxasset", 1, true) then
			local icon = Create("ImageLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = iconSpec, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 2), Parent = iconHolder })
			win:_Bind(icon, "ImageColor3", "Accent")
		else
			win:_Icon(iconHolder, iconSpec, 20, "Accent", { Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 2) })
		end
		textX = 28
	end
	local label = win:_Label({
		Name = "Text", Text = text, TextSize = options.Size or 22, TextWrapped = true, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment[options.Alignment or "Left"], Size = UDim2.new(1, -textX, 0, 0),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Position = UDim2.new(0, textX, 0, 0), LayoutOrder = 1,
		Parent = options.Icon and frame:FindFirstChildOfClass("Frame") or frame,
	}, options.Font or "Bold", options.Color and false or "Text")
	if options.Color then
		label.TextColor3 = options.Color
	end
	element.TitleLabel = label
	element.Title = text

	if options.Gradient then
		local gradient = Create("UIGradient", { Parent = label })
		if type(options.Gradient) == "table" then
			gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({ Keypoint(0, options.Gradient[1]), Keypoint(1, options.Gradient[2] or options.Gradient[1]) })
		else
			win:_OnTheme(function(current)
				gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({ Keypoint(0, current.Accent), Keypoint(1, current.AccentSecondary) })
			end)
		end
		if options.AnimatedGradient ~= false then
			local phase = math.random() * 6.28
			element.Maid:Add(win:_AddStepHook(function(t)
				if win.Effects.AnimatedGradient and Alive(gradient) then
					gradient.Rotation = math.sin(t * 0.6 + phase) * 25
					gradient.Offset = Vector2.new(math.sin(t * 0.9 + phase) * 0.35, 0)
				end
			end))
		end
	end
	if options.Stroke then
		local strokeOpts = type(options.Stroke) == "table" and options.Stroke or {}
		Create("UIStroke", {
			Color = strokeOpts.Color or win:_T("Accent"), Thickness = strokeOpts.Thickness or 1,
			Transparency = strokeOpts.Transparency or 0.4, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Contextual, Parent = label,
		})
	end
	if options.Shadow then
		label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.7
		label.TextStrokeColor3 = win:_T("Shadow")
	end
	if options.Divider then
		local line = Create("Frame", {
			Name = "Divider", BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("Stroke"), BackgroundTransparency = 0.3, BorderSizePixel = 0,
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1), LayoutOrder = 2, Parent = frame,
		})
		if options.Gradient then
			win:_OnTheme(function(current)
				line.BackgroundColor3 = current.Accent
			end)
			Create("UIGradient", { Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumKey(0, 0), NumKey(1, 1) }), Parent = line })
		else
			win:_Bind(line, "BackgroundColor3", "Stroke")
		end
	end
	if options.Typewriter then
		local perChar = type(options.Typewriter) == "number" and options.Typewriter or 0.03
		local count = utf8.len(text) or #text
		label.MaxVisibleGraphemes = 0
		Tween(label, { MaxVisibleGraphemes = count }, perChar * count, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear)
	end

	function element:SetText(newText)
		self.Title = newText
		label.Text = options.Uppercase and string.upper(tostring(newText)) or tostring(newText)
	end
	function element:SetColor(color)
		label.TextColor3 = color
	end
	function element:SetSize(size)
		label.TextSize = size
	end
	return element
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- TEXT / PARAGRAPH
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Options: Title (optional), Text, Size, Font, Color, Alignment, RichText, Typewriter,
--          Background (card style, default true), Padding
function Container:AddText(options)
	if type(options) ~= "table" then
		options = { Text = tostring(options) }
	end
	local win = self.Window
	local element = self:_CreateBase("Text", options, 40, true)
	local frame = element.Frame
	frame.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
	frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
	if options.Background == false then
		if element.Separator then element.Separator.Visible = false end
		frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		element.Stroke.Enabled = false
	end
	local pad = options.Padding or 10
	Create("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, pad), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, pad), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 14), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 14), Parent = frame })
	Create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 4), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = frame })

	if options.Title then
		element.TitleLabel = win:_Label({
			Name = "Title", Text = tostring(options.Title), TextSize = 14, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18), LayoutOrder = 1,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment[options.Alignment or "Left"], Parent = frame,
		}, "Bold", "Text")
	end
	local body = win:_Label({
		Name = "Body", Text = tostring(options.Text or ""), TextSize = options.Size or 13, TextWrapped = true,
		TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None, RichText = options.RichText ~= false, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = 2, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment[options.Alignment or "Left"],
		Parent = frame,
	}, options.Font or "Regular", options.Color and false or (options.Title and "SubText" or "Text"))
	if options.Color then
		body.TextColor3 = options.Color
	end
	element.Body = body
	element.Title = options.Title or tostring(options.Text or "")
	element.Description = tostring(options.Text or "")

	if options.Typewriter then
		local perChar = type(options.Typewriter) == "number" and options.Typewriter or 0.015
		local count = utf8.len(body.Text) or #body.Text
		body.MaxVisibleGraphemes = 0
		Tween(body, { MaxVisibleGraphemes = count }, perChar * count, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear)
	end

	function element:SetText(newText)
		body.Text = tostring(newText)
		self.Description = tostring(newText)
	end
	return element
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- BUTTON
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Options: Title, Description, Callback, Variant ("Default"/"Primary"/"Danger"), Confirm, ConfirmText,
--          Tooltip, Disabled, Icon
-- Options: Title, Description, ButtonText, Callback, Variant ("Secondary" default | "Primary" | "Accent" | "Ghost" | "Danger"),
--          Full (block button spanning the row), Icon, Confirm, ConfirmText, Tooltip, Disabled
-- Layout: with a Description or ButtonText the row shows text on the left and a compact button on the right;
--         otherwise the button itself is the row content, sized to its label (or full width with Full = true).
function Container:AddButton(options)
	options = options or {}
	local win = self.Window
	local variant = options.Variant or "Secondary"
	if variant == "Default" then variant = "Secondary" end
	local hasDesc = options.Description ~= nil and options.Description ~= ""
	local rowMode = hasDesc or options.ButtonText ~= nil
	local height = rowMode and RowHeight(options) or 46
	local element = self:_CreateBase("Button", options, height, true)
	local frame = element.Frame
	local callback = options.Callback
	local disabled = options.Disabled == true
	local confirming = false
	local buttonText = rowMode and (options.ButtonText or "Open") or (options.Title or "Button")
	local originalText = buttonText

	local styled
	if rowMode then
		self:_LabelPair(element, options, 130)
		local holder = Create("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 30), ZIndex = 3, Parent = frame })
		styled = win:_StyledButton(holder, { Text = buttonText, Variant = variant, Height = 30, Icon = options.ButtonIcon, CustomIcon = options.ButtonCustomIcon, TextSize = 12, ZIndex = 3 })
		-- The button measures its own width, so the holder just follows it. With AutomaticSize the holder
		-- grew from anything inside the button, and because it is anchored on the right it crept leftwards.
		local function fitHolder()
			holder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(styled.Button.Size.X.Offset, 30)
		end
		fitHolder()
		element.Maid:Add(styled.Button:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Size"):Connect(fitHolder))
	else
		if element.Separator then
			element.Separator.Visible = false
		end
		frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		element.Stroke.Enabled = false
		if element.HoverState then element.HoverState.Enabled = false end
		local holder = Create("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0.5, -17), Size = options.Full and UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 34) or UDim2.fromOffset(0, 34), ZIndex = 3, Parent = frame })
		styled = win:_StyledButton(holder, { Text = buttonText, Variant = variant, Size = options.Full and UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0) or nil, Height = 34, Icon = options.Icon, CustomIcon = options.CustomIcon, ZIndex = 3 })
		if not options.Full then
			local function fitHolder()
				holder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(styled.Button.Size.X.Offset, 34)
			end
			fitHolder()
			element.Maid:Add(styled.Button:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Size"):Connect(fitHolder))
		end
		element.TitleLabel = styled.Label
	end
	element.Styled = styled

	local function fire()
		if disabled then
			return
		end
		if options.Confirm and not confirming then
			confirming = true
			styled:SetText(options.ConfirmText or "Confirm?")
			task.delay(options.ConfirmTime or 2, function()
				if confirming then
					confirming = false
					styled:SetText(originalText)
				end
			end)
			return
		end
		confirming = false
		styled:SetText(originalText)
		SafeCall(callback, element)
	end
	styled.Button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(fire)
	styled:SetEnabled(not disabled)

	function element:SetCallback(fn)
		callback = fn
	end
	function element:SetDisabled(state)
		disabled = state and true or false
		styled:SetEnabled(not disabled)
	end
	function element:Fire()
		SafeCall(callback, element)
	end
	function element:SetTitle(text)
		self.Title = tostring(text)
		if rowMode then
			if self.TitleLabel then self.TitleLabel.Text = self.Title end
		else
			originalText = self.Title
			styled:SetText(self.Title)
		end
	end
	function element:SetButtonText(text)
		originalText = tostring(text)
		styled:SetText(originalText)
	end
	return element
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- TOGGLE
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Options: Title, Description, Default, Callback, Flag, Tooltip, Keybind (KeyCode), Disabled
function Container:AddToggle(options)
	options = options or {}
	local win = self.Window
	local element = self:_CreateBase("Toggle", options, RowHeight(options))
	local frame = element.Frame
	self:_LabelPair(element, options, 46)

	local state = options.Default == true
	local switch = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Switch", BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("Stroke"), BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(40, 20), ZIndex = 2, Parent = frame,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
	local switchStroke = Create("UIStroke", { Color = win:_T("Stroke"), Transparency = 0.5, Parent = switch })
	local knob = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Knob", BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14), ZIndex = 3, Parent = switch,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
	local knobGlow = Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("Accent"), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 26), ZIndex = 2, Parent = knob,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })

	local function render(animate)
		local time = animate and 0.3 or 0
		local track = state and win:_T("Accent") or win:_T("Stroke")
		local knobColor = state and win:_T("AccentText") or win:_T("Text")
		local knobPos = state and UDim2.new(1, -17, 0.5, 0) or UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, 0)
		if animate then
			Tween(switch, { BackgroundColor3 = track }, time, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
			Tween(knob, { Position = knobPos, BackgroundColor3 = knobColor }, time, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
			Tween(knob, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 14) }, 0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
			task.delay(Timed(0.12), function()
				Tween(knob, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14) }, 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
			end)
			knobGlow.BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("Accent")
			knobGlow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.8
			Tween(knobGlow, { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 30) }, 0.4)
			task.delay(Timed(0.46), function()
				knobGlow.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 26)
			end)
		else
			switch.BackgroundColor3 = track
			knob.Position = knobPos
			knob.BackgroundColor3 = knobColor
		end
		Tween(switchStroke, { Color = state and win:_T("Accent") or win:_T("Stroke") }, time)
	end
	win:_OnTheme(function()
		render(false)
	end)
	element:_SetFlag(state)

	local click = Create("TextButton", { Text = "", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 4, Parent = frame })
	local disabled = options.Disabled == true

	function element:Set(value, silent)
		value = value and true or false
		if value == state then
			return
		end
		state = value
		render(true)
		self:_SetFlag(state)
		if not silent then
			SafeCall(options.Callback, state)
		end
	end
	function element:Get()
		return state
	end
	function element:Toggle()
		self:Set(not state)
	end
	function element:SetDisabled(value)
		disabled = value and true or false
		frame.BackgroundTransparency = disabled and 0.5 or 0
		click.Active = not disabled
	end

	click.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if disabled then
			return
		end
		win:_Ripple(frame)
		win:_Sound("Toggle")
		element:Set(not state)
	end)
	if options.Keybind then
		element.Maid:Add(win:_OnInputAlways("Began", function(input, processed)
			if not processed and input.KeyCode == options.Keybind then
				element:Set(not state)
			end
		end))
	end
	if options.Default and options.Callback and options.FireOnInit == true then
		SafeCall(options.Callback, state)
	end
	return element
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- SLIDER
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Options: Title, Description, Min, Max, Default, Increment, Decimals, Digits, Prefix, Suffix,
--          Mode ("Clamped" | "Smooth" | "VerySmooth"), Callback, Flag, Editable, Tooltip,
--          OnRelease (true: the callback, the flag and anything listening to it wait until the pointer lets go,
--          so one drag is one change instead of one per step)
function Container:AddSlider(options)
	options = options or {}
	local win = self.Window
	local element = self:_CreateBase("Slider", options, RowHeight(options, 18))
	local frame = element.Frame
	self:_LabelPair(element, options, 100)

	local min = options.Min or 0
	local max = options.Max or 100
	if max <= min then
		max = min + 1
	end
	local increment = options.Increment or 0
	element.Step = (increment > 0) and increment or ((options.Max or 100) - (options.Min or 0)) / 100
	local decimals = options.Decimals or (increment > 0 and increment < 1 and 2 or 0)
	local mode = options.Mode or "Smooth"
	local value = math.clamp(options.Default or min, min, max)
	value = RoundTo(value - min, increment) + min

	local modeTime = ({ Clamped = 0, Smooth = 0.14, VerySmooth = 0.45 })[mode] or 0.14
	local modeStyle = (mode == "VerySmooth") and Enum.EasingStyle.Quint or Enum.EasingStyle.Quad

	local valueBox = Create("TextBox", {
		Name = "Value", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", TextSize = 13, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
		TextColor3 = win:_T("Accent"), ClearTextOnFocus = false, TextEditable = options.Editable ~= false,
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(96, 40), ZIndex = 3, Parent = frame,
	})
	win:_Font(valueBox, "Bold")
	win:_Bind(valueBox, "TextColor3", "Accent")
	if options.Description and options.Description ~= "" then
		valueBox.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(96, 36)
	end
