	local trackY = RowHeight(options, 18) - 16
	local track = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Track", BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("ElementActive"), BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, trackY + 1), Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 4), ZIndex = 2, Parent = frame,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
	win:_Bind(track, "BackgroundColor3", "ElementActive")
	local fill = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Fill", BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("Accent"), BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), ZIndex = 3, Parent = track,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
	local fillGradient = Create("UIGradient", { Parent = fill })
	win:_OnTheme(function(current)
		fillGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({ Keypoint(0, current.Accent), Keypoint(1, current.AccentSecondary) })
	end)
	local knob = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Knob", BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12), ZIndex = 5, Parent = track,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
	local knobStroke = Create("UIStroke", { Color = win:_T("Accent"), Thickness = 2, Parent = knob })
	win:_Bind(knobStroke, "Color", "Accent")
	local hit = Create("TextButton", {
		Name = "Hit", Text = "", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, trackY - 11),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 28), ZIndex = 6, Parent = frame,
	})

	local dragging = false
	-- with OnRelease the knob and the number still follow the pointer, but nothing outside the slider hears
	-- about it until the drag ends: no callback, no flag write, no "On element changed"
	local onRelease = options.OnRelease == true
	local committed = value
	local pendingCommit = false
	local function commit(silent)
		pendingCommit = false
		if math.abs(value - committed) <= 1e-9 then
			return
		end
		committed = value
		element:_SetFlag(value)
		if not silent then
			SafeCall(options.Callback, value)
		end
	end
	local function render(animate, forceTime)
		local pct = (value - min) / (max - min)
		local time = forceTime or (animate and modeTime or 0)
		valueBox.Text = FormatNumber(value, decimals, options.Digits, options.Prefix, options.Suffix)
		if time > 0 then
			Tween(fill, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(pct, 1) }, time, modeStyle)
			Tween(knob, { Position = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 0.5, 0) }, time, modeStyle)
		else
			fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(pct, 1)
			knob.Position = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 0.5, 0)
		end
	end
	render(false)
	element.Default = value
	element:_SetFlag(value)

	local function setValue(newValue, animate, silent, forceTime)
		newValue = math.clamp(newValue, min, max)
		newValue = RoundTo(newValue - min, increment) + min
		newValue = math.clamp(newValue, min, max)
		local changed = math.abs(newValue - value) > 1e-9
		value = newValue
		render(animate, forceTime)
		if changed then
			if onRelease and dragging then
				pendingCommit = true
			else
				commit(silent)
			end
		end
	end

	local function fromPosition(x, animate, forceTime)
		local pct = math.clamp((x - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, track.AbsoluteSize.X), 0, 1)
		setValue(min + pct * (max - min), animate, false, forceTime)
	end

	-- the value pops when you let go, the knob grows under the cursor and its ring thickens while held
	local valueScale = Create("UIScale", { Scale = 1, Parent = valueBox })
	local function popValue()
		valueScale.Scale = 1.14
		Tween(valueScale, { Scale = 1 }, 0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
	end
	hit.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		if not dragging then
			Tween(knob, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14) }, 0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		end
	end)
	hit.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		if not dragging then
			Tween(knob, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12) }, 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		end
	end)
	local function press(input)
		if not IsClick(input) then
			return
		end
		dragging = true
		Tween(knob, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(15, 15) }, 0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		Tween(knobStroke, { Thickness = 3 }, 0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		-- clicking somewhere on the track: snap (Clamped) or animate there (Smooth / VerySmooth)
		fromPosition(win:_Pointer().X, true, (mode == "Clamped") and 0 or math.max(modeTime, 0.2))
		win:_Sound("Click")
	end
	hit.InputBegan:Connect(press)
	element._press, element._track = press, track   -- headless input (Tests.lua) presses the track through these
	element.Maid:Add(win:_OnInput("Changed", function(input)
		if dragging and IsMove(input) then
			fromPosition(win:_Pointer().X, mode ~= "Clamped")
		end
	end))
	element.Maid:Add(win:_OnInput("Ended", function(input)
		if dragging and IsClick(input) then
			dragging = false
			Tween(knob, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12) }, 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
			Tween(knobStroke, { Thickness = 2 }, 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
			popValue()
			if pendingCommit then
				commit(false)
			end
		end
	end))
	valueBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		local parsed = tonumber((valueBox.Text:gsub("[^%d%.%-]", "")))
		if parsed then
			setValue(parsed, true)
			popValue()
		else
			render(false)
		end
	end)

	function element:Set(newValue, silent)
		setValue(tonumber(newValue) or min, true, silent)
	end
	function element:Get()
		-- with OnRelease the number on screen runs ahead of the flag while the pointer is down; Get reports what
		-- the rest of the window has been told, so it never disagrees with GetConfig
		return onRelease and committed or value
	end
	function element:SetRange(newMin, newMax)
		min, max = newMin, newMax
		if max <= min then
			max = min + 1
		end
		setValue(value, true, true)
	end
	return element
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- SELECTION  ( Title   [<]  value  [>] )
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Options: Title, Description, Options, Default (value or index), Loop, Callback, Flag, Width
function Container:AddSelection(options)
	options = options or {}
	local win = self.Window
	local width = options.Width or 110
	local element = self:_CreateBase("Selection", options, RowHeight(options))
	local frame = element.Frame
	self:_LabelPair(element, options, width + 70)

	local list = options.Options or {}
	local index = 1
	if options.Default ~= nil then
		if type(options.Default) == "number" and list[options.Default] ~= nil and not table.find(list, options.Default) then
			index = options.Default
		else
			index = table.find(list, options.Default) or 1
		end
	end
	local loop = options.Loop ~= false

	local rightButton = self:_MiniButton(frame, UDim2.fromOffset(26, 26), UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0))
	local rightChevron, rightParts = MakeChevron(rightButton, 11, win:_T("Text"), "Right")
	local leftButton = self:_MiniButton(frame, UDim2.fromOffset(26, 26), UDim2.new(1, -(14 + 26 + 6 + width + 6), 0.5, 0))
	local leftChevron, leftParts = MakeChevron(leftButton, 11, win:_T("Text"), "Left")
	for _, part in ipairs(rightParts) do win:_Bind(part, "BackgroundColor3", "Text") end
	for _, part in ipairs(leftParts) do win:_Bind(part, "BackgroundColor3", "Text") end

	element.SearchExtra = JoinOptions(list)
	local valueHolder = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "ValueHolder", BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), ClipsDescendants = true,
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -(14 + 26 + 6), 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width, 26), Parent = frame,
	})
	local valueLabel = win:_Label({
		Name = "Value", Text = tostring(list[index] or ""), TextSize = 13, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = valueHolder,
	}, "Bold", "Accent")
	element:_SetFlag(list[index])

	local function show(direction, silent)
		local text = tostring(list[index] or "")
		if direction ~= 0 then
			Tween(valueLabel, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-direction * 14, 0), TextTransparency = 1 }, 0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
			task.delay(Timed(0.12), function()
				valueLabel.Text = text
				valueLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(direction * 14, 0)
				Tween(valueLabel, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), TextTransparency = 0 }, 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
			end)
		else
			valueLabel.Text = text
		end
		element:_SetFlag(list[index])
		if not silent then
			SafeCall(options.Callback, list[index], index)
		end
	end

	local function step(direction)
		if #list == 0 then
			return
		end
		local newIndex = index + direction
		if newIndex < 1 then
			if not loop then return end
			newIndex = #list
		elseif newIndex > #list then
			if not loop then return end
			newIndex = 1
		end
		index = newIndex
		win:_Sound("Click")
		show(direction)
		-- the arrow that was used nudges in its direction and springs back
		local chevron = (direction > 0) and rightChevron or leftChevron
		chevron.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, direction * 4, 0.5, 0)
		Tween(chevron, { Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5) }, 0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
	end

	leftButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		win:_Ripple(leftButton)
		step(-1)
	end)
	rightButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		win:_Ripple(rightButton)
		step(1)
	end)

	function element:Next() step(1) end
	function element:Prev() step(-1) end
	function element:Get() return list[index], index end
	function element:Set(value, silent)
		local newIndex = table.find(list, value) or (type(value) == "number" and list[value] ~= nil and value) or nil
		if not newIndex then
			return
		end
		local direction = (newIndex > index) and 1 or ((newIndex < index) and -1 or 0)
		index = newIndex
		show(direction, silent)
	end
	function element:Refresh(newList, default)
		list = newList or {}
		index = table.find(list, default) or 1
		show(0, true)
	end
	return element
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- DROPDOWN
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Options: Title, Description, Options, Default (value or {values} when Multi), Multi, Callback, Flag,
--          Searchable, MaxVisible, Placeholder
function Container:AddDropdown(options)
	options = options or {}
	local win = self.Window
	local baseHeight = RowHeight(options)
	local element = self:_CreateBase("Dropdown", options, baseHeight)
	local frame = element.Frame
	self:_LabelPair(element, options, 160)

	local list = options.Options or {}
	local multi = options.Multi == true
	local maxVisible = options.MaxVisible or 6
	local searchable = options.Searchable
	if searchable == nil then
		searchable = #list > 8
	end
	local selected = {}
	if multi then
		if type(options.Default) == "table" then
			for _, v in ipairs(options.Default) do selected[v] = true end
		end
	elseif options.Default ~= nil then
		selected[options.Default] = true
	end
	local open = false

	local valueLabel = win:_Label({
		Name = "Value", Text = "", TextSize = 13, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, baseHeight / 2), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(150, 20), Parent = frame,
	}, "Medium", "SubText")
	local chevronHolder = Create("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, baseHeight / 2), Parent = frame })
	local _, chevronParts = MakeChevron(chevronHolder, 12, win:_T("SubText"), "Down")
	for _, part in ipairs(chevronParts) do win:_Bind(part, "BackgroundColor3", "SubText") end
	local header = Create("TextButton", { Text = "", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, baseHeight), ZIndex = 4, Parent = frame })

	local listHolder = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "List", BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("Secondary"), BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, baseHeight),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 0), ClipsDescendants = true, ZIndex = 3, Parent = frame,
	})
	win:_Corner(listHolder, 7)
	win:_Bind(listHolder, "BackgroundColor3", "Secondary")
	local listTop = 0
	local searchBox = nil
	if searchable then
		local searchHolder = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("Element"), BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 6), Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 26), Parent = listHolder })
		win:_Corner(searchHolder, 6)
		win:_Bind(searchHolder, "BackgroundColor3", "Element")
		searchBox = Create("TextBox", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", PlaceholderText = "Search...", PlaceholderColor3 = win:_T("Placeholder"),
			TextColor3 = win:_T("Text"), TextSize = 12, ClearTextOnFocus = false, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
			Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, 0), Parent = searchHolder,
		})
		win:_Font(searchBox, "Medium")
		win:_Bind(searchBox, "TextColor3", "Text")
		win:_Bind(searchBox, "PlaceholderColor3", "Placeholder")
		listTop = 36
	end
	local scroll = Create("ScrollingFrame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, listTop + 4), Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, -listTop - 8),
		CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ScrollBarThickness = 3,
		ScrollBarImageColor3 = win:_T("Accent"), ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y, Parent = listHolder,
	})
	win:_Bind(scroll, "ScrollBarImageColor3", "Accent")
	Create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 2), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = scroll })

	local itemButtons = {}
	local function updateValueText()
		local parts = {}
		for _, option in ipairs(list) do
			if selected[option] then
				parts[#parts + 1] = tostring(option)
			end
		end
		local text = (#parts > 0) and table.concat(parts, ", ") or (options.Placeholder or "Select...")
		if valueLabel.Text ~= "" and valueLabel.Text ~= text then
			-- the new value rises into place
			valueLabel.Position = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, baseHeight / 2 + 7)
			valueLabel.TextTransparency = 1
			Tween(valueLabel, { Position = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, baseHeight / 2), TextTransparency = 0 }, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		end
		valueLabel.Text = text
	end
	local function currentValue()
		if multi then
			local values = {}
			for _, option in ipairs(list) do
				if selected[option] then values[#values + 1] = option end
			end
			return values
		end
		for _, option in ipairs(list) do
			if selected[option] then return option end
		end
		return nil
	end
	local function refreshItemVisuals()
		for _, entry in ipairs(itemButtons) do
			local isSelected = selected[entry.Option] == true
			if isSelected and not entry.Check.Visible then
				local pop = entry.Check:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale") or Create("UIScale", { Parent = entry.Check })
				pop.Scale = 0.3
				Tween(pop, { Scale = 1 }, 0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
			end
			entry.Check.Visible = isSelected
			Tween(entry.Label, { TextColor3 = win:_T(isSelected and "Accent" or "Text") }, 0.15)
			entry.Button.BackgroundTransparency = isSelected and 0.6 or 1
		end
	end
	local function visibleCount()
		local count = 0
		for _, entry in ipairs(itemButtons) do
			if entry.Button.Visible then count = count + 1 end
		end
		return count
	end
	local function listHeight()
		return math.min(math.max(visibleCount(), 1), maxVisible) * 30 + listTop + 8
	end
	local function resize(animate)
		local h = open and (baseHeight + listHeight() + 8) or baseHeight
		Tween(frame, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h) }, animate and 0.3 or 0)
		Tween(listHolder, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, open and listHeight() or 0) }, animate and 0.3 or 0)
	end
	local function buildItems()
		for _, entry in ipairs(itemButtons) do entry.Button:Destroy() end
		itemButtons = {}
		for order, option in ipairs(list) do
			local button = Create("TextButton", {
				Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("ElementHover"), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28), LayoutOrder = order, ClipsDescendants = true, Parent = scroll,
			})
			win:_Corner(button, 6)
			win:_Bind(button, "BackgroundColor3", "ElementHover")
			-- Swatches = function(option) -> { Color3, ... } draws a strip of colours at the right of the row
			local stripWidth = 0
			local colours = nil
			if type(options.Swatches) == "function" then
				local ok, result = pcall(options.Swatches, option)
				colours = ok and result or nil
			end
			if type(colours) == "table" and #colours > 0 then
				local shown = math.min(#colours, 6)
				stripWidth = shown * 12 + 6
				local strip = Create("Frame", {
					Name = "Swatches", BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
					Position = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(shown * 12 - 2, 10), ZIndex = 5, Parent = button,
				})
				for index = 1, shown do
					local colour = colours[index]
					if typeof(colour) == "Color3" then
						Create("Frame", {
							BackgroundColor3 = colour, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.fromOffset((index - 1) * 12, 0),
							Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10), ZIndex = 5, Parent = strip,
						}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3) }), Create("UIStroke", { Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Transparency = 0.85 }) })
					end
				end
			end
			local label = win:_Label({ Text = tostring(option), TextSize = 13, Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -36 - stripWidth, 1, 0), Parent = button }, "Medium", "Text")
			local checkHolder = Create("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, 0), Visible = false, Parent = button })
			local _, checkParts = MakeCheck(checkHolder, 12, win:_T("Accent"))
			for _, part in ipairs(checkParts) do win:_Bind(part, "BackgroundColor3", "Accent") end
			button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
				if not selected[option] then Tween(button, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.5 }, 0.12) end
			end)
			button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
				if not selected[option] then Tween(button, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, 0.15) end
			end)
			button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				win:_Ripple(button)
				win:_Sound("Click")
				if multi then
					selected[option] = not selected[option] or nil
				else
					selected = { [option] = true }
				end
				refreshItemVisuals()
				updateValueText()
				element:_SetFlag(currentValue())
				SafeCall(options.Callback, currentValue())
				if not multi then
					element:Close()
				end
			end)
			itemButtons[#itemButtons + 1] = { Button = button, Label = label, Check = checkHolder, Option = option }
		end
		refreshItemVisuals()
		updateValueText()
	end
	buildItems()
	element.SearchExtra = JoinOptions(list)
	element:_SetFlag(currentValue())

	if searchBox then
		searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
			local query = searchBox.Text:lower()
			for _, entry in ipairs(itemButtons) do
				entry.Button.Visible = query == "" or tostring(entry.Option):lower():find(query, 1, true) ~= nil
			end
			resize(true)
		end)
	end

	function element:Open()
		if open then return end
		open = true
		resize(true)
		Tween(chevronHolder, { Rotation = 180 }, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		local shown = 0
		for _, entry in ipairs(itemButtons) do
			if entry.Button.Visible and shown < 12 then
				shown = shown + 1
				entry.Label.TextTransparency = 1
				entry.Label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 0)
				task.delay(Timed(0.03 * shown), function()
					if Alive(entry.Label) then
						Tween(entry.Label, { TextTransparency = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0) }, 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
					end
				end)
			else
				entry.Label.TextTransparency = 0
				entry.Label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
			end
		end
	end
	function element:Close()
		if not open then return end
		open = false
		-- options fade out while the list folds up
		for _, entry in ipairs(itemButtons) do
			if entry.Button.Visible then
				Tween(entry.Label, { TextTransparency = 1 }, 0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
			end
		end
		resize(true)
		Tween(chevronHolder, { Rotation = 0 }, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
	end
	function element:Toggle()
		if open then self:Close() else self:Open() end
	end
	function element:Get()
		return currentValue()
	end
	function element:Set(value, silent)
		selected = {}
		if multi and type(value) == "table" then
			for _, v in ipairs(value) do selected[v] = true end
		elseif value ~= nil then
			selected[value] = true
		end
		refreshItemVisuals()
		updateValueText()
		self:_SetFlag(currentValue())
		if not silent then
			SafeCall(options.Callback, currentValue())
		end
	end
	function element:Refresh(newList, keepSelection)
		list = newList or {}
		if not keepSelection then
			selected = {}
		end
		buildItems()
		self:_SetFlag(currentValue())
		if open then resize(true) end
	end

	header.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		win:_Ripple(frame)
		win:_Sound("Click")
		element:Toggle()
	end)
	element.Maid:Add(win:_OnInput("Began", function(input)
		if open and IsClick(input) then
			local abs, size = frame.AbsolutePosition, frame.AbsoluteSize
			local p = win:_Pointer()
			if p.X < abs.X or p.X > abs.X + size.X or p.Y < abs.Y or p.Y > abs.Y + size.Y then
				element:Close()
			end
		end
	end))
	return element
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- KEYBIND
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Options: Title, Description, Default (KeyCode), Callback(key), OnPressed, OnReleased, Flag
function Container:AddKeybind(options)
	options = options or {}
	local win = self.Window
	local element = self:_CreateBase("Keybind", options, RowHeight(options))
	local frame = element.Frame
	self:_LabelPair(element, options, 100)

	local key = options.Default or Enum.KeyCode.Unknown
	local listening = false
	local button = self:_MiniButton(frame, UDim2.fromOffset(96, 26), UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0))
	local label = win:_Label({ Text = KeyName(key), TextSize = 12, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = button }, "Bold", "Accent")
	element:_SetFlag(key)

	local keyScale = Create("UIScale", { Scale = 1, Parent = label })
	button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		win:_Ripple(button)
		if listening then
			return
		end
		listening = true
		label.Text = "..."
		task.spawn(function()
			while listening and Alive(label) do
				Tween(label, { TextTransparency = 0.65 }, 0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine)
				task.wait(Timed(0.4))
				Tween(label, { TextTransparency = 0 }, 0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine)
				task.wait(Timed(0.4))
			end
			if Alive(label) then
				Tween(label, { TextTransparency = 0 }, 0.15)
			end
		end)
	end)
	element.Maid:Add(win:_OnInputAlways("Began", function(input, processed)
		if listening then
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
				listening = false
				if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
					label.Text = KeyName(key)
					return
				end
				key = input.KeyCode
				label.Text = KeyName(key)
				keyScale.Scale = 1.2
				Tween(keyScale, { Scale = 1 }, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
				element:_SetFlag(key)
				SafeCall(options.Callback, key)
			elseif IsClick(input) then
				local abs, size = button.AbsolutePosition, button.AbsoluteSize
				local p = win:_Pointer()
				if p.X < abs.X or p.X > abs.X + size.X or p.Y < abs.Y or p.Y > abs.Y + size.Y then
					listening = false
					label.Text = KeyName(key)
				end
			end
			return
		end
		if not processed and key ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and input.KeyCode == key then
			-- the key chip bumps whenever its key is pressed
			keyScale.Scale = 1.15
			Tween(keyScale, { Scale = 1 }, 0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
			SafeCall(options.OnPressed, key)
		end
	end))
	if options.OnReleased then
		element.Maid:Add(win:_OnInputAlways("Ended", function(input)
			if key ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and input.KeyCode == key then
				SafeCall(options.OnReleased, key)
			end
		end))
	end

	function element:Set(newKey, silent)
		if typeof(newKey) == "string" then
			local ok, resolved = pcall(function() return Enum.KeyCode[newKey] end)
			newKey = ok and resolved or Enum.KeyCode.Unknown
		end
		key = newKey or Enum.KeyCode.Unknown
		label.Text = KeyName(key)
		self:_SetFlag(key)
		if not silent then SafeCall(options.Callback, key) end
	end
	function element:Get()
		return key
	end
	return element
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- INPUT (text box)
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Options: Title, Description, Default, Placeholder, Numeric, MaxLength, Width, Live, Callback(text, enterPressed), Flag
function Container:AddInput(options)
	options = options or {}
	local win = self.Window
	local width = options.Width or 170
	local element = self:_CreateBase("Input", options, RowHeight(options))
	local frame = element.Frame
	self:_LabelPair(element, options, width)

	local holder = Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("ElementActive"), BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width, 26), ZIndex = 3, Parent = frame,
	})
	win:_Corner(holder, 6)
	win:_Bind(holder, "BackgroundColor3", "ElementActive")
	local stroke = Create("UIStroke", { Color = win:_T("Stroke"), Transparency = 0.3, Parent = holder })
	win:_Bind(stroke, "Color", "Stroke")
	local box = Create("TextBox", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = tostring(options.Default or ""), PlaceholderText = options.Placeholder or "Type here...",
		PlaceholderColor3 = win:_T("Placeholder"), TextColor3 = win:_T("Text"), TextSize = 13, ClearTextOnFocus = false,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, 0),
		TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Parent = holder,
	})
	win:_Font(box, "Medium")
	win:_Bind(box, "TextColor3", "Text")
	win:_Bind(box, "PlaceholderColor3", "Placeholder")
	element:_SetFlag(box.Text)

	-- the field swells slightly while focused and shakes when it refuses more characters
	local holderScale = Create("UIScale", { Scale = 1, Parent = holder })
	local function shake()
		for step, dx in ipairs({ 5, -4, 3, -2, 0 }) do
			task.delay(Timed((step - 1) * 0.04), function()
				if Alive(holder) then
					holder.Position = UDim2.new(1, -14 + dx, 0.5, 0)
				end
			end)
		end
	end
	box.Focused:Connect(function()
		Tween(stroke, { Color = win:_T("Accent"), Transparency = 0 }, 0.2)
		Tween(holderScale, { Scale = 1.03 }, 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
	end)
	box.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
		Tween(stroke, { Color = win:_T("Stroke"), Transparency = 0.3 }, 0.2)
		Tween(holderScale, { Scale = 1 }, 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		element:_SetFlag(box.Text)
		SafeCall(options.Callback, box.Text, enterPressed)
	end)
	box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
		local text = box.Text
		if options.Numeric then
			local cleaned = text:gsub("[^%d%.%-]", "")
			if cleaned ~= text then
				box.Text = cleaned
				return
			end
		end
		if options.MaxLength and #text > options.MaxLength then
			shake()
			box.Text = text:sub(1, options.MaxLength)
			return
		end
		if options.Live then
			element:_SetFlag(text)
			SafeCall(options.Callback, text, false)
		end
	end)

	function element:Set(text, silent)
		box.Text = tostring(text)
		self:_SetFlag(box.Text)
		if not silent then SafeCall(options.Callback, box.Text, false) end
	end
	function element:Get()
		return box.Text
	end
	element.Box = box
	return element
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- SEARCH BOX
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Options: Placeholder, Callback(query), Debounce (seconds), Flag
function Container:AddSearchBox(options)
	options = options or {}
	local win = self.Window
	options.Title = options.Title or "Search"
	options.Filled = true
	local element = self:_CreateBase("SearchBox", options, 38, true)
	local frame = element.Frame
	local stroke = element.Stroke
	local iconHolder = Create("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16), Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0.5, -8), Parent = frame })
	local _, iconParts = MakeSearchIcon(iconHolder, 14, win:_T("SubText"))
	win:_Bind(iconParts[1], "Color", "SubText")
	win:_Bind(iconParts[2], "BackgroundColor3", "SubText")
	local box = Create("TextBox", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", PlaceholderText = options.Placeholder or "Search...", PlaceholderColor3 = win:_T("Placeholder"),
		TextColor3 = win:_T("Text"), TextSize = 13, ClearTextOnFocus = false, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 36, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 1, 0), Parent = frame,
	})
	win:_Font(box, "Medium")
	win:_Bind(box, "TextColor3", "Text")
	win:_Bind(box, "PlaceholderColor3", "Placeholder")
	local clear = Create("TextButton", {
		Text = "×", TextSize = 18, TextColor3 = win:_T("SubText"), BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 24), Visible = false, ZIndex = 3, Parent = frame,
	})
	win:_Font(clear, "Medium")
	win:_Bind(clear, "TextColor3", "SubText")

	local pending = 0
	local function fire()
		element:_SetFlag(box.Text)
		SafeCall(options.Callback, box.Text)
	end
	box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
		clear.Visible = box.Text ~= ""
		local debounce = options.Debounce or 0
		if debounce > 0 then
			pending = pending + 1
			local ticket = pending
			task.delay(debounce, function()
				if ticket == pending then fire() end
			end)
		else
			fire()
		end
	end)
	box.Focused:Connect(function()
		Tween(stroke, { Color = win:_T("Accent"), Transparency = 0 }, 0.2)
	end)
	box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		Tween(stroke, { Color = win:_T("Stroke"), Transparency = 0.2 }, 0.2)
	end)
	clear.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		box.Text = ""
		box:CaptureFocus()
	end)

	function element:Set(text) box.Text = tostring(text) end
	function element:Get() return box.Text end
	function element:Clear() box.Text = "" end
	element.Box = box
	return element
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- DISCORD CARD
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Options: Invite (code or full link), Title, Description, ButtonText, Logo (image id), Callback(link)
function Container:AddDiscord(options)
	options = options or {}
	local win = self.Window
	options.Title = options.Title or "Join our Discord"
	options.Filled = true
	local element = self:_CreateBase("Discord", options, 66, true)
	local frame = element.Frame
	frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(88, 101, 242)
	element.Stroke.Enabled = false
	local shade = frame:FindFirstChild("DepthShade")
	if shade then
		shade:Destroy()
	end
	-- unbind the theme colour: this card keeps Discord's own blurple
	for index = #win._themeBindings, 1, -1 do
		if win._themeBindings[index].Instance == frame then
			table.remove(win._themeBindings, index)
		end
	end
	Create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 20, Color = ColorSequence.new({ Keypoint(0, Color3.new(1, 1, 1)), Keypoint(1, Color3.new(0.82, 0.84, 1)) }), Parent = frame })

	local logoHolder = Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 0.85, BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0.5, -19), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(38, 38), Parent = frame,
	})
	win:_Corner(logoHolder, 11)
	if options.Logo then
		Create("ImageLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = options.Logo, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 22), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Parent = logoHolder })
	else
		local logo = win:_MakeDiscordLogo(logoHolder, 22)
		logo.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		logo.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
	end
	local title = Create("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = options.Title, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 64, 0, 14), Size = UDim2.new(1, -190, 0, 18), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Parent = frame,
	})
	win:_Font(title, "Bold")
	element.TitleLabel = title
	local desc = Create("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = options.Description or "Updates, support and community", TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		TextTransparency = 0.25, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Position = UDim2.new(0, 64, 0, 34), Size = UDim2.new(1, -190, 0, 16),
		TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Parent = frame,
	})
	win:_Font(desc, "Regular")
	local button = Create("TextButton", {
		Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(104, 30), ClipsDescendants = true, ZIndex = 3, Parent = frame,
	})
	win:_Corner(button, 8)
	local buttonLabel = Create("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = options.ButtonText or "Copy invite", TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 80, 200),
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = button,
	})
	win:_Font(buttonLabel, "Bold")
	button.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tween(button, { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 237, 255) }, 0.15) end)
	button.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tween(button, { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1) }, 0.2) end)
	button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		win:_Ripple(button, nil, "Accent")
		win:_Sound("Click")
		local copied = win:CopyDiscordInvite(options.Invite, options.Callback)
		buttonLabel.Text = copied and "Copied!" or "See notification"
		task.delay(1.8, function()
			if Alive(buttonLabel) then
				buttonLabel.Text = options.ButtonText or "Copy invite"
			end
		end)
	end)
	return element
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- CUSTOM ELEMENTS
-- Page:AddCustom({ Title, Height, AutoHeight, Card = true, Hover, Flag, Build = function(frame, element, window) end, OnSet })
-- Aurora:RegisterElement("Name", function(container, options, window) return element end) adds container:AddName(options)
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function Container:AddCustom(options)
	options = options or {}
	local win = self.Window
	options.Title = options.Title or "Custom"
	local element = self:_CreateBase("Custom", options, options.Height or 46, options.Hover ~= true)
	local frame = element.Frame
	if options.Card == false then
		frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		element.Stroke.Enabled = false
		if element.Separator then
			element.Separator.Visible = false
		end
	end
	if options.AutoHeight then
		frame.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
		frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, options.Height or 0)
	end
	element.Content = frame
	if type(options.Build) == "function" then
		local ok, err = pcall(options.Build, frame, element, win)
		if not ok then
			warn("[AuroraUI] AddCustom build failed: " .. tostring(err))
		end
	end
	function element:Set(value, silent)
		self:_SetFlag(value)
		if not silent then
			SafeCall(options.OnSet, value, self)
		end
	end
	function element:Get()
		return self.Value
	end
	return element
end

function Aurora:RegisterElement(name, builder)
	Container["Add" .. name] = function(container, options)
		return builder(container, options or {}, container.Window)
	end
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- DIVIDER
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function Container:AddDivider(options)
	if type(options) ~= "table" then
		options = { Text = options }
	end
	local win = self.Window
	local element = self:_CreateBase("Divider", options, options.Text and 22 or 12, true)
	local frame = element.Frame
	if element.Separator then
		element.Separator.Visible = false
	end
	frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	element.Stroke.Enabled = false
	local textWidth = 0
	if options.Text then
		local label = win:_Label({ Text = tostring(options.Text), TextSize = 11, Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Parent = frame }, "Bold", "SubText")
		element.TitleLabel = label
		textWidth = TextService:GetTextSize(tostring(options.Text), 11, label.Font, Vector2.new(1000, 40)).X + 10
	end
	local line = Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("Stroke"), BackgroundTransparency = 0.3, BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, textWidth, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -textWidth, 0, 1), Parent = frame,
	})
	win:_Bind(line, "BackgroundColor3", "Stroke")
	return element
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- SECTION (collapsible group that has all the Add* methods)
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Options: Title, Collapsible (default true), Open (default true)
function Container:AddSection(options)
	if type(options) ~= "table" then
		options = { Title = tostring(options) }
	end
	local win = self.Window
	local collapsible = options.Collapsible ~= false
	local holder = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Section", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		LayoutOrder = win:_NextOrder(), Parent = self.List,
	})
	local header = Create("TextButton", { Name = "Header", Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30), Parent = holder })
	local titleX = 14
	if options.Icon then
		local iconSpec = win:_ResolveIcon(options.Icon, options.CustomIcon)
		if tostring(iconSpec):find("rbxasset", 1, true) then
			local image = Create("ImageLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = iconSpec, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(13, 13), Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0.5, -6.5), Parent = header })
			win:_Bind(image, "ImageColor3", "SubText")
		else
			win:_Icon(header, iconSpec, 13, "SubText", { Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0.5, -6.5) })
		end
		titleX = 33
	end
	local title = win:_Label({ Text = string.upper(tostring(options.Title or "Section")), TextSize = 10, Position = UDim2.new(0, titleX, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -titleX - 38, 1, 0), Parent = header }, "Bold", "SubText")
	title.TextTransparency = 0.15
	local chevronHolder = nil
	if collapsible then
		chevronHolder = Create("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0.5, 0), Parent = header })
		local _, parts = MakeChevron(chevronHolder, 11, win:_T("SubText"), "Down")
		for _, part in ipairs(parts) do win:_Bind(part, "BackgroundColor3", "SubText") end
	end
	local body = Create("Frame", { Name = "Body", BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("Element"), BackgroundTransparency = 0.35, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 30), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), ClipsDescendants = true, Parent = holder })
	win:_Corner(body, 10)
	win:_Bind(body, "BackgroundColor3", "Element")
	win:_DepthShade(body)
	local bodyStroke = Create("UIStroke", { Color = win:_T("Stroke"), Transparency = 0.7, Parent = body })
	win:_Bind(bodyStroke, "Color", "Stroke")
	local layout = Create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 0), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = body })

	local section = setmetatable({
		Window = win, Page = self.Page, List = body, Frame = holder, Elements = {}, IsOpen = options.Open ~= false, Maid = Maid.new(),
		Title = options.Title,
	}, Section)

	local function targetHeight()
		return section.IsOpen and layout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y or 0
	end
	layout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
		body.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, targetHeight())
	end)
	body.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, targetHeight())
	if chevronHolder then
		chevronHolder.Rotation = section.IsOpen and 0 or -90
	end

	function section:SetOpen(state)
		self.IsOpen = state and true or false
		Tween(body, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, targetHeight()) }, 0.35)
		Tween(bodyStroke, { Transparency = self.IsOpen and 0.7 or 1 }, 0.3)
		if self.IsOpen then
			win:_AnimateEntrance(body, 0.04)
		end
		if chevronHolder then
			Tween(chevronHolder, { Rotation = self.IsOpen and 0 or -90 }, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		end
	end
	function section:Toggle()
		self:SetOpen(not self.IsOpen)
	end
	function section:SetTitle(text)
		title.Text = tostring(text)
	end
	function section:Destroy()
		self.Maid:Clean()
		holder:Destroy()
	end
	if collapsible then
		header.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			win:_Sound("Click")
			section:Toggle()
		end)
		header.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tween(title, { TextColor3 = win:_T("Text") }, 0.15) end)
		header.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tween(title, { TextColor3 = win:_T("SubText") }, 0.2) end)
	end
	self.Sections = self.Sections or {}
	self.Sections[#self.Sections + 1] = section
	return section
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- COLOR PICKER
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Options: Title, Description, Default (Color3), Alpha (bool), DefaultAlpha, Presets {Color3...},
--          Callback(color, alpha), Flag
function Container:AddColorPicker(options)
	options = options or {}
	local win = self.Window
	local baseHeight = RowHeight(options)
	local useAlpha = options.Alpha == true
	local pickerHeight = 122 + 40 + (useAlpha and 0 or 0)
	local element = self:_CreateBase("ColorPicker", options, baseHeight)
	local frame = element.Frame
	self:_LabelPair(element, options, 60)

	local color = options.Default or win:_T("Accent")
	local hue, sat, val = color:ToHSV()
	local alpha = math.clamp(options.DefaultAlpha or 1, 0, 1)
	local open = false

	local preview = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Preview", BackgroundColor3 = color, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, baseHeight / 2), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(46, 22), ZIndex = 3, Parent = frame,
	})
	win:_Corner(preview, 6)
	local previewStroke = Create("UIStroke", { Color = win:_T("Stroke"), Transparency = 0.2, Parent = preview })
	win:_Bind(previewStroke, "Color", "Stroke")
	local header = Create("TextButton", { Text = "", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, baseHeight), ZIndex = 4, Parent = frame })

	local area = Create("Frame", { Name = "Picker", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, baseHeight), Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, pickerHeight), Parent = frame })
	local barsWidth = useAlpha and 48 or 24
	local sv = Create("Frame", { Name = "SV", BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(hue, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, -barsWidth, 0, 122), ZIndex = 2, Parent = area })
	win:_Corner(sv, 6, true)
	local white = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 2, Parent = sv }, {
		Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6) }),
		Create("UIGradient", { Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumKey(0, 0), NumKey(1, 1) }) }),
	})
	local black = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 3, Parent = sv }, {
		Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6) }),
		Create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumKey(0, 1), NumKey(1, 0) }) }),
	})
	local svKnob = Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = color, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12), ZIndex = 5, Parent = sv,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }), Create("UIStroke", { Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Thickness = 2 }) })

	local hueBar = Create("Frame", { Name = "Hue", BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, useAlpha and -24 or 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 122), ZIndex = 2, Parent = area }, {
		Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6) }),
		Create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Color = ColorSequence.new({
			Keypoint(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)), Keypoint(1 / 6, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)), Keypoint(2 / 6, Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)),
			Keypoint(3 / 6, Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255)), Keypoint(4 / 6, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 255)), Keypoint(5 / 6, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 255)),
			Keypoint(1, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)),
		}) }),
	})
	local hueKnob = Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, hue, 0),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 6), ZIndex = 5, Parent = hueBar,
	}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }), Create("UIStroke", { Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.5 }) })

	local alphaBar, alphaKnob, alphaGradient = nil, nil, nil
	if useAlpha then
		alphaBar = Create("Frame", { Name = "Alpha", BackgroundColor3 = color, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 122), ZIndex = 2, Parent = area }, {
			Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6) }),
		})
		alphaGradient = Create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumKey(0, 0), NumKey(1, 1) }), Parent = alphaBar })
		alphaKnob = Create("Frame", {
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1 - alpha, 0),
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 6), ZIndex = 5, Parent = alphaBar,
		}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }), Create("UIStroke", { Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.5 }) })
	end

	-- bottom row: hex input, rgb text, presets
	local hexHolder = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("ElementActive"), BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 132), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(84, 26), Parent = area })
	win:_Corner(hexHolder, 6)
	win:_Bind(hexHolder, "BackgroundColor3", "ElementActive")
	local hexBox = Create("TextBox", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "#" .. color:ToHex(), TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = win:_T("Text"), ClearTextOnFocus = false,
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Parent = hexHolder,
	})
	win:_Font(hexBox, "Code")
	win:_Bind(hexBox, "TextColor3", "Text")
	local rgbLabel = win:_Label({ Text = "", TextSize = 11, Position = UDim2.new(0, 92, 0, 132), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(120, 26), Parent = area }, "Medium", "SubText")
	local presets = options.Presets or {
		Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80), Color3.fromRGB(255, 170, 60), Color3.fromRGB(255, 230, 80), Color3.fromRGB(90, 220, 120),
		Color3.fromRGB(70, 200, 255), Color3.fromRGB(130, 100, 255), Color3.fromRGB(255, 110, 200), Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
	}
	for index, presetColor in ipairs(presets) do
		local swatch = Create("TextButton", {
			Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = presetColor, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
			Position = UDim2.new(1, -(#presets - index) * 22, 0, 136), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18), Parent = area,
		}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5) }) })
		swatch.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			element:Set(presetColor)
		end)
		swatch.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tween(swatch, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(21, 21) }, 0.12) end)
		swatch.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tween(swatch, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18) }, 0.15) end)
	end

	local function apply(silent)
		color = Color3.fromHSV(hue, sat, val)
		sv.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(hue, 1, 1)
		svKnob.Position = UDim2.new(sat, 0, 1 - val, 0)
		svKnob.BackgroundColor3 = color
		hueKnob.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, hue, 0)
		preview.BackgroundColor3 = color
		preview.BackgroundTransparency = useAlpha and (1 - alpha) * 0.7 or 0
		if alphaBar then
			alphaBar.BackgroundColor3 = color
			alphaKnob.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1 - alpha, 0)
		end
		if not hexBox:IsFocused() then
			hexBox.Text = "#" .. color:ToHex()
		end
		rgbLabel.Text = string.format("%d, %d, %d%s", math.floor(color.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(color.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(color.B * 255 + 0.5), useAlpha and string.format("  a %.2f", alpha) or "")
		element:_SetFlag({ Color = color, Alpha = alpha })
		if not silent then
			SafeCall(options.Callback, color, alpha)
		end
	end
	apply(true)
	element.Default = { Color = color, Alpha = alpha }

	local dragTarget = nil
	local function updateFrom(pos)
		if dragTarget == "sv" then
			local abs, size = sv.AbsolutePosition, sv.AbsoluteSize
			sat = math.clamp((pos.X - abs.X) / math.max(1, size.X), 0, 1)
			val = 1 - math.clamp((pos.Y - abs.Y) / math.max(1, size.Y), 0, 1)
		elseif dragTarget == "hue" then
			local abs, size = hueBar.AbsolutePosition, hueBar.AbsoluteSize
			hue = math.clamp((pos.Y - abs.Y) / math.max(1, size.Y), 0, 0.9999)
		elseif dragTarget == "alpha" and alphaBar then
			local abs, size = alphaBar.AbsolutePosition, alphaBar.AbsoluteSize
			alpha = 1 - math.clamp((pos.Y - abs.Y) / math.max(1, size.Y), 0, 1)
		end
		apply(false)
	end
	local function beginDrag(target)
		return function(input)
			if IsClick(input) then
				dragTarget = target
				updateFrom(win:_Pointer())
			end
		end
	end
	sv.InputBegan:Connect(beginDrag("sv"))
	hueBar.InputBegan:Connect(beginDrag("hue"))
	if alphaBar then
		alphaBar.InputBegan:Connect(beginDrag("alpha"))
	end
	element.Maid:Add(win:_OnInput("Changed", function(input)
		if dragTarget and IsMove(input) then
			updateFrom(win:_Pointer())
		end
	end))
	element.Maid:Add(win:_OnInput("Ended", function(input)
		if dragTarget and IsClick(input) then
			dragTarget = nil
		end
	end))
	hexBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		local hex = hexBox.Text:gsub("#", ""):gsub("%s", "")
		if #hex == 6 and hex:match("^%x+$") then
			element:Set(Color3.fromHex(hex))
		else
			hexBox.Text = "#" .. color:ToHex()
		end
	end)

	local function resize()
		Tween(frame, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, open and (baseHeight + pickerHeight + 6) or baseHeight) }, 0.3)
	end
	header.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		win:_Ripple(frame)
		win:_Sound("Click")
		open = not open
		resize()
	end)

	function element:Set(newColor, newAlpha, silent)
		if typeof(newColor) == "table" and newColor.Color then
			newAlpha = newColor.Alpha
			newColor = newColor.Color
		end
		if typeof(newColor) == "Color3" then
			local h, s, v = newColor:ToHSV()
			if s > 0 then hue = h end
			sat, val = s, v
		end
		if type(newAlpha) == "number" then
			alpha = math.clamp(newAlpha, 0, 1)
		end
		apply(silent)
	end
	function element:Get()
		return color, alpha
	end
	function element:Open() open = true; resize() end
	function element:Close() open = false; resize() end
	return element
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- SLIDE BOX (2D pad: X and Y)
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Options: Title, Description, MinX, MaxX, MinY, MaxY, DefaultX, DefaultY, Increment / IncrementX / IncrementY,
--          Decimals, Height, Mode, Callback(x, y), Flag, Crosshair, LabelX, LabelY
function Container:AddSlideBox(options) return self:_Locked("SlideBox", options) end

-- A theme as plain data: { Version, Kind = "theme", Name, Colors = { token = "#hex" }, Code = { token = "#hex" } }.
-- ParseTheme takes that back (or a bare { token = "#hex" } map); colours it does not mention come from Midnight.
-- Five colours that say what a theme looks like, for dropdown rows. Works as a Swatches function directly.
function Aurora.ThemeSwatches(name)
	local theme = Aurora.Themes[tostring(name)]
	if type(theme) ~= "table" then
		return nil
	end
	return { theme.Background, theme.Secondary, theme.Element, theme.Accent, theme.Text }
end

function Aurora:ThemeToTable(theme, name)
	local out = { Version = Aurora.FileVersion, Kind = "theme", Name = name or theme.Name or "Custom", Colors = {}, Code = {} }
	for key, value in pairs(theme) do
		if typeof(value) == "Color3" then
			out.Colors[key] = ColorToHex(value)
		end
	end
	if type(theme.Code) == "table" then
		for key, value in pairs(theme.Code) do
			if typeof(value) == "Color3" then
				out.Code[key] = ColorToHex(value)
			end
		end
	end
	return out
end

function Aurora:EncodeTheme(theme, name)
	if type(theme) ~= "table" then
		return nil
	end
	return Aurora:EncodePreset(Aurora:ThemeToTable(theme, name))
end

function Aurora:ParseTheme(data)
	if type(data) == "string" then
		data = Aurora:ParsePreset(data)
	end
	if type(data) ~= "table" then
		return nil
	end
	local colors = (type(data.Colors) == "table" and data.Colors) or data
	local theme, count = {}, 0
	for key, value in pairs(colors) do
		local color = (type(value) == "string") and HexToColor(value) or nil
		if color then
			theme[key] = color
			count = count + 1
		end
	end
	if count == 0 then
		return nil
	end
	if type(data.Code) == "table" then
		theme.Code = {}
		for key, value in pairs(data.Code) do
			local color = (type(value) == "string") and HexToColor(value) or nil
			if color then
				theme.Code[key] = color
			end
		end
	end
	if type(data.Name) == "string" and data.Name ~= "" then
		theme.Name = data.Name
	end
	return theme
end

function Window:SetTheme(theme)
	self:_LoadTheme(theme)
	self:_ApplyTheme(true)
	if self._refreshThemeEditor then
		self._refreshThemeEditor()
	end
end

function Window:SetAccent(color)
	if typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then
		return
	end
	self._accentOverride = color
	self.Theme.Accent = color
	self:_ApplyTheme(true)
end

function Window:ClearAccent()
	self._accentOverride = nil
	self:_LoadTheme(self.ThemeName)
	self:_ApplyTheme(true)
end

-- Window size that fits the current screen, used on touch devices and after a rotation.
function Window:_ViewportSize()
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	local viewport = camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
	local scale = self.UIScale or 1
	local widthShare = (self.Device == "Phone") and 0.96 or 0.84
	local heightShare = (self.Device == "Phone") and 0.82 or 0.8
	local width = math.clamp(math.floor(viewport.X * widthShare / scale), 300, 1100)
	local height = math.clamp(math.floor(viewport.Y * heightShare / scale), 260, 800)
	return UDim2.fromOffset(width, height)
end

-- "Cinematic" | "Smooth" | "Snappy" | "Reduced motion"
-- The current look as a preset table (JSON-safe, ready for ExportPreset or SavePreset).
function Window:GetPreset(name)
	local carry = self._presetCarry
	local effects = {}
	for key, value in pairs(self.Effects) do
		local kind = type(value)
		local needs = Aurora.EliteEffectKeys[key]
		-- a Free build never writes an effect it cannot do: if it wrote Acrylic = false, the same look
		-- opened in Elite would come back with the glass switched off. Carried holds the real value.
		if (kind == "number" or kind == "string" or kind == "boolean") and (not needs or Aurora:Has(needs)) then
			effects[key] = value
		end
	end
	-- a key that is being carried is left out entirely, so the carried one is the only one in the file
	local background = nil
	if not (carry and carry.Background) then
		background = DeepCopy(self.BackgroundOptions or {})
		background.Name = self.BackgroundName or "Default"
	end
	local custom = nil
	if carry and carry.CustomBackgrounds then
		custom = nil
	elseif Aurora:Has("BackgroundStudio") and next(Aurora.CustomBackgrounds or {}) then
		custom = DeepCopy(Aurora.CustomBackgrounds)
	end
	return {
		Version = Aurora.FileVersion,
		Edition = Aurora.Edition,
		-- everything this build had no code for, exactly as it arrived
		Carried = (carry and next(carry)) and DeepCopy(carry) or nil,
		CustomBackgrounds = custom,
		Name = name or self.PresetName or "Custom",
		Theme = self.ThemeName,
		Accent = ColorToHex(self._accentOverride) or ColorToHex(self.Theme and self.Theme.Accent),
		Font = self.FontFamily,
		Scale = self.UIScale,
		CornerRadius = self.CornerRadius,
		Transparency = self.Transparency,
		Size = { self.Size.X.Offset, self.Size.Y.Offset },
		Perspective = self.SurfaceDistance,
		Motion = self.MotionPreset,
		Optimization = self.Optimization or 1,
		AutoOptimize = self.AutoOptimize and DeepCopy(self.AutoOptimize) or nil,
		AnimationSpeed = Aurora.AnimationSpeed,
		Keybind = (typeof(self.Keybind) == "EnumItem") and self.Keybind.Name or nil,
		Background = background,
		Effects = effects,
		Layout = DeepCopy(self.Layout or Aurora.DefaultLayout),
		-- the colours travel inside the preset when the theme was edited or never saved under a name, so the
		-- look comes back exactly even where that theme does not exist
		ThemeColors = (self._themeDirty or not Aurora.Themes[self.ThemeName]) and Aurora:ThemeToTable(self.Theme, self.ThemeName) or nil,
	}
end

-- What this build would do with a preset, without applying it: the same decisions ApplyPreset makes.
-- Returns { Edition, From, Name, Skipped = { { Key, Needs, Why }, ... } }, or nil if that is not a preset.
function Window:PresetReport(preset)
	if type(preset) == "string" then
		preset = Aurora.Presets[preset] or Aurora:ParsePreset(preset)
	end
	if type(preset) ~= "table" then
		return nil
	end
	preset = Aurora.UnpackCarried(preset)
	local skipped = {}
	local function note(key, feature, why)
		skipped[#skipped + 1] = { Key = key, Needs = feature,
			Why = why or ((Aurora.FeatureNames[feature] or feature) .. " is not in the " .. Aurora.Edition .. " edition") }
	end
	if type(preset.Effects) == "table" then
		for key in pairs(preset.Effects) do
			local needs = Aurora.EliteEffectKeys[key]
			if needs and not Aurora:Has(needs) then
				note("Effects." .. key, needs)
			end
		end
	end
	if type(preset.CustomBackgrounds) == "table" and next(preset.CustomBackgrounds) and not Aurora:Has("BackgroundStudio") then
		note("CustomBackgrounds", "BackgroundStudio")
	end
	local name = (type(preset.Background) == "table") and preset.Background.Name or ((type(preset.Background) == "string") and preset.Background or nil)
	if name and not Aurora.Backgrounds[name] then
		local custom = (type(preset.CustomBackgrounds) == "table") and preset.CustomBackgrounds[name] or nil
		if not (custom and Aurora:Has("BackgroundStudio")) then
			note("Background", "BackgroundPack", "'" .. tostring(name) .. "' is not one of this edition's backgrounds")
		end
	end
	if preset.AutoOptimize ~= nil and not Aurora:Has("AutoOptimize") then
		note("AutoOptimize", "AutoOptimize")
	end
	return { Edition = Aurora.Edition, From = preset.Edition, Name = preset.Name, Skipped = skipped }
end

-- Accepts a registered preset name, a preset table, or a JSON string. Every key is optional.
-- Returns applied, report. The report lists whatever this edition could not use; those values are not
-- dropped, they are carried, and GetPreset writes them back out untouched, so a look can go
-- Elite -> Free -> Elite and arrive as it started.
function Window:ApplyPreset(preset, quiet)
	if type(preset) == "string" then
		preset = Aurora.Presets[preset] or Aurora:ParsePreset(preset)
	end
	if type(preset) ~= "table" then
		warn("[AuroraUI] ApplyPreset: not a preset")
		return false
	end
	preset = Aurora.UnpackCarried(preset)
	local carry, skipped = {}, {}
	local function skip(key, feature, why)
		skipped[#skipped + 1] = { Key = key, Needs = feature,
			Why = why or ((Aurora.FeatureNames[feature] or feature) .. " is not in the " .. Aurora.Edition .. " edition") }
	end
	-- backgrounds someone built in the studio travel with the preset, so they exist before it picks one
	if type(preset.CustomBackgrounds) == "table" and next(preset.CustomBackgrounds) then
		if Aurora:Has("BackgroundStudio") and Aurora.RegisterParametric then
			for customName, spec in pairs(preset.CustomBackgrounds) do
				Aurora:RegisterParametric(customName, spec)
			end
		else
			carry.CustomBackgrounds = DeepCopy(preset.CustomBackgrounds)
			skip("CustomBackgrounds", "BackgroundStudio")
		end
	end
	if type(preset.ThemeColors) == "table" then
		local theme = Aurora:ParseTheme(preset.ThemeColors)
		if theme then
			theme.Name = theme.Name or preset.Theme
			self._accentOverride = nil
			self:SetTheme(theme)
		end
	elseif preset.Theme then
		self._accentOverride = nil
		self:SetTheme(preset.Theme)
	end
	local accent = HexToColor(preset.Accent)
	if accent then
		self:SetAccent(accent)
	end
	if preset.Font or preset.FontFamily then
		self:SetFontFamily(preset.Font or preset.FontFamily)
	end
	if tonumber(preset.Scale) then
		self:SetScale(tonumber(preset.Scale))
	end
	if tonumber(preset.CornerRadius) then
		self:SetCornerRadius(tonumber(preset.CornerRadius))
	end
	if tonumber(preset.Perspective) then
		self.SurfaceDistance = tonumber(preset.Perspective)
	end
	if preset.Motion then
		self:SetMotionPreset(preset.Motion, true)
	end
	if tonumber(preset.Optimization) then
		self:SetOptimization(tonumber(preset.Optimization), true)
	end
	if tonumber(preset.AnimationSpeed) then
		Aurora.AnimationSpeed = tonumber(preset.AnimationSpeed)
	end
	if type(preset.Effects) == "table" then
		for key, value in pairs(preset.Effects) do
			local needs = Aurora.EliteEffectKeys[key]
			if needs and not Aurora:Has(needs) then
				carry.Effects = carry.Effects or {}
				carry.Effects[key] = value
				skip("Effects." .. key, needs)
			else
				self.Effects[key] = value
			end
		end
		self:_ClampEffects()
	end
	if preset.AutoOptimize ~= nil then
		if Aurora:Has("AutoOptimize") then
			local auto = preset.AutoOptimize
			self:SetAutoOptimize(type(auto) == "table" and auto.Enabled ~= false, type(auto) == "table" and auto or nil)
		else
			carry.AutoOptimize = DeepCopy(preset.AutoOptimize)
			skip("AutoOptimize", "AutoOptimize")
		end
	end
	if tonumber(preset.Transparency) then
		self.Transparency = math.clamp(tonumber(preset.Transparency), 0, 0.9)
	end
	local size = preset.Size
	if type(size) == "table" then
		local width = tonumber(size[1]) or tonumber(size.Width) or tonumber(size.X)
		local height = tonumber(size[2]) or tonumber(size.Height) or tonumber(size.Y)
		if width or height then
			self:SetSize(width or self.Size.X.Offset, height or self.Size.Y.Offset, true)
		end
	end
	if preset.Keybind then
		local ok, key = pcall(function()
			return Enum.KeyCode[preset.Keybind]
		end)
		if ok and key then
			self:SetKeybind(key)
		end
	end
	local bgName, bgOptions = nil, nil
	if type(preset.Background) == "string" then
		bgName = preset.Background
	elseif type(preset.Background) == "table" then
		bgOptions = DeepCopy(preset.Background)
		bgName = bgOptions.Name
		bgOptions.Name = nil
	end
	if bgName and not Aurora.Backgrounds[bgName] then
		-- this build has no such scene: keep the whole thing for the edition that does, show the nearest one
		carry.Background = DeepCopy(preset.Background)
		skip("Background", "BackgroundPack", "'" .. tostring(bgName) .. "' is not one of this edition's backgrounds")
		bgName, bgOptions = Aurora.FallbackBackground, nil
	end
	if bgName then
		self.BackgroundOptions = nil
		self:SetBackground(bgName, bgOptions)
	end
	-- Optional element values, for presets that also carry a loadout. A preset applied at startup runs
	-- before the pages are built, so values with nowhere to go yet wait for their element to appear.
	if type(preset.Flags) == "table" then
		self._pendingFlags = self._pendingFlags or {}
		for flag, value in pairs(preset.Flags) do
			local element = self._flagElements[flag]
			if element and type(element.Set) == "function" then
				pcall(element.Set, element, value)
			else
				self._pendingFlags[flag] = value
			end
		end
	end
	if type(preset.Layout) == "table" then
		self:SetLayout(preset.Layout, true)
	end
	self.PresetName = preset.Name
	self._presetCarry = next(carry) and carry or nil
	self:_RefreshAcrylic()   -- re-applies transparency, screen blur, camera effects and the glass
	if self._refreshSettings then
		self._refreshSettings()
	end
	local report = { Edition = Aurora.Edition, From = preset.Edition, Name = preset.Name, Skipped = skipped }
	if #skipped > 0 and not quiet then
		local names = {}
		for _, entry in ipairs(skipped) do
			local label = Aurora.FeatureNames[entry.Needs] or entry.Needs
			if not table.find(names, label) then
				names[#names + 1] = label
			end
		end
		self:Notify({
			Title = "Look applied",
			Content = "Kept for " .. Aurora.EliteName .. ", not applied here: " .. table.concat(names, ", ") ..
				". They travel with this preset and come back when it is opened in " .. Aurora.EliteName .. ".",
			Type = "Info", Duration = 7,
		})
	end
	return true, report
end

function Window:ExportPreset(name)
	return Aurora:EncodePreset(self:GetPreset(name))
end

function Window:ImportPreset(json)
	local preset = (type(json) == "table") and json or Aurora:ParsePreset(json)
	if not preset then
		warn("[AuroraUI] ImportPreset: that is not valid preset JSON")
		return false
	end
	return self:ApplyPreset(preset)
end

function Window:_PresetPath(name)
	return self.ConfigFolder .. "/presets/" .. tostring(name):gsub("[^%w_%- ]", "") .. ".json"
end

function Window:SavePreset(name)
	name = tostring(name or "preset")
	if not self:_EnsureConfigFolder() then
		warn("[AuroraUI] SavePreset: no file system available in this environment")
		return false
	end
	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		local folder = self.ConfigFolder .. "/presets"
		if typeof(isfolder) == "function" and typeof(makefolder) == "function" and not isfolder(folder) then
			makefolder(folder)
		end
		writefile(self:_PresetPath(name), Aurora:EncodePreset(self:GetPreset(name)) or "{}")
	end)
	if not ok then
		warn("[AuroraUI] SavePreset failed: " .. tostring(err))
	end
	return ok
end

function Window:LoadPreset(name)
	if not Aurora.HasFileSystem() then
		return false
	end
	local path = self:_PresetPath(name)
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		if not isfile(path) then
			return nil
		end
		return readfile(path)
	end)
	if ok and type(result) == "string" then
		return self:ImportPreset(result)
	end
	return false
end

function Window:ListPresets()
	local names = {}
	if not Aurora.HasFileSystem() or typeof(listfiles) ~= "function" then
		return names
	end
	pcall(function()
		local folder = self.ConfigFolder .. "/presets"
		if typeof(isfolder) == "function" and not isfolder(folder) then
			return
		end
		for _, path in ipairs(listfiles(folder)) do
			local name = tostring(path):match("([^/\\]+)%.json$")
			if name then
				names[#names + 1] = name
			end
		end
	end)
	table.sort(names)
	return names
end

function Window:DeletePreset(name)
	if not Aurora.HasFileSystem() or typeof(delfile) ~= "function" then
		return false
	end
	return (pcall(function()
		local path = self:_PresetPath(name)
		if isfile(path) then
			delfile(path)
		end
	end))
end

-- Puts the window back exactly as it was created: theme, accent, font, scale, corner radius, transparency,
-- size, position, background, every effect, animation speed, motion preset and the toggle key.
function Window:ResetAppearance()
	if not self._defaults then
		return false
	end
	if self.Minimized then
		self:Minimize(false)
	end
	-- the effects table is shared with the step loop, so it is emptied and refilled rather than replaced
	for key in pairs(self.Effects) do
		self.Effects[key] = nil
	end
	for key, value in pairs(DefaultEffects) do
		self.Effects[key] = value
	end
	self:ApplyPreset(self._defaults)
	self:ResetPosition()
	if self.SearchBox then
		self.SearchBox.Text = ""
	end
	if self.Drag then
		self.Drag.Enable()
	end
	return true
end

function Window:SetMotionPreset(name, quiet)
	local preset = Aurora.MotionPresets[name]
	if not preset then
		warn("[AuroraUI] Unknown motion preset '" .. tostring(name) .. "'")
		return
	end
	self.MotionPreset = name
	for key, value in pairs(preset) do
		if key == "AnimationSpeed" then
			Aurora.AnimationSpeed = value
		else
			self.Effects[key] = value
		end
	end
	if self.Main then
		self:_SetBlur(self.IsOpen)
		self:_SetCameraEffects(self.IsOpen)
	end
	if not quiet and self._refreshEffectControls then
		self._refreshEffectControls()
	end
end

function Window:SetScale(scale)
	self.UIScale = math.clamp(tonumber(scale) or 1, 0.4, 2)
	if not self._loop and not self.Render3D then
		self._scaleObj.Scale = self.UIScale
	end
	if self.Render3D then
		self:_SetScreenOffset(self._screenOffset)
	end
end

function Window:SetCornerRadius(radius)
	radius = math.max(0, tonumber(radius) or 10)
	self.CornerRadius = radius
	self.CornerScale = radius / 10
	local kept = {}
	for _, entry in ipairs(self._corners) do
		if entry.Corner.Parent ~= nil then
			kept[#kept + 1] = entry
			entry.Corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, math.floor(entry.Base * self.CornerScale + 0.5))
		end
	end
	self._corners = kept
end

function Window:SetTransparency(amount)
	self.Transparency = math.clamp(tonumber(amount) or 0, 0, 0.9)
	self:_ApplyTransparency()
end

-- The user's transparency, raised to the Acrylic tint while the glass is showing so the blur can be seen.
function Window:_ApplyTransparency()
	if not self.Main or not self.Sidebar then
		return
	end
	local amount = self.Transparency or 0
	if self:_AcrylicOn() then
		amount = math.max(amount, math.clamp(tonumber(self.Effects.AcrylicTint) or 0.22, 0, 0.9))
	end
	self.Main.BackgroundTransparency = amount
	self.Sidebar.BackgroundTransparency = amount > 0 and math.min(0.92, 0.12 + amount * 0.8) or 0
	self.Main:SetAttribute("AuroraBG", self.Main.BackgroundTransparency)
	self.Sidebar:SetAttribute("AuroraBG", self.Sidebar.BackgroundTransparency)
end

-- Accepts a family name from Aurora.FontFamilies, or a font asset family such as
-- "rbxasset://fonts/families/Inter.json" / "rbxassetid://..." (applied through FontFace).
function Window:SetFontFamily(name)
	local family = Aurora.FontFamilies[name]
	local isAsset = type(name) == "string" and (name:find("rbxasset", 1, true) ~= nil)
	if not family and not isAsset then
		warn("[AuroraUI] Unknown font family '" .. tostring(name) .. "'")
		return
	end
	self.FontFamily = name
	if family then
		self.Fonts = BuildFonts(name)
		self.FontAsset = nil
	else
		self.FontAsset = name
	end
	local weights = { Regular = Enum.FontWeight.Regular, Medium = Enum.FontWeight.Medium, Bold = Enum.FontWeight.Bold }
	local kept = {}
	for _, entry in ipairs(self._fontObjects) do
		if entry.Object.Parent ~= nil then
			kept[#kept + 1] = entry
			if self.FontAsset and entry.Role ~= "Code" then
				local ok = pcall(function()
					entry.Object.FontFace = Font.new(self.FontAsset, weights[entry.Role] or Enum.FontWeight.Regular, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
				end)
				if not ok then
					entry.Object.Font = self.Fonts[entry.Role] or self.Fonts.Medium
				end
			else
				entry.Object.Font = self.Fonts[entry.Role] or self.Fonts.Medium
			end
		end
	end
	self._fontObjects = kept
	if self.Layout and self.Brand then
		self:_ApplyBrandLayout()   -- the title measures differently in a new font
	end
end

-- How hard each logic runtime is working, for the debug overlay and the Logic page.
function Window:LogicLoad()
	local perSecond, running = 0, 0
	for _, element in ipairs(self._logicElements or {}) do
		local runtime = element.Runtime
		if runtime and runtime.Running then
			running = running + 1
			perSecond = perSecond + (runtime._lastRate or 0)
		end
	end
	return { PerSecond = perSecond, Running = running }
end

-- What a page costs to draw: measured while it is in front, with a first guess from what is on it
-- so a page that has never been opened can still be judged.
function Window:PageCost(page)
	if page._cost then
		return page._cost * 1000
	end
	local guess = 0.05
	for _, element in ipairs(page.Elements or {}) do
		local kind = element.Type
		if kind == "LogicEditor" then
			guess = guess + 0.55
		elseif kind == "CodeBox" then
			guess = guess + 0.4
		elseif kind == "Automations" or kind == "Gradient" or kind == "Table" or kind == "Console" then
			guess = guess + 0.2
		else
			guess = guess + 0.02
		end
	end
	for _, section in ipairs(page.Sections or {}) do
		guess = guess + 0.01 * #(section.Elements or {})
	end
	return guess
end

-- Pages the library made (Settings, Presets, Logic) are never put away; only ones the script added.
local function TrimmablePage(window, page)
	return page ~= window.SettingsPage and page ~= window.PresetsPage and page ~= window.LogicPage
		and not page.IsSettings and page.NeverTrim ~= true
end

-- Hides the pages costing more than `budget` milliseconds a frame, worst first, and brings back any
-- that now fit. Called whenever the level changes and every few seconds while trimming is on.
function Window:_TrimPages(budget)
	self._trimmed = self._trimmed or {}
	if not budget then
		for page in pairs(self._trimmed) do
			if page.Hidden then
				page:SetVisible(true)
			end
		end
		self._trimmed = {}
		return 0
	end
	local hidden = 0
	for _, page in ipairs(self.Pages) do
		if TrimmablePage(self, page) then
			local cost = self:PageCost(page)
			if cost > budget and not page.Hidden then
				page:SetVisible(false)
				self._trimmed[page] = cost
				hidden = hidden + 1
			elseif self._trimmed[page] and cost <= budget and page.Hidden then
				page:SetVisible(true)
				self._trimmed[page] = nil
			end
		end
	end
	return hidden
end

-- Puts every trimmed page back and leaves them alone until the level changes again.
function Window:RestoreTrimmedPages()
	local count = 0
	for page in pairs(self._trimmed or {}) do
		if page.Hidden then
			page:SetVisible(true)
			count = count + 1
		end
	end
	self._trimmed = {}
	self._trimPaused = true
	return count
end

function Window:SetAutoOptimize() return false end
function Window:_StepAutoOptimize() end

function Window:TrimReport() return {} end
function Window:PinPage() return false end

-- 1 = exactly what the author set up, 10 = everything off and the heavy pages put away.
-- fromAuto marks a change the automatic optimizer made, so it does not read as somebody choosing by hand.
function Window:SetOptimization(level, quiet, fromAuto)
	if not fromAuto then
		self._optimizeTouchedAt = os.clock()
	end
	level = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(level) or 1), 1, #Aurora.OptimizationLevels)
	local spec = Aurora.OptimizationLevels[level]
	if not self._perfBase then
		-- the window as the script asked for it, so level 1 is a real restore and not a guess
		self._perfBase = { Effects = DeepCopy(self.Effects), Background = self.BackgroundName, BackgroundOptions = DeepCopy(self.BackgroundOptions or {}) }
	end
	local base = self._perfBase
	self.Optimization = level
	self._stepSkip = spec.StepSkip or 0
	for key, value in pairs(base.Effects) do
		self.Effects[key] = value
	end
	for key, value in pairs(spec.Effects or {}) do
		self.Effects[key] = value
	end
	local wantBackground = spec.Background or base.Background or "Default"
	if self.BackgroundName ~= wantBackground then
		if spec.Background then
			self:SetBackground(spec.Background)
		else
			self:SetBackground(base.Background or "Default", DeepCopy(base.BackgroundOptions))
		end
	end
	self._trimPaused = false
	self:_TrimPages(spec.PageBudget)
	self:_RefreshAcrylic()
	if self._refreshSettings then
		self._refreshSettings()
	end
	if not quiet then
		local hidden = 0
		for _ in pairs(self._trimmed or {}) do
			hidden = hidden + 1
		end
		self:Notify({
			Title = "Optimization " .. level .. "  ·  " .. spec.Label,
			Content = spec.Note .. ((hidden > 0) and ("\n" .. hidden .. ((hidden == 1) and " page put away" or " pages put away")) or ""),
			Duration = 3,
		})
	end
	return level
end

function Window:GetOptimization()
	return self.Optimization or 1
end

function Window:SetEffect(name, enabled)
	self.Effects[name] = enabled
	if name == "Blur" then
		self:_SetBlur(self.IsOpen)
	elseif name == "Acrylic" then
		self:_RefreshAcrylic()
	elseif name == "DepthOfField" or name == "WorldDim" or name == "CameraZoom" then
		self:_SetCameraEffects(self.IsOpen)
	end
end

-- Resizes the window; in 3D mode the SurfaceGui canvas grows with it so nothing is clipped.
function Window:SetSize(width, height, animate)
	width, height = math.floor(tonumber(width) or 640), math.floor(tonumber(height) or 460)
	self.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width, height)
	if self.Render3D and self.Surface then
		local margin = self.CanvasMargin
		self.CanvasSize = Vector2.new(width + margin * 2, height + margin * 2)
		self.Surface.CanvasSize = self.CanvasSize
		for _, surface in ipairs(self._surfaces) do
			if surface.Part == self.SurfacePart then
				surface.Canvas = self.CanvasSize
			end
		end
	end
	if self.Minimized then
		return
	end
	if animate then
		Tween(self.Root, { Size = self.Size }, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
	else
		self.Root.Size = self.Size
	end
	if self.Layout and self.Sidebar then
		self:_ApplyLayout(false)   -- the sidebar width cap and the brand's room depend on the window width
	end
end

-- Logo inside the brand mark: built-in icon name, image id, or nil for the title's first letter.
-- Pass tint = false to keep an image's own colours.
function Window:SetLogo(icon, tint, customIcon)
	local mark = self.BrandMark
	if not mark then
		return
	end
	icon = self:_ResolveIcon(icon, customIcon)
	for _, child in ipairs(mark:GetChildren()) do
		if child.Name == "Logo" then
			child:Destroy()
		end
	end
	local logo
	if icon == nil or icon == "" then
		logo = self:_Label({
			Name = "Logo", Text = string.upper(string.sub(self.Title, 1, 1)), TextSize = 15, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = mark,
		}, "Bold", "AccentText")
	elseif tostring(icon):find("rbxasset", 1, true) then
		logo = Create("ImageLabel", { Name = "Logo", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = icon, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Parent = mark })
		if tint ~= false then
			self:_Bind(logo, "ImageColor3", "AccentText")
		end
	else
		logo = self:_Icon(mark, icon, 18, "AccentText", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5) })
		logo.Name = "Logo"
	end
	local pop = Create("UIScale", { Scale = 0.6, Parent = logo })
	Tween(pop, { Scale = self:_LogoScale() }, 0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
end

function Window:SetSubtitle(text)
	self.Subtitle = text
	if self.SubtitleLabel then
		self.SubtitleLabel.Text = tostring(text or "")
		self.SubtitleLabel.Visible = self.SubtitleLabel.Text ~= "" and (not self.Layout or self.Layout.Subtitle ~= false)
		if self.Layout and self.Brand then
			self:_ApplyBrandLayout()
		end
	end
end

function Window:SetFooter(text)
	if self.FooterLabel then
		self.FooterLabel.Text = tostring(text or "")
	end
end

-- Link-style button under the pages: { Text, Icon, Callback, Tooltip, LayoutOrder }
function Window:AddSidebarButton(options)
	options = options or {}
	self._sidebarButtonCount = (self._sidebarButtonCount or 0) + 1
	if self._sidebarButtonCount == 1 and options.Divider ~= false then
		local divider = Create("Frame", { Name = "SidebarDivider", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 11), LayoutOrder = 89999, Parent = self.PageList })
		local line = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = self:_T("Stroke"), BackgroundTransparency = 0.4, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 1), Parent = divider })
		self:_Bind(line, "BackgroundColor3", "Stroke")
	end
	local button = Create("TextButton", {
		Name = options.Text or "Button", Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = self:_T("ElementHover"), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32), ClipsDescendants = true, LayoutOrder = options.LayoutOrder or (90000 + self._sidebarButtonCount), Parent = self.PageList,
	})
	self:_Corner(button, 8)
	self:_Bind(button, "BackgroundColor3", "ElementHover")
	local textX = 12
	local icon, iconIsHolder = nil, false
	if options.Icon then
		local iconSpec = self:_ResolveIcon(options.Icon, options.CustomIcon)
		if tostring(iconSpec):find("rbxasset", 1, true) then
			icon = Create("ImageLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = iconSpec, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, -8), Parent = button })
			self:_Bind(icon, "ImageColor3", "SubText")
		else
			icon = self:_Icon(button, iconSpec, 16, "SubText", { Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, -8) })
			iconIsHolder = true
		end
		textX = 34
	end
