	local label = self:_Label({ Text = options.Text or "Button", TextSize = 13, Position = UDim2.new(0, textX, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -textX - 8, 1, 0), Parent = button }, "Medium", "SubText")
	local function highlight(on, time)
		Tween(button, { BackgroundTransparency = on and 0.5 or 1 }, time)
		Tween(label, { TextColor3 = self:_T(on and "Text" or "SubText"), Position = UDim2.new(0, textX + (on and 3 or 0), 0, 0) }, time, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		if icon then
			if iconIsHolder then self:_IconColor(icon, on and "Text" or "SubText", time) else Tween(icon, { ImageColor3 = self:_T(on and "Text" or "SubText") }, time) end
		end
	end
	button.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if self.Effects.Hover then highlight(true, 0.15) end end)
	button.MouseLeave:Connect(function() highlight(false, 0.2) end)
	button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		self:_Ripple(button)
		self:_Sound("Click")
		SafeCall(options.Callback)
	end)
	if options.Tooltip then
		self:_Tooltip(button, options.Tooltip)
	end
	return {
		Button = button, Label = label,
		SetText = function(_, text) label.Text = tostring(text) end,
		Destroy = function() button:Destroy() end,
	}
end

function Window:SetTitle(text)
	self.Title = tostring(text)
	self.TitleLabel.Text = self.Title
	if self.Layout and self.Brand then
		self:_ApplyBrandLayout()   -- in the main bar the brand is sized to the title
	end
end

-- ============================================================================
-- SETTINGS PAGE (optional)
-- ============================================================================
local function PrettyName(name)
	return (name:gsub("(%l)(%u)", "%1 %2"))
end

-- Everything to do with presets, used by the Presets page.
function Window:_BuildPresetUI(container)
	local function presetNames()
		local names, seen = Aurora:GetPresetNames(), {}
		for _, name in ipairs(names) do
			seen[name] = true
		end
		for _, saved in ipairs(self:ListPresets()) do
			if not seen[saved] then
				names[#names + 1] = saved
			end
		end
		return names
	end

	local applySection = container:AddSection({ Title = "Apply a preset" })
	local presetDropdown = applySection:AddDropdown({
		Title = "Preset", Description = "Built in, plus any you saved", Options = presetNames(),
		Default = self.PresetName, Placeholder = "Pick a preset",
	})
	applySection:AddButton({
		Title = "Apply", Description = "Changes the look straight away", ButtonText = "Apply", Variant = "Primary",
		Callback = function()
			local name = presetDropdown:Get()
			if not name then
				self:Notify({ Title = "No preset picked", Content = "Choose one from the list first.", Type = "Warning" })
				return
			end
			local applied = Aurora.Presets[name] and self:ApplyPreset(name) or self:LoadPreset(name)
			self:Notify({
				Title = applied and "Preset applied" or "Could not apply that preset", Content = name,
				Type = applied and "Success" or "Error", Duration = 3,
			})
		end,
	})
	applySection:AddButton({
		Title = "Back to how it started", Description = "The look this script set up", ButtonText = "Reset", Callback = function()
			self:ResetAppearance()
			self:Notify({ Title = "Reset", Content = "Everything is back to how the window started.", Type = "Success", Duration = 3 })
		end,
	})

	local makeSection = container:AddSection({ Title = "Make your own" })
	local nameInput = makeSection:AddInput({ Title = "Preset name", Placeholder = "My look", Default = "" })
	local function currentName()
		local name = nameInput:Get()
		return (name ~= "" and name) or presetDropdown:Get() or "My preset"
	end
	makeSection:AddText({
		Title = "Where presets go",
		Text = Aurora.HasFileSystem()
			and ("Saved as files in <b>" .. self.ConfigFolder .. "/presets</b>.")
			or "This environment cannot write files, so saving opens a box with the code to copy and keep.",
	})
	makeSection:AddButton({
		Title = "Save the current look", Description = "As a file, or as code you can copy", ButtonText = "Save", Variant = "Primary",
		Callback = function()
			local name = currentName()
			if Aurora.HasFileSystem() and self:SavePreset(name) then
				presetDropdown:Refresh(presetNames(), true)
				self:Notify({ Title = "Preset saved", Content = name, Type = "Success", Duration = 3 })
				return
			end
			-- nowhere to write it: hand the code to the player instead
			self:ShowCodePopup({
				Title = "Your preset",
				Content = "There is no file system here, so keep this code somewhere safe. Paste it back under \"Load from code\" to use it again.",
				Code = self:ExportPreset(name) or "", ReadOnly = true, CopyText = "Copy",
			})
		end,
	})
	makeSection:AddButton({
		Title = "Copy as code", Description = "The current look as JSON", ButtonText = "Copy", Callback = function()
			local json = self:ExportPreset(currentName()) or ""
			local copied = false
			pcall(function()
				if typeof(setclipboard) == "function" then
					setclipboard(json)
					copied = true
				end
			end)
			self:ShowCodePopup({
				Title = "Preset code",
				Content = copied and "This is on your clipboard already." or "Press Copy, or select it and copy by hand.",
				Code = json, ReadOnly = true,
			})
		end,
	})
	makeSection:AddButton({
		Title = "Load from code", Description = "Paste a preset someone sent you", ButtonText = "Paste", Callback = function()
			self:ShowCodePopup({
				Title = "Paste a preset",
				Content = "Paste the JSON below and press Apply.",
				Code = "", Placeholder = "{ \"Theme\": \"Midnight\", ... }", SubmitText = "Apply",
				OnSubmit = function(text)
					local applied = self:ImportPreset(text)
					self:Notify({
						Title = applied and "Preset applied" or "That code did not read as a preset",
						Content = applied and (self.PresetName or "Imported") or "Check it for typos.",
						Type = applied and "Success" or "Error", Duration = 3,
					})
				end,
			})
		end,
	})
	local shareSection = container:AddSection({ Title = "Share the whole setup" })
	shareSection:AddText({
		Title = "Everything in one code",
		Text = "The look, every graph and rule, and every flagged value together. Handy for sending someone your exact setup.",
	})
	shareSection:AddButton({
		Title = "Copy everything as code", Description = "Look + logic + values", ButtonText = "Copy", Variant = "Primary",
		Callback = function()
			local json = self:ExportEverything() or "{}"
			local copied = false
			pcall(function()
				if typeof(setclipboard) == "function" then
					setclipboard(json)
					copied = true
				end
			end)
			self:ShowCodePopup({
				Title = "Everything",
				Content = copied and "This is on your clipboard already." or "Press Copy, or select it and copy by hand.",
				Code = json, ReadOnly = true,
			})
		end,
	})
	shareSection:AddButton({
		Title = "Load everything from code", Description = "Applies the look, the logic and the values", ButtonText = "Paste",
		Callback = function()
			self:ShowCodePopup({
				Title = "Paste a whole setup",
				Content = "Paste a code made by \"Copy everything as code\" and press Load.",
				Code = "", Placeholder = "{ \"Preset\": { ... }, \"Logic\": { ... }, \"Flags\": { ... } }", SubmitText = "Load",
				OnSubmit = function(text)
					local ok, took = self:ImportEverything(text)
					self:Notify({
						Title = ok and "Setup loaded" or "That code did not read as a setup",
						Content = ok and ((took.Preset and "look" or "no look") .. ", " .. took.Logic .. " logic, " .. took.Flags .. " values") or "Check it for typos.",
						Type = ok and "Success" or "Error", Duration = 4,
					})
				end,
			})
		end,
	})

	if Aurora.HasFileSystem() then
		makeSection:AddButton({
			Title = "Delete a saved preset", Description = "The one named above", ButtonText = "Delete", Variant = "Danger", Confirm = true,
			Callback = function()
				local name = currentName()
				local removed = self:DeletePreset(name)
				if removed then
					presetDropdown:Refresh(presetNames(), true)
				end
				self:Notify({
					Title = removed and "Preset deleted" or "Nothing deleted", Content = name,
					Type = removed and "Success" or "Warning", Duration = 3,
				})
			end,
		})
	end
	return container
end

-- A page of its own, so presets are not buried inside Settings.
function Window:AddPresetsPage(options)
	if self.PresetsPage then
		return self.PresetsPage
	end
	options = options or {}
	local page = self:AddPage({
		Name = options.Name or "Presets", Icon = options.Icon or "layers", CustomIcon = options.CustomIcon,
		Tooltip = options.Tooltip or "Looks you can apply, save and share", LayoutOrder = options.LayoutOrder or 99999,
	})
	self.PresetsPage = page
	self:_BuildPresetUI(page)
	return page
end

function Window:AddBackgroundStudioPage(options)
	if self.BackgroundStudioPage then return self.BackgroundStudioPage end
	local page = self:AddPage({ Name = (type(options) == "table" and options.Name) or "Backgrounds", Icon = "visuals" })
	page:_Locked("BackgroundStudio", { Title = "Background studio",
		Description = "Build a scene from shapes, movement and colour, then save and share it" })
	self.BackgroundStudioPage = page
	return page
end

function Window:AddSettingsPage(options)
	if self.SettingsPage then
		return self.SettingsPage
	end
	options = options or {}
	local optimizationNote
	local page = self:AddPage({ Name = options.Name or "Settings", Icon = options.Icon, CustomIcon = options.CustomIcon, _settings = true })
	self.SettingsPage = page

	local appearance = page:AddSection({ Title = "Appearance" })
	local themeDropdown = appearance:AddDropdown({
		Title = "Theme", Description = "Colour palette for the whole window", Options = Aurora:GetThemeNames(),
		Swatches = Aurora.ThemeSwatches,
		Default = self.ThemeName, Callback = function(name)
			if name then
				self._accentOverride = nil
				self:SetTheme(name)
			end
		end,
	})
	self._refreshThemeList = function()
		themeDropdown:Refresh(Aurora:GetThemeNames(), true)
	end
	local accentPicker = appearance:AddColorPicker({
		Title = "Accent colour", Description = "Overrides the theme accent", Default = self.Theme.Accent,
		Callback = function(color)
			self:SetAccent(color)
		end,
	})
	local families = {}
	for name in pairs(Aurora.FontFamilies) do families[#families + 1] = name end
	table.sort(families)
	local fontSelection = appearance:AddSelection({
		Title = "Font family", Options = families, Default = self.FontFamily, Callback = function(name)
			self:SetFontFamily(name)
		end,
	})
	local scaleSlider = appearance:AddSlider({
		Title = "UI scale", Min = 0.6, Max = 1.4, Default = self.UIScale, Increment = 0.05, Decimals = 2, Mode = "Smooth",
		Callback = function(value) self:SetScale(value) end,
	})
	local cornerSlider = appearance:AddSlider({
		Title = "Corner radius", Min = 0, Max = 20, Default = self.CornerRadius, Increment = 1,
		Callback = function(value) self:SetCornerRadius(value) end,
	})
	local transparencySlider = appearance:AddSlider({
		Title = "Window transparency", Min = 0, Max = 0.6, Default = self.Transparency, Increment = 0.05, Decimals = 2,
		Callback = function(value) self:SetTransparency(value) end,
	})
	local widthSlider = appearance:AddSlider({
		Title = "Window width", Min = 480, Max = 1100, Default = self.Size.X.Offset, Increment = 10,
		Callback = function(value) self:SetSize(value, self.Size.Y.Offset, true) end,
	})
	local heightSlider = appearance:AddSlider({
		Title = "Window height", Min = 340, Max = 800, Default = self.Size.Y.Offset, Increment = 10,
		Callback = function(value) self:SetSize(self.Size.X.Offset, value, true) end,
	})

	local layoutSection = page:AddSection({ Title = "Layout" })
	local arrangementOptions = {}
	for index, name in ipairs(Aurora.LayoutPresetOrder) do arrangementOptions[index] = name end
	arrangementOptions[#arrangementOptions + 1] = "Custom"
	local arrangement = layoutSection:AddDropdown({
		Title = "Arrangement", Description = "A named layout to start from; every control below still works on top of it",
		Options = arrangementOptions, Default = self:MatchLayoutPreset() or "Custom",
		Callback = function(name)
			if name and name ~= "Custom" and Aurora.LayoutPresets[name] then
				self:ApplyLayoutPreset(name, true)
				if self._refreshSettings then
					self._refreshSettings()
				end
			end
		end,
	})
	local sidebarSide = layoutSection:AddSelection({
		Title = "Sidebar side", Options = { "Left", "Right" }, Default = self.Layout.SidebarSide,
		Callback = function(value) self:SetLayout({ SidebarSide = value }) end,
	})
	local sidebarWidthSlider = layoutSection:AddSlider({
		Title = "Sidebar width", Description = "Never more than 45% of the window, so the pages keep their room",
		Min = Aurora.LayoutLimits.SidebarWidth[1], Max = Aurora.LayoutLimits.SidebarWidth[2],
		Default = self.Layout.SidebarWidth, Increment = 2, Mode = "Clamped",
		Callback = function(value) self:SetLayout({ SidebarWidth = value }, false) end,
	})
	self._sidebarWidthSlider = sidebarWidthSlider
	local sidebarHeightSlider = layoutSection:AddSlider({
		Title = "Sidebar height", Description = "Percent of the window; a shorter sidebar floats", Suffix = "%",
		Min = Aurora.LayoutLimits.SidebarHeight[1], Max = Aurora.LayoutLimits.SidebarHeight[2],
		Default = self.Layout.SidebarHeight, Increment = 5, Mode = "Clamped",
		Callback = function(value) self:SetLayout({ SidebarHeight = value }, false) end,
	})
	local sidebarAlign = layoutSection:AddSelection({
		Title = "Sidebar position", Description = "Where a shorter sidebar sits", Options = { "Top", "Middle", "Bottom" },
		Default = self.Layout.SidebarAlign, Callback = function(value) self:SetLayout({ SidebarAlign = value }) end,
	})
	local headerSide = layoutSection:AddSelection({
		Title = "Main bar", Description = "The bar with the page title and the window buttons", Options = { "Top", "Bottom" },
		Default = self.Layout.HeaderSide, Callback = function(value) self:SetLayout({ HeaderSide = value }) end,
	})
	local headerHeightSlider = layoutSection:AddSlider({
		Title = "Main bar height", Min = Aurora.LayoutLimits.HeaderHeight[1], Max = Aurora.LayoutLimits.HeaderHeight[2],
		Default = self.Layout.HeaderHeight, Increment = 2, Mode = "Clamped",
		Callback = function(value) self:SetLayout({ HeaderHeight = value }, false) end,
	})
	local brandPlace = layoutSection:AddSelection({
		Title = "Title and logo", Description = "Top of the sidebar, in the main bar, or not shown", Options = { "Sidebar", "Header", "Hidden" },
		Default = self.Layout.Brand, Callback = function(value) self:SetLayout({ Brand = value }) end,
	})
	local logoSlider = layoutSection:AddSlider({
		Title = "Logo size", Min = Aurora.LayoutLimits.LogoSize[1], Max = Aurora.LayoutLimits.LogoSize[2],
		Default = self.Layout.LogoSize, Increment = 1, Mode = "Clamped",
		Callback = function(value) self:SetLayout({ LogoSize = value }, false) end,
	})
	local titleSlider = layoutSection:AddSlider({
		Title = "Title size", Min = Aurora.LayoutLimits.TitleSize[1], Max = Aurora.LayoutLimits.TitleSize[2],
		Default = self.Layout.TitleSize, Increment = 1, Mode = "Clamped",
		Callback = function(value) self:SetLayout({ TitleSize = value }, false) end,
	})
	local offsetXSlider = layoutSection:AddSlider({
		Title = "Title offset X", Description = "Nudge the title and logo sideways from where the placement puts them",
		Min = Aurora.LayoutLimits.BrandOffsetX[1], Max = Aurora.LayoutLimits.BrandOffsetX[2],
		Default = self.Layout.BrandOffsetX or 0, Increment = 1, Mode = "Clamped",
		Callback = function(value) self:SetLayout({ BrandOffsetX = value }, false) end,
	})
	local offsetYSlider = layoutSection:AddSlider({
		Title = "Title offset Y", Description = "Nudge them up or down",
		Min = Aurora.LayoutLimits.BrandOffsetY[1], Max = Aurora.LayoutLimits.BrandOffsetY[2],
		Default = self.Layout.BrandOffsetY or 0, Increment = 1, Mode = "Clamped",
		Callback = function(value) self:SetLayout({ BrandOffsetY = value }, false) end,
	})
	local subtitleToggle = layoutSection:AddToggle({
		Title = "Show subtitle", Default = self.Layout.Subtitle ~= false,
		Callback = function(state) self:SetLayout({ Subtitle = state }, false) end,
	})
	local creditToggle = layoutSection:AddToggle({
		Title = "Show credit chip", Description = "The Made By chip under the title; it only fits in the sidebar", Default = self.Layout.Credit ~= false,
		Callback = function(state) self:SetLayout({ Credit = state }, false) end,
	})
	layoutSection:AddButton({
		Title = "Reset layout", Description = "Back to the default arrangement", ButtonText = "Reset",
		Callback = function()
			self:ResetLayout(true)
			if self._refreshSettings then
				self._refreshSettings()
			end
		end,
	})

	local backgroundSection = page:AddSection({ Title = "Background" })
	local bgDropdown = nil
	self._refreshBackgroundList = function()
		if bgDropdown then
			bgDropdown:Refresh(Aurora.BackgroundOrder, true)
		end
	end
	bgDropdown = backgroundSection:AddDropdown({
		Title = "Background", Description = "Animated scene behind the window content", Options = Aurora.BackgroundOrder,
		Default = self.BackgroundName or "Default", Flag = "__AuroraBackground",
		Callback = function(name) if name then self:SetBackground(name) end end,
	})
	local bgIntensity = backgroundSection:AddSlider({
		Title = "Intensity", Min = 0.1, Max = 2, Default = (self.BackgroundOptions and self.BackgroundOptions.Intensity) or 1, Increment = 0.05, Decimals = 2,
		Flag = "__AuroraBackgroundIntensity", Callback = function(value) self:SetBackgroundOptions({ Intensity = value }) end,
	})
	local bgSpeed = backgroundSection:AddSlider({
		Title = "Speed", Min = 0.1, Max = 3, Default = (self.BackgroundOptions and self.BackgroundOptions.Speed) or 1, Increment = 0.05, Decimals = 2,
		Flag = "__AuroraBackgroundSpeed", Callback = function(value) self:SetBackgroundOptions({ Speed = value }) end,
	})
	local bgColour = backgroundSection:AddSelection({
		Title = "Colour", Options = { "Text", "Accent", "SubText" }, Default = (self.BackgroundOptions and type(self.BackgroundOptions.Color) == "string" and self.BackgroundOptions.Color) or "Text",
		Flag = "__AuroraBackgroundColor", Callback = function(value) self:SetBackgroundOptions({ Color = value }) end,
	})

	local performance = page:AddSection({ Title = "Performance" })
	local optimizationSlider = performance:AddSlider({
		Title = "Optimization", Description = "1 keeps every effect. 10 turns them all off and puts heavy pages away.",
		Min = 1, Max = 10, Default = self:GetOptimization(), Increment = 1, Mode = "Clamped",
		Callback = function(value)
			self:SetOptimization(value)
			if optimizationNote then
				local spec = Aurora.OptimizationLevels[self:GetOptimization()]
				optimizationNote:SetText("<b>" .. spec.Label .. "</b>  ·  " .. spec.Note)
			end
		end,
	})
	optimizationNote = performance:AddText({
		Title = "What this level does",
		Text = "<b>" .. Aurora.OptimizationLevels[self:GetOptimization()].Label .. "</b>  ·  " .. Aurora.OptimizationLevels[self:GetOptimization()].Note,
	})
	performance:AddButton({
		Title = "Bring back hidden pages", Description = "Levels 8 and up put the heaviest pages away", ButtonText = "Restore",
		Callback = function()
			local count = self:RestoreTrimmedPages()
			self:Notify({
				Title = (count > 0) and "Pages restored" or "Nothing was hidden",
				Content = (count > 0) and (count .. ((count == 1) and " page is back" or " pages are back")) or "No page has been put away.",
				Type = (count > 0) and "Success" or nil, Duration = 3,
			})
		end,
	})

	local effects = page:AddSection({ Title = "Effects" })
	local effectToggles = {}
	for _, effectName in ipairs(Aurora.EffectList) do
		local needs = Aurora.EliteEffectKeys[effectName]
		if not needs or Aurora:Has(needs) then
			effectToggles[effectName] = effects:AddToggle({
				Title = PrettyName(effectName), Default = self.Effects[effectName] == true,
				Callback = function(state) self:SetEffect(effectName, state) end,
			})
		end
	end
	local blurSlider = effects:AddSlider({
		Title = "Blur amount", Min = 0, Max = 40, Default = self.Effects.BlurSize or 8, Increment = 1,
		Callback = function(value)
			self.Effects.BlurSize = value
			self:_SetBlur(self.IsOpen)
		end,
	})
	local speedSlider = effects:AddSlider({
		Title = "Animation speed", Description = "Multiplier for every animation", Min = 0.25, Max = 3, Default = Aurora.AnimationSpeed,
		Increment = 0.05, Decimals = 2, Callback = function(value) Aurora.AnimationSpeed = value end,
	})
	local openSlider = effects:AddSlider({
		Title = "Open / close length", Description = "How long the window takes to fly in and out",
		Min = 0.4, Max = 3, Default = self.Effects.OpenDuration or 1, Increment = 0.1, Decimals = 1,
		Callback = function(value) self.Effects.OpenDuration = value end,
	})

	-- one pick that sets speed, open length, tilt and depth together (and updates the controls above)
	local motionSelection
	local keybindElement, dragToggle
	-- Every control on this page reads its value back from the window, so a motion preset, a loaded
	-- profile or Reset appearance leaves nothing showing a stale number.
	self._refreshSettings = function()
		themeDropdown:Set(self.ThemeName, true)
		accentPicker:Set(self.Theme.Accent, nil, true)
		fontSelection:Set(self.FontFamily, true)
		scaleSlider:Set(self.UIScale, true)
		cornerSlider:Set(self.CornerRadius, true)
		transparencySlider:Set(self.Transparency, true)
		widthSlider:Set(self.Size.X.Offset, true)
		heightSlider:Set(self.Size.Y.Offset, true)
		local layout = self.Layout or Aurora.DefaultLayout
		arrangement:Set(self:MatchLayoutPreset() or "Custom", true)
		offsetXSlider:Set(layout.BrandOffsetX or 0, true)
		offsetYSlider:Set(layout.BrandOffsetY or 0, true)
		sidebarSide:Set(layout.SidebarSide, true)
		sidebarWidthSlider:Set(layout.SidebarWidth, true)
		sidebarHeightSlider:Set(layout.SidebarHeight or 100, true)
		sidebarAlign:Set(layout.SidebarAlign or "Top", true)
		headerSide:Set(layout.HeaderSide, true)
		headerHeightSlider:Set(layout.HeaderHeight, true)
		brandPlace:Set(layout.Brand, true)
		logoSlider:Set(layout.LogoSize, true)
		titleSlider:Set(layout.TitleSize, true)
		subtitleToggle:Set(layout.Subtitle ~= false, true)
		creditToggle:Set(layout.Credit ~= false, true)
		local background = self.BackgroundOptions or {}
		bgDropdown:Set(self.BackgroundName or "Default", true)
		bgIntensity:Set(background.Intensity or 1, true)
		bgSpeed:Set(background.Speed or 1, true)
		bgColour:Set((type(background.Color) == "string") and background.Color or "Text", true)
		blurSlider:Set(self.Effects.BlurSize or 8, true)
		speedSlider:Set(Aurora.AnimationSpeed, true)
		openSlider:Set(self.Effects.OpenDuration or 1, true)
		optimizationSlider:Set(self:GetOptimization(), true)
		if optimizationNote then
			local spec = Aurora.OptimizationLevels[self:GetOptimization()]
			optimizationNote:SetText("<b>" .. spec.Label .. "</b>  ·  " .. spec.Note)
		end
		for effectName, toggle in pairs(effectToggles) do
			toggle:Set(self.Effects[effectName] == true, true)
		end
		if motionSelection then
			motionSelection:Set(self.MotionPreset or "Smooth", true)
		end
		if keybindElement then
			keybindElement:Set(self.Keybind, true)
		end
		if dragToggle then
			dragToggle:Set(self.Drag ~= nil, true)
		end
	end
	self._refreshEffectControls = self._refreshSettings
	motionSelection = effects:AddSelection({
		Title = "Motion preset", Description = "Sets speed, open length, tilt and depth in one go",
		Options = Aurora.MotionPresetOrder, Default = self.MotionPreset or "Smooth", Width = 130,
		Callback = function(name)
			self:SetMotionPreset(name)
			self._refreshEffectControls()
		end,
	})

	local controls = page:AddSection({ Title = "Controls" })
	keybindElement = controls:AddKeybind({
		Title = "Toggle window key", Default = self.Keybind, Callback = function(key) self:SetKeybind(key) end,
	})
	dragToggle = controls:AddToggle({
		Title = "Draggable window", Default = self.Drag ~= nil, Callback = function(state)
			if self.Drag then
				if state then self.Drag.Enable() else self.Drag.Disable() end
			end
		end,
	})
	controls:AddButton({
		Title = "Reset window position", ButtonText = "Reset", Callback = function()
			self:ResetPosition()
		end,
	})
	controls:AddButton({
		Title = "Reset appearance", Description = "Theme, accent, font, size, position, background, effects and keybind",
		ButtonText = "Reset", Variant = "Danger", Confirm = true,
		Callback = function()
			self:ResetAppearance()
			self:Notify({ Title = "Settings reset", Content = "Everything is back to how the window started.", Type = "Success" })
		end,
	})
	local presetSection = page:AddSection({ Title = "Presets" })
	presetSection:AddButton({
		Title = "Presets", Description = "Apply, save, copy and import whole looks", ButtonText = "Open", Variant = "Primary",
		Callback = function()
			self:SelectPage(self.PresetsPage or self:AddPresetsPage())
		end,
	})

	-- profiles (only where a file system exists)
	if Aurora.HasFileSystem() then
		local profiles = page:AddSection({ Title = "Profiles" })
		local nameInput = profiles:AddInput({ Title = "Profile name", Placeholder = "default", Default = "default" })
		local profileList = profiles:AddDropdown({ Title = "Saved profiles", Options = self:ListConfigs(), Placeholder = "None yet", Callback = function(name)
			if name then nameInput:Set(name, true) end
		end })
		profiles:AddButton({ Title = "Save profile", ButtonText = "Save", Variant = "Primary", Callback = function()
			local name = nameInput:Get()
			if name == "" then name = "default" end
			if self:SaveConfig(name) then
				self:Notify({ Title = "Profile saved", Content = name, Type = "Success", Duration = 2.5 })
				profileList:Refresh(self:ListConfigs(), true)
			end
		end })
		profiles:AddButton({ Title = "Load profile", ButtonText = "Load", Callback = function()
			local name = nameInput:Get()
			if not self:LoadConfigFile(name) then
				self:Notify({ Title = "Profile not found", Content = name, Type = "Warning" })
			end
		end })
		profiles:AddButton({ Title = "Delete profile", ButtonText = "Delete", Variant = "Danger", Confirm = true, Callback = function()
			self:DeleteConfig(nameInput:Get())
			profileList:Refresh(self:ListConfigs())
		end })
		profiles:AddToggle({ Title = "Auto save", Description = "Saves every change to the autosave profile", Default = self.AutoSave, Callback = function(state)
			self:SetAutoSave(state)
		end })
	end

	local extras = page:AddSection({ Title = "More" })
	local packNames = {}
	for name in pairs(Aurora.SoundPacks) do packNames[#packNames + 1] = name end
	table.sort(packNames)
	extras:AddSelection({ Title = "Sound pack", Options = packNames, Default = self.SoundPack or "Default", Callback = function(name)
		self:SetSoundPack(name)
	end })
	local localeNames = {}
	for code in pairs(Aurora.Locales) do localeNames[#localeNames + 1] = code end
	if #localeNames > 0 then
		table.sort(localeNames)
		table.insert(localeNames, 1, "Default")
		extras:AddSelection({ Title = "Language", Options = localeNames, Default = self.Language or "Default", Callback = function(code)
			self:SetLanguage(code ~= "Default" and code or nil)
		end })
	end
	extras:_Locked("BackgroundStudio", { Title = "Background studio", Description = "Build a scene from shapes, movement and colour, then save and share it" })
	extras:_Locked("ThemeEditor", { Title = "Theme editor", Description = "Edit every colour live, save themes and share them as code" })
	extras:AddToggle({ Title = "Debug overlay", Default = self.Debug == true, Callback = function(state)
		self:SetDebug(state)
	end })
	if self.KeySystem then
		extras:AddButton({ Title = "Forget saved key", ButtonText = "Forget", Callback = function()
			self:ClearSavedKey()
			self:Notify({ Title = "Saved key removed", Type = "Info" })
		end })
	end

	page:AddText({ Title = "About", Text = "Aurora UI v" .. Aurora.Version .. "  ·  Tilt, parallax, ripples, themes and a code editor in one library." })
	return page
end

-- ============================================================================
-- CONFIG (flags)
-- ============================================================================
function Window:GetConfig()
	local out = {}
	for flag, element in pairs(self._flagElements) do
		local value = self.Flags[flag]
		local kind = typeof(value)
		if kind == "Color3" then
			out[flag] = { __type = "Color3", R = value.R, G = value.G, B = value.B }
		elseif kind == "EnumItem" then
			out[flag] = { __type = "KeyCode", Name = value.Name }
		elseif kind == "table" then
			if typeof(value.Color) == "Color3" then
				out[flag] = { __type = "ColorAlpha", R = value.Color.R, G = value.Color.G, B = value.Color.B, A = value.Alpha or 1 }
			else
				out[flag] = { __type = "List", Items = DeepCopy(value) }
			end
		elseif kind == "number" or kind == "string" or kind == "boolean" then
			out[flag] = value
		end
	end
	return out
end

function Window:LoadConfig(config)
	if type(config) ~= "table" then
		return
	end
	for flag, value in pairs(config) do
		local element = self._flagElements[flag]
		if element and type(element.Set) == "function" then
			local decoded = value
			if type(value) == "table" then
				if value.__type == "Color3" then
					decoded = Color3.new(value.R, value.G, value.B)
				elseif value.__type == "ColorAlpha" then
					decoded = { Color = Color3.new(value.R, value.G, value.B), Alpha = value.A }
				elseif value.__type == "KeyCode" then
					local ok, key = pcall(function() return Enum.KeyCode[value.Name] end)
					decoded = ok and key or Enum.KeyCode.Unknown
				elseif value.__type == "List" then
					decoded = value.Items
				end
			end
			local ok, err = pcall(element.Set, element, decoded)
			if not ok then
				warn("[AuroraUI] Failed to load flag '" .. tostring(flag) .. "': " .. tostring(err))
			end
		end
	end
end

-- ============================================================================
-- WINDOW: misc
-- ============================================================================
-- Everything about this window in one blob: how it looks, the logic in it, and every flagged value.
function Window:GetEverything()
	return {
		Version = Aurora.FileVersion, Kind = "everything", Saved = os.time(),
		Preset = self:GetPreset(self.PresetName or "Shared"),
		Logic = self:GetLogic(),
		Flags = self:GetConfig(),
	}
end

function Window:ExportEverything()
	return Aurora:EncodePreset(self:GetEverything())
end

-- Takes whatever it recognises and reports what it took: ok, { Preset, Logic, Flags }.
function Window:ImportEverything(data)
	if type(data) == "string" then
		data = Aurora:ParsePreset(data)
	end
	if type(data) ~= "table" then
		warn("[AuroraUI] ImportEverything: that is not valid code")
		return false, { Preset = false, Logic = 0, Flags = 0 }
	end
	local took = { Preset = false, Logic = 0, Flags = 0 }
	if type(data.Preset) == "table" then
		took.Preset = self:ApplyPreset(data.Preset) and true or false
	end
	if type(data.Logic) == "table" then
		local _, count = self:ImportLogic(data.Logic)
		took.Logic = count or 0
	end
	if type(data.Flags) == "table" then
		self:LoadConfig(data.Flags)
		for _ in pairs(data.Flags) do
			took.Flags = took.Flags + 1
		end
	end
	return (took.Preset or took.Logic > 0 or took.Flags > 0), took
end

function Window:MakeDraggable(object, options)
	return Aurora:MakeDraggable(object, options)
end

function Window:Destroy()
	for index = #(self._glassSpecs or {}), 1, -1 do
		local spec = self._glassSpecs[index]
		if spec.Entry then
			GlassDestroy(spec.Entry)
			spec.Entry = nil
		end
	end
	GlassReleaseDoF()
	self.IsOpen = false
	self:_StopLoop()
	if self.Loader then
		self.Loader:Destroy()
	end
	if self.Drag then
		self.Drag.Destroy()
	end
	for _, element in ipairs(self.Elements) do
		element.Maid:Clean()
	end
	for _, panel in ipairs(self.Panels) do
		pcall(panel.Destroy, panel)
	end
	if self._zoomActive and workspace.CurrentCamera then
		workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = self._fovBase or workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView
	end
	for _, spec in ipairs(self._glassSpecs) do
		GlassDestroy(spec.Entry)
		spec.Entry = nil
	end
	self.Maid:Clean()
	if self.BlurEffect then
		self.BlurEffect:Destroy()
	end
end

-- ============================================================================
-- CREATE WINDOW
-- ============================================================================
function Aurora:CreateWindow(options)
	options = options or {}
	local self = setmetatable({}, Window)
	self.Library = Aurora
	self.Maid = Maid.new()
	self.Pages = {}
	self.Elements = {}
	self.Flags = {}
	self._flagElements = {}
	self._logicElements = {}
	self._themeBindings = {}
	self._themeCallbacks = {}
	self._corners = {}
	self._fontObjects = {}
	self._parallaxLayers = {}
	self._stepHooks = {}
	self._tilt = Vector2.new()
	self._hoverLift = 0
	self._screenOffset = Vector2.new()
	self._surfaces = {}
	self._motionLocks = {}
	self._glassSpecs = {}
	self._inputHooks = { Began = {}, Changed = {}, Ended = {} }
	self._depthShades = {}
	self._view = Vector2.new()
	self._localized = {}
	self.Panels = {}
	self.Language = options.Language
	self.Device = options.Device or DetectDevice()
	self.Touch = self.Device ~= "Desktop"
	self.Mobile = options.Mobile
	if self.Mobile == nil then
		-- phones get the drawer sidebar; tablets keep the full layout, just bigger and touch-friendly
		self.Mobile = self.Device == "Phone"
	end
	self.ConfigFolder = options.ConfigFolder or ("AuroraUI/" .. tostring(options.Title or "Aurora"):gsub("[^%w_%- ]", ""))
	-- where the sidebar, the main bar and the title sit; Settings > Layout changes it live and presets carry it
	local layout = Merge(Aurora.DefaultLayout, options.Layout)
	if options.SidebarWidth then
		layout.SidebarWidth = options.SidebarWidth
	end
	self.Layout = ValidLayout(layout)
	-- themes saved from the theme editor come back under their names, so a preset can point at one
	self:_LoadSavedThemes()
	self:_LoadSavedBackgrounds()
	self.AutoSave = options.AutoSave == true
	self.AutoSaveName = options.AutoSaveName or "autosave"
	self.KeySystem = options.KeySystem
	self.SoundPack = options.SoundPack
	self.Render3D = options.Render ~= "2D"
	self.SurfaceDistance = options.SurfaceDistance or 8
	self.CanvasMargin = 48
	self._layoutOrder = 0
	self.IsOpen = false
	self.Minimized = false
	self.Effects = Merge(DefaultEffects, options.Effects)
	if self.Touch then
		-- touch has no cursor, so hover-only affordances go; phones also drop the heaviest effects
		local given = options.Effects or {}
		local function touchDefault(key, value)
			if given[key] == nil then
				self.Effects[key] = value
			end
		end
		touchDefault("Hover", false)
		touchDefault("Spotlight", false)
		touchDefault("EdgeGlow", false)
		touchDefault("Magnet", false)
		if self.Device == "Phone" then
			touchDefault("Acrylic", false)
			touchDefault("Particles", false)
			touchDefault("Glow", false)
			touchDefault("DepthOfField", false)
		end
	end
	self.Optimization = 1
	if options.Motion then
		self:SetMotionPreset(options.Motion, true)
	end
	self.Sounds = Merge(Aurora.DefaultSounds, options.Sounds)
	if options.SoundPack and Aurora.SoundPacks and Aurora.SoundPacks[options.SoundPack] then
		self.Sounds = Merge(self.Sounds, Aurora.SoundPacks[options.SoundPack])
	end
	self.CornerRadius = options.CornerRadius or 10
	self.CornerScale = self.CornerRadius / 10
	self.FontFamily = options.FontFamily or "Gotham"
	self.Fonts = BuildFonts(self.FontFamily)
	self.Keybind = options.Keybind or Enum.KeyCode.RightControl
	self.Title = options.Title or "Aurora"
	self.Subtitle = options.Subtitle
	self.GuiParent = options.Parent or GetGuiParent()
	-- touch targets need to be bigger, so the whole window is scaled up a little
	self.UIScale = self.Touch and (self.Device == "Phone" and 1.15 or 1.06) or 1
	self.Transparency = 0
	if type(options.AnimationSpeed) == "number" then
		Aurora.AnimationSpeed = options.AnimationSpeed
	end

	self._accentOverride = options.Accent
	self:_LoadTheme(options.Theme or "Void")

	local size = options.Size or UDim2.fromOffset(640, 460)
	if typeof(size) == "Vector2" then
		size = UDim2.fromOffset(size.X, size.Y)
	end
	if self.Touch and options.Size == nil then
		self._autoFit = true
		size = self:_ViewportSize()
	end
	if self.Render3D and (size.X.Scale ~= 0 or size.Y.Scale ~= 0) then
		-- the 3D canvas needs pixel sizes; resolve scale against the current viewport
		local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		local viewport = camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
		size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(size.X.Scale * viewport.X + size.X.Offset), math.floor(size.Y.Scale * viewport.Y + size.Y.Offset))
	end
	self.Size = size

	self:_BuildGui(options)
	self:_BindInput()
	self:_RefreshAcrylic()
	if self._autoFit then
		-- phones and tablets rotate: refit the window when the viewport changes
		local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if camera then
			self.Maid:Add(camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(function()
				self._fitTicket = (self._fitTicket or 0) + 1
				local ticket = self._fitTicket
				task.delay(0.25, function()
					if ticket == self._fitTicket and self._autoFit then
						local fit = self:_ViewportSize()
						self:SetSize(fit.X.Offset, fit.Y.Offset, true)
					end
				end)
			end))
		end
	end
	local background = options.Background
	if type(background) == "string" then
		self:SetBackground(background)
	elseif type(background) == "table" then
		self:SetBackground(background.Name or "Default", background)
	else
		self:SetBackground("Default")
	end
	self:_OnTheme(function()
		if self._backgroundReady and self.BackgroundName and self.BackgroundName ~= "Default" and self.BackgroundName ~= "None" then
			self:SetBackground(self.BackgroundName)
		end
	end)
	self._backgroundReady = true
	-- Pages are judged on what they actually cost, which is only known once they have been opened,
	-- so the trimming is revisited every few seconds while a level that trims is set.
	self:_AddStepHook(function()
		local spec = Aurora.OptimizationLevels[self.Optimization or 1]
		if not spec or not spec.PageBudget or self._trimPaused then
			return
		end
		if os.clock() < (self._trimAt or 0) then
			return
		end
		self._trimAt = os.clock() + 4
		self:_TrimPages(spec.PageBudget)
	end)
	if self.CornerScale ~= 1 then
		self:SetCornerRadius(self.CornerRadius)
	end
	if options.Settings then
		self._wantSettings = true
	end
	-- Presets get their own page next to Settings; Presets = false leaves it out, Presets = true adds it on its own
	if options.Presets ~= false and (options.Settings or options.Presets) then
		self._wantPresets = true
	end
	-- The Logic page appears by itself the moment a graph or an automations card is added.
	-- Logic = false leaves it out for good, Logic = true adds it straight away even when empty.
	self._logicPageWanted = options.Logic ~= false
	if options.Logic == true then
		self._wantLogic = true
	end

	if options.Preset then
		self:ApplyPreset(options.Preset)
	end
	if tonumber(options.Optimization) then
		self:SetOptimization(tonumber(options.Optimization), true)
	end
	-- Snapshot of how this window ended up after its options and preset, so "Reset appearance" can put
	-- every part of it back (the library defaults are not the same thing: the author's settings win).
	self._defaults = self:GetPreset(self.PresetName or "Window defaults")

	local loading = options.Loading
	if loading ~= false and (loading == nil or loading.Enabled ~= false) then
		if type(loading) ~= "table" then
			loading = {}
		end
		self.Loader = Aurora:_CreateLoader(self, loading)
		self.Loader.OnFinish = function()
			self.Loader = nil
			self:_Boot()
		end
	else
		task.defer(function()
			self:_Boot()
		end)
	end
	return self
end


-- ============================================================================
-- CONFIG PROFILES ON DISK  (writefile / readfile environments; no-ops in plain Studio)
-- ============================================================================
local function HasFileSystem()
	return typeof(writefile) == "function" and typeof(readfile) == "function" and typeof(isfile) == "function"
end
Aurora.HasFileSystem = HasFileSystem

function Window:_ConfigPath(name)
	return self.ConfigFolder .. "/" .. tostring(name):gsub("[^%w_%- ]", "") .. ".json"
end

-- Themes as files: <ConfigFolder>/themes/<name>.json, the same JSON "Copy as code" in the theme editor gives you.
function Window:SaveBackground() return false end
function Window:ListBackgrounds() return {} end
function Window:DeleteBackground() return false end
function Window:_LoadSavedBackgrounds() return 0 end

-- Free build: themes are not kept as a library here. A look still arrives whole through a preset
-- (ApplyPreset reads its ThemeColors), and the built-in themes are all present.
function Window:_ThemePath(name) return self.ConfigFolder .. "/themes/" .. tostring(name) .. ".json" end
function Window:SaveTheme() return false, false end
function Window:ListThemes() return {} end
function Window:LoadTheme(name)
	if Aurora.Themes[tostring(name)] then
		self._accentOverride = nil
		self:SetTheme(name)
		return true
	end
	return false, "nofs"
end
function Window:DeleteTheme() return false end
function Window:_LoadSavedThemes() return 0 end

function Window:_EnsureConfigFolder()
	if not HasFileSystem() then
		return false
	end
	pcall(function()
		if typeof(isfolder) == "function" and typeof(makefolder) == "function" then
			local parts = {}
			for part in self.ConfigFolder:gmatch("[^/]+") do
				parts[#parts + 1] = part
				local path = table.concat(parts, "/")
				if not isfolder(path) then
					makefolder(path)
				end
			end
		end
	end)
	return true
end

function Window:SaveConfig(name)
	if not self:_EnsureConfigFolder() then
		warn("[AuroraUI] SaveConfig: no file system available in this environment")
		return false
	end
	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		local payload = { Version = Aurora.FileVersion, Kind = "config", Saved = os.time(), Flags = self:GetConfig() }
		writefile(self:_ConfigPath(name or "default"), game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode(payload))
	end)
	if not ok then
		warn("[AuroraUI] SaveConfig failed: " .. tostring(err))
	end
	return ok
end

function Window:LoadConfigFile(name, silent)
	if not HasFileSystem() then
		return false
	end
	local path = self:_ConfigPath(name or "default")
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		if not isfile(path) then
			return nil
		end
		return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(readfile(path))
	end)
	if ok and type(result) == "table" then
		-- files written before versioning were a bare map of flags; newer ones wrap them
		self:LoadConfig((type(result.Flags) == "table" and result.Version) and result.Flags or result)
		if not silent then
			self:Notify({ Title = "Profile loaded", Content = tostring(name), Type = "Success", Duration = 2.5 })
		end
		return true
	end
	if not ok then
		warn("[AuroraUI] LoadConfigFile failed: " .. tostring(result))
	end
	return false
end

function Window:DeleteConfig(name)
	if not HasFileSystem() or typeof(delfile) ~= "function" then
		return false
	end
	local path = self:_ConfigPath(name)
	local ok = pcall(function()
		if isfile(path) then
			delfile(path)
		end
	end)
	return ok
end

function Window:ListConfigs()
	local names = {}
	if not HasFileSystem() or typeof(listfiles) ~= "function" then
		return names
	end
	self:_EnsureConfigFolder()
	pcall(function()
		for _, path in ipairs(listfiles(self.ConfigFolder)) do
			local name = tostring(path):match("([^/\\]+)%.json$")
			if name then
				names[#names + 1] = name
			end
		end
	end)
	table.sort(names)
	return names
end

function Window:SetAutoSave(enabled, name)
	self.AutoSave = enabled and true or false
	if name then
		self.AutoSaveName = name
	end
end

function Window:_ScheduleAutoSave()
	self._autoSaveTicket = (self._autoSaveTicket or 0) + 1
	local ticket = self._autoSaveTicket
	task.delay(1, function()
		if ticket == self._autoSaveTicket and self.AutoSave then
			self:SaveConfig(self.AutoSaveName)
		end
	end)
end

-- ============================================================================
-- SOUND PACKS
-- ============================================================================
Aurora.SoundPacks = {
	Default = Aurora.DefaultSounds,
	Silent = { Click = "", Hover = "", Toggle = "", Notify = "" },
}

function Window:SetSoundPack(name)
	local pack = Aurora.SoundPacks[name]
	if not pack then
		return
	end
	self.SoundPack = name
	self.Sounds = Merge(Aurora.DefaultSounds, pack)
end

-- ============================================================================
-- STYLED BUTTON (shared by dialogs and the key gate)
-- Variant: "Primary" (solid accent) | "Secondary" (outlined) | "Ghost" (text only) | "Danger"
-- ============================================================================
function Window:_StyledButton(parent, spec)
	spec = spec or {}
	local variant = spec.Variant or "Secondary"
	if variant == "Default" then variant = "Secondary" end
	-- visual recipe per variant: { fill token, fill transparency, text token, stroke token, stroke transparency }
	local recipes = {
		Primary   = { fill = "Text",    fillT = 0,   text = "Background", stroke = "Text",   strokeT = 1 },
		Accent    = { fill = "Accent",  fillT = 0,   text = "AccentText", stroke = "Accent", strokeT = 1 },
		Secondary = { fill = "Element", fillT = 0,   text = "Text",       stroke = "Stroke", strokeT = 0.25 },
		Ghost     = { fill = "Element", fillT = 1,   text = "Text",       stroke = "Stroke", strokeT = 1 },
		Danger    = { fill = "Error",   fillT = 1,   text = "Error",      stroke = "Error",  strokeT = 0.45 },
	}
	local recipe = recipes[variant] or recipes.Secondary
	local hoverRecipe = ({
		Primary   = { fillT = 0,    strokeT = 1,    lighten = 0.12 },
		Accent    = { fillT = 0,    strokeT = 1,    lighten = 0.1 },
		Secondary = { fillT = 0,    strokeT = 0,    hoverFill = "ElementHover" },
		Ghost     = { fillT = 0.5,  strokeT = 1,    hoverFill = "ElementHover" },
		Danger    = { fillT = 0,    strokeT = 0,    text = "AccentText" },
	})[variant] or {}

	-- Width is measured from the label instead of AutomaticSize, so nothing inside the button
	-- (ripples, press scaling, icon swaps) can ever change how wide it is.
	local padX = spec.PaddingX or 14
	local textSize = spec.TextSize or 13
	local iconSize = spec.IconSize or 14
	local function measure(text)
		local font = self.Fonts[spec.Font or "Medium"] or self.Fonts.Medium
		local width = TextService:GetTextSize(tostring(text), textSize, font, Vector2.new(2000, 100)).X
		if spec.Icon then
			width = width + iconSize + ((tostring(text) ~= "") and 8 or 0)
		end
		return math.ceil(width + padX * 2)
	end
	local button = Create("TextButton", {
		Name = spec.Name or variant, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = self:_T(recipe.fill),
		BackgroundTransparency = recipe.fillT, BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = spec.Size or UDim2.fromOffset(measure(spec.Text or "Button"), spec.Height or 34),
		ClipsDescendants = true, LayoutOrder = spec.LayoutOrder or 0, ZIndex = spec.ZIndex or 1, Parent = parent,
	})
	self:_Corner(button, spec.Radius or 8)
	local stroke = Create("UIStroke", { Color = self:_T(recipe.stroke), Transparency = recipe.strokeT, Parent = button })
	local scale = Create("UIScale", { Scale = 1, Parent = button })
	local row = Create("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = (spec.ZIndex or 1) + 1, Parent = button })
	Create("UIListLayout", { FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center, VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center, Padding = UDim.new(0, 8), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = row })
	local icon = nil
	local iconIsHolder = false
	if spec.Icon then
		local iconSpec = self:_ResolveIcon(spec.Icon, spec.CustomIcon)
		if tostring(iconSpec):find("rbxasset", 1, true) then
			icon = Create("ImageLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = iconSpec, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(iconSize, iconSize), ImageColor3 = self:_T(recipe.text), LayoutOrder = 1, ZIndex = (spec.ZIndex or 1) + 2, Parent = row })
		else
			icon = self:_Icon(row, iconSpec, iconSize, recipe.text, { LayoutOrder = 1, ZIndex = (spec.ZIndex or 1) + 2 })
			iconIsHolder = true
		end
	end
	local function iconColor(token, color, time)
		if not icon then
			return
		end
		if iconIsHolder then
			self:_IconColor(icon, token, time)
		else
			Tween(icon, { ImageColor3 = color }, time or 0)
		end
	end
	local label = Create("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = spec.Text or "Button", TextSize = spec.TextSize or 13, TextColor3 = self:_T(recipe.text),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, LayoutOrder = 2,
		ZIndex = (spec.ZIndex or 1) + 2, Parent = row,
	})
	self:_Font(label, spec.Font or "Medium")
	self:_OnTheme(function()
		button.BackgroundColor3 = self:_T(recipe.fill)
		stroke.Color = self:_T(recipe.stroke)
		label.TextColor3 = self:_T(recipe.text)
		iconColor(recipe.text, self:_T(recipe.text), 0)
	end)

	local state = { Button = button, Label = label, Stroke = stroke, Scale = scale, Enabled = true, Variant = variant }
	local function restColors()
		return self:_T(recipe.fill), self:_T(recipe.text)
	end
	button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		if not state.Enabled or not self.Effects.Hover then return end
		local fill, text = restColors()
		if hoverRecipe.lighten then
			fill = fill:Lerp((variant == "Primary") and self:_T("Background") or Color3.new(1, 1, 1), hoverRecipe.lighten)
		elseif hoverRecipe.hoverFill then
			fill = self:_T(hoverRecipe.hoverFill)
		end
		if hoverRecipe.text then text = self:_T(hoverRecipe.text) end
		Tween(button, { BackgroundColor3 = fill, BackgroundTransparency = hoverRecipe.fillT or recipe.fillT }, 0.14, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		Tween(stroke, { Transparency = hoverRecipe.strokeT or recipe.strokeT }, 0.14)
		Tween(label, { TextColor3 = text }, 0.14)
		iconColor(hoverRecipe.text or recipe.text, text, 0.14)
		self:_Sound("Hover")
	end)
	button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		local fill, text = restColors()
		Tween(button, { BackgroundColor3 = fill, BackgroundTransparency = recipe.fillT }, 0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		Tween(stroke, { Transparency = recipe.strokeT }, 0.18)
		Tween(label, { TextColor3 = text }, 0.18)
		iconColor(recipe.text, text, 0.18)
		Tween(scale, { Scale = 1 }, 0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
	end)
	button.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
		if state.Enabled then Tween(scale, { Scale = 0.97 }, 0.08, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad) end
	end)
	button.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function()
		Tween(scale, { Scale = 1 }, 0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
	end)
	button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if not state.Enabled then return end
		if spec.Ripple ~= false then
			self:_Ripple(button, nil, (variant == "Primary") and "Background" or ((variant == "Accent" or variant == "Danger") and "AccentText" or "Text"))
		end
		self:_Sound("Click")
		SafeCall(spec.Callback, state)
	end)
	function state:SetEnabled(enabled)
		self.Enabled = enabled and true or false
		Tween(button, { BackgroundTransparency = self.Enabled and recipe.fillT or math.max(recipe.fillT, 0.5) }, 0.15)
		Tween(label, { TextTransparency = self.Enabled and 0 or 0.5 }, 0.15)
		Tween(stroke, { Transparency = self.Enabled and recipe.strokeT or math.max(recipe.strokeT, 0.7) }, 0.15)
	end
	-- text changes cross-fade and the button eases to its new width
	function state:SetText(text)
		text = tostring(text)
		if label.Text == text then
			return
		end
		label.Text = text
		if not spec.Size then
			Tween(button, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(measure(text), button.Size.Y.Offset) }, 0.24, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		end
		label.TextTransparency = 1
		Tween(label, { TextTransparency = self.Enabled and 0 or 0.5 }, 0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
	end
	function state:Refit()
		if not spec.Size then
			button.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(measure(label.Text), button.Size.Y.Offset)
		end
	end
	return state
end

-- Rotating ring used as a loading indicator (procedural)
function Window:_Spinner(parent, size, token)
	local ring = Create("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size), ZIndex = 5, Parent = parent }, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
	local stroke = Create("UIStroke", { Thickness = 2, Color = self:_T(token or "AccentText"), Parent = ring })
	Create("UIGradient", { Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumKey(0, 0), NumKey(0.5, 0.2), NumKey(0.75, 1), NumKey(1, 1) }), Parent = stroke })
	local conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
		if Alive(ring) then ring.Rotation = ring.Rotation + dt * 380 end
	end)
	return {
		Frame = ring,
		Destroy = function()
			conn:Disconnect()
			ring:Destroy()
		end,
	}
end

-- Staggered reveal: fades each child in, sliding up a few pixels, one after another.
local function StaggerIn(items, step, distance)
	step = step or 0.04
	for index, item in ipairs(items) do
		if item:IsA("GuiObject") then
			local goal = item.Position
			item.Position = goal + UDim2.fromOffset(0, distance or 10)
			FadeTree(item, 1, 0)
			task.delay(0.05 + (index - 1) * step, function()
				if Alive(item) then
					Tween(item, { Position = goal }, 0.32, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
					FadeTree(item, 0, 0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end

-- ============================================================================
-- PROMPTS (modal dialogs inside the window)
-- ============================================================================
-- Window:Prompt({ Title, Content, Buttons = { { Text, Variant, Callback } }, Input = { Placeholder, Default } })
function Window:Prompt(options)
	options = options or {}
	if self.ActivePrompt then
		self.ActivePrompt:Close()
	end
	local theme = self.Theme
	local win = self
	local overlay = Create("TextButton", {
		Name = "PromptOverlay", Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = theme.Shadow, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 40, Parent = self.Main,
	})
	local card = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "Prompt", BackgroundColor3 = theme.Element, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 10), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(380, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ZIndex = 41, Parent = overlay,
	})
	self:_Corner(card, 12)
	self:_Bind(card, "BackgroundColor3", "Element")
	Create("UIStroke", { Color = theme.Stroke, Transparency = 0.35, Parent = card })
	Create("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 24), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 24), PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 22), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 22), Parent = card })
	Create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 10), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = card })
	local cardScale = Create("UIScale", { Scale = 0.96, Parent = card })

	local titleLabel = self:_Label({ Text = options.Title or "Prompt", TextSize = 16, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22), LayoutOrder = 1, Parent = card }, "Bold", "Text")
	local bodyLabel = nil
	if options.Content and options.Content ~= "" then
		bodyLabel = self:_Label({
			Text = options.Content, TextSize = 13, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, TextWrapped = true,
			TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None, LayoutOrder = 2, Parent = card,
		}, "Regular", "SubText")
	end
	local inputBox, inputHolder = nil, nil
	if options.Input then
		inputHolder = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = theme.Background, BackgroundTransparency = 0.4, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36), LayoutOrder = 3, Parent = card })
		self:_Corner(inputHolder, 8)
		self:_Bind(inputHolder, "BackgroundColor3", "Background")
		local inputStroke = Create("UIStroke", { Color = theme.Stroke, Transparency = 0.3, Parent = inputHolder })
		inputBox = Create("TextBox", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = tostring(options.Input.Default or ""), PlaceholderText = options.Input.Placeholder or "",
			PlaceholderColor3 = theme.Placeholder, TextColor3 = theme.Text, TextSize = 13, ClearTextOnFocus = false,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, 0), Parent = inputHolder,
		})
		self:_Font(inputBox, "Medium")
		self:_Bind(inputBox, "TextColor3", "Text")
		inputBox.Focused:Connect(function() Tween(inputStroke, { Color = self:_T("Accent"), Transparency = 0 }, 0.15) end)
		inputBox.FocusLost:Connect(function() Tween(inputStroke, { Color = self:_T("Stroke"), Transparency = 0.3 }, 0.2) end)
	end
	local buttonRow = Create("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36), LayoutOrder = 4, Parent = card })
	Create("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6), Parent = buttonRow })
	Create("UIListLayout", { FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right, Padding = UDim.new(0, 8), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = buttonRow })

	local prompt = { Overlay = overlay, Card = card, Input = inputBox, Closed = false }
	function prompt:Close()
		if self.Closed then
			return
		end
		self.Closed = true
		-- exit: faster than entry, ease-in, card and backdrop move as one unit
		Tween(cardScale, { Scale = 0.97 }, 0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
		Tween(card, { Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 6) }, 0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
		Tween(overlay, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, 0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		FadeTree(card, 1, 0.14, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		task.delay(Timed(0.18), function()
			overlay:Destroy()
		end)
		if win.ActivePrompt == self then
			win.ActivePrompt = nil
		end
	end
	local buttons = options.Buttons or { { Text = "OK", Variant = "Primary" } }
	for order, spec in ipairs(buttons) do
		local variant = spec.Variant or "Secondary"
		if variant == "Default" then variant = "Secondary" end
		self:_StyledButton(buttonRow, {
			Text = spec.Text or "OK", Variant = variant, LayoutOrder = order, ZIndex = 42,
			Callback = function()
				local value = inputBox and inputBox.Text or nil
				prompt:Close()
				SafeCall(spec.Callback, value, spec.Text)
			end,
		})
	end
	if inputBox then
		inputBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter)
			if enter and buttons[#buttons] and not prompt.Closed then
				local value = inputBox.Text
				prompt:Close()
				SafeCall(buttons[#buttons].Callback, value, buttons[#buttons].Text)
			end
		end)
		task.delay(Timed(0.25), function()
			if Alive(inputBox) then
				inputBox:CaptureFocus()
			end
		end)
	end
	if options.DismissOnBackground ~= false then
		overlay.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			prompt:Close()
			SafeCall(options.OnDismiss)
		end)
	end
	-- enter: backdrop 200ms, card fade + scale 0.96 -> 1 + 10px rise over 280ms ease-out, content staggered
	Tween(overlay, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.55 }, 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
	Tween(cardScale, { Scale = 1 }, 0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
	card.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	Tween(card, { Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 0 }, 0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
	local staggerItems = { titleLabel }
	if bodyLabel then staggerItems[#staggerItems + 1] = bodyLabel end
	if inputHolder then staggerItems[#staggerItems + 1] = inputHolder end
	staggerItems[#staggerItems + 1] = buttonRow
	StaggerIn(staggerItems, 0.04, 6)
	self.ActivePrompt = prompt
	return prompt
end

-- Window:ShowCodePopup({ Title, Content, Code, ReadOnly, SubmitText, OnSubmit(text), CopyText })
-- A modal with a selectable multi-line box. Used for preset JSON where there is no file system to write to:
-- the player copies the code out, or pastes one in and presses the action button.
function Window:ShowCodePopup(options)
	options = options or {}
	local theme = self.Theme
	if self.ActiveCodePopup then
		self.ActiveCodePopup:Close()
	end
	local overlay = Create("TextButton", {
		Name = "CodePopupOverlay", Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = theme.Shadow, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 45, Parent = self.Main,
	})
	local width = math.clamp(self.Size.X.Offset - 80, 260, 470)
	local card = Create("Frame", {
		Name = "CodePopup", BackgroundColor3 = theme.Element, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 16), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ZIndex = 46, Parent = overlay,
	})
	self:_Corner(card, 14)
	self:_Bind(card, "BackgroundColor3", "Element")
	local cardStroke = Create("UIStroke", { Color = theme.Stroke, Transparency = 0.35, Parent = card })
	self:_Bind(cardStroke, "Color", "Stroke")
	Create("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 22), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 22), PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 20), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 20), Parent = card })
	Create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 10), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = card })
	local cardScale = Create("UIScale", { Scale = 0.92, Parent = card })

	local titleLabel = self:_Label({ Text = options.Title or "Code", TextSize = 16, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22), LayoutOrder = 1, Parent = card }, "Bold", "Text")
	local bodyLabel = nil
	if options.Content and options.Content ~= "" then
		bodyLabel = self:_Label({
			Text = options.Content, TextSize = 12, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
			TextWrapped = true, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None, LayoutOrder = 2, Parent = card,
		}, "Regular", "SubText")
	end
	local boxHolder = Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = theme.Background, BackgroundTransparency = 0.25, BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 150), ClipsDescendants = true, LayoutOrder = 3, Parent = card,
	})
	self:_Corner(boxHolder, 10)
	self:_Bind(boxHolder, "BackgroundColor3", "Background")
	local boxStroke = Create("UIStroke", { Color = theme.Stroke, Transparency = 0.4, Parent = boxHolder })
	self:_Bind(boxStroke, "Color", "Stroke")
	local scroller = Create("ScrollingFrame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 8), Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -16),
		CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ScrollBarThickness = 3,
		ScrollBarImageColor3 = theme.Accent, Parent = boxHolder,
	})
	self:_Bind(scroller, "ScrollBarImageColor3", "Accent")
	local box = Create("TextBox", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = tostring(options.Code or ""), PlaceholderText = options.Placeholder or "Paste the code here",
		PlaceholderColor3 = theme.Placeholder, TextColor3 = theme.Text, TextSize = 12, MultiLine = true, ClearTextOnFocus = false,
		TextEditable = options.ReadOnly ~= true, TextWrapped = true, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Parent = scroller,
	})
	self:_Font(box, "Code")
	self:_Bind(box, "TextColor3", "Text")
	self:_Bind(box, "PlaceholderColor3", "Placeholder")

	local buttonRow = Create("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 38), LayoutOrder = 4, Parent = card })
	Create("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4), Parent = buttonRow })
	Create("UIListLayout", { FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right, VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center, Padding = UDim.new(0, 8), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = buttonRow })

	local popup = { Overlay = overlay, Card = card, Box = box, Closed = false }
	function popup:Close()
		if self.Closed then
			return
		end
		self.Closed = true
		-- out: the card drops back a little, shrinks and fades with the backdrop
		Tween(cardScale, { Scale = 0.94 }, 0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
		Tween(card, { Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 12) }, 0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
		Tween(overlay, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, 0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		FadeTree(card, 1, 0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		task.delay(Timed(0.2), function()
			overlay:Destroy()
		end)
		if self.Window and self.Window.ActiveCodePopup == self then
			self.Window.ActiveCodePopup = nil
		end
	end
	popup.Window = self

	local copyButton = self:_StyledButton(buttonRow, {
		Text = options.CopyText or "Copy", Variant = "Secondary", Height = 32, TextSize = 12, LayoutOrder = 1, ZIndex = 47,
		Callback = function(state)
			local copied = false
			pcall(function()
				if typeof(setclipboard) == "function" then
					setclipboard(box.Text)
					copied = true
				end
			end)
			if not copied then
				-- no clipboard here: select it all so it can be copied by hand
				box:CaptureFocus()
				box.SelectionStart = 1
				box.CursorPosition = #box.Text + 1
			end
			state:SetText(copied and "Copied" or "Selected")
			task.delay(Timed(1.2), function()
				if state.Button.Parent then
					state:SetText(options.CopyText or "Copy")
				end
			end)
		end,
	})
	if options.OnSubmit then
		self:_StyledButton(buttonRow, {
			Text = options.SubmitText or "Apply", Variant = "Primary", Height = 32, TextSize = 12, LayoutOrder = 2, ZIndex = 47,
			Callback = function()
				local text = box.Text
				popup:Close()
				SafeCall(options.OnSubmit, text)
			end,
		})
	end
	self:_StyledButton(buttonRow, {
		Text = options.CloseText or "Close", Variant = "Ghost", Height = 32, TextSize = 12, LayoutOrder = 3, ZIndex = 47,
		Callback = function()
			popup:Close()
		end,
	})
	if options.DismissOnBackground ~= false then
		overlay.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			popup:Close()
		end)
	end

	-- in: backdrop fades, the card rises and scales up, then the rows arrive one after another
	Tween(overlay, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.55 }, 0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
	Tween(cardScale, { Scale = 1 }, 0.34, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
	card.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	Tween(card, { Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 0 }, 0.34, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
	local staggerItems = { titleLabel }
	if bodyLabel then staggerItems[#staggerItems + 1] = bodyLabel end
	staggerItems[#staggerItems + 1] = boxHolder
	staggerItems[#staggerItems + 1] = buttonRow
	StaggerIn(staggerItems, 0.05, 8)
	if options.ReadOnly ~= true then
		task.delay(Timed(0.3), function()
			if Alive(box) then
				box:CaptureFocus()
			end
		end)
	end
	self.ActiveCodePopup = popup
	return popup
end

function Window:Confirm(options)
	options = options or {}
	return self:Prompt({
		Title = options.Title or "Are you sure?", Content = options.Content,
		Buttons = {
			{ Text = options.CancelText or "Cancel", Variant = "Secondary", Callback = function() SafeCall(options.OnCancel) end },
			{ Text = options.ConfirmText or "Confirm", Variant = options.Danger and "Danger" or "Primary", Callback = function() SafeCall(options.Callback) end },
		},
	})
end

function Window:PromptInput(options)
	options = options or {}
	return self:Prompt({
		Title = options.Title or "Enter a value", Content = options.Content,
		Input = { Placeholder = options.Placeholder, Default = options.Default },
		Buttons = {
			{ Text = "Cancel", Variant = "Secondary", Callback = function() SafeCall(options.Callback, nil) end },
			{ Text = options.ConfirmText or "OK", Variant = "Primary", Callback = function(value) SafeCall(options.Callback, value) end },
		},
	})
end

function Window:PromptChoice(options)
	options = options or {}
	local buttons = {}
	for index, choice in ipairs(options.Options or {}) do
		buttons[index] = { Text = tostring(choice), Variant = (index == #options.Options) and "Primary" or "Secondary", Callback = function()
			SafeCall(options.Callback, choice, index)
		end }
	end
	return self:Prompt({ Title = options.Title or "Choose", Content = options.Content, Buttons = buttons })
end

-- ============================================================================
-- ============================================================================
-- CORE
-- The other half of the library lives in Core/Core: the code box, the extra elements, panels, the theme
-- editor, the key gate, and the whole logic and automations system. It is handed everything this file
-- built -- the metatables included -- and adds its own methods to them, so nothing here needs to know
-- which half a method came from. It has to load before any window is made.
-- ============================================================================
local CoreShared = {
	Alive = Alive,
	Aurora = Aurora,
	CodeEngine = CodeEngine,
	Container = Container,
	Create = Create,
	Element = Element,
	FadeTree = FadeTree,
	FormatNumber = FormatNumber,
	HasFileSystem = HasFileSystem,
	IsClick = IsClick,
	IsMove = IsMove,
	Keypoint = Keypoint,
	Lerp = Lerp,
	Lighting = Lighting,
	LocalPlayer = LocalPlayer,
	Maid = Maid,
	MakeCheck = MakeCheck,
	MakeChevron = MakeChevron,
	Merge = Merge,
	NumKey = NumKey,
	Page = Page,
	Parallax = Parallax,
	Players = Players,
	RoundTo = RoundTo,
	RowHeight = RowHeight,
	RunService = RunService,
	SafeCall = SafeCall,
	Section = Section,
	StaggerIn = StaggerIn,
	TextService = TextService,
	RestingValue = RestingValue,
	Timed = Timed,
	Tween = Tween,
	UserInputService = UserInputService,
	Window = Window,
}

do
	local folder = script:FindFirstChild("Core")
	local module = folder and folder:FindFirstChild("Core")
	if not module or not module:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		error("[AuroraUI] Core is missing. Expected a Folder called 'Core' inside the AuroraUI module, with a ModuleScript called 'Core' in it.", 0)
	end
	local ok, result = pcall(require, module)
	if not ok then
		error("[AuroraUI] Core failed to load: " .. tostring(result), 0)
	end
	if type(result) ~= "function" then
		error("[AuroraUI] Core must return a function that takes the shared table.", 0)
	end
	result(CoreShared)
end

-- Helpers for your own elements and backgrounds
Aurora.Util = { Create = Create, Tween = Tween, FadeTree = FadeTree, IsClick = IsClick, IsMove = IsMove, Merge = Merge, Alive = Alive }

return Aurora


================================================================================
FILE 2: AuroraUI_Tests.lua
================================================================================

--[[
	AuroraUI / Tests
	A self-check that runs inside Roblox, because that is the only place the library can actually run.

	Put it inside AuroraUI (or beside it) as a ModuleScript called Tests, and run it from a
	LocalScript -- the library builds a real UI, so it needs a client:

		local report = require(ReplicatedStorage.AuroraUI.Tests)()
		--> prints a pass/fail line per check and returns { Passed, Failed, Lines }

	It builds a real window off-screen, pokes every part of the library that has broken at least once,
	and tears it down again. It catches the kind of mistake that a parser cannot see: a method that was
	renamed, a local that went out of scope, a saved file that no longer round-trips.

	The same file runs in both editions. A check that needs something only Elite has is skipped in a
	Free build rather than failed, and the summary says how many were skipped and why.
]]

return function(options)
	options = options or {}
	-- the library is either our parent (Tests sits inside AuroraUI) or our sibling (Tests sits beside it)
	local host = script.Parent
	local module = (host:IsA("ModuleScript") and host) or host:FindFirstChild("AuroraUI")
	if not module then
		error("[AuroraUI Tests] Could not find the AuroraUI ModuleScript. Put Tests inside AuroraUI, or beside it.", 0)
	end
	local Aurora = require(module)

	local passed, failed, skipped, lines = 0, 0, 0, {}
	local function record(ok, name, detail)
		if ok then
			passed = passed + 1
			lines[#lines + 1] = "  pass  " .. name
		else
			failed = failed + 1
			lines[#lines + 1] = "  FAIL  " .. name .. (detail and ("  ->  " .. tostring(detail)) or "")
		end
	end
	-- A check that throws is a failure, never a crash of the run. A check that names a feature this
	-- edition does not have is skipped instead: the Free build is not broken for lacking it.
	local function check(name, fn, feature)
		if feature and not Aurora:Has(feature) then
			skipped = skipped + 1
			lines[#lines + 1] = "  skip  " .. name .. "  (needs " .. (Aurora.FeatureNames[feature] or feature) .. ")"
			return
		end
		local ok, result = pcall(fn)
		if not ok then
			record(false, name, result)
		elseif result == false then
			record(false, name, "returned false")
		else
			record(true, name)
		end
	end

	-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- the library loaded at all
	check("library requires", function()
		return type(Aurora) == "table" and type(Aurora.CreateWindow) == "function"
	end)
	check("Core loaded (its methods are on the shared metatables)", function()
		-- these all live in Core; if it did not load, they are nil
		local window = Aurora:CreateWindow({ Title = "probe", Loading = false, Render = "2D" })
		local page = window:AddPage({ Name = "probe" })
		local ok = type(page.AddCodeBox) == "function"
			and type(page.AddTabs) == "function"
			and type(page.AddLogicEditor) == "function"
			and type(page.AddAutomations) == "function"
			and type(window.AddLogicHubPage) == "function"
			and type(window.CreatePanel) == "function"
		window:Destroy()
		return ok
	end)

	-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- one window for the rest
	local window = Aurora:CreateWindow({
		Title = "AuroraUI self check", Loading = false, Render = "2D", Settings = true,
	})
	local page = window:AddPage({ Name = "Checks", Icon = "home" })

	check("every element type builds", function()
		page:AddTitle({ Text = "Title" })
		page:AddText({ Text = "Text" })
		page:AddButton({ Title = "Button", Callback = function() end })
		page:AddToggle({ Title = "Toggle", Flag = "check.toggle" })
		page:AddSlider({ Title = "Slider", Min = 0, Max = 10, Default = 5, Flag = "check.slider" })
		page:AddDropdown({ Title = "Dropdown", Options = { "a", "b" }, Flag = "check.dropdown" })
		page:AddKeybind({ Title = "Keybind", Default = Enum.KeyCode.F })
		page:AddInput({ Title = "Input", Flag = "check.input" })
		page:AddColorPicker({ Title = "Colour", Default = Color3.new(1, 0, 0), Flag = "check.colour" })
		page:AddTabs({ Tabs = { "One", "Two" } })
		page:AddProgress({ Title = "Progress", Value = 40 })
		page:AddTable({ Columns = { "a", "b" }, Rows = { { "1", "2" } } })
		page:AddConsole({ Title = "Console" })
		page:AddCodeBox({ Text = "print('hi')" })
		return true
	end)

	-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- the bugs that actually happened
	check("a slider with OnRelease still commits a scripted Set, once", function()
		local fired = 0
		local slider = page:AddSlider({
			Title = "Release", Min = 0, Max = 100, Default = 10, Increment = 1, OnRelease = true,
			Flag = "check.release", Callback = function() fired = fired + 1 end,
		})
		slider:Set(42)
		return fired == 1 and window.Flags["check.release"] == 42 and slider:Get() == 42
	end)

	check("two elements asking for one flag do not overwrite each other", function()
		local first = page:AddToggle({ Title = "Dup A", Default = true, Flag = "check.dup" })
		local second = page:AddToggle({ Title = "Dup B", Default = false, Flag = "check.dup" })
		return first.Flag ~= second.Flag and window._flagElements[first.Flag] == first
			and window._flagElements[second.Flag] == second
	end)

	check("tabs can be added and removed at runtime", function()
		local tabs = page:AddTabs({ Tabs = { "Alpha" } })
		local added = tabs:AddTab("Beta")
		local names = tabs:TabNames()
		local removed = tabs:RemoveTab("Beta")
		return added ~= nil and #names == 2 and removed == true and #tabs:TabNames() == 1
	end)

	check("preset round-trips through JSON", function()
		local before = window:GetPreset("check")
		local json = window:ExportPreset("check")
		local back = Aurora:ParsePreset(json)
		return type(json) == "string" and type(back) == "table"
			and back.Theme == before.Theme and back.Version == Aurora.FileVersion
	end)

	check("a preset applied before its element exists still reaches it", function()
		window:ApplyPreset({ Name = "late", Flags = { ["check.late"] = true } })
		local late = page:AddToggle({ Title = "Late", Default = false, Flag = "check.late" })
		task.wait()   -- the value is handed over on the next step
		return late:Get() == true
	end)

	check("logic saves under its flag, so same-named cards do not clash", function()
		local a = page:AddLogicEditor({ Title = "Same name", Starter = false, AutoRun = false })
		local b = page:AddLogicEditor({ Title = "Same name", Starter = false, AutoRun = false })
		a:AddNode("action.print")
		local data = window:GetLogic()
		local count = 0
		for _ in pairs(data.Graphs) do count = count + 1 end
		return a.Flag ~= b.Flag and count >= 2
	end, "Logic")

	check("logic round-trips", function()
		local editor = page:AddLogicEditor({ Title = "Round trip", Starter = false, AutoRun = false })
		editor:AddNode("event.start")
		editor:AddNode("action.print")
		local json = window:ExportLogic()
		local ok, applied = window:ImportLogic(json)
		return type(json) == "string" and ok and applied > 0
	end, "Logic")

	check("everything-code carries look, logic and values", function()
		local blob = window:GetEverything()
		local ok = window:ImportEverything(window:ExportEverything())
		return ok and blob.Preset ~= nil and blob.Logic ~= nil and blob.Flags ~= nil
	end)

	check("config profiles load both the old and the new shape", function()
		local flags = window:GetConfig()
		window:LoadConfig(flags)              -- the old shape: a bare map of flags
		local wrapped = { Version = Aurora.FileVersion, Kind = "config", Flags = flags }
		window:LoadConfig(wrapped.Flags)      -- the shape SaveConfig writes now
		return true
	end)

	check("optimization 1 to 10 and back leaves the effects as they were", function()
		local before = {}
		for key, value in pairs(window.Effects) do before[key] = value end
		for level = 1, 10 do
			window:SetOptimization(level, true)
		end
		window:SetOptimization(1, true)
		for key, value in pairs(before) do
			if window.Effects[key] ~= value then
				return false
			end
		end
		return true
	end)

	check("high optimization hides user pages and never the built-in ones", function()
		local heavy = window:AddPage({ Name = "Heavy" })
		heavy._cost = 0.01            -- 10 ms a frame: far over every budget
		window:SetOptimization(10, true)
		local builtInVisible = (not window.SettingsPage or not window.SettingsPage.Hidden)
		local heavyHidden = heavy.Hidden == true
		window:RestoreTrimmedPages()
		local restored = heavy.Hidden ~= true
		window:SetOptimization(1, true)
		return heavyHidden and builtInVisible and restored
	end)

	check("acrylic hands the depth of field back when it goes off", function()
		window:SetAcrylic(true)
		window:SetAcrylic(false)
		local leftovers = 0
		for _, item in ipairs(game:GetService("Lighting"):GetChildren()) do
			if item:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") and item.Name == "AuroraGlass" then
				leftovers = leftovers + 1
			end
		end
		return leftovers == 0
	end)

	check("search finds pages, elements and logic nodes", function()
		local matches = window:SearchPages("toggle")
		window:SearchPages("")
		return type(matches) == "table" and #matches.Elements > 0
	end)

	check("the Logic page is Logic / Settings, and script-made logic lands as a subtab under Logic", function()
		local tab, editor = window:AddLogicPage({ Name = "Scripted", Starter = false, AutoRun = false })
		local outer = window.LogicPageTabs and window.LogicPageTabs:TabNames() or {}
		local inner = window.LogicTabs and window.LogicTabs:TabNames() or {}
		local found = false
		for _, name in ipairs(inner) do
			if name == "Scripted" then found = true end
		end
		return editor ~= nil and tab ~= nil and found
			and outer[1] == "Logic" and outer[2] == "Settings"
			and window:GetPage("Scripted") == nil     -- it is a subtab, not a sidebar page
	end, "Logic")
