	check("the logic page builds and lists what is in the window", function()
		local hub = window:AddLogicHubPage()
		return hub ~= nil and #window:_LogicItems() > 0 and type(window:LogicLoad().PerSecond) == "number"
	end, "Logic")

	check("layout: sidebar on the right and main bar at the bottom, then back", function()
		window:SetLayout({ SidebarSide = "Right", HeaderSide = "Bottom" }, false)
		local flipped = window.Sidebar.Position.X.Scale == 1 and window.TopBar.Position.X.Offset == 0
			and window.TopBar.Position.Y.Scale == 1 and window.Content.Position.Y.Offset == 0
		window:SetLayout({ SidebarSide = "Left", HeaderSide = "Top", SidebarWidth = 200 }, false)
		local back = window.Sidebar.Position.X.Offset == 8 and window.TopBar.Position.X.Offset == 200
			and window.SidebarWidth == 200 and window.Content.Position.Y.Offset == window.HeaderHeight
		window:ResetLayout(false)
		return flipped and back and window.SidebarWidth == Aurora.DefaultLayout.SidebarWidth
	end)

	check("layout: the title and logo move into the main bar, hide, and come back", function()
		window:SetLayout({ Brand = "Header" }, false)
		local inHeader = window.Brand.Parent == window.TopBar and window.Brand.Visible == true
		window:SetLayout({ Brand = "Hidden" }, false)
		local hidden = window.Brand.Visible == false
		window:SetLayout({ Brand = "Sidebar", LogoSize = 40 }, false)
		local backHome = window.Brand.Parent == window.Sidebar and window.Brand.Visible == true
			and window.BrandMark.Size.X.Offset == 40
		window:ResetLayout(false)
		return inHeader and hidden and backHome
	end)

	check("layout: a shorter sidebar sits where the alignment says", function()
		local function near(a, b) return math.abs(a - b) < 0.001 end
		window:SetLayout({ SidebarHeight = 60, SidebarAlign = "Bottom" }, false)
		local bottom = near(window.Sidebar.Size.Y.Scale, 0.6) and near(window.Sidebar.Position.Y.Scale, 0.4)
		window:SetLayout({ SidebarAlign = "Middle" }, false)
		local middle = near(window.Sidebar.Position.Y.Scale, 0.2)
		window:SetLayout({ SidebarAlign = "Top" }, false)
		local top = near(window.Sidebar.Position.Y.Scale, 0) and window.Sidebar.Position.Y.Offset == 8
		window:ResetLayout(false)
		return bottom and middle and top and near(window.Sidebar.Size.Y.Scale, 1)
	end)

	check("layout: the sidebar width is capped by the window width", function()
		local width, height = window.Size.X.Offset, window.Size.Y.Offset
		window:SetSize(480, height, false)
		window:SetLayout({ SidebarWidth = 320 }, false)
		local capped = window.SidebarWidth <= math.floor(480 * Aurora.SidebarWidthShare) and window:GetLayout().SidebarWidth == 320
		window:SetSize(width, height, false)
		local follows = window.SidebarWidth == math.min(320, math.floor(width * Aurora.SidebarWidthShare))
		window:ResetLayout(false)
		return capped and follows
	end)

	check("layout: the window buttons move to the outer corner when the sidebar is on the right", function()
		local close = window.WindowButtons and window.WindowButtons.Close
		if not close then return false end
		window:SetLayout({ SidebarSide = "Right" }, false)
		local mirrored = close.AnchorPoint.X == 0 and close.Position.X.Offset == 16 and window.PageTitle.Position.X.Offset >= 84
		window:ResetLayout(false)
		local back = close.AnchorPoint.X == 1 and close.Position.X.Scale == 1 and window.PageTitle.Position.X.Offset == 22
		return mirrored and back
	end)

	check("layout: the brand in the main bar is sized to the title", function()
		local title = window.Title
		window:SetLayout({ Brand = "Header" }, false)
		local short = window.Brand.Size.X.Offset
		window:SetTitle("A much longer window title than before")
		local long = window.Brand.Size.X.Offset
		window:SetTitle(title)
		window:ResetLayout(false)
		return long > short and window.Brand.Size.X.Offset ~= long
	end)

	check("LoadTheme says why it failed", function()
		local ok, reason = window:LoadTheme("no such theme zz")
		return ok == false and (reason == "missing" or reason == "nofs")
	end)

	check("layout: presets apply and are recognised, brand offsets move the mark", function()
		local applied = window:ApplyLayoutPreset("Floating sidebar", false)
		local matched = applied and applied.SidebarHeight == 70 and window:MatchLayoutPreset() == "Floating sidebar"
			and window.SidebarTrack and window.SidebarTrack.Visible == true
		window:ResetLayout(false)
		window:SetLayout({ BrandOffsetX = 20, BrandOffsetY = -4 }, false)
		local nudged = window.BrandMark.Position.X.Offset == 34 and window.BrandMark.Position.Y.Offset == 7 and window:MatchLayoutPreset() == nil
		window:ResetLayout(false)
		return matched and nudged and window.SidebarTrack.Visible == false and window:MatchLayoutPreset() == "Default"
	end)

	check("dropdown rows can carry colour swatches", function()
		local element = page:AddDropdown({ Title = "Swatch probe", Options = Aurora:GetThemeNames(), Swatches = Aurora.ThemeSwatches })
		local strip = element.Frame:FindFirstChild("Swatches", true)
		return strip ~= nil and #strip:GetChildren() >= 5
	end)

	check("saved themes are read from disk once", function()
		local again = window:_LoadSavedThemes()
		return again == 0
	end)

	check("headless: a slider drag with OnRelease commits once, on release", function()
		local fires = 0
		local slider = page:AddSlider({ Title = "Release probe", Min = 0, Max = 100, Default = 0, Increment = 1, Mode = "Clamped", OnRelease = true, Flag = "__releaseProbe",
			Callback = function() fires = fires + 1 end })
		local track = slider._track
		if not track or track.AbsoluteSize.X < 10 then
			return false
		end
		local x0, w, y = track.AbsolutePosition.X, track.AbsoluteSize.X, track.AbsolutePosition.Y + track.AbsoluteSize.Y / 2
		window:_SimPointer(Vector2.new(x0 + w * 0.25, y))
		slider._press({ UserInputType = Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 })
		for _, pct in ipairs({ 0.4, 0.55, 0.7, 0.9 }) do
			window:_SimPointer(Vector2.new(x0 + w * pct, y))
			window:_SimInput("Changed", Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement)
		end
		local heldBack = fires == 0 and (window.Flags.__releaseProbe or 0) == 0
		window:_SimInput("Ended", Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)
		window:_SimPointer(nil)
		local committed = fires == 1 and window.Flags.__releaseProbe >= 85 and slider:Get() == window.Flags.__releaseProbe
		return heldBack and committed
	end)

	check("headless: shift-drag box-selects nodes on the logic canvas", function()
		local editor = page:AddLogicEditor({ Title = "Box probe", Starter = false, AutoRun = false })
		editor:AddNode("event.start", Vector2.new(100, 100))
		editor:AddNode("action.print", Vector2.new(400, 100))
		window:_SimDrag(editor._pressCanvas, { editor:_ToAbsolute(Vector2.new(60, 60)), editor:_ToAbsolute(Vector2.new(700, 320)) },
		{ [Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift] = true })
		return #editor:Selection() == 2
	end, "Logic")

	check("headless: dragging a node moves it, undo puts it back, redo moves it again", function()
		local editor = page:AddLogicEditor({ Title = "Drag probe", Starter = false, AutoRun = false })
		local node = editor:AddNode("action.print", Vector2.new(300, 300))
		local view = editor:_View(node.Id)
		if not view or not view.Press then
			return false
		end
		local start = editor:_ToAbsolute(Vector2.new(node.X + 20, node.Y + 10))
		window:_SimDrag(view.Press, { start, start + Vector2.new(30, 20), start + Vector2.new(60, 40) })
		local moved = node.X == 360 and node.Y == 340
		local undone = editor:Undo()
		local function find()
			for _, entry in ipairs(editor:GetGraph().Nodes) do
				if entry.Id == node.Id then return entry end
			end
		end
		local back = undone and find() and find().X == 300
		local redone = editor:Redo() and find() and find().X == 360
		return moved and back and redone
	end, "Logic")

	check("headless: undo can go all the way back to an empty canvas", function()
		local editor = page:AddLogicEditor({ Title = "Undo probe", Starter = false, AutoRun = false })
		editor:AddNode("event.start", Vector2.new(100, 100))
		editor:AddNode("action.print", Vector2.new(400, 100))
		local two = #editor:GetGraph().Nodes == 2
		editor:Undo()
		local one = #editor:GetGraph().Nodes == 1
		editor:Undo()
		local none = #editor:GetGraph().Nodes == 0
		editor:Redo()
		return two and one and none and #editor:GetGraph().Nodes == 1
	end, "Logic")

	check("opening and closing six times in a row leaves the window's resting look intact", function()
		-- the sidebar and the page title are always solid at rest; if a fade ever captured a mid-fade moment as
		-- the resting look, their cached rest would drift towards 1 and the reopened window would be empty
		local sidebar, title = window.Sidebar, window.PageTitle
		-- the cached rest if a fade has already seen the sidebar, else what the first fade will read
		local restBG = sidebar:GetAttribute("AuroraBG") or Aurora._RestingValue(sidebar, "BackgroundTransparency")
		for _ = 1, 3 do
			window:Close()
			window:Open()
			window:Close()   -- close again while the open fade is still running
			window:Open()
		end
		local heldBG = sidebar:GetAttribute("AuroraBG") == restBG
		local heldTT = (title:GetAttribute("AuroraTT") or 0) < 0.5
		local ticketed = type(window._fadeTicket) == "number" and (window._fadeState == "in" or window._fadeState == "shown")
		return window.IsOpen == true and window.Root.Visible == true and heldBG and heldTT and ticketed
	end)

	check("a hover tween in flight reports where it is heading, not where it is", function()
		local probe = Instance.new("Frame")
		probe.BackgroundTransparency = 0
		probe.Parent = window.Content
		local tween = Aurora._Tween(probe, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, 2)
		local heading = Aurora._RestingValue(probe, "BackgroundTransparency") == 1 and probe.BackgroundTransparency < 1
		tween:Cancel()
		probe:Destroy()
		return heading
	end)

	check("layout rides inside presets", function()
		window:SetLayout({ SidebarWidth = 220, LogoSize = 36 }, false)
		local preset = window:GetPreset("layout")
		local carried = type(preset.Layout) == "table" and preset.Layout.SidebarWidth == 220 and preset.Layout.LogoSize == 36
		window:ResetLayout(false)
		window:ApplyPreset({ Layout = { SidebarWidth = 240 } })
		local applied = window.SidebarWidth == 240
		window:ResetLayout(false)
		return carried and applied
	end)

	check("a theme round-trips through JSON", function()
		local json = Aurora:EncodeTheme(window.Theme, "probe")
		local back = Aurora:ParseTheme(json)
		return type(json) == "string" and type(back) == "table" and back.Name == "probe"
			and back.Accent == window.Theme.Accent and type(back.Code) == "table" and back.Code.Keyword == window.Theme.Code.Keyword
	end)

	check("a saved theme is registered under its name, listed, and can be deleted; built-ins cannot", function()
		local before = window.ThemeName
		window:SaveTheme("check theme")
		local listed = false
		for _, name in ipairs(Aurora:GetThemeNames()) do
			if name == "check theme" then listed = true end
		end
		local registered = Aurora.Themes["check theme"] ~= nil and listed and window.ThemeName == "check theme"
		local deleted = window:DeleteTheme("check theme")
		local gone = Aurora.Themes["check theme"] == nil
		window:SetTheme(Aurora.Themes[before] and before or "Midnight")
		return registered and deleted and gone and window:DeleteTheme("Midnight") == false
	end, "ThemeFiles")

	check("a preset carries edited colours, not just the theme's name", function()
		window._themeDirty = true
		local preset = window:GetPreset("colours")
		window._themeDirty = nil
		return type(preset.ThemeColors) == "table" and type(preset.ThemeColors.Colors) == "table"
			and preset.ThemeColors.Colors.Accent ~= nil
	end)

	check("the theme editor page builds", function()
		return window:AddThemeEditorPage() ~= nil
	end)

	check("the edition declares itself and every feature has a name", function()
		local named = true
		for _, feature in ipairs(Aurora.FeatureList) do
			if type(Aurora.FeatureNames[feature]) ~= "string" then
				named = false
			end
		end
		local edition = Aurora.Edition == "Free" or Aurora.Edition == "Elite"
		return edition and named and type(Aurora:Has("Logic")) == "boolean"
			and (Aurora.Edition == "Free") ~= Aurora:IsElite()
	end)

	check("a locked row stands in for a missing element and keeps its value", function()
		local locked = page:_Locked("CodeBox", { Title = "Locked probe", Flag = "__lockedProbe", Default = "kept" })
		local answers = type(locked.Set) == "function" and type(locked.Refresh) == "function"
		local starts = locked:Get() == "kept" and window.Flags.__lockedProbe == "kept"
		locked:Set("changed", true)
		-- an Elite script can call anything on it: nothing errors, and nothing pretends to have happened
		local quiet = locked.AddNode() == nil and locked.Start() == nil and locked.Refresh() == nil
		return locked.Locked == true and answers and starts and quiet
			and locked:Get() == "changed" and window.Flags.__lockedProbe == "changed"
	end)

	check("a look shared between editions keeps what this build cannot use", function()
		local hadFeature, hadEffect = Aurora.Features.Acrylic, window.Effects.Acrylic
		Aurora.Features.Acrylic = false            -- stand where a Free build stands, for a moment
		local applied, report = window:ApplyPreset({
			Name = "cross edition", Edition = "Elite", Background = { Name = "Stars" },
			Effects = { Acrylic = true, AcrylicTint = 0.42, Ripples = true },
		}, true)
		local told = false
		for _, entry in ipairs((report and report.Skipped) or {}) do
			if entry.Key == "Effects.Acrylic" and entry.Needs == "Acrylic" then told = true end
		end
		local heldOff = window.Effects.Acrylic == false and window.Effects.Ripples == true
		local carried = window:GetPreset("cross edition")
		local kept = type(carried.Carried) == "table" and type(carried.Carried.Effects) == "table"
			and carried.Carried.Effects.Acrylic == true and carried.Carried.Effects.AcrylicTint == 0.42
		-- and it is never written out as false, which would switch it off in the build that has it
		local notFalsified = carried.Effects.Acrylic == nil
		Aurora.Features.Acrylic = hadFeature
		local restored = true
		if Aurora:Has("Acrylic") then
			window:ApplyPreset(carried, true)
			restored = window.Effects.Acrylic == true and window:GetPreset("x").Carried == nil
		end
		window.Effects.Acrylic = hadEffect
		window._presetCarry = nil
		return applied and told and heldOff and kept and notFalsified and restored
	end)

	check("a preset can be read before it is applied", function()
		local report = window:PresetReport({ Name = "probe", Edition = "Elite", Background = { Name = "No Such Scene" } })
		local found = false
		for _, entry in ipairs(report.Skipped) do
			if entry.Key == "Background" then found = true end
		end
		return type(report) == "table" and report.Edition == Aurora.Edition and found
			and window.BackgroundName ~= "No Such Scene"   -- reading a preset changes nothing
	end)

	check("a scene built from numbers registers, renders and round-trips as code", function()
		local before = window.BackgroundName
		local spec = Aurora:RegisterParametric("test scene", { Shape = "Ring", Flow = "Swirl", Count = 8, Size = 12 })
		local registered = Aurora.Backgrounds["test scene"] ~= nil and spec.Shape == "Ring" and spec.Count == 8
		window:SetBackground("test scene")
		local shown = window.BackgroundName == "test scene"
		local back, name = Aurora:ParseBackground(Aurora:EncodeBackground(spec, "test scene"))
		local removed = Aurora:RemoveParametric("test scene")
		window:SetBackground(before or "Stars")
		return registered and shown and removed and name == "test scene"
			and type(back) == "table" and back.Shape == "Ring" and back.Flow == "Swirl"
			and Aurora.Backgrounds["test scene"] == nil
			and table.find(Aurora.BackgroundOrder, "test scene") == nil
	end, "BackgroundStudio")

	check("automatic optimization climbs to hold a frame rate and stops at its ceiling", function()
		local before = window:GetOptimization()
		window:SetOptimization(1, true)
		window:SetAutoOptimize(true, { Target = 240, Ceiling = 3, Patience = 0.5, Relax = 30 })
		window._optimizeTouchedAt = 0           -- it keeps out of the way after a change made by hand
		for _ = 1, 120 do
			window:_StepAutoOptimize(1 / 20)    -- 20 frames a second against a 240 target
		end
		local climbed = window:GetOptimization() > 1 and window:GetOptimization() <= 3
		window:SetAutoOptimize(false)
		window:SetOptimization(before, true)
		return climbed and window.AutoOptimize == nil
	end, "AutoOptimize")

	check("a page can be kept out of the trimmer", function()
		local report = window:TrimReport()
		local pinned = window:PinPage(page, true)
		local seen = false
		for _, row in ipairs(window:TrimReport()) do
			if row.Pinned then seen = true end
		end
		window:PinPage(page, false)
		return type(report) == "table" and #report > 0 and pinned and seen and page.NeverTrim == false
	end, "PageOptimization")

	check("every background this edition ships builds, steps and settles", function()
		local before = window.BackgroundName
		local built, now = 0, os.clock()
		for _, name in ipairs(Aurora.BackgroundOrder) do
			window:SetBackground(name)
			window:_StepBackground(1 / 60, now)
			window:_StepBackground(1 / 60, now + 0.5)
			if window.BackgroundName == name then
				built = built + 1
			end
		end
		window:SetBackground(before or Aurora.FallbackBackground)
		return built == #Aurora.BackgroundOrder and built >= 5
	end)

	check("the elements this edition does not have come back locked, not missing", function()
		local slide = page:AddSlideBox({ Title = "Slide probe", Flag = "__slideProbe" })
		local code = page:AddCodeBox({ Title = "Code probe", Text = "print(1)" })
		-- whichever edition this is, both answer; only one of them is the real thing
		local answered = slide ~= nil and code ~= nil and type(code.Get) == "function"
		local slideRight = (slide.Locked == true) == (not Aurora:Has("SlideBox"))
		local codeRight = (code.Locked == true) == (not Aurora:Has("CodeBox"))
		return answered and slideRight and codeRight
	end)

	check("a remembered key is sealed to this player and this place", function()
		local salt = window:_KeySalt()
		local sealed = Aurora:Seal("ABC-123", salt)
		return type(sealed) == "string" and sealed ~= "ABC-123"
			and Aurora:Unseal(sealed, salt) == "ABC-123"
			and Aurora:Unseal(sealed, "a different player") ~= "ABC-123"
			and Aurora:Unseal("not hex at all", salt) == nil
	end, "KeySecurity")

	check("window tears down cleanly", function()
		window:Destroy()
		return true
	end)

	local header = ("AuroraUI %s self check: %d passed, %d failed, %d skipped"):format(
		tostring(Aurora.Edition or "?"), passed, failed, skipped)
	if options.Silent ~= true then
		print(header)
		for _, line in ipairs(lines) do
			print(line)
		end
	end
	return { Passed = passed, Failed = failed, Skipped = skipped, Edition = Aurora.Edition, Lines = lines, Header = header }
end


================================================================================
FILE 3: AuroraUI_Core.lua
================================================================================

--[[
	AuroraUI / Core
	The second half of the library. AuroraUI's main module requires this one and calls it with a table of
	everything it has already built, so both halves share one set of metatables, helpers and services.

	Lives at:  AuroraUI (ModuleScript) > Core (Folder) > Core (ModuleScript)

	What is in here:
	  - the code box and its IDE mode (file explorer, tabs, find and replace, minimap, output)
	  - the extra elements: tabs, progress, stat, image, video, chips, table, console, stepper,
	    range slider and the gradient editor
	  - floating panels and the theme editor page
	  - the key gate
	  - the logic runtime, the node library, the node editor, automations and the Logic page
]]

return function(Shared)

	-- everything the main module already built
	local Alive = Shared.Alive
	local Aurora = Shared.Aurora
	local CodeEngine = Shared.CodeEngine
	local Container = Shared.Container
	local Create = Shared.Create
	local Element = Shared.Element
	local FadeTree = Shared.FadeTree
	local FormatNumber = Shared.FormatNumber
	local HasFileSystem = Shared.HasFileSystem
	local IsClick = Shared.IsClick
	local IsMove = Shared.IsMove
	local Keypoint = Shared.Keypoint
	local Lerp = Shared.Lerp
	local Lighting = Shared.Lighting
	local LocalPlayer = Shared.LocalPlayer
	local Maid = Shared.Maid
	local MakeCheck = Shared.MakeCheck
	local MakeChevron = Shared.MakeChevron
	local Merge = Shared.Merge
	local NumKey = Shared.NumKey
	local Page = Shared.Page
	local Parallax = Shared.Parallax
	local Players = Shared.Players
	local RoundTo = Shared.RoundTo
	local RowHeight = Shared.RowHeight
	local RunService = Shared.RunService
	local SafeCall = Shared.SafeCall
	local Section = Shared.Section
	local StaggerIn = Shared.StaggerIn
	local TextService = Shared.TextService
	local Timed = Shared.Timed
	local Tween = Shared.Tween
	local UserInputService = Shared.UserInputService
	local Window = Shared.Window

	function Container:AddCodeBox(options) return self:_Locked("CodeBox", options) end

	-- ============================================================================
	-- MORE ELEMENTS
	-- ============================================================================

	-- TABS (segmented control with a container per tab) -------------------------
	-- Options: Tabs = {"General", "Advanced"}, Default
	function Container:AddTabs(options)
		options = options or {}
		local win = self.Window
		local names = options.Tabs or { "Tab 1", "Tab 2" }
		options.Title = options.Title or table.concat(names, " / ")
		local element = self:_CreateBase("Tabs", options, 0, true)
		local frame = element.Frame
		frame.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
		frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
		frame.ClipsDescendants = false
		frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		element.Stroke.Enabled = false
		if element.Separator then
			element.Separator.Visible = false
		end
		-- the bar scrolls sideways, so tabs added later have somewhere to go
		local bar = Create("ScrollingFrame", {
			Name = "Bar", BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("ElementActive"), BackgroundTransparency = 0.4, BorderSizePixel = 0,
			Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 6), Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 30), CanvasSize = UDim2.new(),
			ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.X, ScrollBarThickness = 0, ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.Never,
			ClipsDescendants = true, Parent = frame,
		})
		win:_Corner(bar, 8)
		win:_Bind(bar, "BackgroundColor3", "ElementActive")
		local pill = Create("Frame", { Name = "Pill", BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("Element"), BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 3), Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 1, -6), ZIndex = 2, Parent = bar })
		win:_Corner(pill, 6)
		win:_Bind(pill, "BackgroundColor3", "Element")
		Create("UIStroke", { Color = win:_T("Stroke"), Transparency = 0.7, Parent = pill })
		local body = Create("Frame", { Name = "Body", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 42), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Parent = frame })

		local tabs = {}
		local current = nil
		local function indexOf(name)
			for index, tab in ipairs(tabs) do
				if tab.Name == name then
					return index
				end
			end
			return nil
		end
		-- Tabs share the bar while they fit and keep a readable width once they do not, at which point the bar scrolls.
		local function relayout(animate)
			local count = #tabs
			pill.Visible = count > 0
			if count == 0 then
				bar.CanvasSize = UDim2.new()
				return
			end
			local available = bar.AbsoluteSize.X
			if available < 10 then
				-- the bar has not been measured yet; lay out once it has
				task.defer(function()
					if Alive(bar) then
						relayout(false)
					end
				end)
				available = 240
			end
			local width = math.max(88, available / count)
			if width * count <= available then
				width = available / count
			end
			for index, tab in ipairs(tabs) do
				tab.Button.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((index - 1) * width, 0)
				tab.Button.Size = UDim2.new(0, width, 1, 0)
			end
			bar.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(width * count, 0)
			pill.Size = UDim2.new(0, width - 6, 1, -6)
			local goal = UDim2.fromOffset((math.max(1, indexOf(current) or 1) - 1) * width + 3, 3)
			if animate then
				Tween(pill, { Position = goal }, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
			else
				pill.Position = goal
			end
		end
		local function selectTab(name)
			if current == name then
				return
			end
			current = name
			for _, tab in ipairs(tabs) do
				local active = tab.Name == name
				tab.Container.List.Visible = active
				Tween(tab.Label, { TextColor3 = win:_T(active and "Text" or "SubText") }, 0.2)
			end
			relayout(true)
			element:_SetFlag(name)
			SafeCall(options.Callback, name)
		end
		local function makeTab(name)
			name = tostring(name)
			local button = Create("TextButton", {
				Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
				Size = UDim2.new(0, 88, 1, 0), ZIndex = 3, Parent = bar,
			})
			local label = win:_Label({ Text = name, TextSize = 12, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = button }, "Bold", "SubText")
			local list = Create("Frame", { Name = name, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Visible = false, Parent = body })
			Create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 6), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = list })
			local container = setmetatable({ Window = win, Page = self.Page, List = list, Elements = {}, Name = name }, Container)
			tabs[#tabs + 1] = { Name = name, Label = label, Button = button, List = list, Container = container }
			button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				win:_Sound("Click")
				selectTab(name)
			end)
			return container
		end
		for _, name in ipairs(names) do
			makeTab(name)
		end
		element.Maid:Add(bar:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function()
			relayout(false)
		end))

		-- tabs:AddTab("Name") -> the new tab's container; tabs:RemoveTab("Name") takes it away again
		function element:AddTab(name)
			name = tostring(name)
			local existing = self:Tab(name)
			if existing then
				return existing
			end
			local container = makeTab(name)
			relayout(false)
			return container
		end
		function element:RemoveTab(name)
			name = tostring(name)
			local index = indexOf(name)
			-- the last tab stays unless the strip was made with AllowEmpty = true
			if not index or (#tabs <= 1 and not options.AllowEmpty) then
				return false
			end
			local tab = tabs[index]
			-- take the rows out properly, or search keeps finding controls that are no longer on screen
			local elements = tab.Container.Elements or {}
			for position = #elements, 1, -1 do
				local child = elements[position]
				if type(child.Destroy) == "function" then
					child:Destroy()
				end
			end
			tab.Button:Destroy()
			tab.List:Destroy()
			table.remove(tabs, index)
			if #tabs == 0 then
				current = nil
				relayout(false)
			elseif current == name then
				current = nil
				selectTab(tabs[math.min(index, #tabs)].Name)
			else
				relayout(false)
			end
			return true
		end
		function element:TabNames()
			local out = {}
			for index, tab in ipairs(tabs) do
				out[index] = tab.Name
			end
			return out
		end
		function element:Tab(name)
			for _, tab in ipairs(tabs) do
				if tab.Name == name then
					return tab.Container
				end
			end
			return nil
		end
		function element:Select(nameOrDelta)
			if type(nameOrDelta) == "number" then
				if #tabs == 0 then
					return
				end
				local index = 1
				for i, tab in ipairs(tabs) do
					if tab.Name == current then index = i end
				end
				index = ((index - 1 + nameOrDelta) % #tabs) + 1
				selectTab(tabs[index].Name)
			else
				selectTab(nameOrDelta)
			end
		end
		function element:Set(name, silent)
			selectTab(name)
		end
		function element:Get()
			return current
		end
		selectTab(options.Default or names[1])
		return element
	end

	-- PROGRESS BAR ---------------------------------------------------------------
	-- Options: Title, Description, Min, Max, Value, Suffix, Decimals, Color (theme token), ShowValue
	function Container:AddProgress(options)
		options = options or {}
		local win = self.Window
		local element = self:_CreateBase("Progress", options, RowHeight(options, 14), true)
		local frame = element.Frame
		self:_LabelPair(element, options, 80)
		local min, max = options.Min or 0, options.Max or 100
		local value = math.clamp(options.Value or min, min, max)
		local valueLabel = win:_Label({ Text = "", TextSize = 12, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(90, 40), Parent = frame }, "Bold", options.Color or "Accent")
		local track = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("ElementActive"), BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 1, -12), Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 5), Parent = frame }, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
		win:_Bind(track, "BackgroundColor3", "ElementActive")
		local fill = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = win:_T(options.Color or "Accent"), BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), Parent = track }, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
		win:_Bind(fill, "BackgroundColor3", options.Color or "Accent")
		local shine = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 0.7, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0), Parent = fill }, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
		-- a light sweeps along the bar whenever it grows
		fill.ClipsDescendants = true
		local sweep = Create("Frame", {
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, -30, 0, 0),
			Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 1, 0), Parent = fill,
		}, { Create("UIGradient", { Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumKey(0, 1), NumKey(0.5, 0.3), NumKey(1, 1) }) }) })
		local function render(animate)
			local pct = (value - min) / math.max(1e-6, max - min)
			if options.ShowValue ~= false then
				valueLabel.Text = options.Percent and (math.floor(pct * 100 + 0.5) .. "%") or FormatNumber(value, options.Decimals or 0, nil, options.Prefix, options.Suffix)
			end
			Tween(fill, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(pct, 1) }, animate and 0.4 or 0, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		end
		render(false)
		element:_SetFlag(value)
		function element:Set(newValue, silent)
			local previous = value
			value = math.clamp(tonumber(newValue) or min, min, max)
			render(true)
			if value > previous then
				sweep.Position = UDim2.new(0, -30, 0, 0)
				sweep.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
				Tween(sweep, { Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0) }, 0.7, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
				task.delay(Timed(0.5), function()
					if Alive(sweep) then
						Tween(sweep, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, 0.2)
					end
				end)
			end
			self:_SetFlag(value)
		end
		function element:Get()
			return value
		end
		function element:SetRange(newMin, newMax)
			min, max = newMin, newMax
			render(true)
		end
		return element
	end

	-- STAT ROW -------------------------------------------------------------------
	-- Options: Title, Description, Value, Suffix, Color, Trend ("up"/"down"/nil)
	function Container:AddStat(options)
		options = options or {}
		local win = self.Window
		local element = self:_CreateBase("Stat", options, RowHeight(options), true)
		local frame = element.Frame
		self:_LabelPair(element, options, 140)
		local valueLabel = win:_Label({ Text = tostring(options.Value or "0") .. (options.Suffix or ""), TextSize = 18, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(150, 24), Parent = frame }, "Bold", options.Color or "Text")
		local lastValue = tonumber(options.Value)
		function element:Set(newValue, animate)
			local text = tostring(newValue) .. (options.Suffix or "")
			local numeric = tonumber(newValue)
			local token = {}
			self._countToken = token
			if animate ~= false and numeric and lastValue and numeric ~= lastValue then
				local up = numeric > lastValue
				Tween(valueLabel, { TextColor3 = win:_T(up and "Success" or "Error") }, 0.15)
				task.delay(Timed(0.6), function()
					Tween(valueLabel, { TextColor3 = win:_T(options.Color or "Text") }, 0.4)
				end)
				valueLabel.Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, up and 6 or -6)
				Tween(valueLabel, { Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0) }, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
				-- the number counts from the old value to the new one
				local from, started = lastValue, os.clock()
				local decimals = math.min(6, #(tostring(newValue):match("%.(%d+)") or ""))
				task.spawn(function()
					while self._countToken == token and Alive(valueLabel) do
						local progress = math.min(1, (os.clock() - started) / 0.45)
						if progress >= 1 then
							break
						end
						local eased = 1 - (1 - progress) ^ 3
						valueLabel.Text = string.format("%." .. decimals .. "f", from + (numeric - from) * eased) .. (options.Suffix or "")
						task.wait()
					end
					if self._countToken == token and Alive(valueLabel) then
						valueLabel.Text = text
					end
				end)
			else
				valueLabel.Text = text
			end
			lastValue = numeric
			self:_SetFlag(newValue)
		end
		function element:Get()
			return lastValue
		end
		return element
	end

	-- IMAGE ---------------------------------------------------------------------
	-- Options: Image, Height, ScaleType, Title, Tint, Rounded
	function Container:AddImage(options)
		options = options or {}
		local win = self.Window
		local height = options.Height or 160
		options.Title = options.Title or "Image"
		local element = self:_CreateBase("Image", options, height + 16, true)
		if element.Separator then
			element.Separator.Visible = false
		end
		local image = Create("ImageLabel", {
			BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("ElementActive"), BorderSizePixel = 0, Image = options.Image or "", Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 8),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, height), ScaleType = options.ScaleType or Enum.ScaleType.Crop, ImageColor3 = options.Tint or Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Parent = element.Frame,
		})
		win:_Corner(image, options.Rounded == false and 0 or 10)
		win:_Bind(image, "BackgroundColor3", "ElementActive")
		function element:Set(id)
			image.Image = id
		end
		element.Image = image
		return element
	end

	-- VIDEO ---------------------------------------------------------------------
	-- Options: Video (asset id), Height, Looped, Playing, Volume, Title
	function Container:AddVideo(options)
		options = options or {}
		local win = self.Window
		local height = options.Height or 180
		options.Title = options.Title or "Video"
		local element = self:_CreateBase("Video", options, height + 16, true)
		if element.Separator then
			element.Separator.Visible = false
		end
		local ok, video = pcall(function()
			return Create("VideoFrame", {
				BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BorderSizePixel = 0, Video = options.Video or "", Looped = options.Looped ~= false,
				Playing = options.Playing ~= false, Volume = options.Volume or 0.5, Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 8), Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, height), Parent = element.Frame,
			})
		end)
		if ok and video then
			win:_Corner(video, 10)
			element.Video = video
		else
			win:_Label({ Text = "VideoFrame is not available here", TextSize = 12, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Parent = element.Frame }, "Medium", "SubText")
		end
		function element:Play() if element.Video then element.Video.Playing = true end end
		function element:Pause() if element.Video then element.Video.Playing = false end end
		function element:Set(id) if element.Video then element.Video.Video = id end end
		return element
	end

	-- CHIPS (multi-toggle pills) -------------------------------------------------
	-- Options: Title, Options, Default (table or value), Multi (default true), Callback(values)
	function Container:AddChips(options)
		options = options or {}
		local win = self.Window
		local element = self:_CreateBase("Chips", options, 0, true)
		local frame = element.Frame
		frame.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
		frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
		local multi = options.Multi ~= false
		local selected = {}
		if type(options.Default) == "table" then
			for _, v in ipairs(options.Default) do selected[v] = true end
		elseif options.Default ~= nil then
			selected[options.Default] = true
		end
		local titleLabel = win:_Label({ Text = tostring(options.Title or ""), TextSize = 14, Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 34), Parent = frame }, "Medium", "Text")
		element.TitleLabel = titleLabel
		local holder = Create("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 32), Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Parent = frame })
		Create("UIPadding", { PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 12), Parent = holder })
		local layout = Create("UIListLayout", { FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, Padding = UDim.new(0, 6), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = holder })
		pcall(function() layout.Wraps = true end)
		local chips = {}
		local function values()
			local out = {}
			for _, option in ipairs(options.Options or {}) do
				if selected[option] then out[#out + 1] = option end
			end
			return out
		end
		local function refresh()
			for _, chip in ipairs(chips) do
				local on = selected[chip.Option] == true
				Tween(chip.Button, { BackgroundColor3 = win:_T(on and "Accent" or "ElementActive") }, 0.15)
				Tween(chip.Label, { TextColor3 = win:_T(on and "AccentText" or "Text") }, 0.15)
			end
		end
		for order, option in ipairs(options.Options or {}) do
			local button = Create("TextButton", {
				Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("ElementActive"), BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 26),
				AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, ClipsDescendants = true, LayoutOrder = order, Parent = holder,
			}, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
			Create("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 12), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 12), Parent = button })
			local label = win:_Label({ Text = tostring(option), TextSize = 12, Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Parent = button }, "Medium", false)
			label.TextColor3 = win:_T("Text")
			chips[#chips + 1] = { Button = button, Label = label, Option = option }
			button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				win:_Ripple(button, nil, "AccentText")
				win:_Sound("Click")
				local bump = button:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale") or Create("UIScale", { Scale = 1, Parent = button })
				bump.Scale = 0.9
				Tween(bump, { Scale = 1 }, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
				if multi then
					selected[option] = not selected[option] or nil
				else
					selected = { [option] = true }
				end
				refresh()
				local current = multi and values() or option
				element:_SetFlag(current)
				SafeCall(options.Callback, current)
			end)
		end
		win:_OnTheme(refresh)
		element:_SetFlag(multi and values() or values()[1])
		function element:Set(value, silent)
			selected = {}
			if type(value) == "table" then
				for _, v in ipairs(value) do selected[v] = true end
			elseif value ~= nil then
				selected[value] = true
			end
			refresh()
			local current = multi and values() or values()[1]
			self:_SetFlag(current)
			if not silent then SafeCall(options.Callback, current) end
		end
		function element:Get()
			return multi and values() or values()[1]
		end
		return element
	end

	-- TABLE ----------------------------------------------------------------------
	-- Options: Columns = {"Name", "Score"}, Rows = {{"a", 1}}, Widths = {0.6, 0.4}, Sortable, Height, RowHeight, OnRowClick(row, index)
	function Container:AddTable(options)
		options = options or {}
		local win = self.Window
		local columns = options.Columns or { "Column" }
		local rowHeight = options.RowHeight or 26
		local maxHeight = options.Height or 200
		options.Title = options.Title or table.concat(columns, ", ")
		options.Filled = true
		local element = self:_CreateBase("Table", options, 30 + 8, true)
		local frame = element.Frame
		local widths = options.Widths or {}
		local header = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("ElementActive"), BackgroundTransparency = 0.5, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30), Parent = frame })
		win:_Bind(header, "BackgroundColor3", "ElementActive")
		local scroll = Create("ScrollingFrame", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 30), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -30), CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
			AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ScrollBarThickness = 3, ScrollBarImageColor3 = win:_T("Accent"), ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y, Parent = frame,
		})
		win:_Bind(scroll, "ScrollBarImageColor3", "Accent")
		Create("UIListLayout", { SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = scroll })
		local function colWidth(index)
			return widths[index] or (1 / #columns)
		end
		local function colX(index)
			local x = 0
			for i = 1, index - 1 do x = x + colWidth(i) end
			return x
		end
		local rows = options.Rows or {}
		local sortColumn, sortAscending = nil, true
		local rowFrames = {}
		local headerLabels = {}
		local function rebuild()
			for _, rowFrame in ipairs(rowFrames) do rowFrame:Destroy() end
			rowFrames = {}
			local ordered = {}
			for index, row in ipairs(rows) do ordered[index] = row end
			if sortColumn then
				table.sort(ordered, function(a, b)
					local va, vb = a[sortColumn], b[sortColumn]
					if type(va) == "number" and type(vb) == "number" then
						if sortAscending then return va < vb else return va > vb end
					end
					if sortAscending then return tostring(va) < tostring(vb) else return tostring(va) > tostring(vb) end
				end)
			end
			for order, row in ipairs(ordered) do
				local rowFrame = Create("TextButton", {
					Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("ElementHover"), BackgroundTransparency = (order % 2 == 0) and 0.7 or 1,
					BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, rowHeight), LayoutOrder = order, Parent = scroll,
				})
				win:_Bind(rowFrame, "BackgroundColor3", "ElementHover")
				for index = 1, #columns do
					local cell = row[index]
					win:_Label({
						Text = tostring(cell == nil and "" or cell), TextSize = 12, Position = UDim2.new(colX(index), 14, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(colWidth(index), -16, 1, 0), Parent = rowFrame,
					}, "Regular", index == 1 and "Text" or "SubText")
				end
				local base = rowFrame.BackgroundTransparency
				rowFrame.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tween(rowFrame, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.3 }, 0.12) end)
				rowFrame.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tween(rowFrame, { BackgroundTransparency = base }, 0.15) end)
				rowFrame.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					SafeCall(options.OnRowClick, row, order)
				end)
				rowFrames[#rowFrames + 1] = rowFrame
			end
			local height = math.min(maxHeight, math.max(rowHeight, #ordered * rowHeight))
			frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30 + height)
			for index, label in ipairs(headerLabels) do
				local arrow = (sortColumn == index) and (sortAscending and "  ▲" or "  ▼") or ""
				label.Text = tostring(columns[index]) .. arrow
			end
		end
		for index, name in ipairs(columns) do
			local button = Create("TextButton", { Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(colX(index), 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(colWidth(index), 0, 1, 0), Parent = header })
			local label = win:_Label({ Text = tostring(name), TextSize = 11, Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, 0), Parent = button }, "Bold", "SubText")
			headerLabels[index] = label
			if options.Sortable ~= false then
				button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					win:_Sound("Click")
					if sortColumn == index then
						sortAscending = not sortAscending
					else
						sortColumn, sortAscending = index, true
					end
					rebuild()
				end)
			end
		end
		rebuild()
		function element:SetRows(newRows) rows = newRows or {}; rebuild() end
		function element:AddRow(row) rows[#rows + 1] = row; rebuild() end
		function element:Clear() rows = {}; rebuild() end
		function element:Sort(columnIndex, ascending) sortColumn, sortAscending = columnIndex, ascending ~= false; rebuild() end
		function element:GetRows() return rows end
		return element
	end

	-- CONSOLE / LOG --------------------------------------------------------------
	-- Options: Title, Height, MaxLines, Timestamps, Filter (search box), Levels colours via theme
	function Container:AddConsole(options)
		options = options or {}
		local win = self.Window
		local height = options.Height or 200
		options.Title = options.Title or "Console"
		options.Filled = true
		local element = self:_CreateBase("Console", options, 34 + height, true)
		local frame = element.Frame
		local title = win:_Label({ Text = options.Title, TextSize = 13, Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0.4, 0, 0, 34), Parent = frame }, "Bold", "Text")
		element.TitleLabel = title
		local clearButton = Create("TextButton", { Text = "Clear", TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = win:_T("SubText"), BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, 17), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(40, 20), Parent = frame })
		win:_Font(clearButton, "Bold")
		win:_Bind(clearButton, "TextColor3", "SubText")
		local filterBox = nil
		if options.Filter ~= false then
			local holder = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("ElementActive"), BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -60, 0, 17), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(130, 22), Parent = frame })
			win:_Corner(holder, 6)
			win:_Bind(holder, "BackgroundColor3", "ElementActive")
			filterBox = Create("TextBox", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", PlaceholderText = "Filter", PlaceholderColor3 = win:_T("Placeholder"), TextColor3 = win:_T("Text"), TextSize = 11, ClearTextOnFocus = false, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, 0), Parent = holder })
			win:_Font(filterBox, "Medium")
			win:_Bind(filterBox, "TextColor3", "Text")
		end
		local area = Create("ScrollingFrame", {
			BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("Code.Background"), BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 34), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -34), CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
			AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ScrollBarThickness = 3, ScrollBarImageColor3 = win:_T("Accent"), ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y, Parent = frame,
		})
		win:_Bind(area, "BackgroundColor3", "Code.Background")
		win:_Bind(area, "ScrollBarImageColor3", "Accent")
		Create("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10), PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6), Parent = area })
		Create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 2), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = area })
		local lines = {}
		local maxLines = options.MaxLines or 200
		local order = 0
		local levelTokens = { Info = "Text", Warn = "Warning", Warning = "Warning", Error = "Error", Success = "Success", Debug = "SubText" }
		local function applyFilter()
			local query = filterBox and filterBox.Text:lower() or ""
			for _, line in ipairs(lines) do
				line.Label.Visible = query == "" or line.Raw:lower():find(query, 1, true) ~= nil
			end
		end
		function element:Log(text, level)
			level = level or "Info"
			order = order + 1
			local stamp = ""
			if options.Timestamps ~= false then
				stamp = os.date("%H:%M:%S") .. "  "
			end
			local raw = stamp .. tostring(text)
			local label = win:_Label({
				Text = CodeEngine and CodeEngine.Escape(raw) or raw, TextSize = 12, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, TextWrapped = true,
				TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None, LayoutOrder = order, Parent = area,
			}, "Code", levelTokens[level] or "Text")
			label.Font = win.Fonts.Code
			lines[#lines + 1] = { Label = label, Raw = raw, Level = level }
			label.TextTransparency = 1
			Tween(label, { TextTransparency = 0 }, 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
			while #lines > maxLines do
				local first = table.remove(lines, 1)
				first.Label:Destroy()
			end
			applyFilter()
			task.defer(function()
				if Alive(area) then
					area.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(0, area.AbsoluteCanvasSize.Y - area.AbsoluteSize.Y))
				end
			end)
			return label
		end
		function element:Info(text) return self:Log(text, "Info") end
		function element:Warn(text) return self:Log(text, "Warn") end
		function element:Error(text) return self:Log(text, "Error") end
		function element:Success(text) return self:Log(text, "Success") end
		function element:Clear()
			for _, line in ipairs(lines) do line.Label:Destroy() end
			lines = {}
		end
		function element:GetLines()
			local out = {}
			for index, line in ipairs(lines) do out[index] = line.Raw end
			return out
		end
		clearButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() element:Clear() end)
		if filterBox then
			filterBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(applyFilter)
		end
		return element
	end

	-- NUMBER STEPPER -------------------------------------------------------------
	-- Options: Title, Description, Min, Max, Default, Increment, Decimals, Suffix, Callback
	function Container:AddStepper(options)
		options = options or {}
		local win = self.Window
		local element = self:_CreateBase("Stepper", options, RowHeight(options))
		local frame = element.Frame
		self:_LabelPair(element, options, 130)
		local min, max = options.Min or -math.huge, options.Max or math.huge
		local increment = options.Increment or 1
		local value = math.clamp(options.Default or 0, min, max)
		element.Step = increment
		element.Default = value
		local minus = self:_MiniButton(frame, UDim2.fromOffset(26, 26), UDim2.new(1, -(14 + 70 + 26 + 6), 0.5, 0))
		local plus = self:_MiniButton(frame, UDim2.fromOffset(26, 26), UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0))
		local function glyph(parent, isPlus)
			local h = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("Text"), BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 2), Parent = parent }, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
			win:_Bind(h, "BackgroundColor3", "Text")
			if isPlus then
				local v = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("Text"), BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(2, 10), Parent = parent }, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
				win:_Bind(v, "BackgroundColor3", "Text")
			end
		end
		glyph(minus, false)
		glyph(plus, true)
		local valueBox = Create("TextBox", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = win:_T("Accent"), ClearTextOnFocus = false, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -(14 + 26 + 3), 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(64, 26), Parent = frame,
		})
		win:_Font(valueBox, "Bold")
		win:_Bind(valueBox, "TextColor3", "Accent")
		local function render()
			valueBox.Text = FormatNumber(value, options.Decimals or 0, nil, options.Prefix, options.Suffix)
		end
		local function setValue(newValue, silent)
			newValue = math.clamp(RoundTo(newValue, increment), min, max)
			local changed = newValue ~= value
			value = newValue
			render()
			if changed then
				local bump = valueBox:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale") or Create("UIScale", { Scale = 1, Parent = valueBox })
				bump.Scale = 1.14
				Tween(bump, { Scale = 1 }, 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
			end
			if changed then
				element:_SetFlag(value)
				if not silent then SafeCall(options.Callback, value) end
			end
		end
		render()
		element:_SetFlag(value)
		local function hold(button, direction)
			local holding = false
			button.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
				holding = true
				win:_Ripple(button)
				setValue(value + increment * direction)
				task.delay(Timed(0.4), function()
					while holding and Alive(button) do
						setValue(value + increment * direction)
						task.wait(0.06)
					end
				end)
			end)
			button.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() holding = false end)
			button.MouseLeave:Connect(function() holding = false end)
		end
		hold(minus, -1)
		hold(plus, 1)
		valueBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
			local parsed = tonumber((valueBox.Text:gsub("[^%d%.%-]", "")))
			if parsed then setValue(parsed) else render() end
		end)
		function element:Set(newValue, silent) setValue(tonumber(newValue) or value, silent) end
		function element:Get() return value end
		return element
	end

	-- RANGE SLIDER (two knobs) ---------------------------------------------------
	-- Options: Title, Description, Min, Max, DefaultMin, DefaultMax, Increment, Decimals, Suffix, Callback(low, high)
	function Container:AddRangeSlider(options)
		options = options or {}
		local win = self.Window
		local element = self:_CreateBase("RangeSlider", options, RowHeight(options, 18))
		local frame = element.Frame
		self:_LabelPair(element, options, 120)
		local min, max = options.Min or 0, options.Max or 100
		local increment = options.Increment or 0
		local low = math.clamp(options.DefaultMin or min, min, max)
		local high = math.clamp(options.DefaultMax or max, min, max)
		element.Default = { low, high }
		local valueLabel = win:_Label({ Text = "", TextSize = 12, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(130, 40), Parent = frame }, "Bold", "Accent")
		local trackY = RowHeight(options, 18) - 15
		local track = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("ElementActive"), BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, trackY), Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 4), ZIndex = 2, Parent = frame }, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
		win:_Bind(track, "BackgroundColor3", "ElementActive")
		local fill = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("Accent"), BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), ZIndex = 3, Parent = track }, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
		win:_Bind(fill, "BackgroundColor3", "Accent")
		local function knob()
			local k = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12), ZIndex = 5, Parent = track }, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
			local stroke = Create("UIStroke", { Color = win:_T("Accent"), Thickness = 2, Parent = k })
			win:_Bind(stroke, "Color", "Accent")
			return k
		end
		local lowKnob, highKnob = knob(), knob()
		local hit = Create("TextButton", { Text = "", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, trackY - 12), Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 28), ZIndex = 6, Parent = frame })
		local function render(animate)
			local pl = (low - min) / (max - min)
			local ph = (high - min) / (max - min)
			valueLabel.Text = FormatNumber(low, options.Decimals or 0, nil, nil, options.Suffix) .. "  –  " .. FormatNumber(high, options.Decimals or 0, nil, nil, options.Suffix)
			local time = animate and 0.12 or 0
			Tween(fill, { Position = UDim2.fromScale(pl, 0), Size = UDim2.fromScale(ph - pl, 1) }, time)
			Tween(lowKnob, { Position = UDim2.new(pl, 0, 0.5, 0) }, time)
			Tween(highKnob, { Position = UDim2.new(ph, 0, 0.5, 0) }, time)
		end
		render(false)
		element:_SetFlag({ low, high })
		local dragging = nil
		local function fromPosition(x)
			local pct = math.clamp((x - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, track.AbsoluteSize.X), 0, 1)
			local value = math.clamp(RoundTo(min + pct * (max - min) - min, increment) + min, min, max)
			if not dragging then
				dragging = (math.abs(value - low) <= math.abs(value - high)) and "low" or "high"
			end
			if dragging == "low" then low = math.min(value, high) else high = math.max(value, low) end
			render(true)
			element:_SetFlag({ low, high })
			SafeCall(options.Callback, low, high)
		end
		hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if IsClick(input) then
				dragging = nil
				fromPosition(win:_Pointer().X)
			end
		end)
		element.Maid:Add(win:_OnInput("Changed", function(input)
			if dragging and IsMove(input) then fromPosition(win:_Pointer().X) end
		end))
		element.Maid:Add(win:_OnInput("Ended", function(input)
			if dragging and IsClick(input) then dragging = nil end
		end))
		function element:Set(newLow, newHigh, silent)
			if type(newLow) == "table" then newLow, newHigh, silent = newLow[1], newLow[2], newHigh end
			low = math.clamp(tonumber(newLow) or low, min, max)
			high = math.clamp(tonumber(newHigh) or high, low, max)
			render(true)
			self:_SetFlag({ low, high })
			if not silent then SafeCall(options.Callback, low, high) end
		end
		function element:Get() return low, high end
		return element
	end

	-- GRADIENT EDITOR ------------------------------------------------------------
	-- Options: Title, Description, Default (ColorSequence), Callback(ColorSequence), Flag, Rotation (show rotation slider)
	function Container:AddGradient(options)
		options = options or {}
		local win = self.Window
		local baseHeight = RowHeight(options)
		local editorHeight = 190 + (options.Rotation and 30 or 0)
		local element = self:_CreateBase("Gradient", options, baseHeight)
		local frame = element.Frame
		self:_LabelPair(element, options, 110)

		local points = {}
		local function loadSequence(sequence)
			points = {}
			for _, kp in ipairs(sequence.Keypoints) do
				points[#points + 1] = { Time = kp.Time, Color = kp.Value }
			end
		end
		loadSequence(options.Default or ColorSequence.new({ Keypoint(0, win:_T("Accent")), Keypoint(1, win:_T("AccentSecondary")) }))
		local rotation = options.DefaultRotation or 0
		local selected = 1
		local open = false

		local function sequence()
			table.sort(points, function(a, b) return a.Time < b.Time end)
			points[1].Time = 0
			points[#points].Time = 1
			local kps = {}
			for index, point in ipairs(points) do
				kps[index] = Keypoint(point.Time, point.Color)
			end
			return ColorSequence.new(kps)
		end
		local function serialise()
			local out = {}
			for index, point in ipairs(points) do
				out[index] = { point.Time, point.Color.R, point.Color.G, point.Color.B }
			end
			out.Rotation = rotation
			return out
		end

		-- header preview
		local preview = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, baseHeight / 2), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(96, 22), Parent = frame })
		win:_Corner(preview, 6)
		local previewGradient = Create("UIGradient", { Parent = preview })
		Create("UIStroke", { Color = win:_T("Stroke"), Transparency = 0.5, Parent = preview })
		local header = Create("TextButton", { Text = "", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, baseHeight), ZIndex = 4, Parent = frame })

		-- editor area
		local area = Create("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, baseHeight), Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, editorHeight), Parent = frame })
		local bar = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 6), Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 26), Parent = area })
		win:_Corner(bar, 6)
		local barGradient = Create("UIGradient", { Parent = bar })
		local barHit = Create("TextButton", { Text = "", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 2, Parent = bar })
		local markers = Create("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 34), Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 18), Parent = area })
		local markerFrames = {}
		local hint = win:_Label({ Text = "Click the bar to add a colour · drag markers · right-click a marker to remove", TextSize = 10, Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 52), Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 14), Parent = area }, "Regular", "SubText")

		-- colour controls for the selected point
		local sv = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 0, 0), BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 72), Size = UDim2.new(0.55, -12, 0, 90), ZIndex = 2, Parent = area })
		win:_Corner(sv, 6, true)
		Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 2, Parent = sv }, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6) }), Create("UIGradient", { Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumKey(0, 0), NumKey(1, 1) }) }) })
		Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 3, Parent = sv }, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6) }), Create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumKey(0, 1), NumKey(1, 0) }) }) })
		local svKnob = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10), ZIndex = 5, Parent = sv }, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }), Create("UIStroke", { Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.4 }) })
		local hueBar = Create("Frame", { BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0.55, 4, 0, 72), Size = UDim2.new(0.45, -10, 0, 14), ZIndex = 2, Parent = area }, {
			Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }),
			Create("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new({ Keypoint(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)), Keypoint(1 / 6, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)), Keypoint(2 / 6, Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)), Keypoint(3 / 6, Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255)), Keypoint(4 / 6, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 255)), Keypoint(5 / 6, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 255)), Keypoint(1, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)) }) }),
		})
		local hueKnob = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 18), ZIndex = 5, Parent = hueBar }, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }), Create("UIStroke", { Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.5 }) })
		local hexHolder = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("ElementActive"), BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0.55, 4, 0, 94), Size = UDim2.new(0.45, -10, 0, 26), Parent = area })
		win:_Corner(hexHolder, 6)
		win:_Bind(hexHolder, "BackgroundColor3", "ElementActive")
		local hexBox = Create("TextBox", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "#FFFFFF", TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = win:_T("Text"), ClearTextOnFocus = false, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Parent = hexHolder })
		win:_Font(hexBox, "Code")
		win:_Bind(hexBox, "TextColor3", "Text")
		local timeLabel = win:_Label({ Text = "", TextSize = 11, Position = UDim2.new(0.55, 4, 0, 126), Size = UDim2.new(0.45, -10, 0, 16), Parent = area }, "Medium", "SubText")
		local presetRow = Create("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 168), Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 18), Parent = area })
		Create("UIListLayout", { FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, Padding = UDim.new(0, 6), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = presetRow })
		local presets = options.Presets or {
			ColorSequence.new({ Keypoint(0, Color3.fromRGB(128, 96, 255)), Keypoint(1, Color3.fromRGB(70, 200, 255)) }),
			ColorSequence.new({ Keypoint(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 92, 150)), Keypoint(1, Color3.fromRGB(255, 160, 90)) }),
			ColorSequence.new({ Keypoint(0, Color3.fromRGB(64, 214, 128)), Keypoint(1, Color3.fromRGB(190, 230, 80)) }),
			ColorSequence.new({ Keypoint(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)), Keypoint(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)), Keypoint(1, Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 255)) }),
			ColorSequence.new({ Keypoint(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)), Keypoint(1, Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 40)) }),
		}
		local rotationSlider = nil
		if options.Rotation then
			local rotTrack = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = win:_T("ElementActive"), BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 200), Size = UDim2.new(1, -72, 0, 4), Parent = area }, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
			win:_Bind(rotTrack, "BackgroundColor3", "ElementActive")
			local rotKnob = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(rotation / 360, 0, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12), ZIndex = 3, Parent = rotTrack }, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
			local rotLabel = win:_Label({ Text = "Rot " .. rotation .. "°", TextSize = 11, Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 192), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(52, 20), Parent = area }, "Medium", "SubText")
			local rotHit = Create("TextButton", { Text = "", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 190), Size = UDim2.new(1, -72, 0, 24), ZIndex = 4, Parent = area })
			rotationSlider = { Track = rotTrack, Knob = rotKnob, Label = rotLabel, Hit = rotHit }
		end

		local function currentColor()
			return points[selected] and points[selected].Color or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		end
		local hue, sat, val = currentColor():ToHSV()
		local dragTarget = nil
		local function refresh(silent)
			local seq = sequence()
			previewGradient.Color = seq
			barGradient.Color = seq
			previewGradient.Rotation = rotation
			for _, marker in ipairs(markerFrames) do marker:Destroy() end
			markerFrames = {}
			for index, point in ipairs(points) do
				local marker = Create("TextButton", {
					Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = point.Color, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
					Position = UDim2.new(point.Time, 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 16), ZIndex = 3, Parent = markers,
				})
				win:_Corner(marker, 4)
				Create("UIStroke", { Color = (index == selected) and win:_T("Accent") or Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Thickness = (index == selected) and 2 or 1, Transparency = (index == selected) and 0 or 0.4, Parent = marker })
				marker.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
					if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then
						if #points > 2 then
							table.remove(points, index)
							selected = math.min(selected, #points)
							hue, sat, val = currentColor():ToHSV()
							refresh()
						end
					elseif IsClick(input) then
						selected = index
						element._dragMarker = (index ~= 1 and index ~= #points) and index or nil
						dragTarget = element._dragMarker and "marker" or nil
						hue, sat, val = currentColor():ToHSV()
						refresh(true)
					end
				end)
				markerFrames[#markerFrames + 1] = marker
			end
			local color = currentColor()
			sv.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(hue, 1, 1)
			svKnob.Position = UDim2.new(sat, 0, 1 - val, 0)
			hueKnob.Position = UDim2.new(hue, 0, 0.5, 0)
			if not hexBox:IsFocused() then
				hexBox.Text = "#" .. color:ToHex()
			end
			timeLabel.Text = string.format("Stop %d of %d  ·  %.0f%%", selected, #points, (points[selected] and points[selected].Time or 0) * 100)
			if rotationSlider then
				rotationSlider.Knob.Position = UDim2.new(rotation / 360, 0, 0.5, 0)
				rotationSlider.Label.Text = "Rot " .. math.floor(rotation + 0.5) .. "°"
			end
			element:_SetFlag(serialise())
			if not silent then
				SafeCall(options.Callback, seq, rotation)
			end
		end
		local function setSelectedColor(color)
			if points[selected] then
				points[selected].Color = color
			end
			refresh()
		end
		local function colorAt(time)
			local seq = sequence()
			local kps = seq.Keypoints
			for index = 1, #kps - 1 do
				local a, b = kps[index], kps[index + 1]
				if time >= a.Time and time <= b.Time then
					local alpha = (time - a.Time) / math.max(1e-6, b.Time - a.Time)
					return a.Value:Lerp(b.Value, alpha)
				end
			end
			return kps[#kps].Value
		end
		barHit.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			local pct = math.clamp((win:_Pointer().X - bar.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, bar.AbsoluteSize.X), 0.02, 0.98)
			points[#points + 1] = { Time = pct, Color = colorAt(pct) }
			table.sort(points, function(a, b) return a.Time < b.Time end)
			for index, point in ipairs(points) do
				if point.Time == pct then selected = index end
			end
			hue, sat, val = currentColor():ToHSV()
			win:_Sound("Click")
			refresh()
		end)
		for order, preset in ipairs(presets) do
			local swatch = Create("TextButton", { Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 18), LayoutOrder = order, Parent = presetRow })
			win:_Corner(swatch, 5)
			Create("UIGradient", { Color = preset, Parent = swatch })
			swatch.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				loadSequence(preset)
				selected = 1
				hue, sat, val = currentColor():ToHSV()
				refresh()
			end)
		end
		local function updateFrom(pos)
			if dragTarget == "sv" then
				sat = math.clamp((pos.X - sv.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, sv.AbsoluteSize.X), 0, 1)
				val = 1 - math.clamp((pos.Y - sv.AbsolutePosition.Y) / math.max(1, sv.AbsoluteSize.Y), 0, 1)
				setSelectedColor(Color3.fromHSV(hue, sat, val))
			elseif dragTarget == "hue" then
				hue = math.clamp((pos.X - hueBar.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, hueBar.AbsoluteSize.X), 0, 0.9999)
				setSelectedColor(Color3.fromHSV(hue, sat, val))
			elseif dragTarget == "marker" and element._dragMarker and points[element._dragMarker] then
				local pct = math.clamp((pos.X - bar.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, bar.AbsoluteSize.X), 0.01, 0.99)
				points[element._dragMarker].Time = pct
				refresh()
			elseif dragTarget == "rotation" and rotationSlider then
				rotation = math.clamp((pos.X - rotationSlider.Track.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, rotationSlider.Track.AbsoluteSize.X), 0, 1) * 360
				refresh()
			end
		end
		sv.InputBegan:Connect(function(input) if IsClick(input) then dragTarget = "sv"; updateFrom(win:_Pointer()) end end)
		hueBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input) if IsClick(input) then dragTarget = "hue"; updateFrom(win:_Pointer()) end end)
		if rotationSlider then
			rotationSlider.Hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input) if IsClick(input) then dragTarget = "rotation"; updateFrom(win:_Pointer()) end end)
		end
		element.Maid:Add(win:_OnInput("Changed", function(input)
			if dragTarget and IsMove(input) then updateFrom(win:_Pointer()) end
		end))
		element.Maid:Add(win:_OnInput("Ended", function(input)
			if IsClick(input) then dragTarget = nil; element._dragMarker = nil end
		end))
		hexBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
			local hex = hexBox.Text:gsub("#", ""):gsub("%s", "")
			if #hex == 6 and hex:match("^%x+$") then
				local color = Color3.fromHex(hex)
				hue, sat, val = color:ToHSV()
				setSelectedColor(color)
			else
				refresh(true)
			end
		end)
		local function resize()
			Tween(frame, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, open and (baseHeight + editorHeight + 8) or baseHeight) }, 0.3)
		end
		header.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			win:_Ripple(frame)
			win:_Sound("Click")
			open = not open
			resize()
		end)
		refresh(true)
		element.Default = serialise()

		function element:Set(value, silent)
			if typeof(value) == "ColorSequence" then
				loadSequence(value)
			elseif type(value) == "table" then
				points = {}
				for _, entry in ipairs(value) do
					points[#points + 1] = { Time = entry[1], Color = Color3.new(entry[2], entry[3], entry[4]) }
				end
				if value.Rotation then rotation = value.Rotation end
				if #points < 2 then loadSequence(options.Default or ColorSequence.new(win:_T("Accent"))) end
			end
			selected = 1
			hue, sat, val = currentColor():ToHSV()
			refresh(silent)
		end
		function element:Get()
			return sequence(), rotation
		end
		function element:Open() open = true; resize() end
		function element:Close() open = false; resize() end
		function element:Toggle() open = not open; resize() end
		return element
	end

	-- ============================================================================
	-- DETACHED FLOATING PANELS (their own camera part; share theme, effects and pointer)
	-- ============================================================================
	local Panel = setmetatable({}, { __index = Container })
	Panel.__index = Panel

	-- Window:CreatePanel({ Title, Size = Vector2, Offset = Vector2 (pixels from screen centre) })
	function Window:CreatePanel(options)
		options = options or {}
		local theme = self.Theme
		local size = options.Size or Vector2.new(320, 380)
		local margin = 28
		local canvas = Vector2.new(size.X + margin * 2, size.Y + margin * 2)
		local panel = setmetatable({
			Window = self, Name = options.Title or "Panel", Elements = {}, Maid = Maid.new(), IsPanel = true, Canvas = canvas, Size = size,
			_screenOffset = options.Offset or Vector2.new(0, 0), _tilt = Vector2.new(), _hoverLift = 0, _hovering = false, Visible = true,
		}, Panel)
		panel.Page = panel

		local rootParent
		if self.Render3D then
			panel.Part = Create("Part", {
				Name = "AuroraPanel", Anchored = true, CanCollide = false, CanQuery = false, CanTouch = false, CastShadow = false,
				Transparency = 1, Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 0.05), Parent = workspace.CurrentCamera,
			})
			panel.Gui = Create("SurfaceGui", {
				Name = "AuroraPanel", Adornee = panel.Part, Face = Enum.NormalId.Back, AlwaysOnTop = true, SizingMode = Enum.SurfaceGuiSizingMode.FixedSize,
				CanvasSize = canvas, LightInfluence = 0, Brightness = 1, MaxDistance = 1e6, ResetOnSpawn = false, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling, Parent = self.GuiParent,
			})
			panel.Maid:Add(panel.Part)
			panel.Maid:Add(panel.Gui)
			panel.SurfaceEntry = { Part = panel.Part, Canvas = canvas, Gui = panel.Gui }
			self._surfaces[#self._surfaces + 1] = panel.SurfaceEntry
			rootParent = panel.Gui
		else
			rootParent = self.Gui
		end

		local root = Create("Frame", {
			Name = "PanelRoot", BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size.X, size.Y), ZIndex = 5, Parent = rootParent,
		})
		if not self.Render3D then
			root.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, panel._screenOffset.X, 0.5, panel._screenOffset.Y)
		end
		panel.Frame = root
		local shadow = Create("Frame", {
			BackgroundColor3 = theme.Shadow, BackgroundTransparency = 0.75, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
			Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 6), Size = UDim2.new(1, 20, 1, 20), ZIndex = 0, Parent = root,
		})
		self:_Corner(shadow, 20)
		self:_Bind(shadow, "BackgroundColor3", "Shadow")
		local main = Create("Frame", { Name = "Main", BackgroundColor3 = theme.Background, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ClipsDescendants = true, ZIndex = 1, Parent = root })
		self:_Corner(main, 12)
		self:_Bind(main, "BackgroundColor3", "Background")
		local stroke = Create("UIStroke", { Color = theme.Stroke, Transparency = 0.35, Parent = main })
		self:_Bind(stroke, "Color", "Stroke")
		panel.Main = main
		-- frosted glass behind the panel (Acrylic effect); a hair nearer than the window's so overlaps stay blurred
		self:_AddGlass(main, {
			Surface = panel.SurfaceEntry, Radius = 12, Bias = 0.004,
			IsVisible = function()
				return panel.Visible and not panel._closing and self.IsOpen
			end,
			Tint = function(on)
				local amount = on and math.clamp(tonumber(self.Effects.AcrylicTint) or 0.22, 0, 0.9) or 0
				main.BackgroundTransparency = amount
				main:SetAttribute("AuroraBG", amount)
			end,
		})

		local header = Create("Frame", { Name = "Header", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44), ZIndex = 3, Parent = main })
		local title = self:_Label({ Text = panel.Name, TextSize = 14, Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0), Parent = header }, "Bold", "Text")
		panel.TitleLabel = title
		local close = Create("TextButton", { Text = "×", TextSize = 18, TextColor3 = theme.SubText, BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 26), Parent = header })
		self:_Font(close, "Medium")
		self:_Bind(close, "TextColor3", "SubText")
		local divider = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = theme.Stroke, BackgroundTransparency = 0.55, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 1, -1), Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 1), Parent = header })
		self:_Bind(divider, "BackgroundColor3", "Stroke")

		local list = Create("ScrollingFrame", {
			Name = "List", BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 44), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -44), CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
			AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ScrollBarThickness = 3, ScrollBarImageColor3 = theme.Accent, ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y, ZIndex = 2, Parent = main,
		})
		self:_Bind(list, "ScrollBarImageColor3", "Accent")
		Create("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 6), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10), PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 12), Parent = list })
		Create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 6), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = list })
		panel.List = list

		-- drag (screen space)
		local dragging, startMouse, startOffset = false, nil, nil
		panel.Maid:Add(header.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if IsClick(input) then
				dragging = true
				startMouse = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
				startOffset = panel._screenOffset
			end
		end))
		panel.Maid:Add(self:_OnInput("Changed", function(input)
			if dragging and IsMove(input) then
				panel._screenOffset = startOffset + (UserInputService:GetMouseLocation() - startMouse)
				if not self.Render3D then
					root.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, panel._screenOffset.X, 0.5, panel._screenOffset.Y)
				end
			end
		end))
		panel.Maid:Add(self:_OnInput("Ended", function(input)
			if dragging and IsClick(input) then dragging = false end
		end))

		-- per-frame placement + tilt
		panel.Maid:Add(self:_AddStepHook(function(t, dt)
			if not panel.Visible then
				return
			end
			local pointer = self:_Pointer()
			local abs, rsize = root.AbsolutePosition, root.AbsoluteSize
			local hovering, target = false, Vector2.new()
			if rsize.X > 0 and rsize.Y > 0 then
				local m = panel._hovering and 40 or 0
				local nx, ny = (pointer.X - abs.X) / rsize.X, (pointer.Y - abs.Y) / rsize.Y
				if nx >= -m / rsize.X and nx <= 1 + m / rsize.X and ny >= -m / rsize.Y and ny <= 1 + m / rsize.Y then
					hovering = true
					target = Vector2.new(math.clamp((nx - 0.5) * 2, -1, 1), math.clamp((ny - 0.5) * 2, -1, 1))
				end
			end
			panel._hovering = hovering
			if not self.Effects.Tilt or dragging then
				target = Vector2.new()
			end
			local alpha = 1 - math.exp(-dt * 7)
			panel._tilt = panel._tilt:Lerp(target, alpha)
			panel._hoverLift = Lerp(panel._hoverLift, hovering and 1 or 0, alpha)
			local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
			if self.Render3D and camera and panel.Part then
				if panel.Part.Parent ~= camera then panel.Part.Parent = camera end
				local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
				local distance = self.SurfaceDistance - 0.6   -- a little closer than the main window: it floats above it
				local studsPerPixel = (2 * distance * math.tan(math.rad(camera.FieldOfView) / 2)) / math.max(1, viewport.Y)
				local scale = self.UIScale * (1 + panel._hoverLift * 0.004)
				panel.Part.Size = Vector3.new(canvas.X * studsPerPixel * scale, canvas.Y * studsPerPixel * scale, 0.05)
				local angle = math.rad(self.Effects.TiltAngle or 6) * (self.Effects.TiltStrength or 1) * (self.Effects.TiltDirection or 1)
				local floatY = self.Effects.Float and math.sin(t * 0.8 + 1.7) * 3 * (1 - panel._hoverLift) or 0
				local offset = panel._screenOffset
