				local targetCF = camera.CFrame * CFrame.new(offset.X * studsPerPixel, -(offset.Y + floatY) * studsPerPixel, -distance) * CFrame.Angles(panel._tilt.Y * angle, panel._tilt.X * angle, 0)
				if self.Effects.Sway and panel._swayCF then
					panel._swayCF = panel._swayCF:Lerp(targetCF, 1 - math.exp(-dt * 16))
				else
					panel._swayCF = targetCF
				end
				panel.Part.CFrame = panel._swayCF
				-- depth layers: the header and the list float a little above the panel
				local look = panel.Part.CFrame:VectorToObjectSpace(camera.CFrame.LookVector)
				local facing = math.max(0.25, -look.Z)
				local amount = (self.Effects.Parallax and 1 or 0) * (tonumber(self.Effects.DepthAmount) or 1)
				local vx, vy = look.X / facing * amount, -look.Y / facing * amount
				header.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(math.clamp(vx * 14, -10, 10), math.clamp(vy * 14, -10, 10))
				list.Position = UDim2.new(0, math.clamp(vx * 24, -12, 12), 0, 44 + math.clamp(vy * 24, -12, 12))
			end
			shadow.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -panel._tilt.X * 4, 0.5, 6 - panel._tilt.Y * 4)
		end))

		function panel:SetVisible(state)
			self.Visible = state and true or false
			root.Visible = self.Visible
			if self.SurfaceEntry then self.SurfaceEntry.Enabled = self.Visible end
			if self.Gui then self.Gui.Enabled = self.Visible end
		end
		function panel:SetTitle(text)
			self.Name = tostring(text)
			title.Text = self.Name
		end
		function panel:Destroy()
			if self._closing then
				return
			end
			self._closing = true
			local popScale = root:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale")
			if popScale then
				Tween(popScale, { Scale = 0.9 }, 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
			end
			FadeTree(root, 1, 0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
			task.delay(Timed(0.2), function()
				SafeCall(self.OnClose, self)
				self.Maid:Clean()
				root:Destroy()
				for index = #self.Window._surfaces, 1, -1 do
					if self.Window._surfaces[index] == self.SurfaceEntry then table.remove(self.Window._surfaces, index) end
				end
				for index = #self.Window.Panels, 1, -1 do
					if self.Window.Panels[index] == self then table.remove(self.Window.Panels, index) end
				end
			end)
		end
		function panel:Close()
			self:Destroy()
		end
		close.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			self:_Sound("Click")
			panel:Destroy()
		end)

		-- pop in
		local popScale = Create("UIScale", { Scale = 0.9, Parent = root })
		Tween(popScale, { Scale = 1 }, 0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		FadeTree(main, 1, 0)
		FadeTree(main, 0, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		self.Panels[#self.Panels + 1] = panel
		return panel
	end

	-- Pops a section out into its own floating panel; closing the panel puts it back.
	function Section:Detach(options)
		options = options or {}
		if self.Panel then
			return self.Panel
		end
		local win = self.Window
		local panel = win:CreatePanel({ Title = options.Title or self.Title or "Section", Size = options.Size, Offset = options.Offset or Vector2.new(300, 0) })
		local originalList = self.List
		for _, child in ipairs(originalList:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("GuiObject") then
				child.Parent = panel.List
			end
		end
		self.List = panel.List
		self.Panel = panel
		self.Frame.Visible = false
		panel.OnClose = function()
			for _, child in ipairs(panel.List:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("GuiObject") then
					child.Parent = originalList
				end
			end
			self.List = originalList
			self.Panel = nil
			self.Frame.Visible = true
		end
		return panel
	end

	function Section:Reattach()
		if self.Panel then
			self.Panel:Destroy()
		end
	end

	-- ============================================================================
	-- THEME EDITOR PAGE
	-- ============================================================================
	function Window:ExportTheme() return nil end
	function Window:AddThemeEditorPage(options)
		if self.ThemeEditorPage then return self.ThemeEditorPage end
		local page = self:AddPage({ Name = (type(options) == "table" and options.Name) or "Theme", Icon = "palette" })
		page:_Locked("ThemeEditor", { Title = "Theme editor", Description = "Edit every colour live, save themes, and share them as code or JSON" })
		self.ThemeEditorPage = page
		return page
	end

	-- ============================================================================
	-- KEY SYSTEM (optional gate shown before the window opens)
	-- ============================================================================
	-- KeySystem = {
	--   Title, Description, Keys = { "ABC123", ... }, KeyUrl = "https://.../keys.txt" (newline/comma list or JSON array),
	--   Validate = function(key) return true end,  -- custom check (may yield)
	--   KeyLink = "https://...",  -- shown on a "Get key" button (copied to clipboard)
	--   SaveKey = true,  -- remember an accepted key on disk (file system environments)
	--   OnSuccess = function(key) end, OnFail = function(key) end,
	-- }

	-- What a remembered key looks like on disk, and what comes back out of it. Both builds write JSON;
	-- only Elite seals it and stamps the account.
	function Window:_PackKey(key)
		local record = { Key = tostring(key), At = os.time() }
		return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode(record)
	end

	-- Returns the key and when it was saved, or nil when the file is not this player's to use.
	function Window:_UnpackKey(raw, cfg)
		local ok, decoded = pcall(function()
			return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(raw)
		end)
		if not ok or type(decoded) ~= "table" or not decoded.Key then
			return tostring(raw), nil   -- a plain key written by an older version
		end
		local key, at = tostring(decoded.Key), tonumber(decoded.At)
		return key, at
	end

	function Window:_KeyFilePath()
		return self.ConfigFolder .. "/key.txt"
	end

	-- djb2 digest. This only keeps keys out of plain sight in the script; it is not security,
	-- because the check still runs on the player's own machine.
	function Aurora:HashKey(key)
		local text = tostring(key)
		local hash = 5381
		for index = 1, #text do
			hash = (hash * 33 + string.byte(text, index)) % 4294967296
		end
		return string.format("%08x", hash)
	end

	-- returns ok, reason ("invalid" or "network")
	function Window:_ValidateKey(key, cfg)
		key = tostring(key or ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
		if key == "" then
			return false
		end
		local function matches(list)
			for _, candidate in ipairs(list) do
				if cfg.CaseSensitive == false then
					if tostring(candidate):lower() == key:lower() then return true end
				elseif tostring(candidate) == key then
					return true
				end
			end
			return false
		end
		if type(cfg.Keys) == "table" and matches(cfg.Keys) then
			return true
		end
		return false, "invalid"
	end

	function Window:_ShowKeyScreen(cfg, onSuccess)
		local theme, fonts = self.Theme, self.Fonts
		local gui = Create("ScreenGui", { Name = "AuroraKey", IgnoreGuiInset = true, ResetOnSpawn = false, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling, DisplayOrder = 9000, Parent = self.GuiParent })
		local backdrop = Create("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = theme.Background, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Parent = gui })
		local blur = nil
		if self.Effects.Blur then
			blur = Create("BlurEffect", { Name = "AuroraKeyBlur", Size = 0, Parent = Lighting })
			Tween(blur, { Size = 12 }, 0.3)
		end

		local card = Create("Frame", {
			BackgroundColor3 = theme.Element, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 14),
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.min(400, math.max(280, (workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize.X or 800) - 36)), 0),
			AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Parent = gui,
		})
		Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 14), Parent = card })
		local cardStroke = Create("UIStroke", { Color = theme.Stroke, Transparency = 0.35, Parent = card })
		Create("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 28), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 28), PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 28), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 26), Parent = card })
		Create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 8), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = card })
		local cardScale = Create("UIScale", { Scale = 0.96, Parent = card })

		-- brand row: mark + product name
		local brandRow = Create("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28), LayoutOrder = 1, Parent = card })
		local mark = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = theme.ElementActive, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 28), Parent = brandRow }, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8) }), Create("UIStroke", { Color = theme.Stroke, Transparency = 0.3 }) })
		Create("TextLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = string.upper(string.sub(self.Title, 1, 1)), Font = fonts.Bold, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = theme.Text, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = mark })
		Create("TextLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = string.upper(self.Title), Font = fonts.Bold, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = theme.SubText, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Position = UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -38, 1, 0), Parent = brandRow })

		local heading = Create("TextLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = cfg.Title or "Enter your key", Font = fonts.Bold, TextSize = 21, TextColor3 = theme.Text, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28), LayoutOrder = 2, Parent = card })
		Create("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 10), Parent = heading })
		local body = Create("TextLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = cfg.Description or "A valid key is required to continue.", Font = fonts.Regular, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = theme.SubText, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextWrapped = true, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = 3, Parent = card })

		local fieldLabel = Create("TextLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = string.upper(cfg.FieldLabel or "Key"), Font = fonts.Bold, TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = theme.SubText, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24), TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Bottom, LayoutOrder = 4, Parent = card })
		local holder = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = theme.Background, BackgroundTransparency = 0.35, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42), LayoutOrder = 5, Parent = card })
		Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 9), Parent = holder })
		local holderStroke = Create("UIStroke", { Color = theme.Stroke, Transparency = 0.3, Parent = holder })
		holder.ClipsDescendants = true
		-- the real key lives in the TextBox; while hidden its glyphs are transparent and a label draws one dot per character.
		-- both use the monospace code font, so the caret stays aligned with the dots.
		local box = Create("TextBox", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", PlaceholderText = cfg.Placeholder or "Paste your key", PlaceholderColor3 = theme.Placeholder, TextColor3 = theme.Text, Font = fonts.Code, TextSize = 14, ClearTextOnFocus = false, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -110, 1, 0), ZIndex = 2, Parent = holder })
		local mask = Create("TextLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", TextColor3 = theme.Text, Font = fonts.Code, TextSize = 14, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Position = box.Position, Size = box.Size, ClipsDescendants = true, ZIndex = 3, Parent = holder })
		local caret = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = theme.Text, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 18), Visible = false, ZIndex = 5, Parent = holder })
		local revealed = cfg.Masked == false
		local toggleVisibility = self:_StyledButton(holder, { Text = revealed and "Hide" or "Show", Variant = "Ghost", Size = UDim2.fromOffset(52, 28), TextSize = 12, Radius = 6, Ripple = false, ZIndex = 4 })
		toggleVisibility.Button.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
		toggleVisibility.Button.Position = UDim2.new(1, -7, 0.5, 0)
		local function renderMask()
			local length = utf8.len(box.Text) or #box.Text
			box.TextTransparency = revealed and 0 or 1
			mask.Visible = not revealed and length > 0
			mask.Text = string.rep("•", length)
			-- keep the end of long keys in view, matching how the TextBox scrolls
			local width = TextService:GetTextSize(mask.Text, 14, fonts.Code, Vector2.new(100000, 42)).X
			mask.TextXAlignment = (width > box.AbsoluteSize.X) and Enum.TextXAlignment.Right or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		end
		box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(renderMask)

		-- the TextBox caret disappears with TextTransparency = 1, so draw our own while masked
		local blinkStart = os.clock()
		local function resetBlink()
			blinkStart = os.clock()
		end
		box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(resetBlink)
		box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CursorPosition"):Connect(resetBlink)
		box.Focused:Connect(resetBlink)
		local caretConnection
		caretConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
			if not Alive(gui) then
				caretConnection:Disconnect()
				return
			end
			if revealed or not box:IsFocused() then
				caret.Visible = false
				return
			end
			local text = box.Text
			local cursor = box.CursorPosition
			if cursor < 1 then
				cursor = #text + 1
			end
			local before = utf8.len(text:sub(1, cursor - 1)) or (cursor - 1)
			local total = utf8.len(text) or #text
			local dot = TextService:GetTextSize("•", 14, fonts.Code, Vector2.new(1000, 42)).X
			local fieldWidth = box.AbsoluteSize.X
			local offset = 0
			if total * dot > fieldWidth then
				offset = fieldWidth - total * dot   -- mask is right-aligned when the key overflows
			end
			local x = math.clamp(before * dot + offset, 0, fieldWidth)
			caret.Position = UDim2.new(0, box.Position.X.Offset + x, 0.5, 0)
			-- solid for half a second after any change, then blink
			local elapsed = os.clock() - blinkStart
			caret.Visible = elapsed < 0.5 or math.floor((elapsed - 0.5) / 0.53) % 2 == 0
		end)
		toggleVisibility.Button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			revealed = not revealed
			toggleVisibility:SetText(revealed and "Hide" or "Show")
			-- quick crossfade between dots and plain text
			if revealed then
				Tween(mask, { TextTransparency = 1 }, 0.12)
				box.TextTransparency = 1
				Tween(box, { TextTransparency = 0 }, 0.12)
				task.delay(Timed(0.12), function()
					if revealed and Alive(mask) then
						mask.Visible = false
						mask.TextTransparency = 0
					end
				end)
			else
				mask.TextTransparency = 1
				renderMask()
				box.TextTransparency = 1
				Tween(mask, { TextTransparency = 0 }, 0.12)
			end
			if box:IsFocused() == false then
				box:CaptureFocus()
			end
		end)
		renderMask()
		local checkHolder = Create("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = theme.Success, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -66, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 22), Visible = false, ZIndex = 4, Parent = holder }, { Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }) })
		local checkScale = Create("UIScale", { Scale = 0, Parent = checkHolder })
		MakeCheck(checkHolder, 11, theme.AccentText)
		local helper = Create("TextLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", Font = fonts.Medium, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = theme.Error, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16), LayoutOrder = 6, Parent = card })

		-- footer: note on the left, ghost "Get a key" and primary "Continue" on the right, like a standard dialog
		local actions = Create("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 46), LayoutOrder = 7, Parent = card })
		Create("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 10), Parent = actions })
		local note = Create("TextLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = (cfg.SaveKey ~= false and HasFileSystem()) and "Remembered on this device" or "", Font = fonts.Regular, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = theme.Placeholder, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Size = UDim2.new(0.45, 0, 1, 0), Parent = actions })
		local buttonRow = Create("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 1, 0), Parent = actions })
		Create("UIListLayout", { FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right, VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center, Padding = UDim.new(0, 8), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = buttonRow })
		local getKeyStyled = nil
		if cfg.KeyLink then
			getKeyStyled = self:_StyledButton(buttonRow, { Text = cfg.KeyLinkText or "Get a key", Variant = "Ghost", Height = 36, LayoutOrder = 1 })
		end
		local submit = self:_StyledButton(buttonRow, { Text = cfg.SubmitText or "Continue", Variant = "Primary", Size = UDim2.fromOffset(116, 36), LayoutOrder = 2 })
		local getKey = getKeyStyled and getKeyStyled.Button or nil
		local footerRow = actions

		local busy = false
		local function setFieldState(state)
			local color = (state == "error") and theme.Error or ((state == "success") and theme.Success or ((state == "focus") and theme.Accent or theme.Stroke))
			Tween(holderStroke, { Color = color, Transparency = (state == "idle") and 0.3 or 0 }, 0.18)
		end
		box.Focused:Connect(function()
			if helper.Text == "" then setFieldState("focus") end
		end)
		box.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter)
			if helper.Text == "" then setFieldState("idle") end
		end)
		box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
			if helper.Text ~= "" then
				helper.Text = ""
				setFieldState(box:IsFocused() and "focus" or "idle")
			end
		end)

		local function closeScreen()
			Tween(cardScale, { Scale = 1.02 }, 0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
			Tween(card, { Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, -8) }, 0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
			FadeTree(card, 1, 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
			Tween(backdrop, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, 0.25)
			if blur then
				Tween(blur, { Size = 0 }, 0.25)
			end
			task.delay(Timed(0.3), function()
				if blur then blur:Destroy() end
				gui:Destroy()
			end)
		end
		local function finish(key)
			if cfg.SaveKey ~= false and HasFileSystem() then
				pcall(function()
					self:_EnsureConfigFolder()
					-- stamped so SaveDays can expire it later; older plain-text files still load
					writefile(self:_KeyFilePath(), self:_PackKey(key))
				end)
			end
			setFieldState("success")
			checkHolder.Visible = true
			Tween(checkScale, { Scale = 1 }, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
			submit:SetText("Verified")
			task.delay(Timed(0.55), function()
				closeScreen()
				task.delay(Timed(0.25), function()
					SafeCall(cfg.OnSuccess, key)
					onSuccess()
				end)
			end)
		end
		local function shake()
			local base = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
			local offsets = { -7, 6, -4, 3, 0 }
			for index, offset in ipairs(offsets) do
				task.delay(Timed((index - 1) * 0.045), function()
					if Alive(card) then
						Tween(card, { Position = base + UDim2.fromOffset(offset, 0) }, 0.045, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear)
					end
				end)
			end
		end
		-- wrong keys are rate limited; a server that cannot be reached is reported as such instead
		local attempts, lockUntil = 0, 0
		local maxAttempts = tonumber(cfg.MaxAttempts) or 5
		local lockSeconds = tonumber(cfg.LockSeconds) or 30
		local function lockCountdown()
			task.spawn(function()
				while Alive(card) and os.clock() < lockUntil do
					helper.Text = string.format(cfg.LockedText or "Too many tries. Wait %ds", math.max(0, math.ceil(lockUntil - os.clock())))
					task.wait(0.25)
				end
				if Alive(card) and os.clock() >= lockUntil then
					helper.Text = ""
					setFieldState("idle")
					submit:SetEnabled(true)
					submit:SetText(cfg.SubmitText or "Continue")
				end
			end)
		end
		local function attempt()
			if busy or os.clock() < lockUntil then
				if os.clock() < lockUntil then shake() end
				return
			end
			local key = box.Text:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
			if key == "" then
				helper.Text = "Enter a key first"
				setFieldState("error")
				shake()
				return
			end
			busy = true
			submit:SetEnabled(false)
			submit:SetText("")
			local spinner = self:_Spinner(submit.Button, 18, "AccentText")
			task.spawn(function()
				local started = os.clock()
				local ok, reason = self:_ValidateKey(key, cfg)
				-- keep the spinner visible long enough to read as feedback
				local elapsed = os.clock() - started
				if elapsed < 0.45 then task.wait(0.45 - elapsed) end
				spinner.Destroy()
				busy = false
				if ok then
					finish(key)
					return
				end
				submit:SetEnabled(true)
				submit:SetText(cfg.SubmitText or "Continue")
				setFieldState("error")
				shake()
				if reason == "network" then
					helper.Text = cfg.NetworkText or "Could not reach the key server. Try again."
					return
				end
				attempts = attempts + 1
				if maxAttempts > 0 and attempts >= maxAttempts then
					attempts = 0
					lockUntil = os.clock() + lockSeconds
					submit:SetEnabled(false)
					submit:SetText(cfg.SubmitText or "Continue")
					lockCountdown()
				else
					helper.Text = cfg.InvalidText or "That key is not valid"
					if maxAttempts > 0 then
						helper.Text = helper.Text .. "  (" .. (maxAttempts - attempts) .. " left)"
					end
				end
				SafeCall(cfg.OnFail, key)
			end)
		end
		submit.Button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(attempt)
		box.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter) if enter then attempt() end end)
		if getKey then
			getKey.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				local copied = false
				pcall(function()
					if typeof(setclipboard) == "function" then setclipboard(cfg.KeyLink); copied = true end
				end)
				helper.TextColor3 = theme.SubText
				helper.Text = copied and "Link copied to your clipboard" or cfg.KeyLink
				task.delay(3, function()
					if Alive(helper) and helper.TextColor3 == theme.SubText then
						helper.Text = ""
						helper.TextColor3 = theme.Error
					end
				end)
			end)
		end

		-- enter: backdrop 200ms, card fade + scale + rise over 300ms, content staggered
		Tween(backdrop, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.3 }, 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
		Tween(cardScale, { Scale = 1 }, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		card.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		cardStroke.Transparency = 1
		Tween(card, { Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 0 }, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		Tween(cardStroke, { Transparency = 0.35 }, 0.3)
		StaggerIn({ brandRow, heading, body, fieldLabel, holder, actions }, 0.045, 8)
		task.delay(Timed(0.5), function() if Alive(box) then box:CaptureFocus() end end)
		self.KeyGui = gui
		return gui
	end

	-- Runs after the loader: key gate (if configured) then open.
	function Window:_Boot()
		local cfg = self.KeySystem
		if not cfg or cfg.Enabled == false then
			self:Open(true)
			return
		end
		-- remembered key: { Key, At } as JSON, or a plain key from an older version
		if cfg.SaveKey ~= false and HasFileSystem() then
			local saved, savedAt = nil, nil
			pcall(function()
				if isfile(self:_KeyFilePath()) then
					saved, savedAt = self:_UnpackKey(readfile(self:_KeyFilePath()), cfg)
				end
			end)
			local days = tonumber(cfg.SaveDays) or 0
			if saved and savedAt and days > 0 and os.time() - savedAt > days * 86400 then
				-- the remembered key has aged out
				self:ClearSavedKey()
				saved = nil
			end
			if saved and saved ~= "" then
				task.spawn(function()
					if self:_ValidateKey(saved, cfg) then
						self:Open(true)
					else
						-- it no longer works, so do not keep asking with it
						self:ClearSavedKey()
						self:_ShowKeyScreen(cfg, function() self:Open(true) end)
					end
				end)
				return
			end
		end
		self:_ShowKeyScreen(cfg, function() self:Open(true) end)
	end

	function Window:ClearSavedKey()
		pcall(function()
			if HasFileSystem() and typeof(delfile) == "function" and isfile(self:_KeyFilePath()) then
				delfile(self:_KeyFilePath())
			end
		end)
	end

	-- Free build: the logic system is not in this module. These keep an Elite script running: pages show
	-- what is missing, and the file API answers with empty data instead of erroring.
	Aurora.Nodes, Aurora.NodeOrder = {}, {}
	function Container:AddLogicEditor(options) return self:_Locked("Logic", options) end
	function Container:AddAutomations(options) return self:_Locked("Logic", options) end
	function Container:AddAutomation(rule)
		return self:_Locked("Logic", { Title = (type(rule) == "table" and rule.Title) or "Automation" })
	end
	local function LockedLogicPage(self, options, name, icon)
		local page = self:AddPage({ Name = (type(options) == "table" and (options.Name or options.Title)) or name, Icon = icon })
		page:_Locked("Logic", { Title = "Logic editor and automations",
			Description = "Build behaviour from nodes, run it live, and save it with your config" })
		return page
	end
	function Window:AddLogicHubPage(options)
		if self.LogicPage then return self.LogicPage end
		self.LogicPage = LockedLogicPage(self, options, "Logic", "nodes")
		return self.LogicPage
	end
	function Window:AddLogicPage(options)
		local page = self:AddLogicHubPage(options)
		return page, nil
	end
	function Window:AddAutomationsPage(options)
		return LockedLogicPage(self, options, "Automations", "automation")
	end
	function Window:_BuildLogicUI() end
	function Window:_RegisterLogic() end
	function Window:_LogicItems() return {} end
	function Window:GetLogic() return {} end
	function Window:ExportLogic() return nil end
	function Window:ImportLogic() return false, 0 end
	function Window:SaveLogic() return false end
	function Window:LoadLogic() return false end
	function Window:ListLogic() return {} end
	function Window:DeleteLogic() return false end
	function Window:StartLogic() return 0 end
	function Window:StopLogic() return 0 end
end


================================================================================
FILE 4: AuroraUI_UIBlur_or_Thanks.lua
================================================================================

-- Thanks to SomeFedoraGuy (me) for spending a lot more
-- than needed time on writing the math in the Update() function
-- For future use: PBR-124652808947078

local UIBlur = {}
UIBlur.__index = UIBlur

local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")

local Player = Players.LocalPlayer
local Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
local DepthOfField = Instance.new("DepthOfFieldEffect")
local Container = Camera:FindFirstChild("UIBlur")

local Corners = script.Corners:GetChildren()

local QualityLevel = UserSettings().GameSettings.SavedQualityLevel

local Offset = 0.05

export type UIBlur = {
	_ScreenGui : ScreenGui,
	_GuiObject : GuiObject,
	_Connections : {RBXScriptConnection},
	_Part : BasePart,
	_BlurEffect : BlurEffect,
	
	_Cornered : boolean?,
	_Rendering : boolean,
	
	Update : (UIBlur) -> (),
	Destroy : (UIBlur) -> (),
}

local function IsBlurEffectValid(Blur:BlurEffect)
	if Blur:IsA("BlurEffect") and Blur.Parent:IsA("GuiObject") then
		local Corner = Blur.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner") :: UICorner
		if Corner then
			Corner = math.clamp(math.floor(Corner.CornerRadius.Scale) + math.clamp(Corner.CornerRadius.Offset/25,1,#Corners),1,#Corners)
		else
			Corner = false
		end
		local BlurredUI = UIBlur.new(Blur.Parent,Corner,Blur.Size == 0)
	end
end
local function CheckForBlurEffect()
	for _,Object:Instance in Player.PlayerGui:GetDescendants() do
		IsBlurEffectValid(Object) 
	end
end
local function GetContainer() : Folder
	if not Container then
		Container = Instance.new("Folder")
		Container.Name = "UIBlur"
		Container.Parent = Camera
	end
	return Container
end
local function PointToRay(Vector:Vector2) : Vector3
	local Ray = Camera:ScreenPointToRay(Vector.X,Vector.Y,Offset)
	return Ray.Origin + Ray.Direction * Offset
end

DepthOfField.Name = "UIBlur"
DepthOfField.FarIntensity = 0
DepthOfField.FocusDistance = 0
DepthOfField.InFocusRadius = 0
DepthOfField.NearIntensity = 1
DepthOfField.Parent = Lighting

function UIBlur.new(GuiObject:GuiObject,Corner:number?,DisableBelowNeededGraphics:boolean?) : UIBlur
	if not RunService:IsClient() then return end
	if not GuiObject then return end
	if not DisableBelowNeededGraphics then DisableBelowNeededGraphics = true end
	
	local self = {} :: UIBlur
	
	self._ScreenGui = GuiObject:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("ScreenGui")
	self._GuiObject = GuiObject
	self._Rendering = true
	self._Connections = {}
	self._BlurEffect = GuiObject:FindFirstChildOfClass("BlurEffect")
	
	self._Part = Corner and Corners[tonumber(Corner)]:Clone() or Instance.new("Part")
	self._Part.CastShadow = false
	self._Part.CanCollide = false
	self._Part.CanQuery = false
	self._Part.CanTouch = false
	self._Part.Anchored = true
	self._Part.Material = Enum.Material.Glass
	self._Part.Name = GuiObject.Name
	self._Part.Parent = GetContainer()
	
	table.insert(self._Connections,GuiObject.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
		if not GuiObject.Parent then
			self:Destroy()
		end
	end))
	
	if DisableBelowNeededGraphics then
		self._Rendering = QualityLevel.Value >= 8 or QualityLevel == Enum.SavedQualitySetting.Automatic
		table.insert(self._Connections,UserSettings().GameSettings:GetPropertyChangedSignal("SavedQualityLevel"):Connect(function()
			QualityLevel = UserSettings().GameSettings.SavedQualityLevel
			self._Rendering = QualityLevel.Value >= 8 or QualityLevel == Enum.SavedQualitySetting.Automatic
		end))
	end
	if self._BlurEffect then
		self._BlurEffect.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
			self:Destroy()
		end)
	end
	if Corner then
		self._Cornered = true
	end
	
	table.insert(self._Connections,RunService.PreRender:Connect(function()
		self:Update()
	end))
	
	return setmetatable(self,UIBlur)
end

function UIBlur.Update(self: UIBlur)
	local Rendering = self._Rendering and self._GuiObject.Visible and self._ScreenGui.Enabled and (self._BlurEffect and self._BlurEffect.Enabled or not self._BlurEffect)
	
	self._Part.Transparency = Rendering and 0.98 or 1
	if Rendering then
		local GuiPos = self._GuiObject.AbsolutePosition
		local GuiSize = self._GuiObject.AbsoluteSize

		local TopLeft = PointToRay(GuiPos)
		local Center = PointToRay(GuiPos + GuiSize / 2)
		
		
		
		self._Part.Size = Vector3.new((PointToRay(GuiPos + Vector2.xAxis * GuiSize.X) - TopLeft).Magnitude,(PointToRay(GuiPos + Vector2.yAxis * GuiSize.Y) - TopLeft).Magnitude)
		self._Part.CFrame = CFrame.new(Center,Center - Camera.CFrame.LookVector) * CFrame.Angles(0,self._Cornered and 0 or math.pi,-math.rad(self._GuiObject.AbsoluteRotation))
	end
end

function UIBlur.Destroy(self:UIBlur)
	for _,Connection in self._Connections do
		Connection:Disconnect()
	end
	self._Part:Destroy()
end

table.sort(Corners,function(a,b)
	return tonumber(a.Name) < tonumber(b.Name)
end)

workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function()
	Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
end)
if script:GetAttribute("AutoAddEnabled") then
	CheckForBlurEffect()
end
Player.PlayerGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(Descendant)
	if script:GetAttribute("AutoAddEnabled") then
		IsBlurEffectValid(Descendant)
	end
end)
script:GetAttributeChangedSignal("AutoAddEnabled"):Connect(function()
	if script:GetAttribute("AutoAddEnabled") then
		CheckForBlurEffect()
	end
end)

return UIBlur
